# CET-4 Vocabulary Stories - assembled manuscript

- chapters: 137
- each chapter carries its English story, its Chinese translation,
  five exercises, and five answer records with explanations.

## CH001 - New Ground: A Signal / 新的起点：一个信号

- theme: 大学生活与个人成长

**1.** Three months ago, Lin Wei had imagined university as a quiet place where success would depend on private effort. Yet on a bright autumn morning, he stood outside Harbor City University's registration hall with a cell phone in one hand and a paper map in the other. The university had recently released an orientation app, but it kept sending students to a locked door. Lately, Lin had tried to appear calm and youthful. Now he watched a creative crowd invent its own directions.

三个月前，林伟曾把大学想象成一个安静的地方，成功全靠个人努力。然而在一个明亮的秋日早晨，他站在港城大学注册大厅外，一手拿着手机，一手拿着纸质地图。学校最近发布了一款迎新应用，但它不断把学生引向一扇上锁的门。最近林伟一直努力显得镇定而有朝气，此刻却看到一群富有创意的人各自发明路线。

**2.** “Good lord, there are plenty of maps, but none of them agree,” said Maya Chen, who was eating a noodle from a paper cup. A visitor nearby waved an unlit cigar and complained that the smoggy air made every building look alike. On one map, a grassy path crossed land marked for drought research; on another, the same path ended beside a tidal laboratory. The campus signs did little to illustrate which route was open.

“天哪，地图多得很，却没有两张说法一致。”陈曼雅一边从纸杯里夹起一根面条一边说。一位访客挥着一支没有点燃的雪茄，抱怨烟雾弥漫的空气让所有建筑看起来都一样。一张地图上的草地小路穿过标为干旱研究区的土地；另一张地图却让同一条路终止在潮汐实验室旁。校园标识完全没有说明哪条路可以通行。

**3.** Then the registration kiosk broke. Its screen automatically moved upward through a list of error messages, including one called “address normalization.” A picky administrator told everyone to conform to the printed instructions. Maya gave an exclamatory reply—“How?”—and asked Lin to summarize the problem. In language class, an infinitive might be easy to identify; in a crowd, “to register” was harder to achieve. Lin used the maps to illustrate the contradiction instead of arguing about blame.

随后注册终端坏了。屏幕自动向上滚动一串错误信息，其中一项叫作“地址规范化”。一名挑剔的管理员让所有人遵照纸质说明。曼雅用感叹的语气反问：“怎么遵照？”并请林伟概括问题。在语言课上，辨认不定式也许很容易；但在人群中，“去注册”却很难实现。林伟用几张地图说明矛盾，没有争论该怪谁。

**4.** A volunteer stretched a rope across the unsafe path, and the line of students moved steadily toward the wrong building. Some looked helpless. Lin expected Maya to brag about finding the error first, but she was supportive. Together they made a simple sign, then added photographs to the app. Each clear image could strengthen one instruction and reinforce the next. Their correction worked with surprising rapidity.

一名志愿者用绳子拦住危险小路，学生队伍却稳定地向错误的大楼移动，有些人显得无助。林伟以为曼雅会吹嘘自己最先发现错误，但她非常支持他。他们一起做了一块简易指示牌，又在应用里加入照片。每张清晰图片都能加强一条说明，并巩固下一条。修正以惊人的速度奏效。

**5.** The old maps were theirs to criticize, but the new solution would be theirs to maintain. Near a wall of remembrance, under a starry design painted by former students, Maya said that a useful system should inspire confidence rather than reward the people who excel at guessing. Lin admitted that silence would have been cowardice. A boastful solution could not dignify its users, and technology alone could not civilize an institution. Their first design had to be adaptive: it should learn from mistakes, purify bad data, and respect people who needed help. That small decision became the beginning of their shared project.

旧地图是前辈们制作的，他们可以批评；新方案则属于他们，也要由他们维护。在一面纪念墙旁、历届学生绘制的星空图案下，曼雅说，一个有用的系统应当激发信心，而不是只奖励擅长猜测的人。林伟承认，保持沉默会是一种怯懦。自夸的方案不能赋予使用者尊严，技术本身也不能使机构文明。第一次设计必须具有适应性：从错误中学习、清除坏数据，并尊重需要帮助的人。这个小决定成了他们共同项目的起点。

### Exercises

- CH001-Q1 [context] In paragraph 3, what does “conform” most nearly mean?
  - options: follow a rule / draw a map / break a machine / leave a crowd
- CH001-Q2 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: reinforce an instruction / reinforce a noodle / reinforce a visitor / reinforce an autumn
- CH001-Q3 [cloze] The design had to be ______ so it could learn from mistakes.
  - options: adaptive / starry / picky / tidal
- CH001-Q4 [meaning] Which word means “概括”?
  - options: summarize / purify / brag / dignify
- CH001-Q5 [recall] Complete the phrase: ______ confidence.
  - options: inspire / broke / conform / depend

### Answers

- CH001-Q1: follow a rule - The administrator asked everyone to follow the printed instructions.
- CH001-Q2: reinforce an instruction - Reinforce commonly means strengthen an idea, rule, or structure.
- CH001-Q3: adaptive - An adaptive design changes in response to new information.
- CH001-Q4: summarize - Summarize means express the main points briefly.
- CH001-Q5: inspire - Inspire confidence is a common collocation.

## CH002 - New Ground: The First Promise / 新的起点：第一次承诺

- theme: 大学生活与个人成长

**1.** Three weeks ago, Lin Wei still believed that a university ran on official notices. Recently he had begun to doubt it. Lately, every evening, a new complaint appeared in the group chat: a locked door, a missing sign, a laboratory that no map admitted. Plenty of people gave up. A freshman from a provincial town stood beside a broken printer on the upper floor of the library and asked a stranger where the lecture hall was. Autumn rain made every wrong turn worse. Lin promised Maya that their project would not depend on anyone else.

三周前，林伟还相信大学是靠官方通知运转的。最近他开始怀疑这一点。近来，每天傍晚群聊里都会出现新的抱怨：一扇锁住的门、一块缺失的指示牌、一间任何地图都不承认的实验室。很多人干脆放弃了。一名来自外省的新生站在图书馆上层一台坏掉的打印机旁，向陌生人打听报告厅在哪里。秋雨让每一次走错都更加难熬。林伟向陈曼雅承诺：他们的项目不会依赖任何人的许可。

**2.** On Tuesday they wrote the promise on a whiteboard in a borrowed club room. Their idea was simple: keep one honest map of campus information and change it whenever reality changed. Professor Zhao, who held the faculty chair for first-year studies, read it twice. “Your map,” he said, “is a hypothesis. Good lord, leadership is not a scholarship or an allowance. A senator or a statesman may sponsor a report, but a map must answer to the student standing in the rain.” Maya thought the remark was subjective; Lin thought it was a test.

周二，他们在一间借来的社团活动室里把承诺写在白板上。他们的想法很简单：维护一份诚实的校园信息地图，只要现实改变就跟着改。为一年级课程担任院系负责人的赵教授看了两遍。“你们的地图，”他说，“只是一个假设。天哪，领导力既不是奖学金也不是零用钱。参议员或政治家可以为一份报告做赞助人，但地图必须对站在雨里的学生负责。”曼雅觉得这番话太主观；林伟觉得这只是初步的考验。

**3.** Their second meeting produced a rate they could defend. The survey had recorded age and sex, but not the need for a ramp. Of two hundred doors in the preliminary count, a measurable proportion opened only for people who knew the trick. A doctorate, a bat, a lion on a crest, or a pair of wet shoes made no difference to the lock. The rear entrance had its own flow of traffic, and nobody had ever recorded it. “Personally,” Maya said, “I do not believe in anticipation. I believe in a note, a number, and a witness.”

第二次会议得出了一个他们能站得住脚的比例。问卷记录了年龄和性别，却没有记录对坡道的需求。在初步统计的两百扇门里，有相当比例只对已经知道诀窍的人打开。博士学位、球棒、纹章上的狮子，或者一双湿鞋，对那把锁毫无影响。后门有自己的人流，却从没有人记录过。“就我个人而言，”曼雅说，“我不相信预感。我相信一条记录、一个数字和一名证人。”

**4.** Lin began to doubt his own originality. A creator needs an original thought, not a semicolon in the right place. Until midnight he sat with the record, and nothing in it looked like anything a designer would admire. He raised his hand once to speak and put it down again. Anyway, the map was not art; it was a promise that had to survive a closed door.

林伟开始怀疑自己的原创能力。创造者需要的是原创的想法，而不是一个位置正确的分号。他对着记录一直坐到午夜，里面没有任何东西像设计师会欣赏的作品。他有一次举手想发言，又放了下来。无论如何，这张地图不是艺术品，而是一个必须在关着的门面前存活下来的承诺。

**5.** On Friday the club agreed to help, because Maya had been supportive. Their support would reinforce the map and strengthen the case for a small budget. She used three photographs to illustrate the failure, and the group voted to conform to one rule: report only what a student sees. A rope across the unsafe path meant that nobody would automatically follow the crowd. Progress came steadily, and they began to believe they could achieve something real before the term review. Their work began to inspire first-year students; even Lin admitted a creative solution was worth it.

周五，社团同意帮忙，因为曼雅从一开始就非常支持。他们的支持会加强这张地图，也会强化申请小额预算的理由。她用三张照片说明故障，大家投票一致同意遵守一条规则：只报告学生真正看得见的东西。危险小路上一根绳子意味着不会有人再自动跟着人群走。此后进展稳定，他们开始相信自己能在学期评审之前做成一件真实的事。他们的工作开始鼓舞一年级学生，连林伟也承认，一个有创意的方案值得付出这些努力。

### Exercises

- CH002-Q1 [context] What did the group promise each other?
  - options: a map that does not depend on permission / a new scholarship / a larger club room / a printed textbook
- CH002-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “预感；预期”?
  - options: anticipation / proportion / semicolon / allowance
- CH002-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: reinforce the case / reinforce the rain / reinforce a printer / reinforce midnight
- CH002-Q4 [cloze] Progress came ______ after the first photographs were shown.
  - options: steadily / subjective / provincial / rear
- CH002-Q5 [recall] What did the group decide to report?
  - options: only what a student can see / every rumour in the chat / the professor's opinion / the cost of the rope

### Answers

- CH002-Q1: a map that does not depend on permission - Lin promised that their project would not depend on anyone else's permission.
- CH002-Q2: anticipation - Maya said she did not believe in anticipation, only in notes, numbers, and witnesses.
- CH002-Q3: reinforce the case - Reinforce commonly strengthens an argument, a structure, or a habit.
- CH002-Q4: steadily - Progress advanced without sudden changes after the evidence was shown.
- CH002-Q5: only what a student can see - The rule limited reports to first-hand observation.

## CH003 - New Ground: A Missing Voice / 新的起点：缺席的声音

- theme: 大学生活与个人成长

**1.** A group of folk who worked the evening shift often passed the club room without stopping. Obviously they had something to say, and neither Lin nor Maya had asked them. Maya walked along the queue with her notebook, raised her hand to greet each worker, and kept a diary of every remark she heard. She was willing to evaluate the evidence without defending her own idea. “Regarding the map,” one worker said, “you are asking the wrong people. Your notes record where the doors are, but not who cannot reach them.” The two problems were equally serious.

一群上晚班的劳动者常常经过社团活动室却从不进来。显然他们有话要说，而林伟和曼雅都没有问过他们。曼雅拿着笔记本沿队伍走过去，举手向每位工人打招呼，把她听到的每句评论都记进日记。她愿意评估证据，而不是维护自己的想法。“关于这张地图，”一位工人说，“你们问错了人。你们的记录写着门在哪里，却没写谁走不到那里。”这两个问题同样严重。

**2.** On Thursday a drunken man in a worn coat leaned sideways against the railing and exclaimed that the east gate had no ramp at all. He was not resistant to help; he was simply accustomed to being asked nothing. When Maya said goodbye, he added, aloud, that the queue needed a suitable ramp and that the gossip in the line was more informative than any official notice. She wrote it down. Anything else, she decided, would be guesswork, and anyway a survey that ignores a queue is not a survey. The form asked for age and sex before it asked whether the door opened.

周四，一个穿着旧外套的醉汉斜靠在栏杆上，大声说东门根本没有坡道。他并不抗拒帮助，只是习惯了没有人问他。曼雅道别时，他又出声补充：队伍需要一条合适的坡道，而且队伍里的闲话比任何官方通知都更有信息量。她把话记了下来。她认定，其他任何做法都只是猜测；无论如何，一份忽略排队的调查算不上调查。表格先问年龄和性别，之后才问门是否能打开。

**3.** The path to the east gate ran down a steep slope with a geological fault under the paving. It was picturesque in photographs and useless in rain. On ceremonial days the gate opened for guests; on ordinary days it awaited a guard. “Liberty,” Maya wrote in her diary, “is a door that opens without a ceremony.” Professor Zhao read the sentence and said that an original observation is worth more than a rate with three decimal places.

通往东门的小路沿一段陡坡向下，铺路石下还有一条地质断层。它在照片里风景如画，在雨里却毫无用处。仪式日大门为来宾开放；平常日子它只等一名警卫。“自由，”曼雅在日记里写道，“是一扇不需要仪式就能打开的门。”赵教授读了这句话，说一段原创的观察比一个精确到小数点后三位的比例更有价值。

**4.** Lin was inclined to be forgiving. The mistake was divisive only if they hid it, and a secretive report would look creditable while proving nothing. With modesty, they listed what they did not know. The submissive tone of the old notice, the stuffy room where it was written, and the joyful assumption that everything worked — all of it pointed onward to one eventuality: someone would be left outside, and their promise of change would never reach that person.

林伟倾向于宽容。这个错误只有在他们隐瞒时才会造成分裂，而一份偷偷摸摸的报告看起来可信，却什么也证明不了。他们带着谦逊，把不知道的部分也列了出来。旧通知里那种顺从的语气、写它时那间闷热的屋子、以及一切运转正常的快乐假设——所有这些都指向同一个可能：有人会被留在外面，而他们的承诺永远到不了那个人身上。

**5.** Maya wiped the whiteboard and turned it outwards so the corridor could read it. She was fond of the small map, but she was impatient with its silence, and she had thought about the missing voice until midnight. The work was not rewarding in the way a score is rewarding; it was rewarding because a stranger could now find the gate. Lin felt contented for the first time that month. “Otherwise,” Maya said, “we are only decorating a wall.”

曼雅擦掉白板，把它转向外侧，好让走廊里的人都能看到。她喜欢这张小地图，却对它的沉默感到不耐烦；她一直想到午夜，想着那个缺席的声音。这份工作不像得分那样令人有回报感；它的回报在于陌生人现在能找到大门了。林伟那个月第一次感到满足。“否则，”曼雅说，“我们只是在装饰一面墙。”

### Exercises

- CH003-Q1 [context] Why did the worker say the group was asking the wrong people?
  - options: the notes ignored who could not reach the doors / the club room was too small / the map was too colourful / the professor was absent
- CH003-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “谦逊”?
  - options: modesty / liberty / slope / gossip
- CH003-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: a suitable ramp / a suitable gossip / a suitable diary / a suitable semicolon
- CH003-Q4 [cloze] The east gate was ______ in photographs and useless in rain.
  - options: picturesque / creditable / stuffy / submissive
- CH003-Q5 [recall] What did Maya do with the whiteboard?
  - options: turned it outwards for the corridor / erased the whole map / sold it to the club / covered it with a coat

### Answers

- CH003-Q1: the notes ignored who could not reach the doors - The worker said the notes recorded where doors were, not who could not reach them.
- CH003-Q2: modesty - They listed what they did not know, which is an act of modesty.
- CH003-Q3: a suitable ramp - Suitable describes something that fits a need, such as a ramp at a gate.
- CH003-Q4: picturesque - The path looked attractive in photographs but failed in rain.
- CH003-Q5: turned it outwards for the corridor - She wanted everyone passing the corridor to read the corrected map.

## CH004 - New Ground: Evidence on the Table / 新的起点：摆上桌面的证据

- theme: 大学生活与个人成长

**1.** Among the three hundred replies, only a hundred described a door that failed. Yes, those answers also included complaints about buses, and the amount of noise in the chat was remarkable. “A survey is not evidence,” Professor Zhao said, “until you can characterize what it leaves out.” Three people came to the table in person: a girl from a suburban district, a waitress who worked nights, and a lass who cleaned the laboratories before dawn.

在三百份回复中，只有一百份描述了打不开的门。是的，那些回答里也包含对公交的抱怨，群聊里的噪声量大得惊人。“问卷不是证据，”赵教授说，“直到你能说清它漏掉了什么。”有三个人亲自来到桌边：一名来自郊区行政区的女孩、一名上夜班的女服务员，还有一名天亮前打扫实验室的姑娘。

**2.** The lady who ran the hostel and a gentleman from the alumni office sat with them. Mister Chen, the porter, brought a plate of ice cream, and the room began to brighten. Nobody altered the record. Whichever version they chose, the merit of the argument depended on one costly detail: the ramp had never been built. Supposedly it existed in a plan from 2019, and the glory of that plan lasted exactly one afternoon.

经营宿舍的女士和一位校友会的先生和他们坐在一起。门房陈先生端来一盘冰淇淋，房间开始亮堂起来。没有人改动记录。无论他们选哪个版本，论点的价值都取决于一个代价高昂的细节：那条坡道从未建成。据说它存在于2019年的一份图纸里，而那份图纸的荣光只维持了一个下午。

**3.** Maya kept her grasp on the practical question. A seedling in a cracked pot stood on the windowsill, and its odorous soil filled the small room. She had no talent for algebra and no interest in an empress or an ornament; she wanted a graceful sentence and a working door. At dusk she watched the light change and momentarily lost the thread of her own argument.

曼雅牢牢抓住实际问题。窗台上有一盆裂了的花盆里的幼苗，带着气味的泥土弥漫在小房间里。她对代数毫无天赋，对皇后或装饰品也毫无兴趣；她想要的是一句优美的句子和一扇能用的门。黄昏时她看着光线变化，一时间失去了自己论证的线索。

**4.** A waitress told them that an angry customer had once tried to force the gate. He had told the locked door to fuck off, and the room went quiet for a moment. Amidst the laughter that followed, the point stood: a locked door makes people angry. Lest they forget it, Maya pinned the shortcoming of the old design to the wall. An ornamental hostess trolley, a manly poster about endurance, and a photograph of an old empire were no substitute for a ramp.

一名女服务员告诉他们，曾有一位愤怒的顾客试图强行推开大门。他对着锁住的门骂了一句脏话，房间里安静了片刻。在随之而来的笑声中，那个要点依然成立：锁住的门会让人愤怒。为了不让他们忘掉，曼雅把旧设计的缺点钉在墙上。一辆装饰用的女主人推车、一张关于忍耐的阳刚海报、一张旧帝国的照片，都替代不了一条坡道。

**5.** The team agreed to familiarize themselves with the site before the next meeting. Maya, who often walked along the corridor after dusk, was willing to test every door herself. Wherever a door failed, they would mark it with a creative, suitable tag, equally visible by day and by night. Regarding the old plan, she said: “Good lord, we have to illustrate the failures, not merely list them.” A hilly district and a suburban one had produced completely different answers, and the saving in effort was the real discovery. Recently they had learned this lesson the hard way; otherwise they would still be arguing about posters. “Obviously,” Lin said, “we cannot depend on a plan drawn ago by people who never came here.” Plenty of folk had said the same thing for years; neither of them had listened, and the evidence was on the table, though they had yet to achieve anything.

团队约定在下一次会议前先熟悉现场。玛雅常在黄昏后沿着走廊走，她愿意亲自去试每一扇门。哪里的门出了问题，他们就在那里贴上一个有创意又合适的标签，让人白天和夜里都同样看得清。说到那张旧图纸，她说：“老天，我们得把失败之处讲清楚，而不只是把它们列出来。”一个丘陵学区和一个是郊区学区给出了完全不同的答案，而真正的发现是省下来的那些力气。最近他们才以艰难的方式学到这一课；否则他们到现在还在为海报争吵。“显而易见，”林伟说，“我们不能依赖一张由从未到过这里的人很久以前画出的图纸。”很多普通人多年来都说过同样的话。他们俩谁也没听，而证据现在终于摆上了桌面。他们还没有做成任何事，但问题本身已经变了。

### Exercises

- CH004-Q1 [context] What did Professor Zhao say a survey must explain?
  - options: what it leaves out / who paid for it / how long the chat was / where the ice cream came from
- CH004-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “优点；价值”?
  - options: merit / dusk / slope / grasp
- CH004-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: familiarize themselves with the site / familiarize themselves with the ice cream / familiarize themselves with the laughter / familiarize themselves with the waitress
- CH004-Q4 [cloze] The ramp had never been built, so the plan was a ______ detail.
  - options: costly / graceful / odorous / manly
- CH004-Q5 [recall] What did Maya pin to the wall?
  - options: the shortcoming of the old design / a plate of ice cream / the hostel key / a seedling

### Answers

- CH004-Q1: what it leaves out - He judged a survey by its silences, not only by its numbers.
- CH004-Q2: merit - The merit of the argument rested on whether the ramp existed.
- CH004-Q3: familiarize themselves with the site - Familiarize with is used for getting to know a place or a system.
- CH004-Q4: costly - The missing ramp was the expensive detail the plan ignored.
- CH004-Q5: the shortcoming of the old design - She pinned the weakness of the old plan where everyone could see it.

## CH005 - New Ground: The Trial Run / 新的起点：第一次试行

- theme: 大学生活与个人成长

**1.** The trial run began on a crowded Monday. Three of them stood at the west gate with a hundred printed cards and asked every visitor a single question. The idea was to keep the test small, adapt it hourly, and describe the result without decorating it. “Yes, it is slow,” Maya admitted, reading the card again. “Those answers change nothing until we can show them to someone else, and your card is the only proof we have.” Their consent form was one sentence long, written for a stranger in a hurry, and the response rate was also better than anyone expected.

试行在一个拥挤的周一开始。他们三个人站在西门，拿着一百张打印卡片，向每位路人问同一个问题。想法是把测试做小、每小时调整一次，并如实描述结果而不加修饰。“是的，这很慢，”曼雅承认，又把卡片读了一遍，“这些回答什么都改变不了，除非我们能拿给别人看。”他们的同意书只有一句话，是为赶时间的陌生人写的，而回复率比任何人预想的都高。

**2.** By midnight the group had collected me a folder of handwritten answers. A girl drew a clock going clockwise and wrote that the new route saved her four minutes. Maya laughed happily, hugged her, and said that such a small, meaningful result was the first real asset the project owned. A quick photo shoot followed, and no harm came of it. The novelty would fade, she warned her friends, but the loneliness of a student standing at a locked gate would not.

到午夜时，小组已经为我收集了一整袋手写回答。一个女孩画了一个顺时针走的钟，并写道新路线为她省下四分钟。曼雅开心地笑了，抱了抱她，说这样一个小小的、有意义的结果是这个项目拥有的第一份真实资产。随后他们快速拍了几张照片，并没有造成任何损害。她提醒朋友们，新鲜感会消退，但一个学生站在锁住的大门前那种孤独不会。

**3.** A collaborative mood was prevalent that week. Two of them went to a winery on the edge of the city to interview drivers, and the manager spoke openly about a knotty problem: the road was well off in summer and impassable in winter. Among the drivers, a dynasty of local growers had kept the same habit for a century, and no recyclable sign had ever been posted for the convenience of visitors. The valley was magnificent, and its magnificence made the failure easy to forgive; that was exactly the danger.

那一周里合作的氛围很普遍。他们中有两个人去了城市边缘的一家葡萄酒厂采访司机，经理坦率地谈起一个棘手的问题：这条路夏天富足好走，冬天却无法通行。在司机当中，一个本地种植者家族把同一个习惯保持了一个世纪，也从来没有为方便来访者设立过可回收的标识。山谷的壮丽让人很容易原谅这次失败，而这恰恰是危险所在。

**4.** Someone had tried to mislead the team with an imitative map copied from an imperialist survey. It was graphically beautiful and barely distinguishable from the real thing. Maya did not misunderstand the intention, but she refused to cherish a document that would pollute the record. A heartfelt apology arrived the next morning, and it was regrettable that it came after the delay rather than before it. She raised her hand at the meeting and asked for the source of every line.

有人试图用一张模仿自帝国主义时期测绘的仿制地图误导团队。它图面漂亮，几乎与真图无法区分。曼雅没有误解对方的意图，但她拒绝珍视一份会污染记录的文书。第二天早上来了一封由衷的道歉，遗憾的是它来得太晚，赶在延误之后而不是之前。她在会上举手，要求说明每一条线的来源。

**5.** They agreed to reunite the two halves of the survey before the term review and to assess the feasibility of a permanent sign. Though the work accelerated quickly, they chose a leisurely evening to check every card anew and to congratulate the volunteers who had fled the rain with them. “Anything else,” Lin said, standing where the old ramp should have been, “can wait until tomorrow.”

他们商定在学期评审前把调查的两部分重新合并，并评估设置长期指示牌的可行性。尽管工作迅速加快，他们还是选了一个悠闲的傍晚，重新核对每张卡片，并向那些和他们一起躲过雨的志愿者表示祝贺。“其他任何事，”林伟站在本该有坡道的地方说，“都可以等到明天。”

### Exercises

- CH005-Q1 [context] Why did the group keep the first test small?
  - options: to adapt it hourly and describe it honestly / to avoid talking to visitors / to save the printed cards / to finish before midnight
- CH005-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “可行性”?
  - options: feasibility / magnificence / novelty / consent
- CH005-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: a heartfelt apology / a clockwise apology / a recyclable apology / a knotty apology
- CH005-Q4 [cloze] The copied map was barely ______ from the real one.
  - options: distinguishable / well off / leisurely / prevalent
- CH005-Q5 [recall] What did Maya refuse to do?
  - options: cherish a document that would pollute the record / interview the drivers / hug the girl / assess the feasibility

### Answers

- CH005-Q1: to adapt it hourly and describe it honestly - The small test let them change the method quickly and report it plainly.
- CH005-Q2: feasibility - They had to judge whether a permanent sign could actually be built.
- CH005-Q3: a heartfelt apology - Heartfelt describes sincere feeling, which fits an apology.
- CH005-Q4: distinguishable - The copied map looked almost identical to the genuine one.
- CH005-Q5: cherish a document that would pollute the record - She rejected the false map rather than keeping it as evidence.

## CH006 - New Ground: Pressure Builds / 新的起点：压力上升

- theme: 大学生活与个人成长

**1.** The second week brought the first real pressure. Maya asked why the project had to grow, and Lin could not answer. A new opportunity appeared: the student union offered a suitable room and a camera, but only if the group promised more work than they could sustain. “We cannot afford to rely on a favor,” Maya said. “We need an honest system, not a kind one.” She was equally tired; to her delight the union also sent digestive biscuits for the folk on the night shift.

第二周带来了第一次真正的压力。曼雅问这个项目为什么必须扩张，林伟答不上来。同一天早上出现了一个新机会：学生会愿意提供一间合适的房间和一台相机，但前提是小组承诺做超出他们承受能力的工作。“我们承担不起靠人情办事的代价，”曼雅说，“我们需要一个诚实的系统，而不是一个善良的系统。”她同样疲惫，而让她高兴的是，学生会还给上夜班的普通人送去了一盘消化饼干。

**2.** Their confidence was smaller than their ambition. Lin tried to assure her that faith in a good idea would be enough, but he did not sound confident, and she was too tired to cheer him. “I am sad,” she admitted, “not discouraged.” The distinction mattered: discouragement ends work, and sadness only slows it. An envious volunteer said the same thing in a less merciful tone, and Maya answered with an appreciative nod. They had to evaluate the plan again that night.

他们的信心小于他们的雄心。林伟试图向她保证：只要对一个好想法有信心就够了；但他听起来并不自信，而她也累得没力气鼓励他。“我很难过，”她承认，“但不是气馁。”这个区别很重要：气馁会终止工作，难过只会让它变慢。一位心怀嫉妒的志愿者用不那么慈悲的口气说了同样的话，曼雅则以感激的点头回应。那天晚上他们不得不再次评估这份计划。

**3.** Money was the next rival. A discount on printing expired, and the sum looked like rubbish against their budget. A woman from the union called their plan encouraging and then asked for a guarantee they could not give. “Do not exploit goodwill,” she said. “It will not renew itself.” Her manner was gracious, and her warning gave Maya a small boost of admiration for the project's own kindness. The union called its own new logo sexy; Maya called it a distraction. Regarding the money, she wrote, the project was already behind.

钱是下一个对手。印刷折扣到期，那笔钱在预算面前看起来像垃圾。学生会的一位女士称他们的方案令人鼓舞，随后又要求一个他们给不出的保证。“不要滥用善意，”她说，“它不会自行更新。”她的举止很亲切，而她这句警告让曼雅对这个项目自身的善意多了一份敬意。学生会说自己的新标志很性感；曼雅说那只是干扰。关于钱，她写道，项目已经落后了。

**4.** Two volunteers began to dislike the crowded evenings. Obviously the evenings were too long. They were alike in one way only: neither could be relied on after midnight, and both often said so. Their reasons did not resemble each other. The group's accord held, though a wary silence crept in. Lin was grateful for the honest statement and sorry for its cause. A lonely walk home along the river made the choice clearer, not easier. “Give me one more week,” he said again.

两名志愿者开始厌恶拥挤的夜晚。显然这些夜晚太长了。他们只有一个共同点：午夜之后谁都靠不住，而两人也常常这么说。他们的理由彼此并不相似。小组的共识还在，尽管一种警惕的沉默慢慢渗了进来。林伟感激那句诚实的话，也为它的起因感到抱歉。沿着河边独自走回家的路并没有让选择更容易，只是让它更清楚。“再给我一周，”他又说了一遍。

**5.** Spring arrived late and snowy. In an appealing, hospitable voice, Professor Zhao reminded them that a project ripens like fruit: slowly, and only if someone waters it. His disapproval of their rush was gentle but complete, and the sorrowful faces in the room told him it had landed. “You are mistaken about the cost,” he said. “The goal is not.” They agreed to re-evaluate the plan, recycle the wasted printing, and describe their limits to every willing volunteer. Otherwise the next version would fail where this one did, and no photo shoot would hide it. The consent form, they decided, was their only real asset.

春天来得晚，还下着雪。赵教授用动人而好客的语气提醒他们：项目像果实一样成熟——很慢，而且只有有人浇水才行。他对他们急躁的不赞成温和却彻底，屋里那些悲伤的脸说明这话已经落地。“你们弄错的不是目标，”他说，“而是代价。”他们同意重新评估计划、回收浪费的印刷品，并向每一位愿意听的志愿者说明自己的限度。否则下一个版本会在这个版本失败的地方原样失败，而再多的拍照也掩盖不了。他们认定，同意书是他们唯一真正的资产。

### Exercises

- CH006-Q1 [context] What did the union want in exchange for the room and camera?
  - options: more work than the group could sustain / a share of the map / a signed consent form / a weekly photo shoot
- CH006-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “气馁；使泄气”?
  - options: discourage / sustain / renew / recycle
- CH006-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: exploit goodwill / exploit a biscuit / exploit the snow / exploit a discount
- CH006-Q4 [cloze] Professor Zhao's ______ of their rush was gentle but complete.
  - options: disapproval / admiration / confidence / delight
- CH006-Q5 [recall] What did the group call their only real asset?
  - options: the consent form / the camera / the discount / the logo

### Answers

- CH006-Q1: more work than the group could sustain - The union attached a heavier workload to its offer.
- CH006-Q2: discourage - Maya said she was sad but not discouraged, meaning her will to continue survived.
- CH006-Q3: exploit goodwill - Exploit is used with abstract resources such as goodwill or trust.
- CH006-Q4: disapproval - He did not approve of their haste, though he expressed it mildly.
- CH006-Q5: the consent form - They decided the one-sentence consent form was the only asset they truly owned.

## CH007 - New Ground: The Disagreement / 新的起点：分歧

- theme: 大学生活与个人成长

**1.** By March the map had grown into an argument. A college leader wanted to hand it to the university administration; a student group wanted to keep it personal. “A president signs a policy,” Maya said, “but a student stands in the rain.” She meant that two priorities could not both come first, and that a student with wet hair in a thin coat had no vote in either plan. The disagreement was not about ability; it was about who owned the story, and individualism was not the enemy of a shared map — silence was.

到三月，那张地图已经变成一场争论。一位学院领导想把它交给学校行政部门；一个学生团体想让它保持个人色彩。“校长可以签署一项政策，”曼雅说，“但学生是站在雨里的那个人。”她的意思是：两种优先事项不可能同时排第一，而一个头发湿透、只穿薄外套的学生在两种方案里都没有投票权。分歧不在于能力，而在于故事归谁所有；个人主义并不是共享地图的敌人——沉默才是。

**2.** On campus the argument had a history. A senior dean with a grey beard blew a small horn for tradition, and a young lecturer brushed the dust off an old statistic: the rise of youth enrolment had changed the undergraduate body, and each dormitory now held a different idea of adulthood. A young woman in the dormitory laughed at the horn, and Maya wrote her words down. Neither a gown nor a lecture could settle the question of identity.

在校园里，这场争论有它的历史。一位留着灰胡子的资深院长为传统吹响小号，一位年轻讲师则刷掉了旧统计数字上的灰尘：青年入学人数的上升改变了本科生群体，每栋宿舍楼现在都抱着不同的成年观。宿舍里的一位年轻女生被小号逗笑了，曼雅把她说的话记了下来。无论是学位袍还是一场讲座，都无法解决身份这个问题。

**3.** Lin's major was environmental engineering, and he trusted numbers. Maya studied journalism, and she trusted persons. “An individual is not a statistic,” she said. Professor Zhao called this the dense nucleus of the problem: growth has an extension, a personality, and a cost. In the spring the campus turned green, and a poster about nutrition and protein looked more urgent than a lecture on cultivation.

林伟的专业是环境工程，他相信数字。曼雅学的是新闻，她相信人。“个人不是统计数字，”她说。赵教授称这是问题的致密核心：增长有它的外延、它的性格，也有它的代价。春天校园变绿，一张关于营养和蛋白质的海报看起来比一堂关于耕作培养的讲座更紧迫。

**4.** “You promote the map,” Maya said, “and they promote themselves.” A rag of an old notice, mold on the wall behind it, and a letter from an academician were the only evidence the office had produced. There was no promotion and no budget line, only a rush to finish before the summer. Maya's grandmother had died of cancer the year before; she did not say it to win the argument, but the room went quiet when she mentioned the bus that took two hours.

“你们在推广这张地图，”曼雅说，“而他们在推广自己。”一张破布似的旧通知、它背后墙上的霉斑，以及一位院士的来信，就是办公室拿出的全部证据。没有推广、没有预算科目，只有赶在夏天之前完成的仓促。曼雅的祖母前一年死于癌症；她说起这件事不是为了赢得争论，但当她提到那趟要坐两小时的公交车时，房间安静了。

**5.** The second opportunity came from the alumni office itself. Among the three hundred replies, a hundred had come from people who could not afford a taxi. “Why do those numbers matter?” a gentleman from that office asked. “Because a lady in the dorm and a girl in the laboratory rely on the same bus,” Maya said. The group agreed to develop the map in two versions, to alter nothing without asking, and also to publish its cost. Confidence, Lin wrote, is not faith; it is the amount of evidence you are willing to show. Professor Zhao encouraged them, and the answer to the disagreement was yes and no at once.

第二个机会来自校友会本身。在三百份回复中，有一百份来自坐不起出租车的人。“这些数字为什么重要？”校友会的一位先生问。“因为宿舍里的女士和实验室里的女孩依赖同一趟公交，”曼雅说。小组同意把地图做成两个版本、未经询问不改动任何内容，并且也公布它的成本。林伟写道：信心不是信仰，而是你愿意展示的证据总量。赵教授鼓励了他们，而这场分歧的答案是“是”与“否”同时成立。

### Exercises

- CH007-Q1 [context] What was the disagreement really about?
  - options: who owned the story / how to print the map / which bus to take / who would pay for the camera
- CH007-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “耕作；培养”?
  - options: cultivation / extension / promotion / nucleus
- CH007-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: develop the map / develop a beard / develop a horn / develop a gown
- CH007-Q4 [cloze] The ______ of youth enrolment had changed the undergraduate body.
  - options: rise / rag / rush / mold
- CH007-Q5 [recall] What did the group agree to publish?
  - options: the cost of the map / the dean's letter / the grandmother's story / the alumni list

### Answers

- CH007-Q1: who owned the story - The conflict was about ownership, not about the group's ability.
- CH007-Q2: cultivation - Cultivation appeared beside nutrition in the poster about growth.
- CH007-Q3: develop the map - Develop naturally combines with a product, plan, or map.
- CH007-Q4: rise - A rise in enrolment changed who studied at the university.
- CH007-Q5: the cost of the map - They decided to publish the real cost alongside the map.

## CH008 - New Ground: What the Numbers Hide / 新的起点：数字隐藏了什么

- theme: 大学生活与个人成长

**1.** The numbers were brand new and, yeah, they also seemed to contradict each other. A councilor praised the rise in enrolment; a pensioner in the same queue said the buses had not improved at all. “Your figures are convincing,” Professor Zhao said, “but they cannot describe what a commuter feels when the bus never comes. You ought to ask what the numbers hide.”

这些数字是崭新的，而且，是的，它们还自相矛盾。一位议员称赞入学率的上升；同一条队伍里的一位领养老金者却说公交毫无改善。“你的数据很有说服力，”赵教授说，“但它们描述不了一个通勤者在公交永不到站时的感受。你应该问问数字藏起了什么。”

**2.** The team listed what they lacked. A single, vivid story is not enough; a hundred forgettable ones are not either. The complexity was real, and the schedule was merely tolerable. “The data vary by street,” Lin said, “and a smelly corridor can be more harmful than a missing sign. The corridor is virtually empty after eight, but the bus is incredibly slow all day.” A naughty child had once kicked the bag of a bully down the stairs; the incident was attributable to a broken lift, not to a bad temper.

团队列出了自己缺少的东西。一个生动的故事不够；一百个容易被遗忘的故事也不够。复杂性是真实的，而日程只是勉强可以忍受。“数据因街道而异，”林伟说，“一条发臭的走廊可能比一块缺失的指示牌更有害。八点以后走廊几乎空无一人，而公交车一整天都慢得难以置信。”一个顽皮的孩子曾把一个恶霸的书包踢下楼梯；这件事应归因于坏掉的电梯，而不是坏脾气。

**3.** Being truly independent was harder than it looked. A talented student could enhance one section, but a diverse team needed the flexibility to rewrite the rest. Accordingly, they invited a pensioner's group to check the route. “I shiver when I think of the old plan,” a comrade said. “It was predictable and useless.” There was a vacancy on the committee, and filling it would complicate everything.

真正独立比看上去更难。一位有天赋的学生可以改善某一部分，但一个多元的团队需要重写其余部分的灵活性。因此，他们邀请了一个领养老金者的团体来核查路线。“一想到旧方案我就发抖，”一位同志说，“它可预测，而且毫无用处。”委员会有一个空缺，而填补它会弄复杂一切。

**4.** The opponent of the project was not a person but a habit. A raging argument about an addictive game, a rumour about brutality in the dormitory, and an elective course on manners all drifted through the same group chat. Lin found it hurtful and, at first, unbelievable. “Am I nosy?” Maya asked. “No,” he said. “You are hopeful, and that is rare.”

项目的对手不是某个人，而是一种习惯。一场关于上瘾游戏的激烈争吵、一则关于宿舍暴行的传闻，以及一门关于礼仪的选修课，都在同一个群聊里飘来飘去。林伟觉得这伤人，起初也觉得难以置信。“我爱打听吗？”曼雅问。“不，”他说，“你有希望，而这很稀有。”

**5.** They agreed to improve the report, to enrol two more volunteers, and to enrich the sample with voices a spreadsheet cannot hold. The work was not glamorous; it was merely useful, though the queue was long. “Yeah,” Maya said, “a leader is someone who asks again.” She had grown used to the smelly corridor and the lady with the umbrella, and so had the president of the student union, who now stood beside them at the gate with wet hair and a personal note in his hand. Three months ago the same man had called the plan forgettable. “Good lord,” he said, “you have achieved something small. My major taught me to develop a model; you taught me to visit one. Give me the list.” The university press wanted a photograph for its college edition.

他们同意改进报告、再登记两名志愿者，并用电子表格装不下的声音来充实样本。这份工作并不光鲜，只是有用，尽管队伍很长。“是的，”曼雅说，“领导者就是那个会再问一次的人。”她已经习惯了那条发臭的走廊和那位撑伞的女士；学生会的会长也一样——他此刻就站在他们身旁的大门口，头发湿着，手里拿着一封私人短笺。三个月前，同一个人还说这个方案不值一提。“天哪，”他说，“你们做成了一件小事。我的专业教我开发模型，你们教我去走访一次。把名单给我。”校报想要一张照片用于学院版。

### Exercises

- CH008-Q1 [context] What did Professor Zhao say the figures could not do?
  - options: describe a commuter's experience / predict the enrolment / convince a councilor / measure a corridor
- CH008-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “可归因于…的”?
  - options: attributable / predictable / tolerable / forgettable
- CH008-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: enrich the sample / enrich the queue / enrich a shiver / enrich the store
- CH008-Q4 [cloze] The incident was ______ to a broken lift.
  - options: attributable / addictive / raging / nosy
- CH008-Q5 [recall] What did the president finally ask for?
  - options: the list / the umbrella / the photograph / the dormitory key

### Answers

- CH008-Q1: describe a commuter's experience - Numbers cannot carry what a person feels while waiting.
- CH008-Q2: attributable - The accident could be attributed to a broken lift rather than to temper.
- CH008-Q3: enrich the sample - Enrich is used when adding depth or quality to data or soil.
- CH008-Q4: attributable - Attributable means able to be explained by a particular cause.
- CH008-Q5: the list - He asked for the list after admitting the project had achieved something.

## CH009 - New Ground: A Better Question / 新的起点：更好的问题

- theme: 大学生活与个人成长

**1.** The better question arrived in the evening, on the back of a report. Which item on the list actually changes a decision? The number of complaints was a score; the quality of one private note was a factor. “A city is not a unit,” Maya said. “It is a league of small arrangements.” Her hand moved across the page and stopped at a single line.

更好的问题在傍晚到来，写在一份报告的背面。清单上哪一项真正改变决定？抱怨的数量只是一个分数；一条私人笔记的品质才是一个因子。“城市不是一个单位，”曼雅说，“它是许多小安排的联盟。”她的手在纸面上移动，停在单独一行上。

**2.** The club had spread its stuff across the table: a map, a gas bill, a cover from an old magazine, and a photograph of a soldier. The group had no property, no estate, and no independent income. Their pride was the only capital they had. “Where is the conflict?” Lin asked. “It is in the distance between what we measure and what people need.”

社团的东西摊了一桌：一张地图、一张燃气账单、一本旧杂志的封面，还有一张士兵的照片。小组没有财产、没有地产，也没有独立收入。他们的自尊是唯一的资本。“冲突在哪里？”林伟问。“它就在我们所测量的和人们所需要的之间的距离里。”

**3.** Mark the limit, then adjust the shape. A voluntary member of the committee, a captain of the rowing team, and a student who studied philosophy all said the same thing: the decline of trust had a consequence that no chart showed. The old colonial map was still on the wall under a protective sheet of plastic. Restraint, not enthusiasm, would decide whether they could keep their idea alive.

标出界限，然后调整形状。委员会里一位自愿参加的成员、赛艇队的队长，以及一名学哲学的学生都说了同一件事：信任的衰退有一个任何图表都显示不出的后果。那张旧的殖民地地图还挂在墙上，盖着一层保护性的塑料膜。决定他们能否让想法活下来的是克制，而不是热情。

**4.** The climax came on a Thursday, when the student union demanded a decision. Lin wanted to clone the successful west-gate experiment; Maya wanted to keep the club's independence. “That is not a choice,” she said. “That is a descent into the same mistake.” Anything else, she thought, would be pride wearing a new coat.

高潮在周四到来，学生会要求他们做出决定。林伟想复制西门的成功实验；曼雅想保住社团的独立性。“这不是选择，”她说，“这是坠入同一个错误。”她心想，其他任何做法都只是披着新外套的自尊。

**5.** They chose a third path. The report would change one line: from “cover every gate” to “ask a better question at every gate.” But the rate of progress was slower than anyone liked, and no one could explain why. A guy from the city desk laughed at their absolute rule, and then wrote it down. “Your notes are the only thing holding this together,” Lin said. “Each member has to share them, and that is an opportunity, not a burden.” Until the summer, a voluntary team would keep the notes public; after that, the arrangement would have to stand on its own. “Yeah,” Maya said, “that is worth the risk.”

他们选了第三条路。报告只改一行：从“覆盖每一道门”改成“在每一道门前问一个更好的问题”。但进展的速度比任何人希望的都慢，也没人说得清为什么。城市版的一位家伙嘲笑他们那条绝对规则，随后又把它记了下来。“你的笔记是唯一撑住这一切的东西，”林伟说，“每个成员都必须共享它们，而这是一个机会，不是负担。”在夏天之前，一支自愿的团队会让笔记保持公开；之后，这套安排必须自己站得住。“是的，”曼雅说，“这值得冒险。”

### Exercises

- CH009-Q1 [context] What did the group change in the report?
  - options: one line about the question at each gate / the name of the club / the price of the map / the colour of the cover
- CH009-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “克制；抑制”?
  - options: restraint / climax / descent / pride
- CH009-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: adjust the shape / adjust the soldier / adjust the gas / adjust the estate
- CH009-Q4 [cloze] The group had no property and no ______ income.
  - options: independent / colonial / voluntary / protective
- CH009-Q5 [recall] How long would the notes stay public?
  - options: until the summer / until the Thursday meeting / for one week / forever

### Answers

- CH009-Q1: one line about the question at each gate - They replaced full coverage with a better question at every gate.
- CH009-Q2: restraint - Restraint, not enthusiasm, would keep the project alive.
- CH009-Q3: adjust the shape - Adjust combines naturally with shape, plan, or setting.
- CH009-Q4: independent - The group had no income of its own.
- CH009-Q5: until the summer - A voluntary team would keep the notes public until the summer.

## CH010 - New Ground: The Second Attempt / 新的起点：第二次尝试

- theme: 大学生活与个人成长

**1.** The second attempt began with a test the group had not designed. A member of the college council, a guy named Wen, handed them a list of five buildings and said that a student leader should walk the route with his eyes closed. He was joking; nobody laughed. Beneath the joke lay a fair demand: the map had to work for someone who had never seen the campus. Hence the rule that Lin, usually the fastest walker, would go first, while the president of the council waited at the gate.

第二次尝试从一场不是他们自己设计的测试开始。学院委员会的一位成员，一个叫文的家伙，递给他们一张写着五栋楼的清单，说学生领袖应该闭着眼睛走完这条路线。他是在开玩笑；没人笑。玩笑底下是一个合理的要求：这张地图必须对一个从未见过校园的人也有用。于是有了这条规则：通常是走得最快的林伟先走，而委员会主席在门口等着。

**2.** Initially the walk went well. Then he lost track of the alley behind the sports hall, where a showy artificial sign pointed nowhere. Both of them stood there, and her patience narrowed. “Normally a map is a promise,” she said. “This one is a luxury.” Elsewhere, water from the swimming pool had accumulated along the avenue, and the ugly cloth banner above the door was too wet to read. A small crowd began to gather behind them, which was worse than the rain.

起初这段路走得很顺。随后他弄不清体育馆后面那条小巷的编号，那里一块艳丽的人造指示牌指向空处。两人站在那里，她的耐心变窄了。“通常地图是一个承诺，”她说，“而这个是奢侈品。”别处，游泳池的水顺着大道积了起来，门上方那块难看的布横幅湿得看不清。一小群人开始在他们身后聚集，这比下雨更糟。

**3.** They resolved to test the wording instead of the route. On behalf of the volunteers, Maya rewrote each line and duly recorded every hesitation. “Preferable” became “better”; “atop the hill” became “at the top of the hill”. Gradually the sentences grew shorter, their eyesight of the real problem sharpened, and the emergence of a telling detail came on the fourth page: nobody had described the door from the outside. “We should often read our own report as a stranger would,” she said.

他们决定测试措辞，而不是路线。曼雅代表志愿者重写了每一行并按时记录了每一次犹豫。“更可取”改成“更好”；“在山顶之上”改成“在山顶”。句子渐渐变短，他们对真正问题的眼力也变锐利了，第四页上浮现出一个说明问题的细节：没有人从外面描述过那扇门。“我们应该常常像陌生人那样读自己的报告，”她说。

**4.** The practicality of the project troubled Lin. To cultivate trust, he thought, you must first sow patience and then reap attention. A blouse soaked by rain, a cap left idly on a bench, and a troublesome locked gate all told the same story: the map was accurate and unusable. Ironically, the second walk missed the gate narrowly, and the narrowest point of the route was the one the designer had photographed most carefully. A major question had been answered, and it had produced a worse answer than doubt.

项目的实用性让林伟不安。他想到，要培养信任，必须先播下耐心，才能收获关注。一件被雨浸透的上衣、一顶被闲闲留在长椅上的帽子、一道麻烦的锁门，都在讲同一个故事：这张地图准确却无法使用。讽刺的是，整条路线中最窄的那一点，正是设计者拍得最仔细的地方。一个重要问题被回答了，而它给出的答案比怀疑更糟。

**5.** The reunification of the two maps took three evenings. It was no luxurious achievement and nobody wanted to celebrate it, but it was an advancement: the group could develop one gate at a time and conquer it in a single pass. The number of wrong turns had fallen, though not far. The smell near the canteen began to sicken Lin, and the queue continued to grow in the rain. “We have not overcome the city,” Maya said. “We have only stopped lying about it.”

两张地图的重新统一花了三个晚上。这不是什么豪华的成就，也没人想庆祝，但它确实是一次进步：小组可以一次只开发一道门，并在一趟之内把它攻克。走错路的次数减少了，虽然减少得不多。食堂附近的气味开始让林伟恶心，队伍在雨里继续变长。“我们并没有战胜这座城市，”曼雅说，“我们只是不再对它说谎。”

### Exercises

- CH010-Q1 [context] What rule did the council member's joke produce?
  - options: Lin had to walk the route without seeing it / the map had to be printed twice / the council would pay for the banner / Maya had to rewrite the report
- CH010-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “逐渐地”?
  - options: gradually / initially / narrowly / duly
- CH010-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: cultivate trust / cultivate a banner / cultivate an alley / cultivate a pool
- CH010-Q4 [cloze] The group could ______ one gate in a single pass.
  - options: conquer / sicken / celebrate / accumulate
- CH010-Q5 [recall] What did the group finally admit?
  - options: that they had stopped lying about the city / that the map was luxurious / that the council was useless / that the rain had stopped

### Answers

- CH010-Q1: Lin had to walk the route without seeing it - The joke became a real test: Lin walked the route as a stranger would.
- CH010-Q2: gradually - The sentences became shorter little by little.
- CH010-Q3: cultivate trust - Cultivate is used with trust, patience, or a habit.
- CH010-Q4: conquer - After the reunification they could handle one gate in one pass.
- CH010-Q5: that they had stopped lying about the city - Maya said they had not overcome the city, only stopped lying about it.

## CH011 - New Ground: Outside Pressure / 新的起点：外部压力

- theme: 大学生活与个人成长

**1.** A legislative aide came to the campus in a purple coat and asked to see the map. She stared at the wall for a long minute, her gaze moving from the gate to the legend. “The potential is obvious,” she said, “but this is controversial, and the council will not fund a hazy promise.” The sheer enthusiasm of the volunteers met a cold room. A funny detail softened nothing: the lady from the council had arrived with a cigaret she was not allowed to light, and she held it like a small, useless tool.

一位立法助理穿着紫色外套来到校园，要求看看那张地图。她盯着墙面看了很久。“潜力很明显，”她说，“但这件事有争议，委员会不会资助一个模糊的承诺。”志愿者们十足的热情遇上了一间冷冰冰的房间。一个滑稽的细节什么也缓和不了：这位来自委员会的女士带来一支不许点燃的香烟，像握着一件无用的小工具。

**2.** The condition was stated precisely: show a working bypass for every closed gate, or lose the room. An enthusiastic volunteer asked whether a sketch would do; he was told that it would not. “That is amazing,” Maya said, meaning the opposite. “It is also impossible.” The shock was that the aide agreed. Those five buildings were, normally, the least interesting part of the campus, and yet the whole project now depended on them. The delay began to weaken their hold on the room. A brutal deadline can sharpen a design or terrify its makers; this one did both.

条件被精确地说明：为每一道关闭的门拿出可用的绕行方案，否则就失去这间房间。一位热情的志愿者问一张草图行不行；他被告知不行。“太惊人了，”曼雅说，意思是相反的意思，“而且也不可能。”令人震惊的是，这位助理同意她的说法。那五栋楼通常来说是校园里最没意思的部分，而如今整个项目都依赖它们。拖延开始削弱他们对这间房间的掌控。残酷的期限可以让设计变锐利，也可以吓坏它的制作者；这一个两者都做到了。

**3.** They worked in a muddy room that began to darken at four o'clock. The mist outside was thick enough to hide the far gate. A creamy soup and a delicious bun arrived, and the volunteers ate standing up, usually in silence. “This is disgusting,” one girl said, meaning the corridor rather than the food. Small delays began to annoy everyone, including the two of them, and a bloody scratch on Lin's hand came from fastening the board to the wall.

他们在一间泥泞的屋子里工作，屋子到四点就开始变暗。外面的雾厚得足以遮住远处的大门。一碗奶油汤和一个美味的面包送来了，志愿者们站着吃，通常沉默不语。“这真恶心，”一个女孩说，指的是走廊而不是食物。小小的延误开始让所有人恼火，包括他们两个人；林伟手上那道流血的划痕，是把板子固定到墙上时弄的。

**4.** Among the three hundred notes, a hundred mentioned a lack of light and an equal amount mentioned the cold. A colorful diagram fascinated the visitors, and the fascination was mutual: the students were in awe of the aide's patience, and she was in awe of their stamina. Both of them watched her read the whole file. “Do not deceive yourselves,” she said at last. “Urbanization is not your problem. Access is.” An awesome responsibility, Lin thought, for one thin folder.

在三百条笔记里，有一百条提到了光线不足，同样数量的笔记提到了寒冷。一张色彩丰富的图表让来访者着迷，而这种着迷是相互的：学生们敬畏这位助理的耐心，她也敬畏他们的耐力。他们两人看着她读完了整份文件。“不要欺骗自己，”她最后说，“城市化不是你们的问题，通行才是。”林伟想，对一份薄薄的文件夹来说，这是一份令人敬畏的责任。

**5.** The overthrow of their own first plan necessitated a new method. They fastened the map to a board, sharpened the wording, and built an adaptive and innovative version that could be edited in an hour. The potentiality was real and the excitement was also real, which horrified Professor Zhao slightly. “Yeah,” Lin said, “but a positive attitude does not improve a locked door, and patience alone will not pay for one.” “Instead of defending the room,” he went on, “we should defend the route.” “Yes,” said the aide, “and that is precisely why I am still here.”

推翻自己第一份计划使新方法成为必需。他们把地图固定在板子上，把措辞磨得更锋利，并做出一个能在一小时内修改的适应型版本。潜在性是真实的，兴奋也是真实的，这让赵教授略微感到恐惧。“是的，”林伟说，“但积极的态度并不能改善一扇锁住的门。” “没错，”助理说，“这正是我还在这里的原因。”

### Exercises

- CH011-Q1 [context] What condition did the council impose?
  - options: a working bypass for every closed gate / a purple uniform / a new swimming pool / a weekly seminar
- CH011-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “使必需；迫使”?
  - options: necessitate / fascinate / soften / darken
- CH011-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: sharpen the wording / sharpen the soup / sharpen the mist / sharpen the awe
- CH011-Q4 [cloze] The aide said the students must not ______ themselves.
  - options: deceive / fasten / weaken / terrify
- CH011-Q5 [recall] Why was the aide still there?
  - options: because a positive attitude does not fix a locked door / because she wanted the cigarette / because the room was warm / because the diagram was colourful

### Answers

- CH011-Q1: a working bypass for every closed gate - The council offered the room only for a complete, testable route.
- CH011-Q2: necessitate - The failure of the first plan made a new method necessary.
- CH011-Q3: sharpen the wording - Sharpen is used with wording, a design, or a skill.
- CH011-Q4: deceive - She warned them not to fool themselves about the cause of the problem.
- CH011-Q5: because a positive attitude does not fix a locked door - She stayed because the practical failure remained.

## CH012 - New Ground: The Decision / 新的起点：决定

- theme: 大学生活与个人成长

**1.** The discussion lasted until midnight. Some members wanted to lend the map to the council; many wanted to keep it private; a few wanted to stop altogether. “Every option has a price,” Lin said. “No option is free.” He sat with his eye on the hole in the tent where the rain came through, and nobody could describe the shape of the decision. There were a number of gates still untested, though the room was warm.

讨论持续到午夜。一些成员想把地图借给委员会；很多人想让它保持私密；少数人想干脆停下。“每个选项都有代价，”林伟说，“没有哪个选项是免费的。”他坐着，眼睛盯着帐篷上漏雨的那个洞，没人能描述这个决定的形状。还有若干道门没测过，尽管屋子里很暖和。

**2.** Each member wrote one sentence on a piece of paper. Most of them were unusable. There were several commas, one plural mistake, and a remark from a tutor about the ultimate purpose of the project. “A single arbitrary rule,” Maya said, “is worse than no rule at all.” She had quite enough of niceties, and which of them had the patience left to argue? “Write the next page for me,” she added, “and keep it to one sentence.”

每位成员在一张纸上写一句话。大多数都用不了。里面有好几个逗号、一个复数错误，还有导师关于项目终极目的的一句评论。“一条单独的武断规则，”曼雅说，“比完全没有规则更糟。”她已经受够了这些细枝末节，而他们当中谁还有耐心继续争论呢？“替我把下一页写出来，”她补充说，“一句话就行。”

**3.** The confrontation came in a seminar on dynamic systems. A guest lecturer in golf trousers and a bright sleeve described how an army of small decisions can cover a million small failures. “You cannot bless a plan into working,” he said with an excess of confidence. “You have to test it.” His umbrella, a parcel of notes, a necklace of coffee stains on the napkin, and a hamburger eaten in three bites made him a highly unlikely authority. The seminar turned into a tutorial on how quickly a good idea can become a habit.

对抗发生在一场关于动态系统的研讨会上。一位穿着高尔夫长裤、袖口鲜亮的客座讲师讲述了：一支由小决定组成的军队，如何覆盖一百万个小的失败。“你没法靠祝福让一个计划运转起来，”他带着过度的自信说，“你必须测试它。”他的雨伞、一包笔记、餐巾上像项链一样的咖啡渍，以及三口吃完的汉堡，使他看起来不太像个权威。这场研讨会变成了一堂课，讲的是一个好想法能多快变成一个习惯。

**4.** The team chose the least dramatic path: multiple small trials, one per gate, with the results of each published beside the respective gate. The lesson was simple and the several commas of the first draft were gone. The extreme option, a single official map, would fail; the ultimate one, no map at all, would fail worse.

团队选择了最不戏剧化的一条路：多个小规模试验，每道门一个，每个结果都公布在对应的门旁。教训很简单。极端方案——一张唯一的官方地图——会失败；终极方案——完全没有地图——会失败得更惨。

**5.** They met again in the city office, where the report was finally accepted. “Again,” the guy from the city desk said, “you have done something small and honest.” Her answer was a smile and one sentence: “That is the whole unit of progress.” It was not a quality they could measure, but it was property they could defend, and that was the point. Every member of the team signed the last page.

他们又在市政办公室见面，报告终于被接受。“又一次，”市政版的那位家伙说，“你们做成了一件小而诚实的事。”她的回答是一个微笑和一句话：“这就是进步的整个单位。”这不是他们能衡量的品质，却是他们能捍卫的财产，而这就是重点。

### Exercises

- CH012-Q1 [context] Which path did the team choose?
  - options: multiple small trials, one per gate / one official map / no map at all / lending the map to the council
- CH012-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “武断的；任意的”?
  - options: arbitrary / respective / dynamic / extreme
- CH012-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: a single arbitrary rule / a single delicious rule / a single muddy rule / a single purple rule
- CH012-Q4 [cloze] The results were published beside the ______ gate.
  - options: respective / excessive / plural / ultimate
- CH012-Q5 [recall] What did Maya say was the whole unit of progress?
  - options: something small and honest / a printed document / a dynamic system / a golf lesson

### Answers

- CH012-Q1: multiple small trials, one per gate - The least dramatic path tested each gate separately.
- CH012-Q2: arbitrary - A rule chosen without evidence is arbitrary.
- CH012-Q3: a single arbitrary rule - Arbitrary describes a rule imposed without reason.
- CH012-Q4: respective - Each result was published beside its own gate.
- CH012-Q5: something small and honest - She summarised the project as small, honest work.

## CH013 - New Ground: Consequences / 新的起点：后果

- theme: 大学生活与个人成长

**1.** The consequences arrived slowly, the way a fox creeps along a hedge. The union had accepted the report, but the registration office wanted a printed copy without changes. “Every edit must be approved,” a young clerk said. “From now on, the map is a document, not a notion.” His phone rang twice while he was speaking, and he did not answer it.

后果来得很慢，像一只狐狸沿着树篱潜行。工会接受了报告，但注册办公室想要一份不得改动的打印版。“每一次编辑都必须获批，”一位年轻职员说，“从现在起，这张地图是一份文件，而不是一个想法。”他说话时手机响了两次，他没有接。

**2.** Several volunteers left. The doubt in the canteen was easy to hear: some said the project had become official, many said it had become useless, and a few said both. “An opinion is not evidence,” Maya said, “but every opinion is a signal.” She kept a bag of cards with the various complaints written on them, one card for each gate.

几名志愿者离开了。食堂里的疑虑很容易听见：有人说这个项目变得官方了，很多人说它变得没用了，少数人说两者都是。“意见不是证据，”曼雅说，“但每一条意见都是信号。”她留着一袋卡片，上面写着各种抱怨，每道门一张。

**3.** Lin went home for a weekend and helped his uncle on the plantation. They planted cabbage and paddy, checked a grape vine, and watched a bud open into bloom. “A crop fails for a hundred reasons,” his uncle said. “A wise farmer saves the seed and keeps the sheep away from the beans.” It was the opposite of a rocket launch: no single event, only survival, and at least a million small decisions.

林伟回家过了一个周末，在种植园里帮叔叔干活。他们种了卷心菜和稻子，查看了一株葡萄藤，看着一个芽苞开成花。叔叔说：“一季作物会因为一百个理由歉收。明智的农夫会留种，也会把羊群挡在豆子外面。”这与发射火箭正好相反：没有单一事件，只有生存，以及至少上百万个小决定。

**4.** The test he needed was simpler than any assurance from the university. He stood on the grass beside the canteen and watched a boy and a lady with a cane take the route he had drawn. The impression was not good. The frame of the sign had been broken by the stem of a fallen tree, and the lead paint on the post had peeled. His eye followed the damage and his usually steady mood slipped.

他需要的测试比大学给出的任何保证都更简单。他站在食堂旁的草地上，看着一个男孩和一位拄着手杖的女士走他画的那条路线。印象并不好。指示牌的框被倒下的一棵树干压坏了，柱子上的铅漆已经剥落。他的眼睛顺着那些破损看去，平日稳定的心情也滑落了。

**5.** Most of the team still wanted to preserve the map. They could not do it without money, and the money was uncertain. “Every plan needs a moon to aim at,” Lin wrote, “and a canteen to meet in.” The produce of that winter was small: one page, per gate, signed by the self that wrote it. He did not know why the sentence pleased him, but he kept it. Enough people cared; quite a few of them were sitting in that room.

团队里大多数人仍想保住这张地图。没有钱就做不到，而钱是不确定的。“每个计划都需要一个瞄准的月亮，”林伟写道，“以及一间可以碰头的食堂。”那个冬天的产出很小：每道门一页，由写下它的那个自我签名。他不知道这句话为什么让自己满意，但他留下了它。在意的人足够多；其中有相当一些人就坐在那间屋子里。

### Exercises

- CH013-Q1 [context] What did the registration office demand?
  - options: a printed copy that cannot be edited freely / a new plantation / a weekly opinion poll / a larger canteen
- CH013-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “不确定的”?
  - options: uncertain / opposite / various / young
- CH013-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: preserve the map / preserve the phone / preserve the moon / preserve the canteen
- CH013-Q4 [cloze] A wise farmer saves the seed and keeps the ______ away from the beans.
  - options: sheep / rocket / fox / crop
- CH013-Q5 [recall] What did Lin find beside the canteen?
  - options: a broken sign frame / a new gate / a bag of cards / a printed report

### Answers

- CH013-Q1: a printed copy that cannot be edited freely - The office turned the map into a document that needed approval for every edit.
- CH013-Q2: uncertain - The funding was uncertain, which put the map at risk.
- CH013-Q3: preserve the map - Preserve combines naturally with a document, a place, or a habit.
- CH013-Q4: sheep - His uncle used the image of protecting crops from sheep.
- CH013-Q5: a broken sign frame - The frame was crushed by a fallen tree stem and the paint had peeled.

## CH014 - New Ground: The Bridge Forward / 新的起点：通向下一程

- theme: 大学生活与个人成长

**1.** The first arc closed, not with a victory, but with a bridge. By the end of the term, the map had grown into three small tools: a printed page, a phone page, and a person who answered questions at the gate. Such work was largely invisible, and the university and the union mentioned it once and twice, respectively. The president of the university wrote one line about it; the young leader of the union wrote four.

第一段弧线不是以胜利收尾，而是以一座桥收尾。到学期末，那张地图长成了三件小工具：一张打印页、一个手机页面，以及一个在门口回答问题的人。这样的工作大部分是看不见的；学校和工会分别提到过它一次和两次。校长写了一行，工会里那位年轻领袖写了四行。

**2.** The existing system did not change. A student stood at the same locked door; a boy from the college office still had to phone his supervisor. What had changed was the likelihood of being seen, and that change was only partly visible. A single angry message could disrupt the whole week, so they answered every one the same day. “We manage a small thing,” Maya said. “That is all.” Her tone was expressive rather than triumphant, and she had begun to develop a thicker skin about it.

现有体系并没有改变。学生仍旧站在同一扇锁住的门前；学院办公室的男孩仍旧得给主管打电话。改变的是被看见的可能性，而这种改变只有一部分是可见的。一封愤怒的消息就能打乱整整一周，所以他们当天回复每一条。“我们管着一件小事，”曼雅说，“就是这样。”她的语气是富有表情的，而不是得意的，而且她已经学会对此更厚脸皮一些。

**3.** The peak of the year came on a gorgeous evening in the living room of the volunteer house. The metro had brought them a box of aging posters and a bag of trash; the lighting was terribly bad. Aerial photographs of the campus lay beside a scenic sketch of the river, and the argument that followed erupted like a volcano. The lead volunteer read a personal note from the head of her major, and the mood turned riotous, then thunderous, then quiet. Their produce for the term fitted in one folder.

一年的高点出现在志愿者之家客厅里一个绚丽的傍晚。地铁给他们带来了一箱老化的海报和一袋垃圾；灯光糟糕得可怕。校园的航拍照片摊在河边一幅风景速写旁边，随后的争论像火山一样爆发。负责的志愿者读了导师写给她专业的一封私人短笺，气氛先喧闹，再如雷，最后安静下来。他们这个学期的产出装进了一个文件夹。

**4.** The disruption was partly internal and partially financial. The resultant delay saddened Lin and made Maya resent the meeting. “It is pointless to build an evolutionary plan and then starve it,” she said. “A plan has to nourish the people in it.” The attainment they wanted, namely a permanent sign, now depended on a budget they did not control. “This is the most populous district in the city,” she added, “and we cannot even repair one frame.”

这次中断一部分是内部的，一部分是财务上的。由此产生的延误让林伟难过，也让曼雅怨恨这次会议。“先建一个进化的计划、再把它饿死，是毫无意义的，”她说，“计划必须滋养其中的人。”他们想要达到的目标，也就是一块长期指示牌，如今取决于一份他们无法控制的预算。“这是全市人口最多的区，”她补充说，“而我们连一个框都修不了。”

**5.** A shameless poster about “super students” hung beside the library, and a voluminous folder of outdated rules sat beneath it. The blossoms in the square had opened and the daylight lasted longer. “Every volunteer had to contribute one page,” Professor Zhao said. “Our work is disruptive in a very small way, and that is exceedingly fruitful.” A phrasal verb in her notebook, she admitted, was only partly relevant, but the sentence she copied beside it was this: “A bridge is not a victory, and without it there is no crossing.” A first map does not teach you a city; it teaches you how many doors you have not tried.

图书馆旁挂着一张无耻的“超级学生”海报，下面放着一大卷过时的规章。广场上的花已经开了，白昼也更长了。“每位志愿者都必须贡献一页，”赵教授说，“我们的工作以一种很小的方式具有破坏性，而这极其富有成果。”她笔记本里的一个短语动词，她承认，只有一部分相关；但她在旁边抄下的这句话是：“桥不是胜利，而没有它就没有跨越。”第一张地图教不会你一座城市；它只教你还剩多少扇门没有试过。

### Exercises

- CH014-Q1 [context] What were the three small tools at the end of the term?
  - options: a printed page, a phone page, and a person at the gate / a camera, a room, and a poster / three printed maps / a budget, a sign, and a sketch
- CH014-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “分别地；各自地”?
  - options: respectively / partially / largely / exceedingly
- CH014-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: nourish the people in a plan / nourish a poster / nourish the metro / nourish a folder
- CH014-Q4 [cloze] The attainment they wanted, ______ a permanent sign, depended on someone else's budget.
  - options: namely / terribly / scenically / riotously
- CH014-Q5 [recall] What sentence did Professor Zhao copy into her notebook?
  - options: that a bridge is not a victory and is still a bridge / that the union mentioned the map twice / that the budget was too small / that the poster was shameful

### Answers

- CH014-Q1: a printed page, a phone page, and a person at the gate - The map became three modest tools by the end of the first arc.
- CH014-Q2: respectively - The university and the union mentioned it once and twice, in that order.
- CH014-Q3: nourish the people in a plan - Nourish is used for people, soil, or a living plan.
- CH014-Q4: namely - Namely introduces the specific item being named.
- CH014-Q5: that a bridge is not a victory and is still a bridge - She copied the sentence about a bridge into her notebook.

## CH015 - Learning How to Learn: A Signal / 学会学习：一个信号

- theme: 学习方法与教育

**1.** The map was finished, and the only question it had left behind was whether anyone had learned anything from it. The second arc opened with a signal, not with a plan. A researcher from the education faculty asked the group a single question at the end of a lecture: how would they prove that the map taught anyone anything? The early answer was a sketch; the later answer was a blueprint. “At every turn,” she said, “you will be asked for evidence, and such a demand is healthy.”

那张地图做完了，而它留下的唯一问题是：究竟有没有人从中学到过任何东西。第二段弧线以一个信号开始，而不是以一个计划开始。教育学院的一位研究人员在一场讲座结束时问了小组一个问题：他们怎么证明这张地图教会了任何人任何东西？最早的答案是草图，后来的答案是蓝图。“在每一个转折点，”她说，“都会有人向你要证据，而这种要求是健康的。”

**2.** The battle was against habit. A teacher, a columnist, and a novelist told the same story in different words: students memorize for a quiz and forget by the weekend. “Knowledge is not a diploma,” the instructor said. “A diploma is a receipt.” Several people in the room nodded; few wrote it down. A kid of nineteen in the second row asked whether a fresh example might help, and got one.

这场战斗针对的是习惯。一位教师、一位专栏作家和一位小说家用不同的话讲了同一个故事：学生为课堂测验背下内容，到周末就忘掉。“知识不是文凭，”讲师说，“文凭只是一张收据。”屋里好几个人点头；写下这句话的人很少。第二排一个十九岁的孩子问，一个新鲜的例子是否有帮助，他得到了一个。

**3.** The team interviewed a vocational trainer, a translator, and a biographer, and each described a different enemy: the commercial summary, the daily rush, and the timely excuse. “Every method has a mythology,” the historian said. “Ours is that reading is the same as learning.” A fishing village on the coast had produced a specialist in reading who could not name a single classic, and an educator who could name a thousand.

团队采访了一位职业培训师、一位翻译和一位传记作者，每个人描述了一个不同的敌人：商业化的摘要、每日的匆忙，以及及时的借口。“每种方法都有自己的神话，”历史学家说，“我们的神话就是：阅读等于学习。”海边一个渔村培养出一位阅读专家，他叫不出一部经典；也培养出一位教育家，她能叫出一千部。

**4.** Maya, a journalist who was now a columnist for the student paper, published a narrative about a dropout who returned. A reviewer called it classic; an essayist called it sentimental. The widow who ran the reading room said it was the first honest page she had read that month, and a sports commentator mailed her an old typewriter with a note: “Leisure reading saved my graduation.” They did not hesitate to print both reactions.

曼雅如今是学生报的专栏作家，发表了一篇关于一名回归校园的退学者的叙事。一位评论者说它经典；一位随笔作家说它感伤。管理阅览室的寡妇说，这是她那个月读到的最诚实的一页；一位体育评论员给她寄来一台旧打字机，附了一句：“休闲阅读救了我的毕业。”

**5.** An amateur planner in the curriculum office asked them to prevent the same mistake twice. Her teaching had one rule: a mystery is not a gap. “You may hotdog about your method,” she said, “but the method is what survives you.” But the eye of the faculty was on them now, and every page had to earn its place. Most of the volunteers agreed; many of them, in fact, had said the same thing a year ago, and no one had listened. By the end of the evening, quite a few of them could sit still with an unanswered question, which was at least a million times better than guessing. “Yeah,” Maya said, “that is the point.” Some of the older notes looked thin by comparison.

课程办公室一位业余的计划者请他们防止同一个错误发生两次。她的教学只有一条规则：谜团不是空缺。“你可以对自己的方法卖弄，”她说，“但方法才是能活得比你久的东西。”但如今教职员工的目光落在他们身上，每一页都必须挣得自己的位置。大多数志愿者都同意；事实上，他们当中有很多人一年前就说过同样的话，却没有人听。到那天晚上结束，有相当一些人能够坐着面对一个未回答的问题，这至少比猜测好上百万倍。“是的，”曼雅说，“这就是重点。”相比之下，一些更早的笔记显得单薄。

### Exercises

- CH015-Q1 [context] What did the researcher ask the group to prove?
  - options: that the map taught someone something / that the union had paid them / that the river was scenic / that the typewriter still worked
- CH015-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “职业的”?
  - options: vocational / sentimental / commercial / elementary
- CH015-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: prevent the same mistake twice / prevent a typewriter / prevent the widow / prevent a village
- CH015-Q4 [cloze] The instructor said that knowledge is not a ______.
  - options: diploma / blueprint / mystery / quiz
- CH015-Q5 [recall] What did the sports commentator send?
  - options: an old typewriter / a fishing net / a blueprint / a diploma

### Answers

- CH015-Q1: that the map taught someone something - She demanded evidence of learning, not of effort.
- CH015-Q2: vocational - A vocational trainer prepares people for a trade.
- CH015-Q3: prevent the same mistake twice - Prevent combines naturally with a mistake, an error, or harm.
- CH015-Q4: diploma - The instructor compared a diploma to a receipt.
- CH015-Q5: an old typewriter - He mailed a typewriter with a note about leisure reading.

## CH016 - Learning How to Learn: The First Promise / 学会学习：第一次承诺

- theme: 学习方法与教育

**1.** Their first promise in the new arc was modest and specific: every claim on the map would have a method behind it. In the library, with a textbook open at his hand, Lin began to write the first page of a study guide. “A vocabulary list is not knowledge,” Maya said. “A method is.” Their idea was to keep one question in front of every reader: which claim does this page support?

他们在新弧线里的第一个承诺既谦逊又具体：地图上的每一条主张背后都要有方法。在图书馆里，林伟把一本教科书摊在手边，开始写学习指南的第一页。“词汇表不是知识，”曼雅说，“方法才是。”他们的想法是在每位读者面前保留一个问题：这一页支持的是哪一条主张？

**2.** The class met twice a week in a classroom above the library. Their instructor, an experienced researcher in sociology, gave them a sample, a specimen of bad analysis, and asked for a comment on each. “Your writing has improved,” she said. “Your literacy has not. Where is the example from your own research?” Her expertise was in reading numbers, and she read theirs quickly.

课程每周在图书馆楼上的教室上两次。他们的讲师是一位经验丰富的社会学研究者，她给他们一份样本——一份糟糕分析的标本——并要求对每一份写评论。“你的写作进步了，”她说，“你的读写能力没有。你自己研究里的例子在哪里？”她的专长是读数字，而她很快就读完了他们的数字。

**3.** An expert on the frontier of educational tech visited for an instance of what not to do: a blog that promised fluent reading in a week. The advanced group laughed; the elementary group memorized it. “Reading a quiz is not reading,” the instructor said. “An analytic mind asks what the quiz rewards.” Her comment stayed with them longer than the visit did.

一位研究教育科技前沿的专家来访，带来一个反例：一个承诺一周内流利阅读的博客。进阶组笑了；初级组把它背了下来。“读一道测验题不是阅读，”讲师说，“善于分析的头脑会问：这道测验奖励的是什么。”她的评论比这次来访留得更久。

**4.** They wrote a basic alphabet book for the language centre, a classical example for the disciplinary board, and a ripe blog post about deliberate practice. Skilful contributors were rare, and a number of them were already tired. “In turn,” Lin noted, “each member of the board gets one early draft and one week to answer it.” A young contributor from the change office wrote three entries in an afternoon and then vanished for a month.

他们为语言中心写了一本基础字母书，为纪律委员会写了一个经典案例，还写了一篇关于刻意练习的成熟博文。灵巧的投稿者很少，而且其中若干人已经疲惫。“依次来说，”林伟写道，“委员会每位成员会拿到一份早期草稿，并用一周作答。”变革办公室一位年轻的投稿者一个下午写了三条，然后消失了一个月。

**5.** The study turned toward analysis. An academic reviewer asked whether a proficient examinee could do anything a skilled one could not, and a sociology student asked the same question from the other side. “Every textbook hides a choice,” Lin wrote in her notebook. “We should show the choice.” The lead question of the whole guide was hers: without it, the guide would be another list, and a list is exactly what the missing students do not need.

研究转向了分析。一位学术评论者问：一名熟练的应试者能做到什么熟练工做不到的事？一名社会学学生从另一面问了同一个问题。“每本教科书都藏着一个选择，”林伟在她的笔记本里写道，“我们应该把这个选择展示出来。”整本指南的首要问题来自她：没有它，指南只会是又一份清单，而清单恰恰是那些缺席的学生不需要的东西。

### Exercises

- CH016-Q1 [context] What was the group's first promise in the new arc?
  - options: every claim would have a method behind it / every volunteer would write a blog / the library would stay open later / the map would be printed twice
- CH016-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “标本；样品”?
  - options: specimen / comment / frontier / literacy
- CH016-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: an analytic mind / an analytic alphabet / an analytic widow / an analytic typewriter
- CH016-Q4 [cloze] The instructor said their writing had improved but their ______ had not.
  - options: literacy / expertise / sociology / sample
- CH016-Q5 [recall] What does every textbook hide, according to Lin?
  - options: a choice / a quiz / a specimen / an alphabet

### Answers

- CH016-Q1: every claim would have a method behind it - The promise tied each claim to a method.
- CH016-Q2: specimen - A specimen is a sample kept for study, here of bad analysis.
- CH016-Q3: an analytic mind - Analytic describes a mind that breaks a question into parts.
- CH016-Q4: literacy - She separated improved writing from improved reading ability.
- CH016-Q5: a choice - Lin wrote that every textbook hides a choice the reader should see.

## CH017 - Learning How to Learn: A Missing Voice / 学会学习：缺席的声音

- theme: 学习方法与教育

**1.** The missing voice arrived in a lecture hall, from a member of the cleaning staff. She had worked in the same building for eleven years, and she had never been asked what she thought of the academy. “You teach the students how to read a page,” she said. “Nobody teaches them how to read a person.” The revelation spread through the room faster than the information, and along the back wall the class stopped taking notes.

那个缺席的声音出现在一间讲座厅里，来自一名保洁人员。她在这栋楼里工作了十一年，却从没有人问过她怎么看这所学院。“你们教学生怎么读一页纸，”她说，“却没人教他们怎么读一个人。”这个启示传遍整个房间的速度比信息本身还快，靠后墙那一排的同学停下了笔记。

**2.** Her own schooling had ended early. She had learned medicine from her mother, literature from the radio, and the art of remembering from a small notebook. “I possess no diploma,” she said, “and I am not ignorant. I am a learner.” She was literate in three languages, and her enlightenment had come from work rather than from a lecture. Not one word of it had been written down until that hour. The writer who took notes for the student paper called her a scholar by the end of the hour.

她自己的学校教育很早就结束了。她从母亲那里学医，从收音机里学文学，从一个小本子里学记忆的艺术。“我没有文凭，”她说，“但我并不无知。我是学习者。”她通晓三种语言，而她的启蒙来自劳作，而不是来自一堂课。在这一个小时之前，这一切没有一个字被写下来。为校报做记录的那位作家在一小时结束时称她为学者。

**3.** The educational review changed shape: for one week, the staff would teach and the students would listen. The first session was on the foundation of a good question; the second was on the discipline of silence. “Skill is not the same as education,” the cleaner said, and both of them wrote it down. Experience, she added, is not transferred by a lecture but by work.

这次教育评审改变了形态：有一周，由职工来讲，由学生来听。第一场讲的是好问题的基础；第二场讲的是沉默的纪律。“技能不等于教育，”保洁员说，他们两人都把它记了下来。她补充说，经验不是靠一场讲座传递的，而是靠工作传递的。

**4.** Circulation of the reading-room books doubled. A zoological exhibit in the hall was visited by a class that had never entered it, and a bright exhibition of student writing hung beside it. The examination results, usually the only measure the school published, looked suddenly thin. Such a result had never appeared in any report the academy had produced.

阅览室图书的流通量翻了一倍。厅里一场动物学展览迎来一个从未进过这里的班级，旁边还挂着一场明亮的学生写作展。考试成绩——学校通常公布的唯一指标——突然显得单薄。这样的结果在这所学院产出过的任何报告里都从未出现过。

**5.** He wrote a review of the week, and she read it aloud at the fellowship meeting. “If we generalize from one cleaner,” the learned professor said, “we learn nothing.” “If we do not,” Maya answered, “we learn less.” His own study of the reading room had used a different method, and he often said so; toward the end of the week he began to research the language of the staff's own example. The learner had taught the academy something that no exam could hold. The first reader of the transcript was a second-year student, and the week had done more to educate the faculty than three years of the map. The faculty would now have to seek a second opinion, and the staff were happy to provide one.

他写了一篇关于这一周的评论，她在联谊会上把它朗读出来。“如果我们从一位保洁员身上做概括，”那位博学的教授说，“我们什么都学不到。”“如果我们不这样做，”曼雅回答，“我们学到的更少。”他自己对阅览室的研究用的是另一种方法，他也常常这么说；到那一周接近尾声时，他开始研究职工自己那个例子的语言。这位学习者教给了学院一些任何考试都装不下的东西。这份记录的第一位读者是一名二年级学生，而这一周对教职员工的教化，比那张地图三年做的还多。学院现在必须去寻求第二种意见，而职工们很乐意提供一种。

### Exercises

- CH017-Q1 [context] What did the cleaner say nobody teaches students?
  - options: how to read a person / how to clean a hall / how to write a review / how to pass an exam
- CH017-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “揭露；启示”?
  - options: revelation / circulation / fellowship / examination
- CH017-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: the foundation of a good question / the foundation of a typewriter / the foundation of a widow / the foundation of a metro
- CH017-Q4 [cloze] Skill is not the same as ______, the cleaner said.
  - options: education / circulation / revelation / exhibition
- CH017-Q5 [recall] What happened to the circulation of the reading-room books?
  - options: it doubled / it stopped / it fell by half / it was never measured

### Answers

- CH017-Q1: how to read a person - She contrasted reading a page with reading a person.
- CH017-Q2: revelation - Her remark was a revelation that spread through the hall.
- CH017-Q3: the foundation of a good question - Foundation combines naturally with a question, a course, or a building.
- CH017-Q4: education - She separated a skill from an education.
- CH017-Q5: it doubled - Borrowing from the reading room doubled during the staff week.

## CH018 - Learning How to Learn: Evidence on the Table / 学会学习：摆上桌面的证据

- theme: 学习方法与教育

**1.** The staff week had to become evidence, and evidence is a demanding master. Among the three hundred pages of notes, only a handful could survive a stranger's reading. “It is easy to be nice about a school,” the teacher said. “It is harder to be accurate. Please treat the week as a problem, not as a story.” Much of the material, she added, was already stale the day it was written.

职工周必须变成证据，而证据是一位要求很高的主人。在三百页笔记中，只有少数几页能经受陌生人的阅读。“对一所学校说好话很容易，”那位教师说，“做到准确更难。请把这个星期当作一个问题，而不是一个故事。”她又说，其中很多材料在写下的当天就已经过时了。

**2.** The team already knew where the weakness was. A pretty table can hide a hasty sample; a cheerful summary can bury a misery. “We must observe, not celebrate,” Lin wrote. “You are crazy,” Maya said, laughing. “But you are also right.” The bulk of the evidence was delicate: a girl who had never been asked, a kid who had learned to read from a cereal box, and a boy who could remember every word of a notice but not the name of the office that posted it.

团队早就知道弱点在哪里。一张漂亮的表格可以掩盖草率的样本；一份快活的摘要可以埋葬一份痛苦。“我们必须观察，而不是庆祝，”林伟写道。“你疯了，”曼雅笑着说，“但你也说对了。”大部分证据都很微妙：一个从未被问过的女孩、一个从麦片盒上学会认字的孩子，以及一个能记住通知里每一个字、却记不住张贴通知的办公室名字的男孩。

**3.** The eventual report was a surgical document. It listed what the group had seen, what it had assumed, and where the two differed. A skilled typist prepared three copies; a handsome folder carried them to the faculty office. An early draft had been much longer. One sentence, quoted later in the student paper, read: “The honor of a school is not in its buildings but in the questions it allows.”

最终的是一份外科手术式的文件。它列出了小组看到的、他们假设的，以及两者不同之处。一位熟练的打字员准备了三份；一只大方的文件夹把它们送到了教工办公室。早期草稿要长得多。其中一句话后来被学生报引用：“一所学校的荣誉不在于它的建筑，而在于它允许被提出的问题。”

**4.** The irony was not lost on them. A university that charged high tuition could not discriminate between students who asked and students who stayed absent. An enterprising professor, eager to help, wanted a mightier conclusion than the data allowed, and the uneasy silence that followed was, in turn, an education. A clever remark about statistics broke it. “You ask for the impossible,” she said. “No,” Maya said. “We ask for the eventual.” The wit in the room was merry; the impatience was not.

其中的讽刺他们并没有错过。一所收取高额学费的大学，不能因为学生是否提问就区别对待他们，也不能对缺席者视而不见。一位有事业心的教授热心想帮忙，想要一个比数据允许的更有力的结论；随之而来的不安沉默，反过来也是一堂教育课。一句关于统计的聪明评论打破了沉默。“你在要求不可能的事，”她说。“不，”曼雅说，“我们在要求那个最终会成立的东西。”屋里的机智是愉快的，急躁则不是。

**5.** A cloudy afternoon brought a fantastic offer: a conference slot, a congratulatory letter, and a chance to present the week as a triumph. Those pages, however, would need a badly lit corridor to look impressive, and the team refused the triumphant part. A commonplace sentence, they decided, was worth more than a pretty one, and an ironic footnote about the information they lacked could heighten the contrast. The work was tricky, the timetable adventurous, and one table already obsolete. Their proficiency would be measured by what they left out. “Yes,” the professor said. “That is the art of it.”

一个多云的下午带来了一个奇妙的提议：一个会议名额、一封祝贺信，以及把这一周说成胜利的机会。然而那些页面要靠一条照明很差的走廊才显得动人，团队拒绝了“胜利”那部分。他们认定，一句平常的话比一句漂亮的话更值钱，而关于他们所缺信息的一句讽刺性脚注，反而能加强对比。工作很棘手，时间表富于冒险，有一张表格已经作废。他们的熟练程度将由他们省略了什么来衡量。“是的，”教授说，“这就是它的艺术所在。”

### Exercises

- CH018-Q1 [context] What did the teacher ask the group to treat the week as?
  - options: a problem rather than a story / a celebration / a conference paper / a private diary
