- CH018-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “微妙；精细”?
  - options: delicate / stale / hasty / merry
- CH018-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: observe the evidence / observe a compliment / observe a folder / observe the tuition
- CH018-Q4 [cloze] The professor warned them that they could not ______ between students who asked and students who did not.
  - options: discriminate / heighten / diversify / compose
- CH018-Q5 [recall] What did the group refuse?
  - options: the triumphant part of the offer / the conference slot / the folder / the second draft

### Answers

- CH018-Q1: a problem rather than a story - She wanted accuracy, not a pleasant narrative.
- CH018-Q2: delicate - The evidence needed careful handling, which delicate describes.
- CH018-Q3: observe the evidence - Observe combines naturally with evidence, behaviour, or a rule.
- CH018-Q4: discriminate - She refused to treat students differently according to whether they asked.
- CH018-Q5: the triumphant part of the offer - They accepted the chance to present but rejected the triumph.

## CH019 - Learning How to Learn: The Trial Run / 学会学习：第一次试行

- theme: 学习方法与教育

**1.** The trial run had a new shape: one afternoon, one line of questions, and a small group of volunteers. Each carried a soft pencil and a printed page. The engineer who designed the form was curious about a single number — how many people could find the answer without help. “Few,” she said, when the first results came in. “That is the problem.”

试行的形态是新的：一个下午、一条问题线，以及一小群志愿者。每人手里一支软铅笔和一张打印页。设计表格的工程师只对一个数字感到好奇——有多少人能够在没有帮助的情况下找到答案。“很少，”第一轮结果出来时她说，“这就是问题。”

**2.** The performance was better than they had feared. A humble typist, an ace with a needle and a length of silk ribbon, fixed the broken sign in ten minutes, and a ham sandwich with butter bought her a grateful hour. A girl in a cat sweater asked whether the map worked for someone who could not read; nobody answered. “That is the catch,” Maya said. “We wrote for the wrong reader.” A rumor went round the library that the group had taken money from a bakery; the toast and butter tasted better than that story.

表现比他们担心的要好。一位谦逊的打字员用一根针和一段丝带，十分钟就修好了坏掉的指示牌，一个火腿三明治换来了她感激的一小时。一个穿猫毛衣的女孩问，这张地图对一个不识字的人是否管用；没有人能回答。“这就是陷阱，”曼雅说，“我们为错误的读者写作。”图书馆里传开一则谣言，说小组收了一家面包店的钱；早餐的吐司都比那个故事更可信。

**3.** The content of the next version had to change. They would compose it in three languages, diversify the examples, and add a line for readers who preferred pictures. “It is awkward,” Lin admitted. “It is also accessible.” A historical exhibit on Christianity gave them an unexpected lesson: a manuscript copied by hand by a Christian scribe whose name was never recorded, with one old note about Christ in the margin. “That is the pioneer of our profession,” the librarian said. “Efficiency came later.” Chalk dust still lay on the shelf beneath it.

下一版的内容必须改变。他们要用三种语言编写，使例子多样化，并为更喜欢图画的读者加一行。 “这很笨拙，”林伟承认，“但它也更容易接近。”阅览室里一场关于基督教的史料展给了他们意外的教训：一份手抄手稿，一个字母一个字母地抄写，抄写者是一位没有留下姓名的基督徒，页边还有一句关于基督的旧注。“这就是我们这一行的先驱，”图书管理员说，“效率是后来的事。”它下面的书架上还落着粉笔灰。

**4.** Recognition arrived in small forms. A professor praised their “purity of method” and then misspelled Maya's name in the citation. A fiber of doubt remained. “I am not hopeless about it,” she said. “I am just not finished.” An innocent remark about a clumsy diagram started an hour-long argument, and her finger stayed on the page the whole time. A responsive reader, they learned, beats ten quiet ones.

认可以小形式到来。一位教授称赞他们“方法的纯净”，随后在引注里把曼雅的名字拼错了。一丝怀疑仍在。“我并不是对它绝望，”她说，“我只是还没做完。”一句关于一张笨拙图表的无心之言引发了长达一小时的争论，而她的手指一直停在那一页上。他们明白了：一个反应灵敏的读者，抵得上十个沉默的读者。

**5.** A mammal, a pizza box, and a book of riddles shared one table by the end of the week. Their flexible schedule allowed one more trial; other volunteers signed up. Not every session was useful; most were not, and nobody could say quite why. “Write it down anyway,” she said. “Again.” Nobody knew how much the answer depended on luck. It was, in his words, a trifle: three hundred grams of paper and a million intentions. “Some things are infectious,” Maya said. “Curiosity is one of them. Wake me when the next batch arrives.” Many notes were his, and the study would carry all of them. She smiled at the clumsy sentence he had just written; the wrinkle beside her eye showed she had slept badly. “No,” she said.

到那个周末，一只哺乳动物、一个比萨盒和一本谜语书共用同一张桌子。他们灵活的安排允许再做一次试行，又有其他志愿者报了名。并不是每一次都有用；大多数没有用，也没人说得清究竟为什么。“还是写下来吧，”她说，“一次再一次。”用他的话说，这只是小事一桩：三百克纸和一百万个好意。“有些东西是会传染的，”曼雅说，“好奇心就是其中之一。下一批到的时候叫醒我。”很多笔记都是他写的，而这份研究会把它们全部保留下来。她对他刚写下的那句笨拙的话笑了笑，眼角那道细纹是她睡得不好的唯一迹象。“不，”她说。

### Exercises

- CH019-Q1 [context] What did the girl in the cat sweater reveal?
  - options: the map did not work for someone who could not read / the sign was too expensive / the library was closed / the rumour was true
- CH019-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “易接近的；可使用的”?
  - options: accessible / awkward / humble / infectious
- CH019-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: diversify the examples / diversify the toast / diversify the rumour / diversify the chalk
- CH019-Q4 [cloze] The librarian said that efficiency came ______ the scribes.
  - options: later / hopelessly / clumsily / purely
- CH019-Q5 [recall] What did Maya call curiosity?
  - options: something infectious / a trifle / a rumour / a wrinkle

### Answers

- CH019-Q1: the map did not work for someone who could not read - The trial exposed a reader the group had never considered.
- CH019-Q2: accessible - Accessible means easy to reach or use, which the new version aimed to be.
- CH019-Q3: diversify the examples - Diversify combines with examples, sources, or a portfolio.
- CH019-Q4: later - The librarian valued the hand-copied manuscript above later efficiency.
- CH019-Q5: something infectious - She said curiosity spreads from person to person.

## CH020 - Learning How to Learn: Pressure Builds / 学会学习：压力上升

- theme: 学习方法与教育

**1.** The first pressure came from a reference in an annual report. A quotation from their own study had been used to support a conclusion they had never drawn. “This is a terrible thing to happen to a young project,” Maya said. “It is also a chance to practice precision.” All of them felt the chill of it.

第一个压力来自一份年报里的一处引用。他们自己研究里的一句话被用来支持一个他们从未得出的结论。“这对一个年轻项目来说太糟糕了，”曼雅说，“但这也是练习精确的机会。”他们所有人都感到了那股寒意。

**2.** They heard the same complaint from a military institute, a laboratory, and a casual reader of the student journal. Each wanted a complete list; none wanted an observation. “A form is not a structure,” Lin said. “A structure holds weight.” Amongst the art on the wall, a boy from the school of education had pinned a note: experience without method is only memory.

他们从一家军事研究所、一间实验室和学生刊物的一个随意读者那里听到了同样的抱怨。每个人都想要一份完整的清单；没有人想要一条观察。“表格不是结构，”林伟说，“结构要承重。”墙上的艺术画之间，教育学院的一个男孩钉了一张纸条：没有方法的经验只是记忆。

**3.** The weak point was the second edition, distributed in a batch of two hundred. On the editing table, amongst the crumbs of bread, a ghost of the old text survived — a stray adjective, a missing noun, one line about coordination that nobody could explain. “We must identify it before we produce the next printing,” she said. “Up to now we have been lucky.”

弱点在第二版，那一批印了两百份。在编辑桌上、面包屑之间，旧文本的幽灵还活着——一个多余的形容词、一个缺失的名词、一行没人解释得清的关于协调的文字。“我们必须在下一次印制之前找出它，”她说，“到目前为止我们只是运气好。”

**4.** A smile from Professor Zhao did not soften the news. The institute wanted a new edition within a minute of the review, and the sensitive material — the quotations from named students — had to be removed. The spell of good humour broke. “You cannot manipulate a quotation into consent,” she said. “That is not innovation, it is decoration.”

赵教授的一个微笑并没有让消息变软。研究所要求评审后一分钟内出新版，而那些敏感材料——来自具名学生的引语——必须删掉。好脾气的那道咒语破了。“你不能把引语操纵成同意，”她说，“那不是创新，是装饰。”

**5.** Here the ignorance of the outside world became useful: nobody had told them that such a correction was impossible. A historical note belonged in the appendix, and they would inform every reader of the correction in a separate letter. “This particular error,” Lin wrote in the margin, “is why we keep everything.” Further work was for the reader to compare, not for the author. The new letter would go from the office to every reader; it would lead with the correction, explain why it mattered, and use one word instead of ten. They had to learn that a correction is also information. “Remember the first line,” he told the group. “Other people will read the rest, without us.”

在这里，外部世界的无知反而有用了：没有人告诉过他们这样的更正是不可能的。一条历史说明应放在附录里，他们还要用一封单独的信告知每一位读者。 “正是这个特别的错误，”林伟在页边写道，“让我们保留一切。”进一步的比较属于读者，而不属于作者。那封新的信会从办公室寄给每一位读者；它会以更正开头，解释它为什么重要，并用一个词代替十个词。他们必须明白：更正也是一种信息。“记住第一行，”他对小组说，“其他人会在没有我们的情况下读剩下的部分。”

### Exercises

- CH020-Q1 [context] What had the annual report done with the group's work?
  - options: used a quotation to support a conclusion they never drew / copied their bread recipe / published their names / asked them to write a new edition
- CH020-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “无知”?
  - options: ignorance / coordination / observation / innovation
- CH020-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: identify the error / identify the bread / identify the chill / identify the smile
- CH020-Q4 [cloze] She said they could not ______ a quotation into consent.
  - options: manipulate / distribute / inform / belong
- CH020-Q5 [recall] What did Lin say a correction is?
  - options: also information / a decoration / a chance to rest / a private note

### Answers

- CH020-Q1: used a quotation to support a conclusion they never drew - Their sentence was quoted out of context in the annual report.
- CH020-Q2: ignorance - The outside world's ignorance of the difficulty let the correction proceed.
- CH020-Q3: identify the error - Identify combines with an error, a cause, or a pattern.
- CH020-Q4: manipulate - She refused to turn a quotation into something it did not mean.
- CH020-Q5: also information - He argued that publishing a correction informs readers rather than embarrassing the team.

## CH021 - Learning How to Learn: The Disagreement / 学会学习：分歧

- theme: 学习方法与教育

**1.** The disagreement began in the lab, over a cake. A classmate had brought it to the cafe, and the merriment faded when Maya said that the bakery case was not evidence. “You cannot ignore the qualitative difference,” she said. “A comparison is not an experiment.” Lin disagreed: an experiment, he argued, needed only one measurable thing. The milky coffee in front of her went cold while they argued.

分歧始于实验室，起因是一块蛋糕。一位同班同学把它带到了咖啡馆；当曼雅说面包店的案例不是证据时，欢乐的气氛消散了。“你不能忽视那个质性的差别，”她说，“一次比较不是一次实验。”林伟不同意：他认为实验只需要一个可测量的东西。他们争论时，她面前那杯奶白色的咖啡凉了。

**2.** Their argument grew comparative and then personal. “You are ashamed of the messy part,” she said. “You would rather pose as a scientist than investigate like one.” He heard the injustice in it and said nothing. The resentment stood between them for a full minute, and the sunny afternoon outside made it worse.

他们的争论先是比较性的，随后变成了针对个人。“你为混乱的那部分感到羞耻，”她说，“你宁愿装成一个科学家，也不愿像科学家那样去调查。”他听出了其中的不公，没有说话。怨恨在他们之间站了整整一分钟，外面晴朗的下午让它更糟。

**3.** The president of the student union proposed a compromise: a sample in the grocery, a survey in the warehouse, and a small study in the realm of the desirable. “That is not inclusive,” Maya said. “It is merely convenient, and it is not comparable to anything we have already done.” Such a plan would compete with better-funded work and lose, and all of them knew it.

学生会主席提出了一个折中方案：在杂货店取样、在仓库做问卷，并在“可取之物”的领域里做一项小型研究。“这不具包容性，”曼雅说，“它只是方便，而且与我们已经在做的事根本无法相比。”这样的方案会与资金更充足的工作竞争并落败，他们所有人都明白这一点。

**4.** The widespread heating failure that week settled the argument by removing the choice. Half the group was sick; the other half was behind. “It is advisable to finish this in the warmer months,” the president said. “Here, in this form, it is merely a guess.” He did not apologize, and she did not apologise either; both were too tired for the ceremony of it. The compromise was honorable, though the coffee was not.

那一周大面积的供暖故障以取消选择的方式结束了争论。小组一半人病了，另一半落后了。“明智的做法是在更暖的月份完成它，”主席说，“在这里、以这种形式，它只是一个猜测。”他没有道歉，她也没有道歉；两人都太累了，没有力气去走那个过场。这个折中是体面的，尽管那杯咖啡不是。

**5.** They inquired, they enquired, they clarified, and eventually they produced an argumentation they could both sign. It was methodical, respectful, and insightful about its own limits, and it dealt honestly with the dealing of the previous month. Her curiosity had survived the month, and so had his. “Not one notable finding,” Lin admitted. “No,” she said. “The commencement of a method. That is all, and that is much.” Their universe was one library, one lab, and one cafe, and the problem was still the same one. “Regardless,” she added, “the numbers went up in the right direction.” An inquisitive mind, he decided, was not a weakness. This was the first thing they had grown together rather than by accident, and his major, he realised, had taught him only half of it.

他们询问、打听、澄清，最终写出了一份两人都能签字的论证。它有方法、有敬意，并且对自己的限度有深刻见解；它也诚实地处理了上个月的那笔交易。她的好奇心熬过了这个月，他的也是。“没有一个值得注意的发现，”林伟承认。“不，”她说，“是一种方法的开端。这就够了，而这也很多了。”他们的宇宙就是一座图书馆、一间实验室和一家咖啡馆，而问题依然是同一个问题。“不管怎样，”她补充说，“数字是在朝正确的方向走。”他断定，一颗爱追问的心不是弱点。这是他们第一次共同成长而非偶然长成的东西；他意识到，自己的专业只教会了他其中一半。

### Exercises

- CH021-Q1 [context] What did Maya refuse to accept as evidence?
  - options: a single bakery case / a laboratory experiment / a written survey / a published study
- CH021-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “令人想望的；可取的”?
  - options: desirable / inclusive / comparable / methodical
- CH021-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: investigate the problem / investigate the cake / investigate the coffee / investigate the merriment
- CH021-Q4 [cloze] The heating failure was ______ that week.
  - options: widespread / sunny / milky / respectful
- CH021-Q5 [recall] What did Maya call the result of their work?
  - options: the commencement of a method / a notable finding / an honourable apology / a competition

### Answers

- CH021-Q1: a single bakery case - She insisted that one example cannot carry a general conclusion.
- CH021-Q2: desirable - The compromise was framed around what seemed desirable rather than what was measurable.
- CH021-Q3: investigate the problem - Investigate combines naturally with a problem, a cause, or a claim.
- CH021-Q4: widespread - The heating failure affected the whole campus.
- CH021-Q5: the commencement of a method - She valued the start of a method above a single finding.

## CH022 - Learning How to Learn: What the Numbers Hide / 学会学习：数字隐藏了什么

- theme: 学习方法与教育

**1.** The charge came early and it was explicit: the group had measured the wrong thing. A visiting examiner glanced at their table and asked what the numbers could mean. “You count arrivals,” she said. “You cannot see comprehension.” There was a lack of wisdom in the early draft, and they knew it. The criticism was not commercial; it was about territory. But their numbers did not increase, and that was the first sign of trouble.

指控来得很早，而且明确：小组测量错了东西。一位来访的考官瞥了一眼他们的表格，问这些数字能意味着什么。“你们数的是到场人数，”她说，“你们看不到理解。”早期草稿里缺少智慧，他们自己也知道。这项批评不是商业性的，而是地盘性的。但他们的数字没有增加，而这是麻烦的第一个信号。

**2.** A sensible answer would separate retention from recognition. A student who can recite a definition may still fail to recognize the same idea in a new context. Memory, they wrote, is not a certificate; it is a presence that decays unless it is used. The decrease was visible in their own data, and the meaning of that line was the catch.

一个明智的答案会把保持与识别分开。一个能背出定义的学生，仍可能在新情境中认不出同一个概念。他们写道：记忆不是一张证书，而是一种会衰败的在场，除非被使用。下降在自己数据里是可见的，而那一行的含义正是陷阱所在。

**3.** The obstacle was perception. A skilled reader could penetrate a vague sentence at a glance, and a beginner could not; an oval diagram of their results made that difference noticeable, and her finger followed it while she talked. “You should instruct with an illustrative example,” Professor Zhao said. “Show the glimpse of understanding, not the purchase of a book.”

障碍在于知觉。熟练的读者一瞥就能穿透含糊的句子，初学者却不能；他们结果的那张椭圆图把这种差别显现得相当明显，她说话时手指一直顺着它移动。“你应该用一个作例证的例子来讲，”赵教授说，“展示理解的一瞥，而不是购买一本书。”

**4.** They began to oppose the easy number. A gifted singer, a rental clerk, and a boy with a rifle on a poster all appeared in the same week's notes, and none of them could qualify as evidence. “We must greet the sceptic,” Maya said, “and not be bothered by her.” It was a sophisticated position, and it took sophistication to defend it without sparkle. Other volunteers agreed; a kid from the first year asked the sharpest question of the evening.

他们开始反对那个容易的数字。一位有天赋的歌手、一位租车店员，以及海报上一个拿着步枪的男孩都出现在同一周的笔记里，而他们当中没有一个能算作证据。“我们必须欢迎怀疑者，”曼雅说，“而不是被她烦到。”这是一个久经世故的立场，而要既捍卫它又不靠卖弄光彩，需要同样的世故。其他志愿者也同意了；一个一年级的孩子提出了当晚最尖锐的问题。

**5.** So they rebuilt the study around two questions: what would a reader remember, and what could a reader do? An invention of their own — a seven-line test — became the new instrument, and its performance improved the whole file. They were readily aware of its limits, and they did not regret stating them. “Do not let the outside world intrude on the meaning,” Lin wrote. “Enlighten it instead.” Later, a fresh teacher asked whether the test prevented guessing; the honest answer was that it did not, and they wrote that down too. “Yeah,” Maya said. “Turn the page and start again.”

于是他们围绕两个问题重建研究：读者会记住什么，读者能做什么？他们自己的一项发明——一份七行测试——成了新工具，它的表现改善了整个档案。他们很快就意识到它的限度，也并不后悔把它写出来。“不要让外部世界闯入意义，”林伟写道，“而是去启发它。”后来，一位新来的教师问这项测试是否防止了猜测；诚实的回答是并没有，他们把这一点也写了下来。“是的，”曼雅说，“翻过这一页，重新开始。”

### Exercises

- CH022-Q1 [context] What did the examiner say the group could not see?
  - options: comprehension / attendance / the timetable / the budget
- CH022-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “保持；保留”?
  - options: retention / perception / presence / decay
- CH022-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: penetrate a vague sentence / penetrate a certificate / penetrate a purchase / penetrate a rental
- CH022-Q4 [cloze] They wrote that memory is not a ______ but a presence.
  - options: certificate / glimpse / sparkle / rifle
- CH022-Q5 [recall] What did they invent for the new study?
  - options: a seven-line test / a new certificate / an oval poster / a dictionary

### Answers

- CH022-Q1: comprehension - Counting arrivals says nothing about understanding.
- CH022-Q2: retention - They separated what is retained from what is recognised.
- CH022-Q3: penetrate a vague sentence - Penetrate means to see through something difficult.
- CH022-Q4: certificate - They argued that memory is a living presence, not a document.
- CH022-Q5: a seven-line test - The group wrote a short test of its own.

## CH023 - Learning How to Learn: A Better Question / 学会学习：更好的问题

- theme: 学习方法与教育

**1.** The better question came from a criticism in the monthly round of the association: what, exactly, does the course prove? “A member of your department could not answer that,” the reviewer wrote. “The blank in your form is not a modest pause; it is a hole.” She meant that the team had been writing around the problem.

更好的问题来自协会月度评议里的一句批评：这门课程究竟证明了什么？“你系里的一位成员回答不了这个问题，”评论者写道，“你表格里的空白不是谦虚的停顿，而是一个洞。”她的意思是：团队一直在绕着问题写作。

**2.** The team gathered in the department office with a picture, an album, and a slide of the campus in snow. “We are ready to dig,” Maya said. “The search has to be narrower.” A gradual correction replaced an early apology: they had been determined to prove too much, and determination is not a method.

团队在系办公室聚齐，带着一张照片、一本相册，以及一张校园雪景的幻灯片。“我们准备好挖下去了，”曼雅说，“但搜索范围必须更窄。”一次渐进的更正取代了早先的道歉：他们曾下定决心要证明太多东西，而决心并不是方法。

**3.** So they specialized. One member kept a drawer of index cards; another ran a calculation on a slice of the data; a third hunted for the sort of question that survives an oral examination. “Be receptive,” Professor Zhao said, “not combative.” The enterprise would suspend its louder claims till the sand settled. A marsh on the edge of the region had taught them the same lesson: water finds the lowest line, and so does a good question.

于是他们做了专门化。一位成员保留一抽屉索引卡；另一位对一片数据做了计算；第三位则去寻找那种能经受口试的问题。“要有接纳力，”赵教授说，“而不是好斗。”这家“事业”将暂缓它更响亮的说法，直到沙尘落定。区域边缘的一片沼泽教过他们同样的道理：水会找到最低的那条线，一个好问题也会。

**4.** A singer from the nobility of the student choir, an aggressive reviewer, and a boy with a nut allergy appeared in the notes of the same week. The team could not concentrate, and the upset was visible in every face at the table. “This is not a novel,” Lin said. “It is a report.” Her initiative was to cut the first page entirely.

学生合唱团里一位出身贵族的歌手、一位咄咄逼人的评论者，以及一个对坚果过敏的男孩出现在同一周的笔记里。团队无法集中，烦躁显而易见。“这不是小说，”林伟说，“这是一份报告。”她的主动之举是把第一页整个删掉。

**5.** They wrote a new first line, which they would defend here and everywhere: every number carries a name. All of them signed it, and her name came last. “Could you say it more plainly?” the reviewer asked. “No,” Maya said, “but we can say it again.” A handy wood frame held the sheet above the desk, where visitors could not avoid it. His own book, which he had not finished, said the same thing; the number of pages meant nothing. This was the point they kept coming up against, and they would write it down until they could hear it.

他们写下了新的第一行，也是他们将在此处和所有地方捍卫的一行：每个数字背后都有一个名字。他们所有人都签了名，她的名字在最后。“你能说得更直白些吗？”评论者问。“不能，”曼雅说，“但我们可以再说一遍。”一只便利的木框把这张纸固定在桌子上方。他自己那本没读完的书说的也是同一件事；页数本身毫无意义。这正是他们反复撞上的那一点，而他们会一直写下去，直到自己能听见它。

### Exercises

- CH023-Q1 [context] What did the reviewer call the blank in the form?
  - options: a hole / a modest pause / a signature / a footnote
- CH023-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “主动行动；首创精神”?
  - options: initiative / nobility / calculation / criticism
- CH023-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: concentrate on the data / concentrate on the sand / concentrate on the drawer / concentrate on the choir
- CH023-Q4 [cloze] Professor Zhao told them to be ______ rather than combative.
  - options: receptive / aggressive / upset / lame
- CH023-Q5 [recall] What was the new first line?
  - options: every number carries a name / every name carries a number / the search must be narrower / this is not a novel

### Answers

- CH023-Q1: a hole - The reviewer saw an unanswered question, not a polite gap.
- CH023-Q2: initiative - She took the initiative and cut the first page.
- CH023-Q3: concentrate on the data - Concentrate combines with data, a task, or a problem.
- CH023-Q4: receptive - He preferred an open mind to a combative one.
- CH023-Q5: every number carries a name - The new opening line tied each figure to a person.

## CH024 - Learning How to Learn: The Second Attempt / 学会学习：第二次尝试

- theme: 学习方法与教育

**1.** The second attempt began in January with a slim brochure and a wide table. There were six copies of the manual, a dozen notebooks, and a mug that said “read me twice”. “So this is the semester of paper,” Maya said. “However, a good handbook is not a bestseller.”

第二次尝试在一月以一本薄薄的小册子和一张宽桌子开始。那里有六份手册、一打笔记本，还有一只写着“读我两遍”的杯子。“所以这就是纸的学期，”曼雅说，“不过，一本好手册不会是畅销书。”

**2.** Both of them collected citations the way other people collect stamps, rather than the way examiners collect mistakes. A quarterly commentary, a monthly dictionary supplement, a weekly song sheet, and a yearly report were piled above the desk. “Cite the page,” Lin said. “Quote the line. Otherwise the reader cannot check us.” The preface of the slim volume was written last, and an additional dozen copies were ordered before anyone had read it.

他们两人像别人收集邮票那样收集引注，而不是像考官收集错误那样。一份季度评论、一份月度词典增补、一份每周歌单，以及一份年度报告，堆在桌子上方。“引用要给出页码，”林伟说，“引语要给出原句。否则读者无法核对。”这本薄书的前言是最后写的，而在任何人读过它之前，又加订了十二本。

**3.** He taught a short course on the introduction of numbers into prose. Experience, he said, is not information; a battalion of facts can hide a single mistake. A coal fire kept the winter evening warm, a jacket hung on the back of a chair, and a bible lay open at a genealogy: all made the same point — the staff usually remember what they can picture.

他开了一门短课，讲数字如何被引入散文。他说，经验不是信息；一营的事实能掩盖一个错误。一个煤黑的冬夜、一件搭在椅背上的外套、一本翻在族谱处的圣经，都在说明同一点：职工通常只记得住自己能想象的东西。

**4.** They sought a dual method: one page for the reader, one page for oneself. A commentary in the margin, a datum in the corner, and a second commentary on the commentary kept them honest. The art of it was restraint, and the education faculty noticed. A monthly review of their own pages, written in pencil, became the habit that held the whole project together.

他们寻求一种双重的方法：一页给读者，一页给自己。页边的评注、角落里的一个数据，以及关于评注的第二层评注，让他们保持诚实。它的艺术在于克制，而教育学院注意到了这一点。每月用铅笔写下一次对自己页面的复查，成了把这整个项目维系在一起的习惯。

**5.** Twelve thousand words later, the department could learn from the mistakes of the first edition. Twenty pages in, nine of them had to be rewritten; he did not mention it to the others. Sometimes they remembered the old wording; mostly they did not. “Seek the sort of sentence that can survive being read aloud,” she said, and so they did, in the school library, with the first snow of the year on the roof. Above the shelves a lamp burned all afternoon. The second attempt was not a correction of the first; it was a different book with the same subject, and both of them knew it.

一万二千字之后，系里能够从第一版的错误中学习了。写到二十页时，其中九页必须重写；他没有向其他人提起这件事。有时他们记得旧的措辞，大多数时候不记得。“去寻找那种能被朗读出来的句子，”她说，于是他们就这样做了，在学校的图书馆里，屋顶上落着那年第一场雪。书架上方的灯整个下午都亮着。第二次尝试不是对第一次的修正；它是一本主题相同而内容不同的书，而他们两人都知道这一点。

### Exercises

- CH024-Q1 [context] What did the mug say?
  - options: read me twice / read me once / drink me slowly / check the page
- CH024-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “双重的”?
  - options: dual / slim / wide / yearly
- CH024-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: cite the page / cite the mug / cite the jacket / cite the snow
- CH024-Q4 [cloze] A battalion of facts can hide a single ______.
  - options: mistake / brochure / preface / semester
- CH024-Q5 [recall] What did they seek for the reader?
  - options: a sentence that survives being read aloud / a bestseller / a monthly report / a new dictionary

### Answers

- CH024-Q1: read me twice - The mug carried the group's rule for careful reading.
- CH024-Q2: dual - The method had two pages with two purposes.
- CH024-Q3: cite the page - Cite combines with a page, a source, or an author.
- CH024-Q4: mistake - Volume of facts does not guarantee accuracy.
- CH024-Q5: a sentence that survives being read aloud - She set a test of readability.

## CH025 - Learning How to Learn: Outside Pressure / 学会学习：外部压力

- theme: 学习方法与教育

**1.** An inspection in the spring brought outside pressure of a very specific kind. A classified report on the language programme in the tower called the map's claims “a decorative curtain”. “It is not false,” the inspector said, “but it is not success either. Unless you can show a real increase in comprehension, the funding goes elsewhere.”

春天的一次检查带来了非常具体的外部压力。一份关于这座塔楼语言项目的机密报告把地图的主张称为“一块装饰性的帷幕”。“它不是假的，”检查员说，“但也不是成功。除非你们能证明理解力有真实的提高，资金就会流向别处。”

**2.** The students had begun to complain, and the complaint was realistic; it stung. A movie about a miracle teacher had made the public expect miracles, and the group had to explain that a quiet change is not a disappearance. “We cannot motivate a funder with beauty,” Maya said. “We have to classify what we actually do.” The silence in the room was the loudest thing in it.

这项抱怨是现实的，也刺痛了他们。一部关于奇迹教师的电影让公众期待奇迹，而小组必须解释：一种安静的变化并不是消失。“我们无法用美来激励出资人，”曼雅说，“我们必须把我们实际做的事分类说清。”屋里最响的东西就是那份沉默。

**3.** The damage was done to their timeline rather than to their data. A report had to be written in prose, with no room for a lame excuse; every verb, adverb, and preposition would be examined. “Do not elevate the language above the evidence,” Professor Zhao said. “Converse with the reader, do not narrate at them.”

受损的是时间表，而不是数据。报告必须用散文写成，没有留给他们一个蹩脚借口的空间；每一个动词、副词和介词都会被审查。“不要把语言抬到证据之上，”赵教授说，“要与读者交谈，而不是对他们叙述。”

**4.** The inspector was intelligent, and her intelligence was not the problem; the problem was the imaginary reader she represented. A vacant office, a mix of old and new rules, and a devil of a deadline made the work harder. The disappointment was real, and the ruin of the old plan was complete. “I would rather be honest than wide,” Lin wrote. “There is a reason we keep the ugly numbers.”

问题不是检查员的智力，而是她所代表的那个想象中的读者。一间空置的办公室、一套新旧规则混杂的制度，以及一个魔鬼般的截止日期，让工作更难。失望是真实的，旧计划的废墟也彻底落定。“我宁可诚实，也不要宽泛，”林伟写道，“我们保留那些难看的数字是有原因的。”

**5.** Awareness grew slowly, and so did the paperwork. The coordinator overheard two students in the corridor and put their sentence at the top of the report: “It is not easy, but it is really ours.” That line did more than the thousand figures; six weeks later, twenty pages of additional material were requested, and the coordinate of their next move was actually simple: publish everything, including the failure. “The hindrance was never the method,” she said. “It was the deceptive quiet of the middle class of donors.” A regretful paragraph replaced it, and the group united behind one sentence: a small true thing, repeated. However, much of the paper was already written, and sometimes a single sheet could carry a speech. They could mean it, which was not nothing.

意识慢慢增长，文书工作也是。协调员在走廊里无意中听到两名学生的话，把他们的句子放在了报告的最前面：“这不容易，但它真的是我们的。”这句话比一千个数字更有用；六周后，二十页补充材料被要求提交，而他们下一步行动的坐标其实很简单：把一切公布出来，包括失败。“障碍从来不是方法，”她说，“而是中产阶级捐赠者那种具有欺骗性的安静。”一段遗憾的文字取代了它，小组团结在一句话背后：一件小而真的事，重复下去。

### Exercises

- CH025-Q1 [context] What did the classified report call the map's claims?
  - options: a decorative curtain / a quiet change / a real increase / a lame excuse
- CH025-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “障碍；妨害”?
  - options: hindrance / coordinate / inspection / awareness
- CH025-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: elevate the language / elevate the curtain / elevate the corridor / elevate the miracle
- CH025-Q4 [cloze] Lin wrote that he would rather be honest than ______.
  - options: wide / false / vacant / regretful
- CH025-Q5 [recall] What sentence did the coordinator overhear?
  - options: it is not easy, but it is really ours / the funding goes elsewhere / beauty is not evidence / publish everything

### Answers

- CH025-Q1: a decorative curtain - The inspector judged the claims ornamental rather than false.
- CH025-Q2: hindrance - She said the obstacle had never been the method itself.
- CH025-Q3: elevate the language - Elevate combines with language, a tone, or a standard.
- CH025-Q4: wide - He chose a narrow, honest claim over a broad one.
- CH025-Q5: it is not easy, but it is really ours - Two students in the corridor supplied the report's opening line.

## CH026 - Learning How to Learn: The Decision / 学会学习：决定

- theme: 学习方法与教育

**1.** The test the group could not control arrived as a single sheet of paper. A regional reading survey would include their method, and the administration wanted a decision by the end of the quarter. “None of us can tell how this will end,” Maya said. “We can only decide what to send.” A grey drawing of the reading tank — their own childish sketch — lay on the table beside the official form, and every line of it looked wrong.

那个小组无法控制的测试，以一张纸的形式到来。一项区域阅读调查将纳入他们的方法，而行政部门要求在本季度结束前给出决定。“我们谁都无法告诉彼此这会怎么结束，”曼雅说，“我们只能决定寄出什么。”一幅灰色的阅读槽图——他们自己幼稚的速写——摊在桌上官方表格的旁边，它的每一行看上去都不对。

**2.** The population of the survey was a million students, and their sample was a few hundred. “To be frank, that is a lot of noise,” the principal said. “Your best hope is a quite small, clean result.” Each of them knew it already. Every classification has an edge, and theirs was the edge of the page. The least they could do was say so in advance.

调查的总体是一百万名学生，而他们的样本只有几百人。“相当坦率地说，那是很大的噪声，”校长说，“你们最好的希望是一个小而干净的结果。”他们每个人早就知道这一点。每一种分类都有它的边缘，而他们的边缘就是这张纸的边。至少他们可以事先说明这一点。

**3.** They decided to send the method itself, not the results. A vertical line, a signature, a stamp, and a bundle of cord went into one parcel, which looked like a costume rather than a proposal. “If the survey finds nothing,” Lin wrote, “that is a confirmation, not a disaster.” A spark of doubt remained, but the chorus of agreement was louder than the doubt.

他们决定寄出方法本身，而不是结果。一条垂直线、一个签名、一枚图章和一捆绳子装进一个包裹，看上去更像一套戏服而不是一份提案。“如果调查什么都没发现，”林伟写道，“那也是一种确认，而不是灾难。”怀疑的火花仍在，但赞同的合唱比怀疑更响。

**4.** They discussed the wording for an hour. To throw the old version away was tempting, but a continuation is not a rejection; it is a steel beam added under a wooden floor. “Browse the appendix,” Maya said. “A panda, a wolf, and a dragon in a child's drawing taught us more than the survey ever will.” The tap dripped, and nobody moved to close it.

他们为一个措辞讨论了一个小时。把旧版本扔掉很诱人，但延续不是拒绝；它是在木地板下加进一根钢梁。“去翻附录吧，”曼雅说，“一个孩子画里的大熊猫、狼和龙，教给我们的东西比这项调查能教的还多。”水龙头在滴水，没人去关它。

**5.** A slip of white paper, a leaf from an organic farm, a pound of spare paper, and a small bottle of liquid were their belongings on the last night of the term. “You are the hero of your own report,” Professor Zhao said, “and that is exactly the problem.” Several of them sat in silence; some smiled at the other volunteers. “Granted, you have very little time,” she went on. “But a thread is enough to hold a signature together, and a crane can lift what a hand cannot.” There was no solution, only a decision: a new line, which many of them preferred to most of the old ones. Her eye caught the remaining problem in it, but really there was no time.

一张白纸片、一片有机农场的叶子，以及一小瓶液体，是他们在学期最后一晚的全部所有物。“你是你自己报告里的英雄，”赵教授说，“而这恰恰是问题所在。”他们中有几个人沉默地坐着，有些人朝其他志愿者笑了笑。“就算如此，你们的时间很少，”她接着说，“但一根线就足以把签名固定住，而起重机能举起手举不起的东西。”没有解决方案，只有一个决定：一条新的线，他们中许多人更喜欢它，胜过大多数旧的线。她的眼睛看出了其中残留的问题，但说实话，已经没有时间了。

### Exercises

- CH026-Q1 [context] What did the group decide to send to the survey?
  - options: the method, not the results / a grey drawing / a bundle of cord / the principal's letter
- CH026-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “坦白的；率直的”?
  - options: frank / stiff / vertical / organic
- CH026-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: discuss the wording / discuss the tank / discuss the tap / discuss the panda
- CH026-Q4 [cloze] Lin wrote that a confirmation is not a ______.
  - options: disaster / chorus / classification / spark
- CH026-Q5 [recall] What is a continuation in Lin's image?
  - options: a steel beam under a wooden floor / a new costume / a grey drawing / a bottle of liquid

### Answers

- CH026-Q1: the method, not the results - They sent the procedure and let the survey produce its own numbers.
- CH026-Q2: frank - The principal spoke frankly about the noise in the data.
- CH026-Q3: discuss the wording - Discuss combines with wording, a plan, or a problem.
- CH026-Q4: disaster - A negative result would confirm the method rather than destroy it.
- CH026-Q5: a steel beam under a wooden floor - He described continuation as reinforcement, not rejection.

## CH027 - Learning How to Learn: Consequences / 学会学习：后果

- theme: 学习方法与教育

**1.** The consequences came in the form of a school report. A kindergarten headmaster, a therapist, and a poet were listed as beneficiaries of the study, and not one of them had ever been asked. “Admittedly, that is a fault of ours,” Maya said. “We cater to the people who read reports, not to the people inside them.”

后果以一份学校报告的形式到来。一位幼儿园园长、一位治疗师和一位诗人被列为这项研究的受益人，而他们当中没有一个曾被问过。“诚然，这是我们的过失，”曼雅说，“我们迎合的是读报告的人，而不是报告里面的人。”

**2.** The first acknowledgement arrived with a loaf of bread and a jar of jam. A silvery morning, a rainbow over the sports hall, and the smell of a loaf cooling on a windowsill made the whole argument feel splendid and slightly unreal. “Sugar, honey, candy, biscuits,” Lin wrote. “The vocabulary of comfort is doing the work of the vocabulary of proof.”

第一份致谢随一条面包和一罐果酱到来。一个银色的早晨、体育场上空的彩虹，以及窗台上冷却面包的气味，让整个论证显得灿烂又有点不真实。“糖、蜂蜜、糖果、饼干，”林伟写道，“舒适的语言正在干证明的语言的活。”

**3.** He was frustrated by the tiny scope. Young volunteers wanted a glorious result, and the darkness of the numbers was itself the answer. “A peach and an orange are not the same fruit,” Maya said, “even when both are sweet.” Furniture was moved to make room for a new shelf, and the glow of the reading lamp became the room's only decoration.

他被这微小的范围弄得沮丧。年轻的志愿者想要一个光荣的结果，而数字里的黑暗本身就是答案。“桃子和橘子不是同一种水果，”曼雅说，“即使两者都甜。”家具被搬动，为一只新书架腾出位置，阅读灯的微光成了房间里唯一的装饰。

**4.** The children taught themselves faster than the adults did; the method itself was not the problem. A bushy hedge, a tomato plant, and a rose in a wine bottle stood on the windowsill. “A teen can tell when a greeting is automatic,” the therapist said. “That is the awful part.” A dark joke about the yellow folder did not help.

孩子们教自己的速度比大人更快；方法本身并不是问题。窗户上放着一丛茂密的树篱、一株番茄，以及插在葡萄酒瓶里的一朵玫瑰。“青少年能分辨出问候是不是自动的，”治疗师说，“那才是可怕的地方。”关于那只黄色文件夹的一个黑色玩笑并没有帮上忙。

**5.** A scholastic prize, a sandwich, and a shine of sweat on the window told him nothing about what the children had actually learned. They wrote a philosophical page about measurement and a sleepy one about the six forms of feedback. Then they stopped, because the poetry of the thing was becoming a substitute for the thing. “From here on,” Lin said, “we lead with what we do not know.” All of it was given to the school without conditions; however, there was a lot they could not hear from the corridor. So this was the other side of the yellow folder: a fortunate habit of writing things up, and nobody up there to read them. A movie would have ended here; a project does not.

他们写了一页关于测量的哲学文字，又写了一页关于六种反馈的、令人困倦的文字。然后他们停下来了，因为这件事的诗意正在取代这件事本身。“从这里开始，”林伟说，“我们用我们不知道的东西开头。”所有这些都被无条件地交给了学校；然而，有很多东西他们从走廊里根本听不见。所以这就是那只黄色文件夹的另一面：一种写下来的幸运习惯，而上面没有人去读。一部电影会在这里结束；一个项目不会。

### Exercises

- CH027-Q1 [context] Who was listed as beneficiaries of the study?
  - options: a headmaster, a therapist, and a poet / the student union / the registration office / three examiners
- CH027-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “承认；致谢”?
  - options: acknowledgement / continuation / classification / confirmation
- CH027-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: cater to readers / cater to a rainbow / cater to a hedge / cater to a shelf
- CH027-Q4 [cloze] Maya said that a peach and an ______ are not the same fruit.
  - options: orange / onion / olive / oval
- CH027-Q5 [recall] What did Lin say they would lead with from then on?
  - options: what they did not know / the vocabulary of comfort / the school report / the new shelf

### Answers

- CH027-Q1: a headmaster, a therapist, and a poet - The report named beneficiaries who had never been consulted.
- CH027-Q2: acknowledgement - The first acknowledgement came with bread and jam.
- CH027-Q3: cater to readers - Cater to means to serve a particular group's tastes.
- CH027-Q4: orange - She used the fruit comparison against false equivalence.
- CH027-Q5: what they did not know - He wanted the unknown to lead the report rather than hide inside it.

## CH028 - Learning How to Learn: The Bridge Forward / 学会学习：通向下一程

- theme: 学习方法与教育

**1.** The examiner arrived with a briefcase, a paperback, and a very stiff manner. “Sir,” Maya said, “we can examine the method in front of you, if you like.” He wanted to know where the idea came from, and the honest answer was that it came from everywhere and was not particularly helpful.

检查人带着一只公文包、一本平装书和一副非常生硬的姿态到来。“先生，”曼雅说，“如果您愿意，我们可以在您面前检验这套方法。”他想知道这个想法从何而来，而诚实的答案是：它来自各处，而且并没有特别的帮助。

**2.** A fellow in a dark jacket asked how a literary project could claim fluency. “It cannot,” Lin said. “But it can locate the point where fluency fails.” A dog barked below the window, someone gave a shout in the yard, and a horse went past with a cart.

一位穿深色夹克的同行问，一个文学项目怎么能声称流畅。“它不能，”林伟说，“但它能定位出流畅失效的那一点。”窗下有一条狗在叫，院子里有人喊了一声，一匹马拉着车经过。

**3.** A magical claim would have been easier. A poetic one, a Confucian one, an artistic one: any of them could have hidden the gap. “Do not boast,” Professor Zhao said. “A scripture and an X-ray have this in common: neither cares what you believe.” The whisper in the corridor was that the funding had already been decided.

神奇的说法会更容易。诗意的、儒家的、艺术的说法：其中任何一种都能掩盖这个缺口。“不要自夸，”赵教授说，“一部经书和一张X光片有一个共同点：两者都不在乎你相信什么。”走廊里的低语说，资金其实早已定好了。

**4.** They knelt by the low shelf to weigh the evidence, and a sketchy diagram of their own ancestry of ideas — a map of who had taught whom — lay between them. “A manipulative summary is worse than a selfish one,” she said. “At least selfishness is honest.” An editorial in the student paper called the project a quiet success, and by that it meant a small one. The university senate had asked the same question, and its answer would have to exemplify the existing method rather than replace it.

他们跪在低矮的书架旁权衡证据，一幅他们自己思想血统的粗略图表——一张谁教过谁的地图——摊在两人之间。“操纵性的总结比自私的总结更糟，”她说，“至少自私是诚实的。”学生报的一篇社论称这个项目是一次安静的成功，而它指的是：一次很小的成功。大学评议会问过同样的问题，而它的答案必须示范现有的方法，而不是取代它。

**5.** The arc closed with an unresolved sentence. “We can manage what we can measure, and we can ignore what we cannot,” Lin wrote. “That is not a reason to stop; it is a reason to say so.” Such honesty damaged their chances, and they knew it themselves. The everyday work — a colonized shelf of borrowed books, a damaged sign, a dictation nobody had typed — continued anyway, and a worldwide network of three people is still a network. A movie would have ended here. A project does not. Unless the survey said otherwise, the map itself would stay. Somewhere between the speech he had prepared and the speech he actually gave, he found the sentence: a knowledgeable reader is not the same as a knowledgeable writer, and an old oriental art catalogue is not a method. “It has a peculiar magic,” Maya said. “Do not pronounce it finished.” She asked him to explore the existing shelf and to choose, really, one book for him. Learning how to learn had cost them a year; the only thing they could show for it was a better question.

这段弧线以一句未解决的句子收尾。“我们能管理我们能测量的东西，也能忽视我们不能测量的东西，”林伟写道，“这不是停下的理由，而是把它说出来的理由。”这样的诚实损害了他们的机会，他们自己也知道。日常的工作——一排借来的书构成的殖民地式书架、一块损坏的指示牌、一段没人打出来的口述——仍然继续着；而一个由三个人组成的全球网络，仍然是一个网络。一部电影会在这里结束。一个项目不会。除非调查得出别的结论，那张地图本身会留下来。在他准备好的那篇讲话和他实际讲出的那篇讲话之间的某处，他找到了那句话：一个博学的读者不等于一个博学的作者，而一份古老的东方艺术目录不是一种方法。“它有一种奇特魔力，”曼雅说，“别说它已经完成。”她请他去探查现有的那个书架，并且真正为自己挑一本书。

### Exercises

- CH028-Q1 [context] What did the examiner want to know?
  - options: where the idea came from / how much the shelf cost / who owned the cart / when the paper was printed
- CH028-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “流利；流畅”?
  - options: fluency / ancestry / dictation / editorial
- CH028-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: weigh the evidence / weigh the whisper / weigh the briefcase / weigh the scripture
- CH028-Q4 [cloze] Professor Zhao warned them not to ______ about the project.
  - options: boast / kneel / locate / colonize
- CH028-Q5 [recall] What does Lin say honesty is a reason for?
  - options: saying so / stopping the project / hiding the numbers / printing a paperback

### Answers

- CH028-Q1: where the idea came from - The examiner probed the origin of the method.
- CH028-Q2: fluency - The project claimed reading fluency, which Lin qualified.
- CH028-Q3: weigh the evidence - Weigh combines naturally with evidence or a decision.
- CH028-Q4: boast - She warned against claiming more than the evidence supported.
- CH028-Q5: saying so - He treated honesty as a reason to state limits openly.

## CH029 - Promises and Pressure: A Signal / 承诺与压力：一个信号

- theme: 友谊、家庭与人际关系

**1.** The map had taught them to measure a gate in a minute; it had not taught them how to be at home. The third arc opened at home, in a kitchen with a fridge that hummed like a small, unhappy mule. Lin's mother was a textile worker, a daughter of farmers, and the mother of a boy who was never at home. “You reckon you are busy,” she said. “Ensure that you eat.”

那张地图教会他们在一分钟里测完一道门；它没有教会他们如何待在家里。第三段弧线在家里开始，在一间厨房里，冰箱像一头不快活的小骡子那样嗡嗡作响。林伟的母亲是纺织工人、农民的女儿，也是一个从不在家的男孩的母亲。“你以为你很忙，”她说，“要保证吃饭。”

**2.** His grandson — his sister's boy — was doing a biographical project on an actress nobody in the family had heard of. The early pages would reveal a bad scare, a disastrous first draft, and the name of a granddaughter; three notebooks do not constitute a biography, and she said so whilst stirring the soup. “It is a thorough piece of work,” the boy said, “and it is a disaster.” A donkey brayed in a field, and a deer and a lioness were on the poster above the sink. “A tigress is not a house cat,” said the grandmother, “and neither is a daughter.”

他的孙子——他姐姐家的男孩——正在做一个关于某位女演员的传记项目，而家里没人听过那个名字。开头几页会揭示一场可怕的惊吓、一份灾难性的初稿，以及一个孙女的名字；三个笔记本构不成一部传记，而她在搅汤的时候就是这么说的。“这是一份非常彻底的作业，”男孩说，“而且是一场灾难。”田里一头驴在叫，水槽上方的海报里有一头鹿和一头母狮。“母虎不是家猫，”祖母说，“女儿也不是。”

**3.** A suspicious neighbour, a scary story about a shaver left plugged in, and a horrible smell from the drain made the evening eventful. “Do not behave like a hero,” his mother said. “Negotiate.” The Olympic flag on the wall, faded by the sun, was the only decoration in the room, and the whole evening seemed to revolve around the cooker.

一个多疑的邻居、一个关于电动剃刀插着电的吓人故事，以及下水道传来的可怕气味，让这个傍晚多事。“不要表现得像个英雄，”他母亲说。“去谈。”墙上那面被太阳晒褪色的奥运旗帜是屋里唯一的装饰，而整个晚上似乎都绕着灶台旋转。

**4.** They talked about the baby boom, a baby boomer aunt, and a cock that crowed at four in the morning. “Humanity is not a measurable thing,” she said, “and that is the pity of it.” The doom of the small project was written into the timetable, and they both knew it. Any talk of plans was tragically short, and joyous only at the edges.

他们谈起婴儿潮、一位婴儿潮一代的姑妈，以及一只凌晨四点打鸣的公鸡。“人性不是可测量的东西，”她说，“而这正是可惜之处。”这个小项目的厄运写在时间表里，他们两人都知道。任何关于计划的谈话都短得悲惨，只在边缘处显得快乐。

**5.** What astonished him was the rhythm of the household: a rhythmic clatter, a humorous remark, a bowl of white rice, and a lot of unspoken resentment. “Yes, sir,” the boy said, when his grandfather asked whether he would help. “I would tell you to centralize your work,” his mother said, “but your father could not do it either.” The best teacher he ever had was a kid of nine, later a neighbour, who knew the difference between a mean remark and a pound of rice. He could not amaze her with a plan; he could only turn up, particularly on the bad days, and that was the increase she had asked for.

让他惊讶的是这个家庭的节奏：一阵有节奏的响声、一句幽默的评论、一碗白米饭，以及许多没有说出口的怨恨。“是的，先生，”男孩在祖父问他是否愿意帮忙时说。“我本想告诉你要把工作集中起来，”他母亲说，“但你父亲也做不到。”他有过的最好的老师是一个九岁的孩子，后来成了邻居，他知道一句刻薄的话和一磅米之间的区别。他无法用一个计划让她惊叹；他只能在糟糕的日子里去出现——尤其是那些日子——而那正是她所要求的增加。

### Exercises

- CH029-Q1 [context] What did Lin's mother tell him to ensure?
  - options: that he eats / that he studies / that he telephones the actress / that he fixes the drain
- CH029-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “商议；谈判”?
  - options: negotiate / behave / reckon / astonish
- CH029-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: a thorough piece of work / a scary piece of work / a suspicious piece of work / a joyous piece of work
- CH029-Q4 [cloze] The grandmother said that a tigress is not a house ______.
  - options: cat / cock / deer / mule
- CH029-Q5 [recall] What could Lin do instead of amazing his mother with a plan?
  - options: turn up, particularly on the bad days / centralize his work / buy a pound of rice / write a biography

### Answers

- CH029-Q1: that he eats - Her first concern was that he was not feeding himself.
- CH029-Q2: negotiate - She told him to negotiate rather than play the hero.
- CH029-Q3: a thorough piece of work - Thorough describes work done completely.
- CH029-Q4: cat - She compared a daughter to a wild animal, not a pet.
- CH029-Q5: turn up, particularly on the bad days - Presence, not plans, was what his family asked of him.

## CH030 - Promises and Pressure: The First Promise / 承诺与压力：第一次承诺

- theme: 友谊、家庭与人际关系

**1.** The collision happened on a Sunday. Lin's mother was in the hospital ward where his grandmother had worked as a nurse, and the project meeting was at the same hour. “What do you expect me to say?” he asked. “That the study is more faithful than a family?” His sister said nothing; his brother laughed. The photograph on the wall — a wedding in black and white, a honeymoon in the hills — was the only picture there.

冲突出现在一个周日。林伟的母亲在医院的病房里——他祖母曾在那里当护士——而项目会议正好在同一时间。“你想让我说什么？”他问。“说研究比家庭更忠实？”他妹妹什么也没说，他哥哥笑了。墙上那张照片——一场黑白婚礼、一次山间蜜月——是屋里唯一的画。

**2.** The elder of the family, his grandfather, had been an orphan; the orphanage had given him a name and very little else. “A parent is a person,” he said. “Not an objective.” Lin felt the old fear of being superficial — an acquaintance of everyone, attached to no one. The word isolation pierced him more than the argument did, and something in his soul went quiet.

家里的长辈、他的祖父，曾是个孤儿；孤儿院给了他一个名字，别的东西很少。“父母是人，”他说，“不是一个目标。”林伟感到那种古老的恐惧：成为一个肤浅的人——认识所有人，却谁也不亲近。“隔绝”这个词比争论更刺穿了他，而他灵魂里的某样东西安静了下来。

**3.** His mother's voice was quiet too. “You have a domestic heart,” she said, “and a research heart, and only one portion of attention.” She spoke about influence, connection, attachment, and the slow transference of love from one generation to the next. A relative would have agreed. A pet rat in a junior class, a quarrel between lovers in the dormitory, a marriage that had held for thirty years: all of it belonged to the same study of how people keep promises.

他母亲的声音也很轻。“你有一颗家庭的心，”她说，“也有一颗研究的心，而注意力只有一份。”她谈到影响力、联系、依恋，以及爱如何一代一代缓慢转移。任何一位亲戚都会说同样的话。少年班教室里的一只宠物鼠、宿舍里情人的争吵、维持了三十年的婚姻：所有这些都属于同一项关于人如何守信的研究。

**4.** They agreed on three roles and wrote them on the back of a photograph: Lin would come home every second Sunday; Maya would host the Thursday meeting alone; the group would embrace a smaller scope until the spring. “This is not a separation,” Maya said. “It is a renegotiation, and a confidential one if you need it to be.” Confucianism, she added, had a word for it, but the plain word was enough. The orientation of the whole project changed by a few degrees that afternoon.

他们定了三个角色，写在一张照片的背面：林伟每隔一个周日回家；曼雅独自主持周四的会议；小组把范围缩到春天为止。“这不是分开，”曼雅说，“这是一次重新商定；如果你需要，它也可以是保密的。”她补充说，儒家思想里有一个词专门说这件事，但用平常的话就够了。整个项目的方向在那个下午偏转了几度。

**5.** He kept that promise five weeks. The book he was reading, the class he was teaching, the example of good practice set by the department: none of it mattered on the Sunday his grandfather died. His mother said only that she was glad he had come. “A voice on the telephone is not a person,” she said. “You know that better than your father did.” He wrote it down in the language he used for research, and then he wrote it again in his own, because the first version was the method talking and the second was him. Of course the group understood it: the project could not arouse the interest of a grandparent by itself, and the adoption of a smaller scope was the only honest sort of promise. They taught themselves to turn toward the people in the room.

他坚持了五个星期。他正在读的书、他教的课、系里当作良好实践的例子：在他祖父去世的那个周日，这些都不重要了。他母亲只说，她很高兴他回来了。“电话里的声音不是一个人，”她说，“这一点你比你父亲明白。”他用做研究时的语言把它写了下来，然后又用自己的语言写了一遍——因为第一版是方法在说话，第二版才是他。当然，小组明白这一点：这个项目本身唤不起一位祖辈的兴趣，而缩小范围是唯一诚实的承诺方式。他们学着把注意力转向屋子里的那些人。

### Exercises

- CH030-Q1 [context] What caused the collision on that Sunday?
  - options: a hospital visit and a project meeting at the same hour / a wedding and a lecture / two examinations / a broken photograph
- CH030-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “隔绝；孤立”?
  - options: isolation / attention / transference / orientation
- CH030-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: keep a promise / keep a photograph / keep a ward / keep a soul
- CH030-Q4 [cloze] His mother said he had a domestic heart and only one ______ of attention.
  - options: portion / relative / elder / orphan
- CH030-Q5 [recall] What did they write on the back of the photograph?
  - options: three roles / a telephone number / a wedding date / a hospital address

### Answers

- CH030-Q1: a hospital visit and a project meeting at the same hour - Family duty and the project demanded the same Sunday.
- CH030-Q2: isolation - He feared being superficially connected to everyone and close to no one.
- CH030-Q3: keep a promise - Keep combines naturally with a promise or a word.
- CH030-Q4: portion - She described attention as something divided into portions.
- CH030-Q5: three roles - The renegotiation was written on the back of a photograph.

## CH031 - Promises and Pressure: A Missing Voice / 承诺与压力：缺席的声音

- theme: 友谊、家庭与人际关系

**1.** A neighbor in the dormitory, a careful woman who had never once complained, asked to speak to the group. She was a delegate from the night shift, and her story had no art in it: an alcoholic father, a mother who worked six days a week, and a brother who had left school at fifteen. “I do not hate them,” she said. “I just remember everything.” Six months ago the project had one voice; see how a single person can change the parent document.

宿舍里的一位邻居，一位从不抱怨的细心女人，请求和小组谈一谈。她是夜班的代表，她的故事里没有艺术：一个酗酒的父亲、一个每周工作六天的母亲，以及一个十五岁就离开学校的哥哥。“我不恨他们，”她说，“我只是记得一切。”六个月前这个项目只有一个声音；看看一个人如何改变那份母文件。

**2.** Her expression did not change when she described the accident, but her hands did. “There was no violence in our house,” she said, “only a fright every evening.” The group had expected a story about money; what emerged was a story about a clock, a lap, and a chair by the window. “So the survey missed the substance,” Maya said. However, the information was still thin, and not one word of it had been checked.

她描述那场事故时表情没有变化，但她的手变了。“我们家没有暴力，”她说，“只有每天晚上的一场惊吓。”小组原本期待一个关于钱的故事；浮现出来的却是关于一只钟、一个膝头，以及窗边一把椅子的故事。“所以调查漏掉了实质，”曼雅说。然而，这些信息仍然很薄，而且其中没有一个字被核对过。

**3.** The dominant voice in the room had always belonged to people with qualifications. “A qualification is a permission, not a person,” the woman said. “Do not be a coward about the scope.” A thoughtful word, a homely detail, a neat list of small comforts: the group wrote them down with the devotion of students who had learned to listen.

屋里占主导的声音一直属于那些有资格的人。“资格是一种许可，不是一个人，”那位女士说，“不要因为范围就做个懦夫。”一句体贴的话、一个朴实的细节、一份整洁的小小安慰清单：小组用学会了倾听的学生的虔诚把它们记下来。

**4.** The palace of theory was immune to such evidence, and that was the sting. “An intellectual project that cannot communicate grief is a bore,” Professor Zhao said. “Not a failure — a bore.” The sting of the word angered Maya, and she was careless about her own tone in reply; the black coat made her look older than she was. “Any of you would have done the same,” she said, and regretted it at once.

理论的宫殿对这类证据免疫，而这就是刺痛之处。“一个无法传达悲痛的智识项目是令人厌烦的，”赵教授说，“不是失败——是令人厌烦。”这个词的刺痛让曼雅愤怒，而她回话时的语气也很粗心。“你们任何人都会这么做，”她说，随即就后悔了。

**5.** They invited the woman to sit on the panel and allowed her to set the agenda. She stimulated one change immediately: the survey would ask what people tend in their own homes, not what they wear to the interview. “There is a probable reason nobody asked before,” Lin wrote. “The people who write surveys are the people who answer them.” His mother telephoned that evening, and he told her about the woman; she said only, “Good,” which was, from her, a long speech. A conscientious student would have apologized at once; the shame of the moment was that she had not. A charitable reading was possible, but it did not make the room any warmer, and the dominance of the loudest voice in it was itself a kind of violence that no contemporary account would ever record. She had seen enough suffering to know the difference.

他们邀请这位女士进入评审小组，并允许她来设定议程。她立刻促成了一项改变：调查要问人们在自家照料什么，而不是问他们穿着什么来面试。“没有人早先问过，有一个很可能的理由，”林伟写道，“写调查的人，就是回答调查的人。”那天晚上他母亲打来电话，他跟她讲了这位女士；她只说了一句“好”——而对她来说，那就是一段很长的讲话。一个尽责的学生本会立刻道歉；此刻的耻辱正在于她没有。做一种善意的解读是可能的，但这并不会让屋里更暖一些；而屋里最响的那个声音所占的支配地位，本身就是一种暴力，任何当代的记述都不会把它记下来。她见过的苦难足够多，因此分得清其中的差别。

### Exercises

- CH031-Q1 [context] What did the woman say about qualifications?
  - options: a qualification is a permission, not a person / a qualification is a family duty / a qualification is a survey / a qualification is a comfort
- CH031-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “体贴的；深思的”?
  - options: thoughtful / careless / skinny / neat
- CH031-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: communicate grief / communicate a pillow / communicate a violin / communicate a clock
- CH031-Q4 [cloze] Professor Zhao said an intellectual project that cannot communicate grief is a ______.
  - options: bore / delegate / sting / coward
- CH031-Q5 [recall] What change did the woman's panel seat produce?
  - options: the survey asked what people tend at home / the survey was cancelled / the night shift was closed / the dormitory was renamed

### Answers

- CH031-Q1: a qualification is a permission, not a person - She refused to let credentials stand in for experience.
- CH031-Q2: thoughtful - The group recorded her small, considerate details.
- CH031-Q3: communicate grief - Communicate combines with grief, a finding, or a feeling.
- CH031-Q4: bore - He judged dullness harsher than failure.
- CH031-Q5: the survey asked what people tend at home - Her intervention changed the survey's opening question.

## CH032 - Promises and Pressure: Evidence on the Table / 承诺与压力：摆上桌面的证据

- theme: 友谊、家庭与人际关系

**1.** The evidence meeting had a different shape since the survey had been rewritten. One measure replaced twelve: whether a person could find, in one minute, the thing they came for. “When the number is smaller than the argument, the argument wins,” Maya said. “Until we can stay small and honest, we should not grow.”

证据会议的样子和以往不同，因为调查已经被重写。一项指标取代了十二项：一个人能否在一分钟内找到他来的目的。“当数字比论证小的时候，赢的是论证，”曼雅说，“在我们能够既小又诚实之前，我们不该长大。”

**2.** They pinned a chart to the retreat wall and compared two households in the southwest: one with a car and a reliable piano teacher, one with a dependent grandmother and four bicycles. The rate of attendance was the same; the sadness was not. “Apparently that is called a clash of measures,” Lin wrote. “Really it is a clash of lives.”

他们把一张图表钉在休养所的墙上，比较西南部的两户人家：一户有汽车和一位可靠的钢琴老师，另一户有一位需要照料的祖母和四辆自行车。出席率是一样的，悲伤却不一样。“据说这叫指标的冲突，”林伟写道，“其实这是生活的冲突。”

**3.** A parental question on the committee asked whether the project could remain neutral, and the verbal answer was easy. “Communication is not a crowd,” the enthusiast with the violin said. “It is a dialog.” Her singular habit of speaking in the present tense had already changed the room, and the change involved the status of every volunteer.

委员会里一位父母辈的人问，这个项目能否保持中立，口头上的回答很容易。“交流不是人群，”那位带小提琴的热心者说，“它是一场对话。”她只用现在时说话的这个独特习惯已经改变了房间，而这个改变牵涉到每位志愿者的地位。

**4.** A skinny boy in worn clothing brought a pillow and a pencil to the gathering and asked to stay. “Hand me the list,” he said. “I will check one entry.” They allowed him to isolate three errors in an hour, and not one of them was small. “Your idea is a conjunction,” he said, “not a sentence.”

一个穿着破旧衣服的瘦男孩带着一只枕头和一支铅笔来到聚会，要求留下。“把名单递给我，”他说，“我来核对一条。”他们允许他在一小时内孤立出三个错误，而其中没有一个是小的。“你的想法是一个连词，”他说，“不是一个句子。”

**5.** A calculating visitor from the city office asked whether the project could be utilized by anyone else. “That is the whole reason it exists,” Maya said. “Keep the till open.” Her confident front concealed a real problem: an encounter with a nude statue in the art building had produced the best photograph of the year, and no data at all. “Put it on the wall anyway,” she said. They could see where the argument was going, and their very assumption that a number could settle it was the problem. Much of the analysis expected a clean answer, and none was there. The human relationship behind every number was the part no table could confirm; a herd of students in the corridor was not a relation, and a photograph was not a measurement.

市政办公室一位精于算计的来访者问，这个项目能否被别人利用。“这正是它存在的全部理由，”曼雅说，“把柜台开着。”她自信的外表掩盖了一个真实的难题：在艺术楼里与一座裸体雕像的一次邂逅，产出了全年最好的一张照片，却没有任何数据。“还是把它挂到墙上吧，”她说。他们看得出这场论证正走向哪里，而他们那个“一个数字就能解决它”的假设本身就是问题。大部分分析都期待一个干净的答案，而一个都没有。每个数字背后的人际关系是任何表格都无法确认的部分；走廊里的一群学生不是一种关系，而一张照片也不是一次测量。

### Exercises

- CH032-Q1 [context] What single measure replaced twelve?
  - options: whether a person can find what they came for in a minute / how many volunteers attend / the cost of the retreat / the number of bicycles
- CH032-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “可靠的；可信赖的”?
  - options: reliable / calculating / dependent / verbal
- CH032-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: confirm a finding / confirm a pillow / confirm a violin / confirm a herd
- CH032-Q4 [cloze] The enthusiast said that communication is a ______, not a crowd.
  - options: dialog / conjunction / retreat / clash
- CH032-Q5 [recall] What did Maya say to do with the photograph?
  - options: put it on the wall anyway / delete it / send it to the council / use it as a measurement

### Answers

- CH032-Q1: whether a person can find what they came for in a minute - The group replaced a dozen indicators with one usable test.
- CH032-Q2: reliable - One household had a dependable piano teacher.
- CH032-Q3: confirm a finding - Confirm combines with a finding, a date, or a suspicion.
- CH032-Q4: dialog - She described communication as an exchange between two people.
- CH032-Q5: put it on the wall anyway - She valued the photograph even though it proved nothing.

## CH033 - Promises and Pressure: The Trial Run / 承诺与压力：第一次试行

- theme: 友谊、家庭与人际关系

**1.** The trial run for the third arc had one subject: care. A home-care worker, a night nurse, and a housewife who had raised four children agreed to be interviewed by people who had never done any of those things. “You want to know what love costs,” the worker said. “Write it in a file, then come and do a shift.” No one had measured what care cost in the home.

第三段弧线的试行只有一个主题：照料。一位居家照护员、一位夜班护士，以及一位养育了四个孩子的家庭主妇，同意接受从未做过这些事的人的采访。“你想知道爱要付出什么代价，”那位照护员说，“把它写进档案，然后来做一班。”

**2.** A patient in the ward had a photograph of her husband in a black suit; an old copper coin, a bottle of medicine, and a white envelope sat on the cabinet beside her. “He was an agent for a textile company,” she said. “He had a lovely laugh and a lot of debts.” The strike at the plant that winter was the only thing in her file that anyone had bothered to record.

病房里一位病人有一张她丈夫穿黑色套装的合影；她旁边的柜子上放着一枚旧铜币、一瓶药和一封白色信封。“他是一家纺织公司的代理商，”她说，“他笑起来很可爱，欠了很多债。”那年冬天厂里的罢工，是她档案里唯一有人费心记录的事。

**3.** The interview drifted toward inheritance: what a mother passes to a daughter, what a father cannot pass at all. “A child is not stock,” she said. “And a marriage is not an alliance.” Her own intimacy with her husband had survived exile from the south, an empty counter, and a queue for everything.

访谈转向了传承：母亲传给女儿什么，父亲有什么完全传不下去。“孩子不是存货，”她说，“婚姻也不是结盟。”她与丈夫之间的亲密，挺过了从南方来的流亡、一只空柜台，以及为一切排队的日子。

**4.** Her companion in the next bed was a former teacher; his buddy came every Thursday with a lamb stew and a paper tag on the container. “A friend is not a limb,” she said. “You cannot cut one off and call the rest whole.” The terror of the empty chair was familiar to everyone in the ward, and the hostility of the young nurse toward tears was, in its own way, an affection. She held his arm like a professional.

她邻床的伙伴是一位退休教师；他的好友每周四带着一锅羔羊肉炖菜来，容器上贴着纸标签。“朋友不是肢体，”她说，“你不能砍掉一个，然后说剩下的还完整。”空椅子的恐怖对病房里的每个人都很熟悉，而那位年轻护士对眼泪的敌意，从它自己的方式来说，也是一种深情。

**5.** A woman from the city cabinet arrived to represent the council, wearing a suit and carrying a lens in a leather case. “Tell me what you found,” she said. “That is what we know: one pulse, one queen, one snake in the grass.” Her pulse was steady; her tail, as she left, was a cold goodbye. “Since you asked,” Maya said, “we found that people care whether the person writing the file can imagine their life.” A line of chairs, a single emotion on every face: this was the evidence nobody had asked for. One ally in the cabinet was often enough; when the new file reached the other desk, a person who had never met them would read it, and that was more than what the old system could claim. Whether the council would act was another matter.

市政内阁的一位女士前来代表议会，穿着套装，皮套里装着一枚镜片。“告诉我你们发现了什么，”她说。“这是我们知道的：一次脉搏、一位女王、草丛里的一条蛇。”她的脉搏很稳；她离开时的背影是一句冷淡的告别。“既然你问了，”曼雅说，“我们发现的是：人们在意写这份档案的人是否能想象他们的生活。”内阁里有一个盟友往往就够了；当新档案送到另一张桌上时，一个从未见过他们的人会读它——这已经比旧体系所能声称的更多。议会是否会行动，则是另一回事。

### Exercises

- CH033-Q1 [context] What did the home-care worker tell the group to do?
  - options: write the cost of love in a file and then work a shift / interview four children / buy a copper coin / read the patient's file
- CH033-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “遗产；遗传”?
  - options: inheritance / intimacy / hostility / affection
- CH033-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: represent the council / represent a lamb / represent a pulse / represent a lens
- CH033-Q4 [cloze] The patient said that a child is not ______.
  - options: stock / an agent / a suit / a cabinet
- CH033-Q5 [recall] What did the group say people care about?
  - options: whether the writer can imagine their life / the price of the file / the colour of the envelope / the length of the queue

### Answers

- CH033-Q1: write the cost of love in a file and then work a shift - She demanded that the interviewers experience the work.
- CH033-Q2: inheritance - The interview turned to what parents can and cannot pass on.
- CH033-Q3: represent the council - Represent combines with a body, a group, or an interest.
- CH033-Q4: stock - She refused to treat a child as property or inventory.
- CH033-Q5: whether the writer can imagine their life - The trial's finding was about the writer, not only the reader.

## CH034 - Promises and Pressure: Pressure Builds / 承诺与压力：压力上升

- theme: 友谊、家庭与人际关系

**1.** The cost of the renegotiated roles arrived as a letter. The three of them had promised to be at every Thursday meeting; by the third week, one of them was not there. “I cannot be in two places,” Lin said. “This is not a gesture. I am telling you what is true.” His grandmother was in hospital, and the trial of the new care plan fell in the same week.

重新商定的角色带来的代价，以一封信的形式到来。他们三人承诺出席每一次周四会议；到第三周，其中一人没有出现。“我不能同时在两个地方，”林伟说，“这不是姿态。我是在告诉你事实。”他祖母住院了，而新照料方案的试行正好落在同一周。

**2.** Maya took the minutes alone for three weeks. “The majority of the work is invisible,” she wrote. “A trustee of a small thing is a guardian of other people's time.” She was sure of one point only: a promise that depends on one person is not a promise, it is a hope. Her will was stronger than his schedule, and both of them knew it.

曼雅独自记录了三周的会议纪要。“大部分工作是看不见的，”她写道，“一件小事的受托人，是别人时间的监护人。”她只确定一点：一个依赖某一个人的承诺不是承诺，而是希望。她的意志比他的时间表更强，两人都明白这一点。

**3.** A man from the mill, an old friend of his grandfather, came to the house on the eve of the funeral and spoke about trust. “Your grandfather was a radical,” he said. “He burned the lease in the yard, and three families kept their homes because of it.” It was a model of a certain kind of courage, and it was also a warning.

一位来自磨坊的男人，他祖父的老朋友，在葬礼前夜来到家里，谈起信任。“你祖父是个激进分子，”他说，“他在院子里烧掉了租约，三户人家因此保住了住处。”这是某类勇气的典范，同时也是一记警告。

**4.** At the grave, an uncle and two aunts stood with the children. “Yes,” his auntie said, “he was a proper witness to a bad century.” A violent storm came up, and the Medicare papers in his bag got wet. “You cannot substitute a plan for a person,” his mother said. “That is the profound part, and it is the part nobody writes down.”

在墓旁，一位叔父和两位姑母与孩子们站在一起。“是的，”他的姨妈说，“他是一个糟糕世纪的合格见证人。”一场猛烈的风暴袭来，他包里的医保文件被打湿了。“你不能用一个计划替代一个人，”他母亲说，“那才是深刻的部分，也正是没人写下来的部分。”

**5.** He went back to the dormitory and wrote himself a note: do not lose the thread, and do not bribe yourself with busyness. A desperate week is not a failure; it is a critical week, and judgment is easy from the outside. His peer group — a friendly cyclist and a young woman from the third floor — offered to guard the Thursday slot. “The return will be slow,” Maya said. “Be sure that it happens. Ask yourself what you would tackle first if you had one clear shot at it.” All of those promises were still on the board when he left. “I hear you,” he said, “and here is what I can carry.” Him, of all people, she trusted. People came and went; the ones who stayed were the ones who said honestly what they could hold. He wanted to see whether the promise would hold. He watched the others go away down the corridor and understood that obedience to a timetable is not the same as loyalty.

他回到宿舍，给自己写了一张纸条：不要丢掉线索，也不要用忙碌来贿赂自己。绝望的一周不是失败，而是关键的一周；而从外面看，判断总是容易的。他的同伴们——一位友善的骑车人和三楼的一位年轻女士——主动提出替他守周四那一班。“回报会很慢，”曼雅说，“务必让它发生。问问你自己：如果只有一次干净的机会，你会先抓住哪件事？”他离开时，所有这些承诺都还写在白板上。“我听到了，”他说，“而这是我能承担的部分。”她信任的是他，偏偏是他。人来来去去；留下来的，是那些诚实说出自己能拿多少的人。他想看看这个承诺能不能守住。他望着其他人沿着走廊走远，明白了一件事：服从一张时间表，并不等于忠诚。

### Exercises

- CH034-Q1 [context] What made Lin miss the Thursday meeting?
  - options: his grandmother was in hospital / he lost the minutes / the mill closed / he was away on holiday
- CH034-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “受托人；理事”?
  - options: trustee / guardian / substitute / witness
- CH034-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: keep a promise / keep a grave / keep a lease / keep a bribe
- CH034-Q4 [cloze] His mother said you cannot ______ a plan for a person.
  - options: substitute / burn / insure / dominate
- CH034-Q5 [recall] What did Maya say a promise depending on one person really is?
  - options: a hope / a lease / a model / a judgment

### Answers

- CH034-Q1: his grandmother was in hospital - Family duty and the meeting fell in the same week.
- CH034-Q2: trustee - She described herself as a trustee of other people's time.
- CH034-Q3: keep a promise - Keep combines naturally with a promise or a word.
- CH034-Q4: substitute - She refused to let a plan stand in for a person.
- CH034-Q5: a hope - A promise resting on one person is only a hope.

## CH035 - Promises and Pressure: The Disagreement / 承诺与压力：分歧

- theme: 友谊、家庭与人际关系

**1.** The disagreement began with a slight. “You did not listen to me,” Maya said. “You hear me, but you do not listen.” Lin was certainly tired, and he said something foolish about supervision. Everybody in the room went quiet. “Hello?” she said. “Is anybody there?”

分歧始于一次轻慢。“你没有听我说话，”曼雅说，“你听见了，但你没有听。”林伟当然疲惫，他说了一句关于监督的蠢话。屋里所有人都安静下来。“喂？”她说，“有人在那儿吗？”

**2.** She had a distinctive way of arguing: firstly the fact, then the feeling, then the request. “I owe you an apology,” he said, “and you owe me one.” She admired his honesty but not his timing. “That is undoubtedly true,” she said, “and utterly useless.”

她争论的方式很有特色：先是事实，再是感受，最后是请求。“我欠你一个道歉，”他说，“你也欠我一个。”她钦佩他的诚实，但不欣赏他的时机。“那毫无疑问是真的，”她说，“而且完全没用。”

**3.** The real subject was the shape of the work. She wanted a mainstream project with a proper structure; he wanted a deliberate, slow one. “You cannot distinguish the two any more,” she said. “One of us has to be the beast.” A fisherman on the television talked about tides, and the sofa was covered in papers.

真正的问题在于工作的形态。她要一个结构合理的主流项目；他要一个深思熟虑、缓慢的项目。“你已经分不清这两者了，”她说，“我们中的一个必须当那头野兽。”电视里一位渔夫在讲潮水，沙发上铺满了纸。

**4.** They inspected the last three months like accountants. The parenting column, the letter from the preacher, a witty idiom, a tasty recipe: everything they had expended on pleasing other people. “This is a scar, not a strategy,” she said. “We are insuring a wreck. And the word for it is attributive: we keep describing, and never deciding.”

他们像会计一样检查了过去三个月。育儿专栏、传道士的来信、一句诙谐的习语、一份可口的食谱：他们把一切都花在了取悦别人上。“这是伤疤，不是策略，”她说，“我们在给一艘沉船投保。而形容这件事的词是‘定语性的’：我们一直在描述，从不做决定。”

**5.** He was exhausted and, he admitted, easily distracted. “A figurative promise is worthless,” she said. “A priceless one costs something.” She spoke about sightseeing in an underdeveloped region and the way a town will beautify its front street while the back lanes flood. “A lick of paint is not a plan,” she said. Again and again he came back to one thing. “You are sure,” he said. “I am. Since March, definitely. And when I say it, I mean it literally — not as an excellent metaphor, not as a superb line, but as the plain fact that the work is ours.” “Any of it?” “All of it.” “Then it is invaluable,” he said, “and I will not lose it.” Clearly, one of them would have to dominate the schedule, though neither wanted to. An old man, a fisherman with a wooden boat, told them on the beach that a tide does not remain; whether you stay or go away is your own business, and the sea will never be more than the sea.

他精疲力尽，而且他承认，很容易分心。“一个比喻式的承诺不值钱，”她说，“无价的承诺是要付出代价的。”她谈到在一个不发达地区的观光，以及一个城镇如何美化它的前街，而后巷却在淹水。“刷一层漆不是计划，”她说。他一次又一次回到同一件事上。“你确定吗？”他问。“我确定。从三月起，明确无疑。我说的时候是字面意思——不是一句出色的比喻，不是一句华丽的台词，而是一个朴素的事实：这份工作是我们的。”“全部吗？”“全部。”“那它就是无价的，”他说，“我不会丢掉它。”显然，他们中得有一个人来主导日程，虽然两人都不愿意。一位老人，一个划木船的渔夫，在海滩上告诉他们：潮水不会停留；你是留下还是离开是你自己的事，而海永远只是海。

### Exercises

- CH035-Q1 [context] What did Maya say Lin did instead of listening?
  - options: he heard her but did not listen / he read the minutes / he left the room / he called the preacher
- CH035-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “深思熟虑的；故意的”?
  - options: deliberate / distinctive / foolish / witty
- CH035-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: inspect the work / inspect the sofa / inspect the tide / inspect the recipe
- CH035-Q4 [cloze] She said that a ______ promise is worthless.
  - options: figurative / tasty / excellent / mainstream
- CH035-Q5 [recall] What did Lin finally say he would not do?
  - options: lose the work / listen again / insure the wreck / beautify the street

### Answers

- CH035-Q1: he heard her but did not listen - She distinguished hearing from listening.
- CH035-Q2: deliberate - He wanted a deliberate and slow project.
- CH035-Q3: inspect the work - Inspect combines with work, a site, or a document.
- CH035-Q4: figurative - A promise that exists only in metaphor costs nothing.
- CH035-Q5: lose the work - He promised not to let the shared project go.

## CH036 - Promises and Pressure: What the Numbers Hide / 承诺与压力：数字隐藏了什么

- theme: 友谊、家庭与人际关系

**1.** The reporter arrived with a photographer, a film crew, a documentary camera, and a question the group could not answer: why does the same failure look different in every home? A cyclist, a pianist, and a cartoonist gave three different accounts of one afternoon in the clinic, and an ancient habit of counting only the first one won again.

记者带着一名摄影师、一个摄制组，以及一个小组答不上来的问题到来：为什么同一次失败在每个家庭里看起来都不一样？一位骑车人、一位钢琴家和一位漫画家，对诊所里同一个下午给出了三种不同说法；而只统计第一个说法的那种古老习惯又一次赢了。

**2.** The numbers were a glass wall: transparent and hard. A user of the service, a client of the council, a debtor on the fifth floor: each had a different estimation of the same wait. “The constant is care,” Maya said. “Everything else is a bubble.”

数字是一堵玻璃墙：透明而坚硬。服务的使用者、议会的客户、五楼的债务人：每个人对同一次等待都有不同的估计。“恒量是照料，”曼雅说，“其他一切都是泡沫。”

**3.** A spokesman for the council, an interviewer from the radio, and an informant who asked not to be named described the playground as a success. “A brick, a diamond, and a jewel are all hard,” Lin said. “Only one of them is the gift of a ruler.” The emperor of that sentence was, he admitted, a fool, and the answer he gave the reporter was a stupid one.

议会的一位发言人、电台的一位采访者，以及一位要求匿名的线人，把那个运动场说成一次成功。“砖块、钻石和珠宝都很硬，”林伟说，“但其中只有一件是统治者的礼物。”他承认，写出这句话的皇帝是个傻瓜，而他给记者的回答是个愚蠢的回答。

**4.** A photographer wrapped a blanket around a child who had been waiting since morning, and took a photo of the queue. A woman with a bandaged arm waited with a second photo in her hand. “Certainly you have room for one more,” she said, and the camera turned toward her. “That is a laugh and a sigh in one gesture,” the assistant said. “Our transparency is childish,” the designer replied, “and the offender is the system, not the blanket.” A contestant in the queue agreed, and asked for a second blanket.

一位摄影师把一条毯子裹在一个从早上就开始等待的孩子身上，并拍下了队伍的照片。一位手臂缠着绷带的女士在等待，手里拿着第二张照片。“你当然还有位置再加一个，”她说，镜头转向了她。“那是一个手势里的笑声与叹息，”助理说。“我们的透明很幼稚，”设计师回答，“有罪的是这个系统，不是这条毯子。”队伍里一位参赛者表示同意，并要了第二条毯子。

**5.** The recipient of the service, a prisoner on day release, a buyer of the cheapest plan, and an adventurer who had walked the whole route: none of them had been asked for a single line. “A messenger is not a liar,” the spokesperson said, “but a messenger is not a witness either.” The group had its highest increase in trust that week, and its lowest in love. A patient would have said it better; a friend would have said it more kindly. The mean version was this: that the subject of the survey had never been the people in it.

服务的接受者、一位白天获释的囚犯、最便宜方案的购买者，以及一位走完全程的冒险者：他们当中没有一个人被问过一句话。“信使不是说谎者，”那位发言人说，“但信使也不是证人。”那一周小组在信任上增幅最高，在爱上却是最低。一位病人会说得更好，一位朋友会说得更温和。刻薄的版本是这样的：调查的主题从来不是其中的人。

### Exercises

- CH036-Q1 [context] What question could the group not answer?
  - options: why the same failure looks different in every home / how much the film crew cost / who owned the playground / when the clinic opened
- CH036-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “估计；判断”?
  - options: estimation / transparency / increase / bubble
- CH036-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: wrap a blanket around a child / wrap a brick around a child / wrap a jewel around a child / wrap a ruler around a child
- CH036-Q4 [cloze] Maya said the constant is ______.
  - options: care / glass / noise / the film
- CH036-Q5 [recall] Whom had the group never asked for a single line?
  - options: the people who used the service / the council spokesman / the radio interviewer / the photographer

### Answers

- CH036-Q1: why the same failure looks different in every home - The same problem has a different shape in each household.
- CH036-Q2: estimation - Each person estimated the same wait differently.
- CH036-Q3: wrap a blanket around a child - Wrap combines with a blanket, a parcel, or a wound.
- CH036-Q4: care - She called care the only constant in the data.
- CH036-Q5: the people who used the service - Users, debtors, and prisoners had never been interviewed.

## CH037 - Promises and Pressure: A Better Question / 承诺与压力：更好的问题

- theme: 友谊、家庭与人际关系

**1.** The better question came from a nurse in the maternity house: whose family is this, biologically speaking? A baby had two mothers on the form — one who carried him and one who would raise him — and the old system had only one line for both. A great part of the control belonged to whoever wrote the first column.

更好的问题来自产房的一位护士：从生物学上说，这是谁的家庭？表格上一个婴儿有两位母亲——一位生了他，一位要养育他——而旧系统只为两者留了一行。很大一部分控制权属于写下第一栏的人。

**2.** The head of the department, a modern man with a quiet voice, asked what a fingerprint was for. “Identification,” the deputy said. “No,” the nurse said. “It is a pure fact that has nothing to do with kin.” There was a little contempt in the room, and a great deal of envy.

系主任是一位声音安静的现代人，他问指纹有什么用。“身份识别，”副主任说。“不，”护士说，“它是一个与血缘毫无关系的纯粹事实。”屋里有一点轻蔑，也有很多嫉妒。

**3.** A priest, a spouse, a parent, and a student stood around the table. “The whole function of a form is to decide what counts,” the priest said. “A palm, a wound, a bite on the arm: three ways of saying the same thing.” Half the name, a figure, an active feature of the throat: the primitive grammar of the form decided everything before the nurse spoke. The president of the union wrote it down.

一位神父、一位配偶、一位家长和一名学生围在桌旁。“表格的整个功能就是决定什么算数，”神父说，“一只手掌、一处创伤、手臂上的一口咬痕：三种说法，同一件事。”半个名字、一个数字、喉咙的一项活跃特征：表格那原始的语法在护士开口之前就决定了一切。工会主席把它记了下来。

**4.** A sympathetic social worker showed them a secret file with a facial photograph of a baby, a blood test, and a feeding schedule. “Is that biology or care?” she asked. “Both,” Maya said. “And the form makes you choose.” A black cat crossed the litter in the yard, indifferent to all of it.

一位富有同情心的社工给他们看了一份秘密档案：一张婴儿的面部照片、一次血液检验和一份喂养时间表。“那是生物学还是照料？”她问。“都是，”曼雅说，“而表格逼你二选一。”一只黑猫穿过院子里的杂物，对这一切漠不关心。

**5.** The envelope on the table contained two forms: one biological, one social. “Which one does the breast belong to,” the nurse asked, “when the baby will not feed?” Nobody answered. The baby was weighed again, the figures were written down, and the second mother came back from the corridor with a bottle and a blanket, then carried the empty tray away.

桌上的信封里装着两份表格：一份生物学的，一份社会性的。“当婴儿不肯吃奶的时候，乳房属于哪一份？”护士问。没有人回答。婴儿被重新称重，数字被记下，第二位母亲从走廊回来，手里拿着奶瓶和一条毯子，随后把空托盘端走了。
