**6.** The expansive claim — that a commune could replace a family — was met with silence. “A relay is not a race,” Lin wrote, “and a harness is not a horse.” The oceanic feeling of the room was mostly fatigue. “Something has to change in the double column,” the nurse said. “The skin of the form, not the skin of the child.” Of course the father of the second mother was sure that the form would lose the case; but a person who has never filled in such a form has no idea what it costs.

那个扩张性的主张——社区可以取代家庭——遇到了沉默。“接力不是赛跑，”林伟写道，“马具也不是马。”屋里那种海洋般的感觉多半是疲惫。“双栏里必须改点什么，”护士说，“改表格的皮，不是孩子的皮。”当然，第二位的母亲的父亲认定这场官司会输；但一个从未填过这种表格的人，根本不知道它要付出什么。

### Exercises

- CH037-Q1 [context] What problem did the maternity form have?
  - options: it had one line for two mothers / it had no fingerprint / it was written by the priest / it asked about the cat
- CH037-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “漠不关心的”?
  - options: indifferent / sympathetic / expansive / primitive
- CH037-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: decide what counts / decide the litter / decide the harness / decide the envy
- CH037-Q4 [cloze] The nurse said a fingerprint is a ______ fact that has nothing to do with kin.
  - options: pure / secret / double / facial
- CH037-Q5 [recall] What did Lin say a harness is not?
  - options: a horse / a relay / a race / a column

### Answers

- CH037-Q1: it had one line for two mothers - The old form could not hold two kinds of motherhood.
- CH037-Q2: indifferent - The cat was untouched by the argument.
- CH037-Q3: decide what counts - Decide combines with what counts, a course, or a date.
- CH037-Q4: pure - She called the fingerprint a pure fact, unrelated to kinship.
- CH037-Q5: a horse - He distinguished the instrument from the living thing it serves.

## CH038 - Promises and Pressure: The Second Attempt / 承诺与压力：第二次尝试

- theme: 友谊、家庭与人际关系

**1.** The second attempt began with a funeral and a birthday in the same week. The cousin of his former wife, a tall actor and a convinced optimist with an unlikely surname, came to the house, and the family had to decide who would sit where. “You are a saint,” his aunt said. He was not. He was a pessimist with an appointment with the dentist, and he said so. Her son, a boy of nine, was there with his own question about the ceremony: he wanted to know whether a family could be chosen rather than given.

第二次尝试以同一周里的一场葬礼和一个生日开始。他前妻的表亲，一位高个子的男演员、一个坚定的乐观主义者，带着一个不太可能的姓氏来到家里，而全家人必须决定谁坐在哪里。“你是个圣人，”他姑妈说。他不是。他是一个带着牙医预约的悲观主义者，而且他当面说了出来。她的儿子，一个九岁的男孩，也在场，带着他自己关于仪式的疑问：他想知道，一个家庭是可以挑选的，还是只能被给予的。

**2.** They were wed in a small ceremony in the nursery garden, and the bride carried a bunch of flowers instead of a doll. The bridegroom, a salesman from the next town, had a regular job and an infinite patience for children. “Whoever adopts a surname in this family,” the grandmother said, “adopts a history.”

他们在一座小苗圃花园里举行了小规模婚礼，新娘手里捧着一束花而不是一个洋娃娃。新郎是邻镇的推销员，有一份稳定的工作和对孩子无限的耐心。“在这个家里，谁采用了一个姓氏，”祖母说，“就是采用了一段历史。”

**3.** The husband of his cousin was a young giant of a man who worked with cows and could lift a calf. “She is my grandchild,” the grandmother said, “and so are the other grandchildren.” A nasty pill, a small creature, a youngster with a blue bicycle, an ethnic dish on the table, and a bitch with three puppies in the yard: the house was full of them throughout the afternoon. A little of something was always missing, and the head of the table never let it show.

他表姐的丈夫是个年轻的巨人，与牛打交道，能举起一头小牛。“她是我的孙辈，”祖母说，“其他的孙辈也一样。”一颗难吃的药丸、一只小小的生物、一个推着蓝色自行车的少年、桌上的一道民族菜肴，以及院子里一只带着三只幼崽的母狗：整个下午屋子里都是他们。总有一点什么东西是缺的，而坐在上首的人从不让它显露出来。

**4.** At the initial meeting of the family council, they were asked to say what they expected. “We cannot control who marries whom,” Maya said. “But we can see that six people are not asked to carry one person's part.” The latter remark was, however, the one that stuck, and the figure on the whiteboard was not the real figure at all.

在家庭委员会的首次会议上，他们被要求说出自己的期待。“我们无法控制谁和谁结婚，”曼雅说，“但我们能看到，不能让六个人去承担一个人的那份。”然而，真正留下的是后半句；白板上的那个数字根本不是真正的数字。

**5.** So the second attempt was smaller and more regular. I myself wrote the schedule; we allowed ourselves one evening a week; a homosexual couple in the building lent us their table and their kettle. “Yeah,” Maya said, “so that is the great reform: a table, an honest name, and nobody pretending that a family is a fixed thing.”

于是第二次尝试变得更小、更有规律。我自己写了时间表；我们允许自己每周有一个晚上；楼里一对同性恋伴侣把他们的桌子和水壶借给了我们。“是的，”曼雅说，“所以这就是伟大的改革：一张桌子、一个诚实的名字，以及没有人再假装家庭是一件固定不变的东西。”

### Exercises

- CH038-Q1 [context] What two events fell in the same week?
  - options: a funeral and a birthday / two weddings / a dentist visit and a sale / a birthday and a funeral for a cow
- CH038-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “后者的”?
  - options: latter / former / initial / regular
- CH038-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: adopt a surname / adopt a bicycle / adopt a kettle / adopt a puppy
- CH038-Q4 [cloze] The grandmother said that whoever adopts a surname adopts a ______.
  - options: history / nursery / calf / kettle
- CH038-Q5 [recall] What did Maya call the great reform?
  - options: a table, an honest name, and no pretence / a larger office / a new surname / a formal contract

### Answers

- CH038-Q1: a funeral and a birthday - Grief and celebration arrived in the same week.
- CH038-Q2: latter - The second remark, the latter one, was the one that stayed.
- CH038-Q3: adopt a surname - Adopt combines with a name, a habit, or a child.
- CH038-Q4: history - She treated the family name as an inheritance.
- CH038-Q5: a table, an honest name, and no pretence - The second attempt was smaller and more honest.

## CH039 - Promises and Pressure: Outside Pressure / 承诺与压力：外部压力

- theme: 友谊、家庭与人际关系

**1.** The external pressure came as a detailed report from the city. One member of the committee, a very attentive woman, wrote that the project had been neglectful of its own staff. “You cannot continue like this,” she said. “The number of unpaid hours is a scandal, not a virtue.”

外部压力以一份来自市里的详尽报告到来。委员会里一位非常留心的女士写道，这个项目一直忽视自己的工作人员。“你们不能这样继续下去，”她说，“无偿工时的数字是一桩丑闻，不是美德。”

**2.** The accusation was partly fair. Behind the small office, an elderly diner kept a ledger of every hour they had worked, and the total was a challenge to their own honesty. “We are in a siege of our own making,” Maya said. “Since the report has been filed, all we can do is answer it.”

这项指控有一部分是公正的。在小办公室后面，一位年长的用餐者保存着一本记录他们每一小时工作的账本，总数是对他们自己诚实的挑战。“我们处在一场自己造成的围困中，”曼雅说，“既然报告已经提交，我们能做的就是回应它。”

**3.** A champion of the project, a communicative woman with a possessive streak, proposed a fight: raise the fee, commit the council, and let the quarrel become public. “That is a provocative move,” Lin said. “It is also a slippery one. Haste would dampen the very thing we are trying to prove.”

项目的一位拥护者，一位爱交际、带点占有欲的女士，提议打一仗：提高费用、让议会承诺、让争吵公开化。“那是一步挑衅性的棋，”林伟说，“也是一步很滑的棋。匆忙会浇灭我们正想证明的东西。”

**4.** The moderate path was harder than the visionary one. It meant a cubic metre of space instead of an office, a part-time electrician instead of a team, an electric kettle instead of a kitchen, and a moderate fee for the volunteers. “We must not neglect the needy member of this group,” she said, “and we must not rub salt into the wound either.”

温和的道路比幻想式的道路更难走。它意味着一立方米的空间而不是一间办公室，一位兼职电工而不是一个团队，以及给志愿者一份适度的报酬。“我们既不能忽视这个群体里贫困的成员，”她说，“也不能往伤口上撒盐。”

**5.** The sitting ended with a compromise and a maiden speech from the youngest volunteer. “Diligence is not the same as obedience,” she said, “and a feverish week is not a plan.” The ball was in the council's court; she really did threaten to resign, and the affectionate letter that followed did not change the dimensional cast of the problem: a contest between a prison of small habits and a situated, human schedule. The squeeze was on the budget, not on her patience, and the question was whether the electric bill counted as a part of the project's true dimension. She wrote that sentence twice, and then a third time, because a dimension she could not name was exactly the thing that had defeated them in March. They had committed themselves to a smaller scope and a clearer scenery of responsibility. “Than the last one,” Lin added, “which is to say: better, when you can tell the difference.”

会议以一项妥协和那位最年轻志愿者的首次发言结束。“勤奋不等同于服从，”她说，“而发烧般的一周不是计划。”球在议会那一边；她确实威胁过辞职，而随后那封深情的信并没有改变问题的维度结构：这是一场小习惯的监狱与一个被妥善安放的、有人情味的时间表之间的竞赛。被压缩的是预算，不是她的耐心；而问题在于，电费算不算这个项目真正维度的一部分。她把这个句子写了两遍，然后又写了第三遍，因为一个她叫不出名字的维度，正是三月里击败他们的那样东西。他们承诺了更小的范围与更清晰的职责图景。“比上一份好，”林伟补充说，“也就是说：更好——前提是你能分辨差别。”

### Exercises

- CH039-Q1 [context] What did the city report accuse the project of?
  - options: neglecting its own staff / wasting printer paper / moving the office / hiring an electrician
- CH039-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “勤奋”?
  - options: diligence / haste / siege / scenery
- CH039-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: raise the fee / raise the ledger / raise the siege / raise the scenery
- CH039-Q4 [cloze] Lin warned that ______ would dampen what they were trying to prove.
  - options: haste / diligence / moderation / affection
- CH039-Q5 [recall] What did the youngest volunteer say diligence is not?
  - options: the same as obedience / a plan / a fee / a virtue

### Answers

- CH039-Q1: neglecting its own staff - The report counted the unpaid hours the volunteers had worked.
- CH039-Q2: diligence - She separated diligence from obedience.
- CH039-Q3: raise the fee - Raise combines with a fee, a price, or a question.
- CH039-Q4: haste - He argued that rushing would undermine the evidence.
- CH039-Q5: the same as obedience - She insisted that hard work is not the same as compliance.

## CH040 - Promises and Pressure: The Decision / 承诺与压力：决定

- theme: 友谊、家庭与人际关系

**1.** The decision was taken around a warm table in the family house. His mom invited both families, and the debate that followed was, in her words, a lot of fun and very tense. “He will tell you what happened,” she said. “I will say only this: the marital contract is not the marriage.”

决定是在家里一张温暖的桌边做出的。他妈妈请来了两家人，随后的辩论用她的话说既有很多乐趣又非常紧张。“他会告诉你们发生了什么，”她说，“我只说一点：婚姻的契约不是婚姻。”

**2.** The beginning of the meeting was formal. A motion was proposed, a request for a triple enhancement of the schedule, and a nod from the head of the table. “Apply for the money,” the fatherly uncle said. “Beg if you must, but do not borrow.” “Do not panic,” the motherly aunt added. “The concern of the woman who raised the child has been received, and at home it is great.”

会议的开头很正式。有人提出一项动议、一份把时间表增加三倍的请求，以及上首那一位的点头。“去申请这笔钱，”像父亲一样的叔父说，“必要时去求，但不要借。”像母亲一样的姑妈补充：“不要惊慌。抚养那个孩子的女人的担忧已经收到，而在家里，这份担忧很大。”

**3.** There was a demonstrative moment: the beloved grandmother borrowed a pen, signed the paper, and put it down with a slap that disposed of three months of spite in one second. “The scent of a new plan is better than the curse of an old quarrel,” she said, and the talkative cousin, for once, said nothing at all.

有一个极具表现力的瞬间：心爱的祖母借了一支笔，在文件上签了名，然后一拍放下，一秒钟里处置掉了三个月的怨恨。“新计划的香气胜过旧争吵的诅咒，”她说；而那位爱说话的堂兄，这一次什么也没说。

**4.** The divorce of the two projects had begun quietly. One would continue; the other would be transmitted to a replacement who could give it more time. “I know,” Lin said. “I like the little one better.” A steep fall in his own hours had taught him that, and the plunging temperature outside made the kitchen feel like a harbor.

两个项目的分离早已悄悄开始。一个将继续；另一个会被移交一位能给它更多时间的接替者。“我知道，”林伟说，“我更喜欢小的那个。”他自己工时的陡降教会了他这一点，而外面骤降的气温让厨房感觉像一座港湾。

**5.** The wonder was that the decision had been reached without anyone raising a voice. The steamy windows, the warm plates, the little indications that people were still there: everything indicated a family that had decided something. “Something has to commence,” the cousin finally said. “Call it a fancy name if you like; the people are the same people.” Some of it was luck, and some of it was the part nobody had planned: the cousin's silence, the aunt's arithmetic, the way a family decides without deciding. They had spent a month on the schedule and one evening on the marriage, which was, in the end, the correct proportion of the two.

令人惊奇的是，决定是在没有人提高嗓门的情况下作出的。起雾的窗户、温热的盘子、人们还在那里的细小迹象：一切都表明这是一个已经做出了决定的家庭。“总要开始点什么，”堂兄最后说，“如果你愿意，给它起个花哨的名字；人还是那些人。”其中一部分是运气，另一部分是没有人计划过的部分：堂兄的沉默、姑妈的算计，以及一个家庭在不作决定的情况下作决定的方式。他们花了一个月安排日程，用一个晚上处理婚姻——说到底，这才是两者正确的比例。

### Exercises

- CH040-Q1 [context] Who invited both families?
  - options: his mom / the cousin / the uncle / Maya
- CH040-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “恶意的怨恨”?
  - options: spite / scent / motion / panic
- CH040-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: apply for the money / apply for the kitchen / apply for the harbour / apply for the temperature
- CH040-Q4 [cloze] The grandmother said the marital contract is not the ______.
  - options: marriage / motion / schedule / replacement
- CH040-Q5 [recall] Which project did Lin say he preferred?
  - options: the little one / the larger one / neither / the transmitted one

### Answers

- CH040-Q1: his mom - She gathered both families around one table.
- CH040-Q2: spite - Three months of resentment were set aside with one gesture.
- CH040-Q3: apply for the money - Apply combines with money, a post, or a rule.
- CH040-Q4: marriage - She distinguished the contract from the relationship.
- CH040-Q5: the little one - He chose the smaller project he could actually sustain.

## CH041 - Promises and Pressure: Consequences / 承诺与压力：后果

- theme: 友谊、家庭与人际关系

**1.** The consequences were emotional and, at the core, simple. The last question in the survey asked what a family is for, and the answers exposed a vacuum where a definition should have been. A prince, a princess, and a philosopher gave the same reply: for holding what cannot be held anywhere else.

后果是情感性的，而核心很简单。调查的最后一个问题问的是：家庭是为了什么？答案暴露出一个本应放着定义的空洞。一位王子、一位公主和一位哲学家给出了同样的回答：为了承载别处无法承载的东西。

**2.** The appearance of a stranger at the door changed the morning. A porter, a waiter, and a fireman came to the residence to repair the rail; a hunter in a blue coat brought a rabbit for the infant next door. “Anybody could have done it,” the tailor said. “Nobody did.”

门口一个陌生人的出现改变了这个早晨。一位门房、一位侍者和一名消防员来到住处修理栏杆；一位穿蓝色外套的猎人给隔壁的婴儿带来了一只兔子。“任何人都能做到，”裁缝说，“可没有人做。”

**3.** The intention of the donor was good, and the delivery was terrible. They received a melon, a vacuum cleaner, and a bench with a mysterious inscription from an ancestor of the family of the butcher. A second parcel arrived for him the same afternoon, addressed to the wrong man and opened by everyone. “They will dismiss it as charming,” Maya said. “That is the exception, not the rule.”

捐赠者的意图是好的，交付却很糟糕。他们收到了一只甜瓜、一台吸尘器，以及一条长椅，上面刻着屠夫家一位祖先留下的神秘铭文。同一天下午，又有一个包裹寄给他，收件人写错了，被所有人拆开看了。“他们会把它当作可爱的东西打发掉，”曼雅说，“那是例外，不是常规。”

**4.** He was away when the kiss happened, and he had to hear of it from her. “You will say I am like the man in the old story,” she said, “and I am not sure that I am not.” The chilly answer was that the germ of the quarrel had been there since the first week, and he had to be sure of that before he could say it aloud.

那个吻发生的时候他不在场，只能听她说。“你会说我就是老故事里的那个男人，”她说，“而我也不确定我不是。”冷淡的回答是：这场争吵的细菌从第一周起就已经在那里了；而在他能把这句说出口之前，他必须先对这一点确信无疑。

**5.** Dad was living in a small flat around the corner, and it had taken real talent to be so ordinary. The imaginative descendant of a blond Homo sapiens and a diligent tailor wrote a parade of small substitutions: a bench for a bed, a rabbit for a rabbit, a question for a question. The hatred between the two families had not disappeared; it had simply gone back to being what it had been at the beginning — a habit they could lose. The tailor had mended the same coat three times, and the butcher had sold him the same cut of meat for twenty years; neither of them had ever said the word hatred aloud, and that silence, too, was a kind of inheritance.

爸爸住在街角一间小公寓里，而要把日子过得如此普通，其实需要真正的才能。一个金发的智人与一位勤勉裁缝的富有想象力的后裔，写下了一连串小小的替代：用长椅替代床、用兔子替代兔子、用问题替代问题。两个家庭之间的仇恨并没有消失；它只是又变回了它一开始的样子——一种他们可以丢掉的习惯。那位裁缝把同一件外套补过三次，那位屠夫二十年来卖给他同一块肉；他们两人谁也没有把“仇恨”这个词说出口，而那份沉默，同样是一种遗产。

### Exercises

- CH041-Q1 [context] What did the last survey question ask?
  - options: what a family is for / who repaired the rail / how much the melon cost / where the prince lived
- CH041-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “后裔；子孙”?
  - options: descendant / ancestor / donor / porter
- CH041-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: repair the rail / repair the rabbit / repair the melon / repair the vacuum
- CH041-Q4 [cloze] The donor's intention was good, but the ______ was terrible.
  - options: delivery / appearance / hatred / parade
- CH041-Q5 [recall] What had the hatred become at the end?
  - options: a habit they could lose / a legal case / a family tradition / a mystery

### Answers

- CH041-Q1: what a family is for - The final question asked about the purpose of a family.
- CH041-Q2: descendant - The descendant of a tailor and an ancestor wrote the substitutions.
- CH041-Q3: repair the rail - Repair combines with a rail, a roof, or a relationship.
- CH041-Q4: delivery - The gift was well meant but badly delivered.
- CH041-Q5: a habit they could lose - The inherited hatred persisted only as habit.

## CH042 - Promises and Pressure: The Bridge Forward / 承诺与压力：通向下一程

- theme: 友谊、家庭与人际关系

**1.** The third arc closed with a lunch that was supposed to be a victory. The transferred project had continued for six weeks without Lin; the participant numbers were abundant, and every volunteer had stayed. “Nobody expected that,” the spokeswoman said. Her joy was real, and so was her insistence that the report mention the last setback. Suddenly there was an incentive to do what nobody had asked: part of the money was for the ongoing work, and any of the million small tasks could now be paid. Each of them had an eye on the door, and no one sat still.

第三段弧线以一顿本该算是胜利的午餐收尾。移交出去的项目在没有林伟的情况下继续了六周；参与者数量充足，每位志愿者都留了下来。“没人料到会这样，”女发言人说。她的喜悦是真实的，她坚持要在报告里提到最近那次挫折也是真实的。突然间出现了一种动机，去做没人要求过的事：一部分钱是给这项持续工作的，那一百万件小事中的任何一件现在都能拿到报酬。每个人眼睛都盯着门口，没有人安稳坐着。

**2.** He was not triumphant. Sitting in his overcoat, he watched the rain and felt an ache in his thumb from carrying boxes. “We invested a year,” he said. “Some of it was a triumph. Most of it was not.” A few of the guests agreed; several did not.

他并不得意。穿着外套坐着，他看着雨，感到拇指因搬箱子而隐隐作痛。“我们投入了一年，”他说，“其中一部分是凯旋，大部分不是。”有几位客人同意，有几位不同意。

**3.** A jealous colleague, a drunk broker, and a defiant young man from the hut settlement all spoke at the luncheon. “Nowadays everybody wants a conqueror,” the broker said. “You want a blackboard.” The relevance of the remark was not obvious to anyone, least of all to him.

一位心怀嫉妒的同事、一个醉酒的经纪人，以及一个来自小屋聚落的挑衅青年，都在午宴上发了言。“如今人人都想要征服者，”经纪人说，“而你们想要的是一块黑板。”这句话的相关性对谁都不明显，对他尤其如此。

**4.** The frustrating part was the handwriting on the report: his own. Oftentimes the least influential person in the room writes the document that sways the room, and he had written it without noticing which side he was on. “Do not continue out of laziness,” Maya said. “Continue out of impulse, if you must, but keep the shelter open, and do not raise a fist at the people who read it.”

令人沮丧的是报告上的笔迹：他自己的。常常是屋里最没有影响力的人写下那份左右全屋的文件，而他写下它时并没有留意自己站在哪一边。“不要因为懒惰才继续下去，”曼雅说，“如果非要继续，那就凭着冲动继续，但要让庇护所开着，也不要对读它的人举起拳头。”

**5.** Several participants disapproved of the final paragraph. A hat and a glove were, quite simply, not a policy, and the insistent demand for a cleaner number could not be met without deleting somebody's name. Many of them would persevere; some would not. “The interviewee is not the interviewee of the question,” the spokeswoman said. “That is why we keep asking.” The question, at least, had survived the defeat, and there was no shame in that. “Shit,” the broker said, when the final total appeared on the screen; he was immensely relieved, and he said so, twice. The promise had survived the pressure, and the price of it had a name in every room they entered.

好几位参与者不赞成最后一段。一顶帽子和一只手套，说白了，不是政策；而要满足那坚持要一个更干净数字的要求，就得删掉某个人的名字。他们中很多人会坚持下去，有些人不会。“被采访者不是问题的被采访者，”女发言人说，“所以我们才要一直问。”至少那个问题在失败中活了下来，而这并不丢人。“见鬼，”当最终数字出现在屏幕上时，经纪人说；他如释重负，而且他说了两遍。那个承诺在压力中活了下来，而它的代价，在他们走进的每一个房间里都有一个名字。

### Exercises

- CH042-Q1 [context] What did the spokeswoman insist the report mention?
  - options: the last setback / the lunch menu / the broker's joke / Lin's handwriting
- CH042-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “坚持；坚决主张”?
  - options: insistence / relevance / impulse / laziness
- CH042-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: keep the shelter open / keep the fist open / keep the glove open / keep the hut open
- CH042-Q4 [cloze] Oftentimes the least influential person writes the document that ______ the room.
  - options: sways / deletes / triumphs / descends
- CH042-Q5 [recall] What did Lin say most of the year had been?
  - options: not a triumph / a victory / a defeat / a holiday

### Answers

- CH042-Q1: the last setback - She wanted the failure recorded beside the success.
- CH042-Q2: insistence - Her insistence was that the report stay honest.
- CH042-Q3: keep the shelter open - Keep combines with a shelter, a promise, or a record.
- CH042-Q4: sways - The quiet writer often moves the room.
- CH042-Q5: not a triumph - He refused to call the year a victory.

## CH043 - The First Real Deadline: A Signal / 第一个真实期限：一个信号

- theme: 求职、职场与职业发展

**1.** At home they had learned to write down who did what; the office preferred a deadline to a promise. The fourth arc began downtown, in a spacious office with a gigantic carpet and a view of the orbital tower. A prominent businessman said “hi” to them without looking up, and the management team arrived ten minutes later. “We want a film about our new technological platform,” the woman said. “You will supervise the script, and you will receive a small fee.” Various people around the table produced a phone and took notes, like a boy at his first interview.

在家里，他们学会了把谁做什么写下来；而办公室更喜欢一个截止日期，而不是一个承诺。第四段弧线从市中心开始，在一间宽敞的办公室，铺着巨大的地毯，窗户正对轨道塔。一位有名的商人头也不抬地对他们说了声“嗨”，管理团队十分钟后才到。“我们想拍一部关于我们新科技平台的影片，”那位女士说，“你们来监督脚本，会收到一笔小小的报酬。”桌边各种各样的人掏出手机做记录，像第一次参加面试的男孩。

**2.** The agenda was mostly financial. A secretary took the minutes; a broker consulted his phone; a reactionary older man wanted to commercialize everything before the test. “Progress is not a pace,” Lin said. “It is a direction.” “Then straighten your stride,” the businessman replied, “and delete the last paragraph.” From that morning the work would be supervised by the client, not by the university.

议程主要是财务性的。一位秘书做记录；一位经纪人查手机；一位年长而反动的先生想在测试之前就把一切商业化。“进步不是步速，”林伟说，“而是一个方向。”“那就把你的步子走直，”商人回答，“把最后一段删掉。”从那天早上起，工作由客户而不是大学来监督。

**3.** Their departure from the campus was compulsory for the summer: an internship, they were told, would teach them more than a lecture. A young daughter of the union organizer worked there too, and a boy from the mailroom applied for the same post. “You will discover,” said the woman, “that most of what we make is invisible. That is what makes it hard to manage.”

他们夏天离开校园是强制性的：有人告诉他们，一段实习能教给他们比一堂课更多的东西。工会组织者年轻的女儿也在那里工作，收发室的一个男孩申请了同一个岗位。“你们会发现，”那位女士说，“我们做的大部分事是看不见的。这正是它难以管理的原因。”

**4.** The work itself was to administrate a small civic tool: a tool that would industrialize nothing, defy the market, and never be profitable. “It will fail,” the broker said. “It does not need to be profitable,” Maya replied. “It needs to be accurate.” Without that, the whole platform was a film about a building.

工作本身是管理一件小小的市政工具：一件不会让任何东西工业化、敢于违抗市场、也永远不会赚钱的工具。“它会失败，”经纪人说。“它不需要赚钱，”曼雅回答，“它需要准确。”没有这一点，整个平台就只是一部关于一栋建筑的影片。

**5.** Wealth descended and rose around them, and the young persisted. “We do not receive praise here,” she said. “We receive deadlines.” A farewell lunch was held for the last intern, and an email went upwards and downwards through the company before he had finished his coffee. A big question imposed itself: could a pupil from a public school succeed here, where a secretarial salary was a dime above the housework rate? Nobody could say what would happen next, and nobody wanted to lead with that. His face, like hers, said that the work would continue without them, and that this was the price of a real deadline.

财富在他们周围下降又上升，而年轻人坚持了下来。“我们在这里得不到表扬，”她说，“我们得到的是截止日期。”公司为最后一位实习生办了告别午餐，一封邮件在他喝完咖啡之前就上传下达了整个公司。一个大问题压了上来：一个公立学校出来的学生，能在这里成功吗？在这里，一份秘书的薪水只比家务工的时薪高一分钱。没有人说得清接下来会发生什么，也没有人愿意拿这件事开头。他的脸和她的脸都在说：工作会在没有他们的情况下继续下去，而这就是一个真实期限的代价。

### Exercises

- CH043-Q1 [context] What did the client want filmed?
  - options: their new technological platform / the orbital tower / the mailroom / the career fair
- CH043-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “监督；管理”?
  - options: supervise / commercialize / straighten / descend
- CH043-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: manage a budget / manage a carpet / manage a dime / manage a farewell
- CH043-Q4 [cloze] Maya said the tool did not need to be ______, only accurate.
  - options: profitable / spacious / secretarial / prominent
- CH043-Q5 [recall] What did the woman say most of their work would be?
  - options: invisible / profitable / industrial / secretarial

### Answers

- CH043-Q1: their new technological platform - The client commissioned a film about its platform.
- CH043-Q2: supervise - The client would supervise the script rather than the university.
- CH043-Q3: manage a budget - Manage combines with a budget, a team, or a risk.
- CH043-Q4: profitable - She valued accuracy above profit.
- CH043-Q5: invisible - Most of the work was invisible to the customer.

## CH044 - The First Real Deadline: The First Promise / 第一个真实期限：第一次承诺

- theme: 求职、职场与职业发展

**1.** The first promise of the internship was small and specific: every claim in the film would have an account behind it. The office had a special room for the material, a manager for the organization, and a career counsellor who had once worked in banking. “Use the label,” the clerk said. “If it is not labelled, it is not evidence.”

这段实习的第一个承诺既小又具体：影片里的每一条主张背后都要有一份说明。办公室有一间专门放材料的房间、一位负责组织工作的经理，以及一位曾在银行业工作过的职业顾问。“用标签，”职员说，“没有标签，就不是证据。”

**2.** The field was employment, and the business was real. A customer with unemployment behind them, a retired teller, a cooperative of small suppliers, and an employee on a zero-hours commission all sat in the same room. “The competition is not the enemy,” the counsellor said. “The expense is.”

这个领域是就业，而生意是真实的。一位有过失业经历的顾客、一位退休的出纳员、一个由小供应商组成的合作社，以及一名拿零时佣金合同的雇员，都坐在同一个房间里。“竞争对手不是敌人，”顾问说，“开支才是。”

**3.** There was a depression in the industry that year, and a recession in the trade papers. The ministry had cut the publication budget, and the mail brought a dismissal notice to a colleague who had worked there for nine years. “This is what an employer looks like when the money stops,” the manager said. “Write it down. It is part of the application.”

那一年这个行业正处在萧条中，行业报刊也处在衰退里。部里削减了出版预算，邮件给一位在此工作九年的同事送来了免职通知。“雇主在钱停下来时就是这个样子，”经理说，“写下来。这也是申请材料的一部分。”

**4.** The partnership with the client had one intangible clause: the team could refuse a submission. “That is a strange kind of power,” Lin said. “It is the only kind,” she answered. “Profession, not employment, is the difference between them and us.”

与客户的合作关系里有一条无形的条款：团队可以拒绝提交。“这是一种奇怪的权利，”林伟说。“这是唯一的一种，”她回答，“把我们和他们区分开的，是职业，而不是雇佣。”

**5.** Their earnings for the summer were enough for a train ticket and a rented room. “Remove the word career from the next version,” the manager told them. “Put in the word work.” The accountancy clerk agreed, the tourism intern did not, and the specialization they were offered — a permanent post in the bank — was the first real choice of the arc. To take it was to accept that a deadline could be a life, and not only a date.

他们那个夏天的收入够买一张火车票和租一间房。“把‘事业’这个词从下一版里去掉，”经理对他们说，“换上‘工作’。”会计部职员同意，旅游部的实习生不同意；而提供给他们的专业化机会——银行里的一个固定职位——就是这段弧线里的第一个真实选择。接受它，就等于承认一个截止日期可以是一种人生，而不只是一个日期。

**6.** On the last Friday, a black car brought the head of the family to the office: her father, a great supporter of the project, with his mother and her little brother in the back. They asked one question that nobody could answer — what would happen to them if the post were taken? “Such a name in a small house,” her father said. “Big or little, it is by the work that a person is known.” The facility had an operator who could employ three of them, and Maya wrote something down before she answered.

最后一个周五，一辆黑色轿车把这一家的主事人送到办公室：她的父亲，项目的一位重要支持者，后座上是他的母亲和她的小弟。他们问了一个没人能回答的问题——如果这个职位被拿走了，他们会怎样？“在这样一栋小房子里用这样一个名字，”她父亲说，“无论大小，人靠工作被人认识。”机构里有一位经营者，能雇用他们三个人；曼雅在回答之前先记下了点什么。

### Exercises

- CH044-Q1 [context] What was the internship's first promise?
  - options: every claim would have an account behind it / every label would be printed twice / the film would be profitable / the bank would hire everyone
- CH044-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “难以明了的；无形的”?
  - options: intangible / profitable / cooperative / retired
- CH044-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: remove the word / remove the carpet / remove the mail / remove the tourism
- CH044-Q4 [cloze] The manager said this is what an ______ looks like when the money stops.
  - options: employer / expense / account / application
- CH044-Q5 [recall] What did the manager tell them to put in the next version?
  - options: the word work / the word career / the word bank / the word profit

### Answers

- CH044-Q1: every claim would have an account behind it - Each statement needed a documented basis.
- CH044-Q2: intangible - The right to refuse was real but invisible.
- CH044-Q3: remove the word - Remove combines with a word, a label, or an obstacle.
- CH044-Q4: employer - The manager showed them what an employer does under financial pressure.
- CH044-Q5: the word work - He replaced the idea of a career with the fact of work.

## CH045 - The First Real Deadline: A Missing Voice / 第一个真实期限：缺席的声音

- theme: 求职、职场与职业发展

**1.** The missing voice belonged to a woman with a serious face who had been paid off in the last layoff. She came to the office with a purse, a folder, and a paragraph she had written by hand. “You are printing a story about people like me,” she said. “I would like to see the text before it goes to the printer.”

那个缺席的声音属于一位面容严肃的女士，她在上一次裁员中被买断。她带着一个钱包、一个文件夹和一段手写的文字来到办公室。“你们要印一个关于像我这样的人的故事，”她说，“我希望在它进印刷厂之前看到文本。”

**2.** The team assumed she wanted money. She wanted accuracy. “The collection of interviews you have is a drama,” she said. “It is not a poll. My name is on your list, and I am available to be interviewed properly, together with the others.”

团队以为她要钱。她要的是准确。“你们收集的访谈是一部戏剧，”她说，“不是一次民意调查。我的名字在你们的名单上，我可以和别人一起，被正式地采访。”

**3.** She was right about the diagram, too: the arrows pointed upwards, and the payroll line went downwards. “Regulate that,” she said, “or your illustration will do the work of a layoff.” A mature engineer from the founding team agreed, and the young kid from the mailroom, who had expected a special bonus, went quiet.

她关于图表的说法也是对的：箭头朝上，而工资线朝下。“去把那一条规范掉，”她说，“否则你们的插图就会干裁员的活。”创始团队一位成熟的工程师表示同意，而那个本以为会拿到特别奖金的收发室孩子沉默了。

**4.** She asked one question that the panel could not answer: who buys a house on a zero-hours contract? “The means are not the same as the opportunity,” she said. “Your film will deliver a story about insurance and copyright, and it will miss the sale of a person's whole week.” The office was open and idle that afternoon; the craze for the platform had not yet reached the third floor.

她问了一个评审小组答不上来的问题：谁会拿着一份零时合同去买房子？“手段不等于机会，”她说，“你们的影片会讲述一个关于保险和版权的故事，却会漏掉一个人整周的出售。”那天下午办公室空敞而闲散；对那个平台的狂热还没有传到三楼。

**5.** They gave her the text and the location of every print run. “I am glad you asked,” Maya said. “I am not asking,” the woman replied. “I am telling you what an appropriate interview looks like.” The interactive apparatus in the corner hummed; nobody looked at it. The paragraph she had written was better than the one they had, and they used it, including her name at the top.

他们把文本和每一版印刷的所在地都给了她。“我很高兴你问了，”曼雅说。“我不是在问，”女士回答，“我是在告诉你，一次恰当的采访是什么样子。”角落里那台互动装置嗡嗡作响；没有人看它。她写的那段比他们写的好，他们采用了它，包括把她的名字放在最上面。

**6.** For the panel, the interview was also a test of the illustration: was the diagram accurate, and did it serve the reader? A mature engineer from the founding team said the print run was too early, and that the payroll should be regulated before the sale of any subscription. “You assume a small office,” she said. “Allow me to say that a kid from the mailroom can see what a copyright does to a hire.” They were glad to be corrected, together, in a spot the apparatus could record. The purse stayed closed; the drama, at least, was now available to buy. The teacher of the evening class, who had left the industry two years later, said the business of the field was to protect the reader, and that no royalty, however effective, could engage a person who had already turned away.

对评审小组来说，这次采访也是对插图的检验：图表是否准确，是否对读者有用？创始团队一位成熟的工程师说印得太早了，工资名单应当在任何订阅出售之前被规范。“你假定这是一间小办公室，”她说，“请允许我说：收发室的孩子也能看出版权对一个聘用意味着什么。”他们很高兴被纠正，一起，在一个装置能记录下来的地点。钱包始终没有打开；至少这出戏剧现在可以买到了。那位晚间班的老师，两年后离开了这个行业，说这一行的本分是保护读者；而任何版税，无论多么有效，都无法吸引一个已经转身离开的人。

### Exercises

- CH045-Q1 [context] What did the woman want before printing?
  - options: to see the text / a bonus / a new diagram / an insurance policy
- CH045-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “工资名单；发放的工资总额”?
  - options: payroll / purse / panel / poll
- CH045-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: deliver a story / deliver a purse / deliver a layoff / deliver a craze
- CH045-Q4 [cloze] She said the means are not the same as the ______.
  - options: opportunity / insurance / royalty / printing
- CH045-Q5 [recall] What did the team do with the paragraph she wrote?
  - options: they used it with her name / they deleted it / they sold it / they sent it to the panel

### Answers

- CH045-Q1: to see the text - She asked to check the text before publication.
- CH045-Q2: payroll - The payroll line dropped while the arrows pointed up.
- CH045-Q3: deliver a story - Deliver combines with a story, a parcel, or a promise.
- CH045-Q4: opportunity - She distinguished the means from the chance to use them.
- CH045-Q5: they used it with her name - Her paragraph replaced theirs and she was named.

## CH046 - The First Real Deadline: Evidence on the Table / 第一个真实期限：摆上桌面的证据

- theme: 求职、职场与职业发展

**1.** The evidence meeting was held in a shop that had been converted into an office. A wooden counter, a traditional sign, and a book of invoices remained from the previous owner. “We need to establish what we owe,” the woman said. “The cost of the film is not the cost of the work.” One part of the room was still a shopping aisle, and his face when he saw it was worth the trip.

证据会议在一家被改造成办公室的商店里举行。前任店主留下了一张木柜台、一块传统的招牌和一本发票簿。“我们需要确立我们欠什么，”那位女士说。“影片的成本不是工作的成本。”房间的一角还是购物通道，而他看到它时的表情值得这趟路程。

**2.** Their pay came from a corporation with an outstanding tax case and a pension fund that it had quietly replaced with a loan. An investor and a banker came to the exhibition; a lobbyist came too, and stayed for the wine. “The consumer pays twice,” the dealer said. “Once in cash, once in attention.” Since the contract began, no one had written that sentence down until now.

他们的薪资来自一家有未结税务案件的公司，以及一笔被它悄悄换成贷款的养老金。一位投资者和一位银行家来看了展览；一位说客也来了，并留下来喝完了酒。“消费者付两次钱，”经销商说，“一次用现金，一次用注意力。”自合同开始以来，没有人把这句话写下来过，直到现在。

**3.** The arrival of the supply figures changed the argument. A supplier of advertising space, a musician who wrote derivative jingles, and a young capitalist from the party of the premier all agreed on one point: the demand for goods was a threat to the small shop, not a help. They could read the writing on the wall, and they could not write it off as pessimism.

供应数据的到来改变了争论。一位广告位供应商、一位写派生广告曲的音乐家，以及总理本党的一位年轻资本家，都在一点上意见一致：对商品的需求对这个小店是威胁，而不是帮助。他们读得懂墙上的字，也无法把它当作悲观主义一笔勾销。

**4.** The team published a one-page report, with a workshop and an open workout for anyone who wanted to read it. Participation was voluntary, the initiation was a cup of tea, and the overtime was unpaid. “That is the compensation,” Maya said. “The mutual appreciation of people who cannot afford to leave.” Together they checked every figure twice; whether the client would accept it remained a question.

团队发布了一份一页纸的报告，并举办了一场工作坊和一次公开演练，供任何想读的人参加。参与是自愿的，入会仪式是一杯茶，加班是无偿的。“这就是补偿，”曼雅说，“那些走不起的人之间相互的赏识。”他们一起把每个数字核了两遍；客户是否会接受，仍然是个问题。

**5.** Their earnings were smaller than the assistance they had received, and the second part of the study was harder to write. Some of them would fulfil the contract; some would convey the problem to the council instead. It was a valuable lesson and an earnest one, and the shopping street outside told the same story. The income was small and the room was big; a copy of the contract was available in the corridor, and nobody had read it. “When the consumer is also the servant,” Lin wrote, “the exhibition is the only honest form of advertising.” They would continue, but they could not remain silent, and the difference between those two verbs was the whole fourth arc.

他们的收入少于他们得到的协助，而研究的第二部分更难写。他们中有些人会履行合同；有些人会把问题转达给委员会。这是一堂有价值的课，也是一堂诚挚的课，而外面的商业街讲的也是同一个故事。收入很少，房间很大；走廊里放着一份合同的副本，而没有人读过它。“当消费者同时又是仆人时，”林伟写道，“展览是唯一诚实的广告形式。”他们会继续，但不能保持沉默，而这两个动词之间的差别，就是整个第四段弧线。

### Exercises

- CH046-Q1 [context] Where was the evidence meeting held?
  - options: in a shop converted into an office / in the bank / in the exhibition hall / in Lin's family home
- CH046-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “杰出的；未偿付的”?
  - options: outstanding / traditional / earnest / derivative
- CH046-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: establish what we owe / establish a wooden counter / establish a cup of tea / establish the wine
- CH046-Q4 [cloze] The dealer said the consumer pays twice: once in cash, once in ______.
  - options: attention / compensation / overtime / supply
- CH046-Q5 [recall] What did Lin call the only honest form of advertising?
  - options: the exhibition / the workshop / the report / the contract

### Answers

- CH046-Q1: in a shop converted into an office - The meeting reused an old shop counter and sign.
- CH046-Q2: outstanding - The corporation had an unsettled tax case.
- CH046-Q3: establish what we owe - Establish combines with an obligation, a fact, or a rule.
- CH046-Q4: attention - The customer pays in money and in attention.
- CH046-Q5: the exhibition - He argued that showing the work honestly is the only honest advertising.

## CH047 - The First Real Deadline: The Trial Run / 第一个真实期限：第一次试行

- theme: 求职、职场与职业发展

**1.** The trial run was built around one task: take a recent proposal, roll it out for a week, and see what happens when people have to use it. A thin cup, a pale sheet of paper, and a musical recording were the only props. “Let us combine the two strategies,” the coordinator said. “Connect the school to the shop and see what the child does.”

试行围绕一个任务展开：拿一份最近的提案，试用一周，看看人们不得不用它时会发生什么。一只薄杯子、一张浅色的纸和一段音乐录音是仅有道具。“让我们把两种策略结合起来，”协调人说，“把学校和小店连接起来，看看孩子会怎么做。”

**2.** An artist in the team wanted color; a democrat on the committee wanted advice from every acre of the district. “This is a raid, not a consultation,” the rebel said, and the spill of complaints that followed proved her right. The external review had a territorial clause, and a treaty of mutual assistance with another ward had never been signed.

团队里的一位艺术家想要色彩；委员会里的一位民主主义者想要从全区每一英亩收集意见。“这是一次突袭，不是一次协商，”那位反叛者说，随后涌来的抱怨证明她说得对。外部评审有一条领土条款，而与另一个辖区相互协助的条约从未签署。

**3.** The pursuit of a breakthrough required a dose of patience and a ton of paperwork. “We must cooperate with the continental office,” the coordinator said, “or the completion date will move again.” A hectare of wheat, a manufacturer of agricultural tools, and a blend of two local dialects gave the trial its surprising harmony.

追求突破需要一剂耐心和一吨文书。“我们必须与大陆办公室合作，”协调人说，“否则完成日期又要推迟。”一公顷小麦、一家农具制造商，以及两种当地方言的混合，给了这次试行意想不到的和谐。

**4.** The urgent part was the settlement. A bench, a roll of tape, and a written plan were all the team had; the rest was possession and hope. “Collaborate with the people who will be blamed if it fails,” Maya said, “and not only with the ones who will be praised.”

紧急的部分是安置。一条长凳、一卷胶带和一份书面计划就是团队拥有的全部；其余的是占有与希望。“去和那些失败后会被指责的人合作，”曼雅说，“而不只是和那些成功后会受表扬的人。”

**5.** The masterpiece they wanted was three lines long: what we did, what happened, what we will change. “A cent of honesty is worth a hundred tons of style,” Lin wrote. A forgery in the appendix nearly destroyed the whole document, and the strategic review that followed was more valuable than the launch.

他们想要的杰作只有三行：我们做了什么、发生了什么、我们将改变什么。“一分诚实抵得上一百吨风格，”林伟写道。附录里的一处伪造几乎毁掉了整份文件，而随后进行的战略复盘比发布本身更有价值。

**6.** Back home, the information was harder to read like a story than like a table. “A woman in the queue asked me for help,” Maya said. “She did not ask for pay.” The business of the shop, the manufacture of a single bench, and the harmonious settlement of two complaints took the last three days of the trial. “Settle it with a word,” the coordinator said, “and do not make it a speech.”

回到家里，这些信息读起来更像一张表格，而不是一个故事。“队伍里一位女士请我帮忙，”曼雅说，“她没有要求报酬。”小店的生意、一条长凳的制造，以及两起投诉的和睦了结，占用了试行的最后三天。“用一个词把它了结，”协调人说，“不要把它变成一场演讲。”

### Exercises

- CH047-Q1 [context] What was the trial run's single task?
  - options: to roll out a recent proposal for a week / to sign a treaty / to paint a masterpiece / to harvest a hectare of wheat
- CH047-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “伪造；伪造物”?
  - options: forgery / breakthrough / harmony / pursuit
- CH047-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: cooperate with the office / cooperate with the cup / cooperate with the color / cooperate with the acre
- CH047-Q4 [cloze] The coordinator said they must combine the two ______.
  - options: strategies / cent / tons / props
- CH047-Q5 [recall] What nearly destroyed the document?
  - options: a forgery in the appendix / a spilled cup / a thin sheet / a musical recording

### Answers

- CH047-Q1: to roll out a recent proposal for a week - The trial tested one proposal under real use.
- CH047-Q2: forgery - A forged page in the appendix almost ruined the report.
- CH047-Q3: cooperate with the office - Cooperate combines with a body, a partner, or a team.
- CH047-Q4: strategies - The coordinator combined two approaches into one trial.
- CH047-Q5: a forgery in the appendix - The forged page put the whole document at risk.

## CH048 - The First Real Deadline: Pressure Builds / 第一个真实期限：压力上升

- theme: 求职、职场与职业发展

**1.** The pressure built for a fortnight, day by day. The council moved the court hearing; the industrial client added a charge for the delay; the magazine and the newspaper both asked for the same interview. “Go back and check the title,” the professional said. “If the surface is wrong, the whole file is wrong.”

压力持续了两周，一天接一天。委员会推迟了法庭听证；工业企业客户为延误追加了一笔费用；杂志和报纸都要同一场采访。“回去核对标题，”那位专业人士说，“如果表面是错的，整份文件都是错的。”

**2.** A colleague in the gallery had already written a review, and a critic in the same building had written the opposite. “You will gain credit with one and lose it with the other,” the accountant said. “That is the margin you are working in.” The manufacturer, the merchant, and the unemployed detective who shared the rack in the corridor all had an opinion.

画廊里的一位同事已经写了一篇评论，同一栋楼里的一位批评家写了相反的一篇。“你会因此得到一个人的信任，又失去另一个人的，”会计说，“这就是你正在其中工作的余量。”制造商、商人，以及共用走廊那排架子的失业侦探，都有自己的看法。

**3.** The fundamental problem was rent. The office cost more than the rice and the wash of the whole team, and the applicant with the managerial experience had already gone away. “Be sure of the question before you answer it,” the librarian said. “A gardener does not water a web.”

根本问题是租金。办公室的开销超过整个团队的米饭和洗衣费用，而那位有管理经验的申请人早已离开。“回答问题之前先确定问题，”图书管理员说。“园丁不会给一张网浇水。”

**4.** The entrepreneur in the sack coat wanted a spy in the opposite team; the clerical marshal wanted lace on the cover. “Much of this is occupational theatre,” Lin said, “and some of it is last-minute panic.” They were already late, and the face of the client on the screen was not a friendly one.

穿麻布外套的企业家想在对手团队里安一个间谍；教士身份的执法官想给封面加上花边。“这里面很多是职业性的戏剧，”林伟说，“还有一些是最后一刻的惊慌。”他们已经迟了，而屏幕上客户的脸并不友善。

**5.** Together the team bought back two days by cutting a correspondent from the schedule. “Will it be enough?” Maya asked. “It will be something,” he said, “and something is not nothing.” The old problem returned, as it always did: a professional must answer for the whole, not for the part they touched.

团队一起从日程里砍掉一名通讯记者，换回了三天。“这够吗？”曼雅问。“总算是点东西，”他说，“而点东西不是没有。”那个老问题又回来了，一如既往：专业人士必须为整体负责，而不只是为自己碰过的那部分。

**6.** An insider from the newspaper, a painter from the gallery, and a gardener from the roof came to the office on the last day. “The heart of the file is the margin,” the insider said. “Everything else is decoration.” A copy was available on the rack, and the man from the sack coat read it twice before he bought the magazine. The will to continue was there; the time was not. It was already quite dark outside by then.

最后一天，来自报社的一位内部人士、来自画廊的一位画家，以及来自屋顶的一位园丁来到办公室。“这份文件的核心是那处空白，”内部人士说，“其他都是装饰。”架子上有一份可取的副本，穿麻布外套的那个人读了它两遍，才买下那本杂志。继续下去的意志还在，时间不在。外面已经黑了。

### Exercises

- CH048-Q1 [context] What did the client charge for?
  - options: the delay / the lace / the interview / the rice
- CH048-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “余量；页边空白”?
  - options: margin / charge / surface / gain
- CH048-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: gain credit / gain lace / gain a sack / gain a spy
- CH048-Q4 [cloze] The librarian said a gardener does not water a ______.
  - options: web / rack / gallery / margin
- CH048-Q5 [recall] How did the team buy back time?
  - options: by cutting a correspondent from the schedule / by hiring an accountant / by moving the office / by selling the gallery

### Answers

- CH048-Q1: the delay - The industrial client added a charge when the work slipped.
- CH048-Q2: margin - The accountant described the narrow room for error.
- CH048-Q3: gain credit - Gain combines with credit, time, or experience.
- CH048-Q4: web - She used a gardener's image against wasted effort.
- CH048-Q5: by cutting a correspondent from the schedule - They removed a role to recover two days.

## CH049 - The First Real Deadline: The Disagreement / 第一个真实期限：分歧

- theme: 求职、职场与职业发展

**1.** The disagreement was about one week: ship on Friday, or wait beyond the weekend and lose the holiday slot. “We are always doing this,” Maya said. “We spend Monday to Thursday arguing and then hurry on Friday.” The police had asked for a signed guideline; the inspector had asked for two more days. “Certainly the worst option is a dash at dawn,” she said. “And the catch is that we have already promised.”

分歧关于一周中的某一天：周五发布，还是等到周末之后、丢掉假期的档期。“我们总是这样，”曼雅说，“周一到周四争论，然后周五赶工。”警方要求一份签署的指导方针；检查员要求再给两天。“最糟的选择毫无疑问是黎明时的冲刺，”她说，“而陷阱在于我们已经答应了。”

**2.** Lin argued for a speedy release. A successful release would be a Christmas present for the council; a delayed one would cost the acquisition and lose the festival coverage. “The era of the perfect launch is over,” he said. “The customary thing is to go out on Monday and fix it on Tuesday.”

林伟主张迅速发布。一次成功的发布将是给委员会的圣诞礼物；一次延期则会损失这次收购，并错过节庆报道。“完美上线的时代已经结束了，”他说，“惯常的做法是周一发布，周二修。”

**3.** Maya argued for a durable release. “Wednesday is the only weekday the inspector can come,” she said. “Thursday is the marathon; Saturday is the day we sleep. A pregnancy of nine months does not become negotiable because Thanksgiving arrives.” The prohibitive cost of a recall was the part he kept overlooking.

曼雅主张持久的发布。“周三是检查员唯一能来的平日，”她说，“周四是马拉松；周六是我们睡觉的日子。九个月的孕期不会因为感恩节到来就变成可磋商的。”召回那笔高昂到禁止性的成本，是他一直忽略的部分。

**4.** Amid the argument, the coordinator produced the sequential figures: the accumulative risk of the last three releases, measured evenly across the weekend and the weekday. “Two successive releases went out overnight,” she said. “The excellence of the first hid the failure of the second. In the meantime, nobody ran the test.”

在争论中，协调人拿出了连续的数据：最近三次发布的累积风险，在周末与平日之间均匀地衡量。“连续两次发布都是在一夜之间上线的，”她说，“第一次的优秀掩盖了第二次的失败。与此同时，没有人跑过测试。”

**5.** The compromise was expensive and honest: release on Friday for the festival, and hasten the inspection so that the new guideline could be established by the following week. “The usual guideline is written for the best case,” the inspector said. “Write the other one.” They listened to each other for the first time that day, and the cost of the argument was one weekend of running repairs.

妥协是昂贵而诚实的：周五为节庆发布，同时加紧检查，让新的指导方针能在下一周确立。“常规的指导方针是为最好的情况写的，”检查员说，“把另一种情况也写出来。”那天他们第一次真正听了彼此的话，而这场争论的代价，是一个周末的边跑边修。

**6.** For the council, the cost was one more day in court; for the team, it was a new line in the guideline. “Certainly the catch is that we must go back and establish who pays,” Maya said. “If we do not listen to the other side, the help we give will be a move in the wrong direction.” They put their hearts into the last paragraph, and the inspector, in the end, signed it.

对委员会来说，代价是多上一天法庭；对团队来说，是指导方针里多出的一行。“当然，陷阱在于我们必须回头确立由谁付钱，”曼雅说，“如果我们不听另一方的意见，我们给出的帮助就会变成方向错误的一步。”他们全心全意地写下了最后一段，而检查员最终签了字。

### Exercises

- CH049-Q1 [context] What did the police ask for?
  - options: a signed guideline / a Christmas present / two more inspectors / a smaller council
- CH049-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “禁止性的；高得吓人的”?
  - options: prohibitive / negotiable / successive / customary
- CH049-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: hasten the inspection / hasten the pregnancy / hasten the marathon / hasten the dawn
- CH049-Q4 [cloze] The coordinator said the excellence of the first release hid the ______ of the second.
  - options: failure / gain / margin / credit
- CH049-Q5 [recall] What did the inspector ask them to write?
  - options: the other guideline / a new era / a holiday plan / a faster dash

### Answers

- CH049-Q1: a signed guideline - The police required a written, signed procedure.
- CH049-Q2: prohibitive - The cost of a recall was too high to consider.
- CH049-Q3: hasten the inspection - Hasten combines with an inspection, a reply, or a decision.
- CH049-Q4: failure - One good release masked the next one's failure.
- CH049-Q5: the other guideline - The inspector wanted a rule for the worst case as well.

## CH050 - The First Real Deadline: What the Numbers Hide / 第一个真实期限：数字隐藏了什么

- theme: 求职、职场与职业发展

**1.** By the first minute of Monday's management meeting, the team could hear that something was wrong. Here was a wonderful dashboard showing a huge increase in admission requests—especially from a mountainous district—but the figures did not match the paper receipt kept by each local office. All sixteen charts used the same framework, and each looked somewhat convincing. Management had always called this a big success and asked the team to show it to investors; Maya reminded them that the missing forms belonged to real people. Maya, now practicing journalism at the company, asked one particular question: did the missing applications still exist?

周一管理会议开始不到一分钟，团队就听出事情不对。仪表盘漂亮地显示某山区的申请数量大幅上升，尤其显眼；但这些数字与当地各办公室保存的纸质收据不符。十六张图表全部使用同一个框架，看起来都颇有说服力。管理层一直把这称为一次巨大成功，并要求团队向投资者展示；曼雅提醒他们，丢失的表格属于真实的人。正在公司实习做新闻工作的曼雅提出了一个具体问题：那些消失的申请是否仍然存在？

**2.** Grace opened fourteen sample files, then thirteen more. A considerable number had disappeared after a regulatory update. Seventeen users had tried repeatedly, but the system treated every resumption as a new session. The problem was increasingly serious. A conditional rule was meant to prohibit duplicate claims; instead, it could violate equal-access policy by blocking people with slow connections. The apparent avoidance of fraud had created a discriminatory result.

刘佳打开十四份样本文件，又查看了十三份。大量记录在一次监管更新后消失。十七名用户反复尝试，但系统把每次恢复操作都当成新会话。问题越来越严重。一条附带条件的规则原本用于禁止重复申报，却可能因拦截网络较慢的人而违反平等准入政策。表面上的防欺诈措施造成了歧视性结果。

**3.** The software vendor wanted to simplify the report and authorize release. Its representative described the loss as petty, then praised the vast benefits for an immense user base. Lin felt deeply disappointed. Good oversight could not abolish evidence merely because the evidence was inconvenient. A preferential success rate for well-connected users was not beneficial to the public, and no form of professional admission could excuse concealment.

软件供应商希望简化报告并批准发布。代表称损失微不足道，随后大谈庞大用户群能够获得的广泛好处。林伟深感失望。良好的监督不能因为证据不方便就废除证据。只让网络条件良好的用户享受优惠成功率并不符合公共利益，任何职业资格都不能成为隐瞒的借口。

**4.** Daniel placed a metallic box on the table. It held the wreckage of a damaged field terminal, a manual, an old coinage sample used in training, and a printed receipt. The physical evidence made the immensity of the digital gap visible. In areas of scarcity, equipment was not plentiful; a commuter might spend a week traveling to submit one form. The team asked the police liaison only about device theft, not about applicants. That separation mattered.

丹尼尔把一个金属盒放到桌上，里面装着损坏现场终端的残骸、一本手册、培训中使用的旧币样本和一张打印收据。实物证据让数字鸿沟的巨大程度变得可见。在资源匮乏地区，设备并不充足；通勤者可能要花一周时间才能提交一份表格。团队只向警方联络员询问了设备失窃，却没有询问申请人。这种割裂非常关键。

**5.** Maya wrote up the finding without deceit or exaggeration. The accountant calculated the mounting cost of correction, while Grace prepared a lawful resumption plan. The company would prohibit automatic rejection, restore the missing records, and publish the oversight. The decision would disappoint investors expecting an immediate launch, but it protected admittance for real users. Faced with the immensity of the repair, Lin still felt some puzzlement; yet the team's practice was now clear: strong journalism, careful management, and honest collaboration had to come before a promising deadline.

曼雅如实写下发现，没有欺骗，也没有夸张。会计核算不断上升的修复成本，刘佳则制定合法的恢复计划。公司将禁止自动拒绝，恢复丢失记录，并公布监督失误。这个决定会让期待立即上线的投资者失望，却保护了真实用户的准入权。面对庞大的修复工作，林伟仍有些困惑，但团队的做法已经明确：有力的新闻调查、谨慎的管理和诚实合作必须优先于一个看似前景光明的期限。

### Exercises

- CH050-Q1 [context] Why was the dashboard misleading?
  - options: It hid missing applications / It used no charts / It counted only police records / It was written by applicants
- CH050-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “监督；疏忽”?
  - options: oversight / coinage / wreckage / admittance
- CH050-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: regulatory update / regulatory noodle / regulatory mountain / regulatory Monday
- CH050-Q4 [cloze] The rule produced a ______ result by blocking one group unfairly.
  - options: discriminatory / metallic / plentiful / wonderful
- CH050-Q5 [recall] What did the company decide to restore?
  - options: the missing records / the old coinage / the investors / the mountains

### Answers

- CH050-Q1: It hid missing applications - The charts looked positive because failed records had disappeared.
- CH050-Q2: oversight - Oversight can mean supervision or an unintentional failure to notice something.
- CH050-Q3: regulatory update - Regulatory commonly modifies rules, requirements, or changes governed by authorities.
- CH050-Q4: discriminatory - The rule treated users differently because of their connection quality.
- CH050-Q5: the missing records - Restoring records was part of the resumption plan.

## CH051 - The First Real Deadline: A Better Question / 第一个真实期限：更好的问题

- theme: 求职、职场与职业发展

**1.** The better question arrived over dinner on the eighth of August. “Our deadline is overdue,” Maya said, “and the renewal is not the priority.” A delegation from the council had asked for a description of how the figures were used, and the statistical office had sent back something that was not a description at all. The little office, the court date, and the great unfinished film were all in the same report for the first time.

更好的问题在八月八日的晚餐上到来。“我们的期限已经逾期了，”曼雅说，“而续期并不是优先事项。”委员会的一个代表团要求说明这些数字是如何被使用的，而统计办公室回过来的东西根本算不上说明。那间小办公室、法庭的日期，以及那部伟大而未完成的影片，第一次出现在同一份报告里。

**2.** The false assumption was simple: that a punctual team is a keen team. “We recommend a different matter for today,” Lin said. “Yesterday we sewed the same sleeve twice.” The telescope, the chef, and the commodity trader in the same lift all had a theory about the following month, and the march of dates was the thrust of the whole argument.

那个错误的假设很简单：准时的团队就是热心的团队。“我们今天推荐换个题目，”林伟说，“昨天我们把同一只袖子缝了两遍。”同一部电梯里的望远镜、厨师和商品交易员，都对下个月有一套理论，而日期的行进正是整场争论的推力所在。

**3.** The trademark of the film was its authenticity, and authenticity was indicative of something else: they had not foreseen the extra work. A buckle, a supplementary line, and the twelfth paragraph had been added after the launch, and the consequent delay was now in the report. A day after the following one, they would go back and count the cost again.

这部影片的商标就是它的真实性，而真实性又指示着另一件事：他们没有预见到额外的工作。一个皮带扣、一行补充文字，以及第十二段，都是在发布之后加进去的，而随之而来的延误如今写进了报告。再过一天，他们会回头重新计算代价。

**4.** The subsequent meeting was short. They concluded that the push for control had cost them the enjoyment of the work, and that the desirability of a fast release was a matter of the head, not of the house. “Immediately” became “by the ninth”, and “we must” became “we will”. The family name on the office door, for the moment, meant more to them than the deadline.

随后的会议很短。他们得出结论：对控制的推动让他们失去了工作的乐趣，而快速发布的合意性是头脑的问题，不是房子的问题。“立即”变成了“在九号之前”，“我们必须”变成了“我们将”。此刻，办公室门上的那个家姓，对他们比期限更重要。

**5.** The little business went on with its small tasks. “A flock of them is not a plan,” Maya said. “Something has to move, or nothing will.” She was right, and she was also tired; the better question, in the end, was not when the film would be finished but who would be blamed if it were not. It was likely that nobody would thank them, and likely that the following month would be worse. They were, in a word, tired, and the tiredness was the extra cost that nobody had described.

这桩小生意继续着它的小任务。“一群这样的事不是计划，”曼雅说，“总得有什么动起来，否则什么都不会动。”她说得对，她也很累；说到底，更好的问题不是影片何时完成，而是如果完不成，责任会落在谁头上。很可能没有人会感谢他们，而下个月很可能更糟。一句话，他们累了，而这份疲惫是没有人描述过的额外代价。

### Exercises

- CH051-Q1 [context] What did the delegation ask for?
  - options: a description of how the figures were used / a new telescope / a cheaper dinner / the film's trademark
- CH051-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “随后的；接下去的”?
  - options: subsequent / indicative / punctual / keen
- CH051-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: meet a deadline / meet a buckle / meet a chef / meet a flock
- CH051-Q4 [cloze] Maya said the renewal is not the ______.
  - options: priority / trademark / commodity / dinner
- CH051-Q5 [recall] What was the false assumption?
  - options: that a punctual team is a keen team / that the film was finished / that the council had paid / that dinner was late

### Answers

- CH051-Q1: a description of how the figures were used - The council wanted the method behind the numbers.
- CH051-Q2: subsequent - The meeting that followed was short.
- CH051-Q3: meet a deadline - Meet combines with a deadline, a standard, or a need.
- CH051-Q4: priority - She ranked the renewal below the immediate question.
- CH051-Q5: that a punctual team is a keen team - They had confused speed with commitment.

## CH052 - The First Real Deadline: The Second Attempt / 第一个真实期限：第二次尝试

- theme: 求职、职场与职业发展

**1.** The second attempt began with a plan on a plastic desk under a low ceiling. The committee had asked them to perform one investigation before they spent another month: could the tool be built without the equipment they did not have? “You must realize,” the landlord said, “that the smoke alarm is the only thing in this building that always works.”

第二次尝试从一张计划开始，放在低矮天花板下的一张塑料桌上。委员会要求他们在再花一个月之前先做一次调查：这件工具能否在没有那些他们并不拥有的设备的情况下造出来？“你必须意识到，”房东说，“这栋楼里唯一一直好用的东西就是烟雾报警器。”

**2.** The advantage of the small team was its availability: a photographer, a lecturer, a retailer, and a chemist were all free that week. Together they could construct a demo on a desktop in three days, and they could buy the spare parts from a second hand vendor in the cave of a market. “The face of the customer is the only specification that means anything,” Maya said.

小团队的优势在于可调配：一位摄影师、一位讲师、一位零售商和一位化学家那一周都有空。他们一起可以在三天内在一台台式机上搭出一个演示，并能从市场上一个洞穴般的二手摊贩那里买到备件。“客户的脸色是唯一真正有意义的规格说明，”曼雅说。

**3.** It would be a promising performer, this tool, if they could keep it honest. “A composer, a robot, and an opera singer all use the same punch card,” she said. “The difference is what they do with the sound.” They would spend the week on the quantification of something that could not be counted, and they would do it together.

如果能让它保持诚实，这会是一件有前途的表演者。“一位作曲家、一个机器人和一位歌剧歌手用的是同一张打孔卡，”她说，“区别在于他们怎么处理声音。”他们会把这一周花在对某种无法计数之物的量化上，而且会一起做。

**4.** The competitive pressure was speculative and therefore endless. A coupon in a cancelled buffet, a fry cook's opener, and the notional promise of a hack from a rival firm: all of it had to be folded into the plan. “You could say the software would evolve,” Lin wrote, “but evolution is not fiction, and cancellation is not a strategy.”

竞争压力是投机性的，因此没有尽头。一张被取消的自助餐的优惠券、一位油炸厨师的开门器，以及对手公司一次黑客行为的想象承诺：全都得折进计划里。“你可以说软件会进化，”林伟写道，“但进化不是虚构，而取消不是策略。”

**5.** The industrious part was the tireless part. Today they counted what the tool did; tomorrow they would count what it failed to do. “The month is not the unit,” she said. “The failure is.” It was a mean way to put it, and it was also the only way that would keep them honest. The police had asked whether the tool would be available to everyone, and the answer was not yet available either. A small parade of questions followed: could a second hand vendor sell it, would the committee buy the plan, must they spend the month on the demonstration, and would they, together, be able to face the small print? They would, they said, and today they meant it.

勤勉的部分就是不知疲倦的部分。今天他们数这件工具做到了什么；明天他们要数它没能做到什么。“月份不是单位，”她说，“失败才是。”这样说是刻薄的，也是唯一能让他们保持诚实的说法。警察问过，这件工具是否会对所有人开放，而这个问题同样还没有答案。随后是一小串问题：二手商贩可以卖它吗？委员会会买这个方案吗？他们必须把这个月花在演示上吗？而他们一起，能面对那些小字条款吗？他们说能，而今天他们是当真的。

### Exercises

- CH052-Q1 [context] What did the committee ask for before another month of spending?
  - options: one investigation / a new landlord / a buffet / an opera ticket
- CH052-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “可利用性；有效性”?
  - options: availability / advantage / cancellation / speculation
- CH052-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: construct a demo / construct a ceiling / construct a coupon / construct a chemist
- CH052-Q4 [cloze] The small team's advantage was its ______.
  - options: availability / machinery / seniority / postage
- CH052-Q5 [recall] What did Lin say evolution is not?
  - options: fiction / a plan / a tool / a demo

### Answers

- CH052-Q1: one investigation - The committee wanted evidence before further spending.
- CH052-Q2: availability - The four specialists were all free that week.
- CH052-Q3: construct a demo - Construct combines with a demo, a model, or an argument.
- CH052-Q4: availability - Their advantage was having the right people free at the right time.
- CH052-Q5: fiction - He refused to treat evolution as a story they could invent.

## CH053 - The First Real Deadline: Outside Pressure / 第一个真实期限：外部压力

- theme: 求职、职场与职业发展

**1.** The outside pressure came from the company itself. The central firm, a modest agency with one garage and a division of four, asked them to join a general workshop on the farm: a recruit, a veteran, and a commander would all be there. “You have the ability,” the boss said. “What you do not have is unity.”

外部压力来自公司本身。那家中央公司——一个只有一间车库、四个部门的小小代理机构——请他们参加农场上一场普通的研讨会：一名新兵、一位老兵和一位指挥官都会到场。“你们有能力，”老板说，“你们缺少的是团结。”

**2.** Behind the plan was a committee of veterans who administered the course. They had a troop of volunteers, a gang of part-time drivers, a mess hall, and a laundry. “We better continue the sort in the same order,” the moderator said. “Separation is not dignity.” A penny of speculation was worth more to them than a stripe of seniority.

计划背后是一个由老兵组成的委员会，他们管理者这门课程。他们有一队志愿者、一群兼职司机、一间食堂和一处洗衣房。“我们最好按同样顺序继续分类，”主持人说，“分离不是尊严。”一分钱的投机，对他们来说比一道资历条纹更值钱。

**3.** The workshop was about reproduction, ore, and the ant: small things that a company can command, and large things it cannot. “Do not offend the part of the picture you cannot see,” the heroine of the film said. “That is the tangible chore of management.”

研讨会的主题是繁殖、矿石和蚂蚁：公司能指挥的小东西，以及它无法指挥的大东西。“不要冒犯画面中你看不见的那部分，”影片的女主角说，“那才是管理中实实在在的零工。”

**4.** The master plan required a successor, a separation payment, and a knit of two departments that had never spoken. “I realize the cost,” the commander said, “and I help you establish it in writing.” The impress of the whole scheme was, especially at first, one of quiet administration.

总计划需要一个继任者、一笔离职补偿，以及两个从未说过话的部门之间的编织。“我明白代价，”指挥官说，“我帮你把它写下来确立。”整个方案的印记，尤其在一开始，是一种安静的管理。

**5.** The tangible result was postage: they mailed the computation, the plan, and a picture of the garage to the department, and the department raised a question about the pay. “The tangible part is the part we can raise,” the boss said. “The rest is what we believe about ourselves.” Two veterans were due to retire, and the firm had not yet named a successor for either. “You can impress a board,” the commander said, “but you cannot impress a garage.” The general plan, the central workshop, and the modest farm were all part of the same course: the ability to command a troop is not the ability to administer a division, and a company that forgets this ends in a mess. A reproduction of a nineteenth-century contract, with its clause on slavery, hung in the corridor behind the laundry; nobody had read it in years.

实实在在的结果是邮资：他们把计算、计划和一张车库的照片寄给了部门，而部门对薪酬提出了一个问题。“实在的部分就是我们能提高的那部分，”老板说，“其余的是我们对自己的相信。”两位老兵即将退休，而公司还没有为其中任何一位指定继任者。“你可以让一个董事会印象深刻，”指挥官说，“但你无法让一间车库印象深刻。”总体方案、中央车间和那片不大的农场，都属于同一条路径：能指挥一个排，不等于能管理一个师；而一家忘了这一点的公司，最终会收不了场。走廊里洗衣房后面的墙上挂着一份十九世纪合同的复制品，上面有关于奴隶制的条款；已经很多年没有人读过它了。

### Exercises

- CH053-Q1 [context] What did the boss say the team lacked?
  - options: unity / ability / money / a garage
- CH053-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “尊严”?
  - options: dignity / seniority / separation / speculation
- CH053-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: administer a course / administer an ant / administer a stripe / administer ore
- CH053-Q4 [cloze] The workshop was about reproduction, ore, and the ______.
  - options: ant / farm / gang / penny
- CH053-Q5 [recall] What was the tangible result?
  - options: postage / a new farm / a promotion / a workshop

### Answers

- CH053-Q1: unity - He valued their ability but doubted their cohesion.
- CH053-Q2: dignity - The moderator insisted that separation is not dignity.
- CH053-Q3: administer a course - Administer combines with a course, a fund, or an office.
- CH053-Q4: ant - The workshop studied small things a company can command.
- CH053-Q5: postage - The visible output was the cost of mailing the file.

## CH054 - The First Real Deadline: The Decision / 第一个真实期限：决定

- theme: 求职、职场与职业发展

**1.** The decision was taken around a table in the studio, with the minister, the chairman, and the secretary of the embassy all present. The official from the registrar's office attended by phone. “We must decide today,” the minister said. “The obligation is not temporary.” There were six of them around the table, and one empty chair for the volunteer who had resigned.

决定是在工作室的一张桌边做出的，部长、主席和大使馆的秘书都在场。登记处的那位官员通过电话参加。“我们今天必须决定，”部长说，“这项义务不是临时的。”桌边有六个人，还有一把空椅子，属于那位辞职的志愿者。

**2.** The general case was simple. The service would receive a contribution, a negotiation, and a fee; in exchange the team would carry the debt, the penalty, and the marketing. “That is not a partnership,” Maya said. “That is a job with a nicer name.” However, the company had been like this for a month, and the team had to say something.

一般的情形很简单。服务部门会收到一笔捐款、一次谈判和一笔费用；作为交换，团队要承担债务、罚金和营销。“这不是合伙关系，”曼雅说，“这是一份名字更好听的雇佣。”然而，公司这样已经一个月了，团队必须表态。

**3.** The expenditure on paper alone was larger than the donation. A player from the football club, a barber, a cashier, and a secretary from the personnel office came to offer their labor for nothing; a slave in the old architecture of the headquarters building had once done the same for less. “A vocation is not a penalty,” the chairman said. “Say what you want and we will write it.”

光纸张上的开支就大过那笔捐赠。足球俱乐部的一位球员、一位理发师、一位出纳员，以及人事办公室的一位秘书都来无偿提供劳动；总部大楼旧建筑风格里的一个奴隶，当年为更少的东西做过同样的事。“天职不是刑罚，”主席说，“说出你想要什么，我们就写下来。”

**4.** The resignation of the registrar had made the negotiation harder. The bonus, the retirement plan, the appliance allowance, and the supermarket coupon were all temporary; the work was not. “I like the sunrise better than the sunset,” Lin said. “I will take the smaller one.” It was likely that the chairman would be angry; it was certain that the team would have to receive the answer together.

登记官的辞职让谈判变得更难了。奖金、退休计划、电器补贴和超市优惠券都是暂时的；而工作不是。“比起日落，我更喜欢日出，”林伟说，“我要那个小的。”主席很可能会发怒；而团队一定会一起收到这个答复。

**5.** In the end they said no to the permanent post and yes to the undertaking. The headquarters wanted a retirement and got a resignation; the embassy wanted a donation and got a homework club instead. “There is a commerce in this that is not about money,” Maya wrote, “and there is a service in it that is not about service.” What would happen to the studio was a matter of some interest; so was the question of whether a small undertaking could thrive without a salary. It could, she said, if they were willing to work at sunrise.

最后他们对那个固定职位说了不，而对那项事业说了是。总部想要一个退休，得到的是一份辞呈；大使馆想要一笔捐赠，得到的却是一个家庭作业俱乐部。“这里面有一种与钱无关的商业，”曼雅写道，“也有一种与服务无关的服务。”工作室会怎样，是一件颇有意思的事；而一个小小的事业能否在没有薪水的情况下兴旺，也同样有意思。她说，能的——只要他们愿意在日出时就开始工作。

### Exercises

- CH054-Q1 [context] Who attended the decision by phone?
  - options: the official from the registrar's office / the minister / the barber / the football player
- CH054-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “开支；费用”?
  - options: expenditure / donation / bonus / penalty
- CH054-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: carry the debt / carry the sunrise / carry the appliance / carry the barber
- CH054-Q4 [cloze] The chairman said a vocation is not a ______.
  - options: penalty / bonus / coupon / studio
- CH054-Q5 [recall] What did the embassy want, and what did it get?
  - options: a donation, but got a homework club / a resignation, but got a bonus / a coupon, but got a fee / a retirement, but got a barber

### Answers

- CH054-Q1: the official from the registrar's office - The registrar's official joined by telephone.
- CH054-Q2: expenditure - Their spending on paper exceeded the donation.
- CH054-Q3: carry the debt - Carry combines with a debt, a cost, or a risk.
- CH054-Q4: penalty - He refused to treat a calling as a punishment.
- CH054-Q5: a donation, but got a homework club - The embassy's expectation was replaced by something more useful.

## CH055 - The First Real Deadline: Consequences / 第一个真实期限：后果

- theme: 求职、职场与职业发展

**1.** The consequences arrived as a notification on a very ordinary day. The officer from the council submitted a conduct report, and the candidate who had lost the recruitment had gone to court over it. “It was possible,” the minister said, “that we caused this.” Indeed, the offer carried a corresponding obligation, and nobody had read the last page. They would go back to the case and move the reason onto the record.

后果在一个非常普通的日子里，以一条通知的形式到来。委员会的那位官员提交了一份行为报告，而那位在招聘中落选的候选人已经为此上了法庭。“有可能，”部长说，“是我们造成了这件事。”的确，那份录用带有一项相应的义务，而没有人读过最后一页。他们会回到案件上，把原因写进记录。

**2.** The removal of the seasonal officer was a surprise. A competitor with a presidency to protect, a counterpart in the other office, and a resident of the third storey all wanted a rating, and the rating obliged them to decorate the cafeteria before the visit. The workforce had been seasonal for eighteen years, and nobody had thought to tell the residents.

那位季节性官员的免职令人意外。一位要保住主席职位的竞争者、另一间办公室里的对等人物，以及三楼上的一位住户都想要一个评级，而这个评级迫使他们在那次来访之前把自助餐厅装饰一番。这支劳动力队伍已经季节性存在了十八年，却没有人想到要告诉那些住户。

**3.** The work was not really useful to them, and that was the question: for whose job was this a service? “A furnace and a gadget are both machines,” Maya said. “One heats a house, the other sells a house.” The wire, the lid, and the pig on the poster were the only decoration in the room, and the table was covered in outgoing mail.

这份工作对他们其实没什么用，而问题正在于此：这是为谁的工作在提供一种服务？“火炉和小玩意都是机器，”曼雅说，“一个给房子加热，另一个把房子卖掉。”电线、盖子和海报上的那头猪，是屋里仅有的装饰，而桌上堆满了待寄出的邮件。

**4.** The outgoing chair had an urge to computerize everything and a wish to instal a new system by the winter. “We lean on the young workforce,” Lin wrote, “and then we call their fatigue a repayment.” A suspense of three weeks followed, and the congratulation that finally came was signed by someone who had never visited.

即将卸任的主席有一股冲动，想把一切都电脑化，并希望在冬天之前安装一套新系统。“我们倚靠年轻的劳动力，”林伟写道，“然后把他们的疲惫称作报偿。”随后是三周的悬念，而最终到来的祝贺，签名的是一位从未到访过的人。

**5.** The cause was, in the end, an orientation: the department was built for the orient and not for the town, and its recruitment followed the same map. “That is a proud way to fail,” the officer said, and everyone at the table knew that she was right. Their clothes, their dress, and their very habits in the corridor were part of the evidence. They had learned to operate the old system by habit, and the habit would haunt them for another year at least. Actually, the whole case had been in front of them for a month.

原因说到底是一种取向：这个部门是为东方、而不是为这座城建的，它的招聘遵循同一张地图。“这是一种骄傲的失败方式，”那位官员说，桌边每个人都知道她说得对。他们的衣物、他们的穿着，乃至他们在走廊里的习惯，都是证据的一部分。他们已经习惯于按老办法操作这套旧系统，而这个习惯至少还会缠着他们一年。事实上，整个案件已经摆在他们面前一个月了。

### Exercises

- CH055-Q1 [context] What did the officer submit?
  - options: a conduct report / a new furnace / a seasonal rating / a poster
- CH055-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “强迫；使感激”?
  - options: oblige / submit / decorate / computerize
- CH055-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: submit a report / submit a furnace / submit a pig / submit a lid
- CH055-Q4 [cloze] Lin wrote that the young workforce is leaned on and their fatigue is called a ______.
  - options: repayment / rating / presidency / recruitment
- CH055-Q5 [recall] Why was the department built the way it was?
  - options: for the orient, not for the town / for the winter / for the cafeteria / for the competitors

### Answers

- CH055-Q1: a conduct report - The officer filed a report on the department's conduct.
- CH055-Q2: oblige - The rating obliged them to redecorate before the visit.
- CH055-Q3: submit a report - Submit combines with a report, a claim, or an application.
- CH055-Q4: repayment - He described exhaustion being called a reward.
- CH055-Q5: for the orient, not for the town - The department's orientation never matched the town it served.

## CH056 - The First Real Deadline: The Bridge Forward / 第一个真实期限：通向下一程

- theme: 求职、职场与职业发展

**1.** The fourth arc closed with a contract and a conference. The overall theme was “the opening of the year”, and the executive from the central company arrived with a conventional bid and an extraordinary recommendation: hire the founder, not the contractor. The police were at the door, and the offer came with a white envelope.

第四段弧线以一份合同和一场会议收尾。总主题是“一年的开幕”，中央公司的那位高管带着一个常规报价和一条非凡的建议到来：雇那位创立者，而不是承包商。警察就在门口，而那份录用附着一个白色信封。

**2.** The agreement had a loophole: a contractor could leave after one visit, and the placement of the mining permit was left to the general office. “We always know the best position and never the price,” the attendant said. The petrol bill, the processor, and the specification for the jeans were all in the annex.

协议里有一个漏洞：承包商可以在一次到访后离开，而采矿许可的安置被留给了总办公室。“我们总是知道最好的位置，从不知道价格，”那位随从说。汽油账单、处理器和牛仔裤的规格说明都在附件里。

**3.** In the evening they joined the police and the company at a memorial dinner. “It is possible to spend a week on a white memorial and a pound on a plug,” Maya said. “The functional part is the part nobody photographs.” A contraction of the budget followed, and the design of the balcony had to be redone.

傍晚，他们和警察、公司一起参加了一场纪念晚宴。“可以用一周时间做一块白色纪念碑，花一英镑买一个插头，”曼雅说，“功能性的部分就是没人拍照的那部分。”随后预算出现了一次收缩，阳台的设计不得不重做。

**4.** The shift in the market was indeed a cause of the closure, but not the only one. “A lot of small engagements are one engagement,” the executive said, and the room noticed the white sheet under the forge. The forge, the garden, and the night shift were all that remained of the older plan.

市场上的转变的确是倒闭的原因之一，但不是唯一的原因。“很多小的约定其实只是一个约定，”那位高管说，屋里的人注意到了冶铁炉下那张白纸。冶铁炉、花园和夜班，是旧计划留下的一切。

**5.** They created a small notice, an ad, and an explanation of the estimate in three languages. The acceptance of the offer, when it came, was shorter than the offer itself. “Tell them we are not a department,” she said. “We are a position, a detail, and a year of work.”

他们用三种语言做了一份小通告、一则广告和一份对估算的解释。那份对录用的接受，到来时比录用通知本身还短。“告诉他们，我们不是一个部门，”她说，“我们是一个位置、一个细节和一年的工作。”

**6.** The general office wrote back within a week. It accepted the contract, ignored the loophole, and sent a second attendant to the balcony with a functional plug and a printed estimate. “A conventional bid is a promise in a suit,” the founder said. “An extraordinary one is a promise in jeans.” They spent the last of the month on petrol and a processor, and told the police exactly what they had spent and why the loophole mattered to a public contract. The deadline had been met; what it had cost them was the belief that a deadline is the hard part.

总办公室在一周内回了信。它接受了合同，忽略了那个漏洞，并派了第二位随从到阳台，带着一个功能正常的插头和一份打印的估算。“常规报价是穿西装的承诺，”创立者说，“非凡的那一种是穿牛仔裤的承诺。”他们把那个月剩下的钱花在汽油和一台处理器上，并如实告诉警察他们花了多少，以及那个漏洞为什么对一份公共合同至关重要。期限是赶上了；它让他们付出的代价，是“期限才是最难的”这个信念。

### Exercises

- CH056-Q1 [context] What was the conference's overall theme?
  - options: the opening of the year / the closing of the forge / the price of petrol / the police visit
- CH056-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “漏洞（条款中的）”?
  - options: loophole / estimate / contract / acceptance
- CH056-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: join the police at dinner / join the petrol / join the balcony / join the specification
- CH056-Q4 [cloze] Maya said the ______ part is the part nobody photographs.
  - options: functional / extraordinary / conventional / seasonal
- CH056-Q5 [recall] What three things did she say they were?
  - options: a position, a detail, and a year of work / a department, an office, and a budget / a contract, a bid, and an ad / a forge, a garden, and a night shift

### Answers

- CH056-Q1: the opening of the year - The conference framed the year's start.
- CH056-Q2: loophole - A clause allowed the contractor to leave early.
- CH056-Q3: join the police at dinner - Join combines with a group, a table, or a service.
- CH056-Q4: functional - She defended the unglamorous, working part of the system.
- CH056-Q5: a position, a detail, and a year of work - She refused the word department.

## CH057 - Across Borders: A Signal / 跨越边界：一个信号

- theme: 旅行、交通与文化交流

**1.** The film was delivered and the office was behind them; the next deadline would be in another language. The fifth arc began with a journey: a plane, a truck, a jeep, and a tunnel in the same week. Aisha met them at the station with a thermometer in her bag and a pronunciation guide in her hand. “The tone of a language is part of its safety,” she said. “I is not the same as I am.” They themselves had left a service job for this.

影片交付了，办公室留在了身后；下一个期限会用另一种语言到来。第五段弧线以一段旅程开始：飞机、卡车、吉普车和隧道都出现在同一周。艾莎在车站接他们，包里装着一支温度计，手里拿着一份发音指南。“一种语言的语调是它安全的一部分，”她说，“‘我’和‘我是’不是一回事。”他们自己是放弃了服务工作才来到这里的。

**2.** The big case was the rail line itself: a barrel of fluid, a coil of cable, and a cargo of berries had been waiting three days. The management of the rail service blamed the tide; the minister blamed the weather. “We survive by rumor here,” Aisha said, “and rumor is a rocky road.” We have to tell them that, and the night shift at the table was the position where the work of a year stood.
