最大的案子就是那条铁路线本身：一桶液体、一卷电缆和一批浆果已经等了三天。铁路管理部门怪潮水；部长怪天气。“我们这里靠传言活着，”艾莎说，“而传言是一条多石的路。”这一点我们必须告诉他们，而桌边的那一班夜岗，正是这一年的工作所站的位置。

**3.** The hope was a quiet one: that a small team could steal a march on a system with a billion reasons to ignore them. A football match on the platform, a piano playing jazz in the hall, and a drum and a whip in the market gave the place a rhythm they did not know. Him they watched most closely of all; itself, the station, kept its own time.

那份希望是安静的：一支小团队能在一个有十亿个理由忽视他们的系统里抢占一步。站台上的足球赛、大厅里弹爵士乐的钢琴，以及市场里的鼓和鞭子，给了这个地方一种他们不熟悉的节奏。他们最仔细打量的是他；而车站本身，按着自己的时间走。

**4.** The prime difficulty was not technical. “A mushroom grows overnight; a habit does not,” their host said. A pan, a chop, a knot of wire, and a ray of light through the carriage window: their host used objects to explain usage, and the therapy of it was patience.

最主要的困难不是技术上的。“蘑菇一夜之间就能长出来；习惯不能，”接待他们的人说。一口平锅、一块排骨、一团打结的电线和一缕穿过车厢窗户的光：主人用实物来解释用法，而其中的疗愈之处就是耐心。

**5.** The hardship of the journey gave them a spur: a curl of paper with the timetable, a spit of rain on the glass, and the quiet endurance of a station cat. “They will treat us as tame,” Lin said, “until we are useful.” Aisha, who had created the timetable herself, said nothing. The king of the district had died that spring, and the station was still quiet; the journey home via the coast road was over before the fifth day. They would remember the tunnel, the tide, and the tone of a farewell for years.

旅途的艰难给了他们一个激励：一张写着时刻表的卷纸、玻璃上的一阵雨沫，以及一只站台猫安静的忍耐。“他们会把我们当作温顺的，”林伟说，“直到我们有用。”而亲手做出那张时刻表的艾莎，什么也没说。那年春天，这个地区的国王去世了，车站仍然很安静；沿海岸公路回家的旅程在第五天之前就结束了。他们会记住那条隧道、那道潮水，以及一句告别的语调，记很多年。

### Exercises

- CH057-Q1 [context] What did Aisha say about the tone of a language?
  - options: that it is part of its safety / that it is a rumor / that it is a rocky road / that it is overnight
- CH057-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “忍耐；忍耐力”?
  - options: endurance / hardship / spur / usage
- CH057-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: steal a march / steal a thermometer / steal a piano / steal a mushroom
- CH057-Q4 [cloze] Their host said a ______ grows overnight but a habit does not.
  - options: mushroom / barrel / tunnel / cargo
- CH057-Q5 [recall] What did their host use to explain usage?
  - options: objects / a piano / a jeep / a thermometer

### Answers

- CH057-Q1: that it is part of its safety - She treated tone as a safety matter, not decoration.
- CH057-Q2: endurance - The station cat's quiet endurance impressed them.
- CH057-Q3: steal a march - Steal a march means to gain an advantage secretly or early.
- CH057-Q4: mushroom - He contrasted fast growth with slow habit.
- CH057-Q5: objects - The host used everyday objects as teaching aids.

## CH058 - Across Borders: The First Promise / 跨越边界：第一次承诺

- theme: 旅行、交通与文化交流

**1.** The first promise of the fifth arc was made on a Sunday, near the bottom of a hill, under the roof of a station cafe. “We proceed through the exchange office,” Aisha said. “Through, not around.” A visa, a tight connection, and a loose plan were the whole of it, and the run of the first week was worse than any of them had feared.

第五段弧线的第一个承诺是在一个星期天做出的，在山脚附近、车站咖啡馆的屋檐下。“我们通过兑换办公室走，”艾莎说，“是穿过，不是绕开。”一张签证、一次紧张的换乘和一个松散的方案就是全部内容，而第一周的那种奔跑比他们任何人担心的都糟。

**2.** The field was new, and the industry was not. An immigrant family ran a parking service near the airline counter; their van, their bike, and their carriage were the only transport for a hundred metres. “A cab is not a decoration,” the mistress of the house said. “It is a roof.”

这个领域是新的，行业却不是。一户移民家庭在航空公司柜台旁经营着一项停车服务；他们的货车、自行车和马车是一百米之内唯一的交通方式。“出租车不是装饰，”那家房屋的女主人说，“它是一片屋顶。”

**3.** The distant hills were on the television in the lounge, and the overhead lights of the carriage stayed on all night. “A homeless man understands the interior of a station better than any engineer,” she said. “That is a familiarity we cannot buy.”

休息室里电视上放着远处的山丘，车厢顶灯整夜亮着。“一个无家可归的人比任何工程师都更懂车站的内部，”她说，“这是一种我们买不到的熟悉感。”

**4.** The bark of a dog, a procession of claps, and the countdown to a spacecraft launch: the cafe television had everything except the freight timetable. “It is ridiculous,” Lin said, “that a starter can find the fuel but not the train.” The trip, for him, was over before it began.

一条狗的吠叫、一阵掌声的队列，以及航天飞船发射的倒计时：咖啡馆的电视什么都有，只没有货运时刻表。“太荒谬了，”林伟说，“新手找得到燃料，却找不到火车。”对他来说，这趟旅程还没开始就结束了。

**5.** They would relax for one evening, then smash the last problem: a hijack of the schedule by a cruiser of tourists who had bought every seat. “Any business has a special month,” Aisha said. “This is ours, and it matters particularly to the office in the capital.” A note in the margin said: “I must say that we are still far from the offer.”, and it is likely that the baggage rules will change today; a passenger cannot comprehend a rule that the staff themselves cannot restrain. It was possible that a ragged timetable would never be intimate with a stranger's need; the trip had taught them that much. A distant hill, a tight connection, and a loose plan: the pattern of the whole arc was already visible in the first Sunday afternoon of the journey. It was, in the end, a promise about the use of a timetable.

他们会放松一个晚上，然后击碎最后一个问题：一船游客买下了所有座位，劫持了整张时刻表。“任何生意都有它的特殊月份，”艾莎说，“这就是我们的，而且对首都那间办公室尤其重要。”页边的一条注记写着：“我必须说我们离那份报价还很远。”，而且行李规定很可能今天就变；一名乘客无法理解一条连工作人员自己都无法约束的规则。一张粗糙的时刻表，也许永远不会与一个陌生人的需要亲密无间；这趟旅程至少教会了他们这一点。一座远处的山、一次紧凑的换乘、一个松散的计划：整段弧线的图案，在旅程的第一个周日下午就已经看得见了。说到底，这是一个关于如何使用时刻表的承诺。

### Exercises

- CH058-Q1 [context] How did Aisha say they would pass the exchange office?
  - options: through it, not around it / around it, not through it / by train / with a visa only
- CH058-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “无家可归的”?
  - options: homeless / distant / ridiculous / intimate
- CH058-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: run a parking service / run a roof / run a bark / run a countdown
- CH058-Q4 [cloze] The mistress of the house said a cab is not a decoration but a ______.
  - options: roof / visa / bike / smash
- CH058-Q5 [recall] What hijacked the schedule?
  - options: a cruiser of tourists / a spacecraft / a procession / a freight train

### Answers

- CH058-Q1: through it, not around it - Aisha insisted on dealing with the office directly.
- CH058-Q2: homeless - She said a homeless man knows the station's interior best.
- CH058-Q3: run a parking service - Run combines with a service, a business, or a risk.
- CH058-Q4: roof - She described the cab as shelter, not ornament.
- CH058-Q5: a cruiser of tourists - The tourists bought every seat and broke the timetable.

## CH059 - Across Borders: A Missing Voice / 跨越边界：缺席的声音

- theme: 旅行、交通与文化交流

**1.** The missing voice belonged to a woman who ran a tiny motel on the corridor between the station and the port. Her livelihood was the queue: a taxi, a tram, a trolley, and a cart all stopped at her door. “A vehicle is not a conveyance,” she said. “It is a promise that someone will come back.” Her face, when she said it, was entirely sincere, and she had been saying it for a year.

那个缺席的声音属于一位在车站与港口之间那条走廊上经营小汽车旅馆的女士。她的生计就是那条队伍：出租车、电车、手推车和马车都会停在她的门口。“车辆不是运输，”她说，“它是一个有人会回来的承诺。”她说这话时的表情完全真挚，而她已经说了一年。

**2.** The committee had never asked her anything. According to the plan, the new line would shut the corridor at night and serve only the far end of the district, leaving her one open hour. “We hereby revise nothing,” the introductory letter said, electronically and without sincerity. A recreational bicycle, a soccer ball, and a postcard from the kingdom of the north were the only things in her window. We had no automobile and no auto of any kind, and the fireplace in the waiting room had not been lit for a year.

委员会从来没有问过她任何事。按照计划，新线路将在夜里关闭这条走廊，只服务这个区最远的一端，只给她留一个开放的小时。“我们据此不作任何修订，”那封介绍性的信写道，以电子方式，而且毫无诚意。她橱窗里只有一辆休闲自行车、一个足球，以及一张来自北方王国明信片。我们没有任何汽车，候车室的壁炉也已经一年没有点过。

**3.** A psychologist from the exchange campus came with an exhaustive report: the sore shoulder of a passenger, the lazy habit of a distributor, and the inch of platform that made a wheelchair impossible. “A boat and an aircraft are both rooms,” she said. “The difference is where you keep the ax.” A volunteer would accompany her on the walk through the depot, and the depot was where the report had to be handed over.

交换校园的一位心理学家带来了一份详尽的报告：一位乘客酸痛的肩、一位分销商懒惰的习惯，以及那一英寸让轮椅无法通行的站台。“船和飞机都是房间，”她说，“区别在于你把斧头放在哪里。”一名志愿者会陪她走过车辆段，而那份报告必须在车辆段递交。

**4.** They were slightly late for the meeting, and the mortgage on the depot was due the same week. “We cannot mechanize a promise,” Maya said. “We can only mechanize a gate.” A ballet company travelled through with their costumes, a tram driver with forty years of service, and a taxi owner with a sore hand: all of them had signed the same petition, and all of them were available to speak.

他们开会稍微迟到了，而车辆段的抵押贷款就在同一周到期。“我们无法把承诺机械化，”曼雅说，“我们只能把一道闸门机械化。”一个芭蕾舞团带着服装经过，一位有四十年工龄的电车司机，以及一位手疼的出租车车主：他们都签了同一份请愿书，而且他们都可以出面作证。

**5.** The instructive part was the queue itself. Together they measured it: so many seconds per person, so many horsepower per hour, and thereby a number that the committee could not ignore. “You must create one open position for the night run,” Aisha said, “including the stop that serves nobody but her.” And they bought the postcard, and put it in the report. Near the pond, a distributor of tickets would acquaint every new passenger with the rules, and the distributive effect of the new timetable was obvious in her ledger.

有教益的部分就是那条队伍本身。他们一起丈量了它：每人多少秒、每小时多少马力，并由此得出一个委员会无法忽视的数字。“你们必须为夜间班次设一个开放的位置，”艾莎说，“包括那个只服务她一个人的站。”于是他们买下了那张明信片，把它放进了报告。池塘附近，一位售票的分发者会让每一位新乘客熟悉规则；而新时刻表在分配上的效果，在她的账本里一目了然。

### Exercises

- CH059-Q1 [context] What did the woman call a vehicle?
  - options: a promise that someone will come back / a mechanical gate / an exhaustive report / a recreational toy
- CH059-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “生计；营生”?
  - options: livelihood / conveyance / sincerity / horsepower
- CH059-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: shut the corridor / shut the sincerity / shut the postcard / shut the ax
- CH059-Q4 [cloze] The psychologist said a boat and an aircraft are both ______.
  - options: rooms / queues / trams / kingdoms
- CH059-Q5 [recall] What did they put into the report?
  - options: the postcard / the ax / the ballet costume / the mortgage

### Answers

- CH059-Q1: a promise that someone will come back - She defined transport as a promise, not a machine.
- CH059-Q2: livelihood - The queue at her door was her living.
- CH059-Q3: shut the corridor - Shut combines with a corridor, a door, or a line.
- CH059-Q4: rooms - She compared both vessels to enclosed spaces.
- CH059-Q5: the postcard - The postcard became evidence in the report.

## CH060 - Across Borders: Evidence on the Table / 跨越边界：摆上桌面的证据

- theme: 旅行、交通与文化交流

**1.** The evidence meeting was held in a hotel near the international port, and every stage of the journey was on the table: the local road, the express coach, the camp site, and the crossing itself. “What we call traffic is a chain of small appointments,” Aisha said. “Break the chain and the passenger pays the cost.” I hoped the company would see it that way; my father and mother both worked in transport, and the general view in our house was that a ticket is a promise.

证据会议在国际港口附近的一家旅馆举行，旅程的每一个阶段都摆在桌上：本地的道路、特快快车、营地，以及那次横渡本身。“我们所谓的交通是一连串小小的约会，”艾莎说，“把这条链打断，代价由乘客来付。”我希望公司也能这样看；我父母都在交通行业工作，我们家的一般看法是：车票就是承诺。

**2.** The foreign delegation brought a campaign poster, a passport, and a mine map. “An export is not a journey,” their carrier said. “It is an occupation.” A black eagle on the poster, a universal sign for a port, and the word import in three languages made the point better than any speech could.

外国代表团带来了一张运动海报、一本护照和一张矿区地图。“出口不是旅程，”他们的承运人说，“它是一种占领。”海报上那只黑色的鹰、一个通用的港口标志，以及用三种语言写成的“进口”一词，比任何演讲都更有说服力。

**3.** The local committee had one question: who would help the person who could not ride a bicycle, a car, or a ship? “A vehicle is a room with a door,” the coach said, “and a door is a question.” Still, nobody in the room could answer it, and the voice of the person affected was not in the room at all.

本地委员会只有一个问题：谁来帮助那个既不能骑车、不能开车、也不能乘船的人？“车辆是一间有门的房间，”教练说，“而门是一个问题。”尽管如此，屋里没有人能回答它，而受影响那个人的声音根本不在屋里。

**4.** A young astronaut, a swift circus performer, and a sailor aboard a cargo ship had all filled in the same form. The brake of the number 4 bus, the switch at the crossing, and the eagle on the customs seal were the only things anyone had photographed.

一位年轻的宇航员、一位敏捷的马戏团演员，以及一位在货轮上工作的水手都填了同一份表格。4路公交车的刹车、道口的开关，以及海关印章上那只鹰，是唯一有人拍下来的东西。

**5.** The voyage abroad was postponed, and over the winter they rewrote the whole case together. “I join the campaign,” Maya said, “and I bring the cost with me.” It was, in the end, a foreign idea that a port belongs to the people who cross it, and a very local one that they should be asked first. The local area, the street outside the hotel, and the culture of the crossing itself were not in any report. “A commute is not travel,” the carrier said, “and nationalism is not a timetable.” An exportation licence, a parent with three children, and a bicycle chained to a lamp post: the committee had photographed none of them. “The best evidence is the evidence nobody wants,” Aisha said, and they wrote it down.

出国航行被推迟了，而在整个冬天里他们一起重写了整个案子。“我加入这场运动，”曼雅说，“我把代价一起带来。”说到底，这是一个外来的想法：港口属于穿越它的人；也是一个非常本土的想法：应当先问他们。本地那片区域、旅馆外的街道，以及穿越这件事本身的文化，都没有出现在任何报告里。“通勤不是旅行，”承运人说，“而民族主义不是一张时刻表。”一份出口许可证、一位带着三个孩子的家长、一辆锁在路灯柱上的自行车：委员会一张也没有拍下来。“最好的证据就是没人想要的那种证据，”艾莎说，而他们把它写了下来。

### Exercises

- CH060-Q1 [context] What did Aisha call traffic?
  - options: a chain of small appointments / a universal sign / an export / a campaign
- CH060-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “承运人；运送者”?
  - options: carrier / crossing / brake / import
- CH060-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: break the chain / break the passport / break the hotel / break the eagle
- CH060-Q4 [cloze] The carrier said an export is not a journey but an ______.
  - options: occupation / appointment / eagle / express
- CH060-Q5 [recall] Whose voice was missing from the committee room?
  - options: the person who could not ride or drive / the astronaut / the sailor / the coach

### Answers

- CH060-Q1: a chain of small appointments - She described traffic as a linked sequence of commitments.
- CH060-Q2: carrier - The delegation's carrier moved goods and people.
- CH060-Q3: break the chain - Break combines with a chain, a promise, or a habit.
- CH060-Q4: occupation - He refused to separate trade from occupation.
- CH060-Q5: the person who could not ride or drive - The committee discussed but never invited the affected traveller.

## CH061 - Across Borders: The Trial Run / 跨越边界：第一次试行

- theme: 旅行、交通与文化交流

**1.** The trial run began in April and ran, with interruptions, until December. Across February and June, the team was simply too small; in July and September the enrolment in the preparatory course was enough, and in October and November it was necessary to postpone the demonstration. “Maybe in May,” the local minister said. “Perhaps next year.” Nevertheless, the work went on alongside the cycling club and the roadside BBQ, which was more popular than any of their workshops.

试行从四月开始，时断时续地持续到十二月。二月到六月期间，团队实在太小；七月和九月，预备课程的报名人数足够了；而十月和十一月，不得不把演示推迟。“也许五月吧，”地方部长说，“也许明年。”然而，工作仍在继续，与骑行俱乐部和路边烧烤同时进行，后者比他们的任何一场工作坊都受欢迎。

**2.** The case was a geographic one: a faulty road, a damp crossing, and a hint of corruption in the luggage service. “I can predict a delay,” Aisha said, “but I cannot prescribe a remedy without the local people.” A Marxist essay, a poem about a spaceship, and a geometric diagram of the intersection were all submitted as evidence, and all of them were criticized in the same week.

这个案子是地理性的：一条有缺陷的道路、一处潮湿的道口，以及行李服务中一丝腐败的气味。“我可以预测延误，”艾莎说，“但没有当地人，我开不出药方。”一篇马克思主义的随笔、一首关于宇宙飞船的诗，以及一张交叉路口的几何图，都被作为证据提交，而它们都在同一周受到了批评。

**3.** The instant the trial began, a quarrelsome driver was arrested for trafficking a spare tyre; the contextual part of the story was that his brother had lost the family job the year before. “We must pursue the system, not the man,” the team wrote, “and we must retell the case without the fear.”

试行开始的那一刻，一位爱吵架的司机因贩卖一只备用轮胎而被捕；这个故事的情境部分是：他的哥哥前一年失去了家里的工作。“我们必须追查系统，而不是这个人，”团队写道，“而且我们必须不带恐惧地重述这个案子。”

**4.** The indispensable part was the spontaneity of the queue: people simply stepped forward when the timetable failed. “It is not exploration,” Maya said, “it is improvisation.” They wrote it down, and the essay that came out of it was better than the report.

不可缺少的部分是那条队伍的自发性：时刻表一失效，人们就自己上前。“这不是探索，”曼雅说，“这是即兴。”他们把它写了下来，而由此产生的那篇随笔比报告更好。

**5.** What the team could not demonstrate was the one thing the council wanted: that a small change would survive a large system. “Maybe it will, perhaps it will not,” Lin wrote. “We can only see it through.” That sentence went into the report, across the whole width of the page, in their own hand. The local minister had said the team could expect nothing, and the team, despite everything, still had to allow for the service to fail. A car, a door, an area, a street, and a road: the small words of the report did most of the work. “If a problem arises far from the depot, we will run through it together,” Aisha said, “and that is a job nobody else wants.”

团队无法证明的，恰恰是委员会想要的那一件事：一个小的改变能否在一个大系统里存活下来。“也许会，也许不会，”林伟写道，“我们只能一路看下去。”这句话被写进了报告，横跨整页的宽度，用的是他们自己的笔迹。地方上的部长说过，这个团队什么也别指望；而这个团队，尽管经历了这一切，仍然必须为服务失败留出余地。一辆车、一扇门、一片区域、一条街、一条路：报告里这些小词承担了大部分工作。“如果问题出现在离车站很远的地方，我们就一起把它跑一遍，”艾莎说，“而这是一份没人愿意做的工作。”

### Exercises

- CH061-Q1 [context] When did the trial begin, and when did it run until?
  - options: April to December / May to October / July to September / February to June
- CH061-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “不可缺少之物”?
  - options: indispensable / preparatory / contextual / quarrelsome
- CH061-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: predict a delay / predict a poem / predict a spaceship / predict a BBQ
- CH061-Q4 [cloze] The team wrote that they must pursue the system, not the ______.
  - options: man / tyre / essay / hint
- CH061-Q5 [recall] What could the team not demonstrate?
  - options: that a small change survives a large system / that the timetable failed / that the course enrolled enough / that the driver was arrested

### Answers

- CH061-Q1: April to December - The trial stretched across most of the year.
- CH061-Q2: indispensable - The travellers' spontaneity proved essential.
- CH061-Q3: predict a delay - Predict combines with a delay, a result, or a trend.
- CH061-Q4: man - They insisted on examining the system rather than blaming the driver.
- CH061-Q5: that a small change survives a large system - The council's real question remained unproven.

## CH062 - Across Borders: Pressure Builds / 跨越边界：压力上升

- theme: 旅行、交通与文化交流

**1.** The pressure built after a lengthy morning in court. The council had moved the hearing twice, and the township had offered one more decade of patience and no more money. May and June had gone; maybe the offer would come in September, and perhaps it would not come at all. Since the outset, the team had promised one thing: they would remain until the service was efficient or until it was clear that it could not be.

压力在法庭上一个漫长的早晨之后不断累积。委员会两次推迟了听证，而小镇又给了十年的耐心，却没有多给一分钱。五月和六月过去了；也许报价会在九月到来，也许根本不会来。从一开始，团队就承诺了一件事：他们会留下，直到服务变得有效率，或者直到清楚它不可能有效率。

**2.** A pessimistic councillor supposed that the whole exercise was a pastime for students. “I refuse to praise a report that refuses to name a price,” he said. “You deserve the applause you get, and no more.” The rejection was brief and utter, and the applause that followed it was for him.

一位悲观的地方议员推测，整个做法不过是学生们的消遣。“我拒绝称赞一份拒绝给出价格的报告，”他说，“你得到多少掌声就配多少，不多不少。”拒绝简短而彻底，而随后的掌声是给他的。

**3.** The gratitude of the travellers was harder to dismiss. A trader in illegal trafficking had been confined for a month; a hateful landlord had raised the rent of the inn; a contemptible clause in the contract had been signed repeatedly. “A worthwhile project is one that survives its own bad decade,” Aisha said. “We are presently in it.”

旅客们的感激更难被推开。一位从事非法交易的商人被关了两个月；一位可恨的房东提高了客栈的租金；合同里一条可鄙的条款被反复签署。“值得做的项目，是能挺过自己糟糕的十年的项目，”艾莎说，“而我们目前正在十年里。”

**4.** They would finally interact with the council through a single document, and they would maintain one copy of it in the local language. “I bet they postpone the vote,” Lin wrote. “I bet they resign the question instead.” The lump in his throat was not fear; it was the thank you he had not said to his father, who had died that spring.

他们最终会通过一份文件与委员会互动，并会保留一份用当地语言写成的副本。“我打赌他们会推迟表决，”林伟写道，“我打赌他们会把问题搁置。”他喉咙里那个肿块不是恐惧；那是他没能对父亲说出口的那句谢谢——父亲在那年春天去世了。

**5.** The eventual answer arrived shortly before the election. It was not a marvel, and it was not admirable; it was a permission with three conditions. “Be thankful for the small ones,” Maya said. “Forever is not our business.” The group applauded once, quietly, and went back to work on the timetable. Across the day and the court, the council moved once more, and the offer remained possible. “Maybe we go, perhaps we stay,” Lin wrote. “One thing is certain: the permission has been ours only since May.” They had to maintain the service for a decade, to praise nothing they had not seen, and to thank the clerk who had lost her own job to help them. A century of habit was harder to reject than a clause; eventually they would pray for a better vote, contend with the popularity of the old route, and ask whether a confinement of one month could ever be justified when the alternative was nothing.

最终的答复在选举前不久到来。它不是奇迹，也不值得钦佩；它是一份带三个条件的许可。“要为这些小东西感到感激，”曼雅说，“永远不是我们的事。”小组轻轻鼓了一次掌，然后回去继续做时刻表。日子一天天过去，法庭那边，议会又一次行动，而那份报价也仍然有可能。“我们也许走，也许留，”林伟写道，“有一件事是确定的：这份许可从五月起才属于我们。”他们必须把这项服务维持十年，必须不称赞任何他们没见过的东西，必须感谢那位为了帮他们而丢了自己工作的职员。一个世纪的习惯，比一条条款更难拒绝；最终他们会为一次更好的表决而祈祷，会去应付旧路线的受欢迎程度，也会问：当替代方案是什么都没有时，一个月的封闭是否真的能被证明正当。

### Exercises

- CH062-Q1 [context] What had the team promised since the outset?
  - options: to remain until the service was efficient or clearly could not be / to leave after one year / to raise the rent of the inn / to postpone the vote
- CH062-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “值得花时间的；值得做的”?
  - options: worthwhile / pessimistic / lengthy / utter
- CH062-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: maintain a copy / maintain a lump / maintain a pastime / maintain a marvel
- CH062-Q4 [cloze] The councillor said he refused to praise a report that refused to name a ______.
  - options: price / decade / pastime / permission
- CH062-Q5 [recall] What was the eventual answer?
  - options: a permission with three conditions / a rejection / a decade of patience / an applause

### Answers

- CH062-Q1: to remain until the service was efficient or clearly could not be - They committed to staying until the outcome was clear.
- CH062-Q2: worthwhile - She judged the project by whether it survived its bad decade.
- CH062-Q3: maintain a copy - Maintain combines with a copy, a road, or a standard.
- CH062-Q4: price - He attacked the report for avoiding a cost figure.
- CH062-Q5: a permission with three conditions - The council finally granted a conditional permission.

## CH063 - Across Borders: The Disagreement / 跨越边界：分歧

- theme: 旅行、交通与文化交流

**1.** The disagreement was about ownership, and it had a famous ancestor. “After a year of work, who has the right to the patent?” the chief of the local committee asked. “The navy has a rule for a wreck, a dictator has a rule for a ballot, and a diplomat has a rule for a tyre. We have none.” A woman in the front row began to explain the distinction, and the room went quiet.

分歧在于所有权，而它有一个著名的祖先。“工作一年之后，谁有权拿到这项专利？”本地委员会的领袖问。“海军对沉船有规则，独裁者对选票有规则，外交官对轮胎有规则。我们什么都没有。”前排一位女士开始解释这个区别，屋里安静下来。

**2.** Generally speaking, the team wanted a salary, a wage, and a share; specifically, they wanted the right to privatize nothing and to inherit the name. “A privatization is not a pardon,” Aisha said, “and a patent is not a reward.” A warrior of the old school, a well-known local businessman, and a selfless volunteer all nodded, which was disappointing to nobody. They could not obtain the forthcoming decision in writing, and the sum was allegedly unpaid, but that was to be expected.

一般来说，团队想要薪水、工资和一份份额；具体来说，他们想保留不私有化任何东西的权利，并继承这个名字。“私有化不是赦免，”艾莎说，“专利也不是奖赏。”一位老派的战士、一位著名的本地商人，以及一位无私的志愿者都点了头，这一点没有让任何人失望。他们拿不到即将到来的书面决定，款项据称也未支付，但这也在预料之中。

**3.** In accordance with their own rule, they tossed a coin instead of taking a vote. The result was a historic one for the area: the right would be held by the people who used the road, not by the company that had built it. “We are not solely a service,” Maya said. “We are also a habit, and a habit is harder to punish than a crime.”

按照他们自己的规则，他们抛硬币而不是投票。对这个地区来说，结果具有历史意义：这项权利将由使用这条道路的人持有，而不是由修建它的公司持有。“我们不仅仅是一项服务，”曼雅说，“我们还是一种习惯，而习惯比罪行更难惩罚。”

**4.** After a week of talking, the chief dictated a short clause and the local people signed it. “Yourselves, not the wireless, not the webcast, and not the gadgetry,” she said. “You cannot leave an old-fashioned promise to a machine.” A considerate neighbour brought woolen blankets, and a fluent speaker of three languages read the clause aloud to the queue.

谈了一周之后，领袖口述了一条简短的条款，当地人签了字。“你们自己，而不是无线电、不是网络直播、也不是那些小玩意，”她说，“你们不能把一个老式的承诺留给机器。”一位体贴的邻居拿来了羊毛毯，一位能说三种语言的流利者把条款向队伍朗读了一遍。

**5.** They agreed to spend the money sparingly and to keep one exclamation out of the report: “At last!” It was an altogether better ending than a quarrel, and their friend the motel owner said so. A child from the night queue, a local car owner, and a woman who had lived there all her life stood behind them at the counter. The people at home always have the last word, and that was fine.

他们同意节省地花钱，并在报告里只留一句感叹：“终于！”这比一场争吵要好得多，他们的朋友——那位汽车旅馆老板娘——也这么说。一个夜里排队的孩子、一位本地车主，以及一位在此生活了一辈子的女士，站在柜台后面支持他们。家里的人总是有最后一句，这没什么不好。

### Exercises

- CH063-Q1 [context] What did the chief say the committee lacked?
  - options: a rule for ownership / a navy / a diplomat / a ballot box
- CH063-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “所有权”?
  - options: ownership / pardon / severity / sponsorship
- CH063-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: obtain a decision / obtain a tyre / obtain a webcast / obtain a blanket
- CH063-Q4 [cloze] In accordance with their own rule, they ______ a coin instead of voting.
  - options: tossed / dictated / punished / inherited
- CH063-Q5 [recall] Who would hold the right?
  - options: the people who used the road / the company that built it / the navy / the diplomat

### Answers

- CH063-Q1: a rule for ownership - They had rules for wrecks, ballots, and tyres, but none for their own work.
- CH063-Q2: ownership - The whole argument was about who owned the result.
- CH063-Q3: obtain a decision - Obtain combines with a decision, a licence, or consent.
- CH063-Q4: tossed - They tossed a coin instead of holding a vote.
- CH063-Q5: the people who used the road - The right went to the users, not the builders.

## CH064 - Across Borders: What the Numbers Hide / 跨越边界：数字隐藏了什么

- theme: 旅行、交通与文化交流

**1.** The numbers hid a desert of small failures. Hardly anyone had received the training they were promised, and a gift of two tickets was given twice to the same family. “I may be wrong, perhaps,” Aisha said, “but an survey that only counts participants will always be sure of itself.” I own the mistake, she added, and the room went quiet.

这些数字掩盖了一片由小失败组成的沙漠。几乎没有一个人得到过承诺过的培训，而一份两张票的礼物被同一家人拿走了两次。“我也许错了，或许吧，”艾莎说，“但一项只统计参与者的调查，总会对自己充满确信。”她补充说，这个错误算我的——屋里安静下来。

**2.** Primarily the report measured sponsorship, and the sponsorship was dirty money from a dusty road fund. “An incredible friendship is worth more than warmth from a machine,” Maya wrote. “We are seemingly measuring geography when we are actually measuring power.” Owing to the frosty reception, the numbers went into the appendix, and the appendix went moldy in the damp.

这份报告主要衡量的是赞助，而赞助来自一笔走漏到肮脏道路基金的钱。“一段难以置信的友谊比机器带来的温暖更值钱，”曼雅写道，“我们表面上在测量地理，实际上却在测量权力。”由于那次冷淡的接待，数字被放进了附录，而附录在潮湿中发霉了。

**3.** The excessive intent behind the count was not hidden from the biochemist who joined them for a month. “You cannot compute holiness,” she said, “and a repetitive figure will overtake the truth if you let it.” Downward pressure on the budget followed; the severity of it was overwhelming, and the mood in the office turned gloomy.

这次计数背后过度的意图，没能瞒过和他们共事一个月的生物化学家。“你无法计算神圣，”她说，“而一个重复的数字，只要你放任它，就会超越真相。”预算随之承受向下的压力；其严酷程度是压倒性的，办公室里的气氛变得阴郁。

**4.** Lastly, the rarity of the good numbers made them even more dangerous. “An eternal average is a lie with a long life,” the old man said. “Do not let them ridicule the messy part, and do not exaggerate the clean one.” A speechless trainee sympathized and said nothing; the perilous part was the silence.

最后，那些好数字的罕见反而使它们更危险。“一个永恒的均值，是一条长寿的谎言，”老人说。“不要让他们嘲弄混乱的那部分，也不要夸大干净的那部分。”一位说不出话的实习生表示同情，却什么也没说；危险的部分正是这份沉默。

**5.** They decided to globalize nothing and to publish a second sheet: one for the figures and one for the frosty mornings. It was not a dramatic return, but it was a return, and they were desirous of one thing only: that the report be readable by the people it described. Maybe it would be; perhaps it would not. Across the pavement, the training had been offered to anyone who wished to participate, and hardly anyone had. “We hope they will not depart before the vote,” the biochemist said, “and we hope they will not obey a heaven made of averages.” Still, the man who had come back from the desert said the mood was overwhelmingly better than the year before, and the figures that had been thrown away were carried back into the room. They would not lose the argument; it was over before the second sheet was printed.

他们决定不把任何东西全球化，而是发布第二张纸：一张写给数字，一张写给那些结霜的早晨。这不是一次戏剧性的回归，但它是一次回归；他们只渴望一件事：让这份报告能被它所描述的人读懂。也许能，也许不能。人行道对面，那项培训向任何愿意参加的人开放，而几乎没有人参加。“我们希望他们不要在表决之前离开，”那位生物化学家说，“也希望他们不要去服从一个由平均数造出来的天堂。”尽管如此，那位从沙漠回来的人说，气氛比前一年好了太多，而那些被扔掉的数字被重新搬回了屋里。他们不会输掉这场论证；在第二张纸印出来之前，它就已经结束了。

### Exercises

- CH064-Q1 [context] What had hardly anyone received?
  - options: the promised training / the second ticket / the frosty morning / the appendix
- CH064-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “压倒性地”?
  - options: overwhelmingly / seemingly / primarily / downward
- CH064-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: compute a figure / compute a friendship / compute holiness / compute a gift
- CH064-Q4 [cloze] The biochemist said a ______ figure will overtake the truth if you let it.
  - options: repetitive / perilous / eternal / frosty
- CH064-Q5 [recall] What second sheet did they decide to publish?
  - options: one for the frosty mornings / one for the sponsors / one for the budget / one for the biochemist

### Answers

- CH064-Q1: the promised training - The promised training had almost never happened.
- CH064-Q2: overwhelmingly - The severity of the cuts was overwhelming.
- CH064-Q3: compute a figure - Compute combines with a figure, a total, or a cost.
- CH064-Q4: repetitive - A repeated number can displace the truth.
- CH064-Q5: one for the frosty mornings - They published a second sheet for lived experience.

## CH065 - Across Borders: A Better Question / 跨越边界：更好的问题

- theme: 旅行、交通与文化交流

**1.** The better question came from a pedestrian at a rural stop, late in the season. “Do you understand what a short walk to the station means here?” she asked. “In the countryside, a walk is not exercise. It is the whole approach to the world.” The team had a fact, a date, and a century of assumption, and none of them answered her.

更好的问题来自一位在乡村车站候车的行人，时令已晚。“你们明白在这里走到车站代表什么吗？”她问，“在乡下，走路不是锻炼。它是对待整个世界的完整方式。”团队有一个事实、一个日期和一百年的假设，而没有一样能回答她。

**2.** The royal reception, the agricultural bulletin, and the local championship had all been covered; the scholarship and the fellowship had not. “A partner is not a trainer,” the sailor said. “A trainee is not a medal.” Their uniform, their tune, and their romance with the city were the background of the report, and the report was flattering to the wrong people.

皇家招待会、农业简报和本地锦标赛都被报道过；奖学金和研究员职位没有。“合伙人不等于教练，”那位水手说，“实习生也不等于奖章。”他们的制服、他们的曲调和他们与这座城市的一段浪漫，是这份报告的背景，而这份报告讨好的是错误的人。

**3.** The dramatic episode, when it came, was a telephone call: the sponsor had abandoned the membership list to a transmitter in the capital. “I speak for the pedestrian,” Aisha said. “He is alone, and he is not a cartoon.” It was a descriptive sentence, not a scholarly one, and it was the best line in the file.

那个戏剧性的插曲，是一通电话：赞助人把会员名单丢给了首都的一台发射机。“我为行人说话，”艾莎说，“他独自一人，而他不是一幅漫画。”这是一句描述性的句子，不是学术性的，却是整份文件里最好的一句。

**4.** The false assumption was that a flourish in the figures meant a flourishing service. “The companionship of the queue is not an instalment plan,” the trainer said. “You cannot steer a village with a spreadsheet.” The reserved young man from the lodge, the last sailor of the old line, and the manager of the reception desk all agreed with him.

那个错误的假设是：数字上的花哨意味着服务兴旺。“队伍的陪伴不是分期付款计划，”教练说，“你无法用一张电子表格引导一个村庄。”客栈里那位腼腆的年轻人、老线路上的最后一位水手，以及前台接待处的经理，都同意他的看法。

**5.** After the meeting, the team stopped writing about colonialism and began writing about the stop itself: who waited there, what they carried, and how long they had been waiting. It was certainly a smaller question, and it was finally the right one. They thanked the pedestrian, ran the numbers again through the month, and put her sentence at the top. After all, the date was far off, and it was likely that the matter would run into another month. Yesterday it had seemed an ordinary affair; today it was a dear one. “We must thank the training, and we must relate the new figure to the old,” she said. It was certainly the better question, and finally they could say so — an answer they had been walking through since the morning of the first day, for a century.

会后，团队不再写殖民主义，而开始写那个车站本身：谁在那里等、他们带着什么、他们等了多久。这当然是个更小的问题，而它终于是对的那个。他们感谢了那位行人，把数字在整月里重新跑了一遍，并把她的那句话放在了开头。毕竟日期还远，而这件事很可能会拖到下一个月。昨天它看起来还是一件平常事；今天它变得贵重了。“我们必须感谢那项培训，也必须把新的数字与旧的数字联系起来，”她说。这当然是那个更好的问题，而他们终于可以说出来了——一个他们从第一天早上起就一直在走进去的答案，走了一个世纪。

### Exercises

- CH065-Q1 [context] What did the pedestrian ask the team to understand?
  - options: what a short walk to the station means in the countryside / how to steer a village / why the sponsor left / when the train arrives
- CH065-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “腼腆的；保留的”?
  - options: reserved / descriptive / scholarly / royal
- CH065-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: abandon a membership list / abandon a tune / abandon a medal / abandon a uniform
- CH065-Q4 [cloze] The trainer said you cannot ______ a village with a spreadsheet.
  - options: steer / flourish / relate / abandon
- CH065-Q5 [recall] What did they put at the top of the report?
  - options: the pedestrian's sentence / the sponsor's letter / the fellowship list / the agricultural bulletin

### Answers

- CH065-Q1: what a short walk to the station means in the countryside - She reframed distance as meaning, not metres.
- CH065-Q2: reserved - The young man from the lodge said little.
- CH065-Q3: abandon a membership list - Abandon combines with a list, a plan, or a person.
- CH065-Q4: steer - He rejected managing a community by spreadsheet.
- CH065-Q5: the pedestrian's sentence - Her sentence opened the rewritten report.

## CH066 - Across Borders: The Second Attempt / 跨越边界：第二次尝试

- theme: 旅行、交通与文化交流

**1.** The second attempt was built on a large sheet of paper with a strict charter beside it. “The committee must explain the plan to the nation in the context of the war,” the technician said. “We have cash for one month, a licence for one line, and a grade of patience that is nearly exhausted.” Their approach had to change, and they realized it together.

第二次尝试建立在一大张纸上，旁边放着一部严格的章程。“委员会必须在战争的背景下向全国解释这个计划，”技师说，“我们有一个月的现金、一条线路的许可，以及一份几乎耗尽的耐心。”他们的做法必须改变，而他们一起意识到了这一点。

**2.** The collective load was carried by a wagon, a tractor, and a portable radio. “A brand is not a blade,” the marine said, “and a grip is not a temple.” A duck on the deck, a candle in the lodge, and a crab in the bucket: the wild harbour had every kind of cargo except a timetable.

集体的负荷由一辆货车、一台拖拉机和一台便携收音机承担。“品牌不是刀刃，”那位海军陆战队员说，“而紧握不是庙宇。”甲板上的一只鸭子、客栈里的一支蜡烛、桶里的一只螃蟹：这个野性的港口拥有各种货物，唯独没有时刻表。

**3.** The extensive survey had one fault: it measured speed and mileage but not the subway ride home. “A pirate on the curb can steal a bicycle faster than we can chart a flood,” the engineer said. “What we need is a bumper and a sheet of plywood, not another report.”

那份广泛的调查有一个缺陷：它测量了速度和英里数，却没有测回家坐地铁的那一段。“路缘上的海盗偷一辆自行车，比我们绘制一场洪水还快，”工程师说，“我们需要的是一个保险杠和一张胶合板，而不是又一份报告。”

**4.** The dove on the roof, the second watch of the night, and the sail of the fishing boat marked the hours. “Bondage is a strong word,” Aisha said, “but a timetable that ignores the elbow of a crowded deck is a kind of bondage.” Their own committee could not explain that sentence to the ministry, so they printed it without explanation.

屋顶上的鸽子、夜里第二班值守，以及渔船的帆标记着时间。“奴役是个很重的词，”艾莎说，“但一张忽视拥挤甲板上手肘的时刻表，就是一种奴役。”他们自己的委员会无法向部里解释这句话，于是他们不加解释地把它印了出来。

**5.** The film of the trial was late, as usual, and the short season was over. They had to obtain one more month, and they had to relate the new plan to the old one. “Speak plainly,” the committee chair said. “Understand that we do not own the road.” The stop, the walk, and the fly on the window were all in the final shot. The approach, the plan, the fact, and the film were all late; the committee had to explain, once more, how they had obtained the licence with their own cash and a short season. “We realize that a royal charter is not a plain licence,” she said. “We must speak to the people who walk and stop at the station.” The season was over, and the second attempt had one month left. A crate of china and a meter of cable were still on the deck.

试行的影片照例迟了，而短暂的季节已经过去。他们必须再争取一个月，并且必须把新计划与旧计划联系起来。“说清楚点，”委员会主席说，“要明白我们并不拥有这条路。”那个车站、那段步行，以及窗上的那只苍蝇，都出现在最后的镜头里。方案、计划、事实和影片全都迟了；委员会不得不再次解释，他们是如何用自己的现金和一个短暂的季节拿到这张许可的。“我们明白，一份皇家特许状不是一张普通许可，”她说，“我们必须去和那些在车站步行、停留的人说话。”季节结束了，第二次尝试只剩下一个月。甲板上还放着一箱瓷器和一米电缆。

### Exercises

- CH066-Q1 [context] What did the technician say the committee must explain?
  - options: the plan in the context of the war / the price of a tractor / the route of the subway / the colour of the charter
- CH066-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “广泛的；大量的”?
  - options: extensive / strict / collective / portable
- CH066-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: carry the load / carry the temple / carry the candle / carry the dove
- CH066-Q4 [cloze] The survey measured speed and mileage but not the ______ ride home.
  - options: subway / wagon / sail / deck
- CH066-Q5 [recall] What did the chair tell them to understand?
  - options: that they do not own the road / that the season is short / that the film is late / that the war is over

### Answers

- CH066-Q1: the plan in the context of the war - The technician demanded a public explanation of the plan.
- CH066-Q2: extensive - The survey was broad but blind to one leg of the journey.
- CH066-Q3: carry the load - Carry combines with a load, a cost, or a risk.
- CH066-Q4: subway - The survey ignored the underground leg of the commute.
- CH066-Q5: that they do not own the road - The chair reminded them who the road belongs to.

## CH067 - Across Borders: Outside Pressure / 跨越边界：外部压力

- theme: 旅行、交通与文化交流

**1.** The outside pressure came from the ambassador of a country that had become famous for its cheap tickets. A banner on the wall read FREEDOM OF THE COAST, and a nationwide sweep had closed the little station by the fence. “A comprehensive review,” she said, “will tell us whether the road can cope.” About half the family had been born inland, and the rest had migrated to the mainland, and not one of them had been asked.

外部压力来自一个以廉价车票出名的大使。墙上一条横幅写着“海岸的自由”，而一场全国性的清扫关掉了栅栏边那个小站。“一份全面的复审，”她说，“会告诉我们这条道路能否应付。”这家人约有一半出生在内陆，其余迁到了大陆，而他们当中没有一个人被问过。

**2.** Meanwhile the tonnage through the port had begun to fluctuate, and the odds of a second war had widened the breadth of every insurance table. A missile on a banner, a cool breeze from the sea, and a towel on the fence of the resort: the place had the makeup of a dull postcard. “Things vanish here,” the head of the local company said. “Slowly, and then all at once.”

与此同时，通过港口的吨位开始波动，而第二场战争的概率拓宽了每一张保险表的宽度。横幅上的一枚导弹、海上吹来的一阵凉风，以及度假地栅栏上的一条毛巾：这个地方有着一张乏味明信片的妆容。“这里的东西会消失，”本地公司的负责人说，“慢慢地，然后一下子全没了。”

**3.** The transportation plan was a leap in the dark: a collaboration between an area office and a great house on the coast. A little car, a large door, and the name of a general on the letterhead gave it authority, and the startle it produced in the village was real. “Nothing about us without us,” the occupant of the second house said, and the ambassador wrote it down.

交通计划是黑暗中的一跃：一个地区办公室与海岸上一座大宅之间的合作。一辆小汽车、一扇大门，以及信头上一位将军的名字给了它权威，而它在村里引起的惊愕是真实的。“没有我们的参与，就不要替我们决定，”第二栋房子的住户说，大使把它记了下来。

**4.** The tremble in her voice was not fear. “The war blurred the map once,” she said. “Do not let a blast of patriotism do it again.” A slipper by the door, an insertion in the local paper, and a broad sweep of the inland road were the only evidence they had, and it was enough to postpone the vote.

她声音里的颤抖不是恐惧。“战争曾经把地图弄模糊过一次，”她说，“不要让一阵爱国主义的爆炸再来一次。”门口的一只拖鞋、本地报纸上的一条插入文字，以及内陆道路上一次广泛的清扫，就是他们仅有的证据，而这已足以推迟表决。

**5.** What became clear was that they had to broaden the road without widening the gap. “Something has to change,” the ambassador said, “and it will not be the family.” The odds of that had faded from likely to possible, and the company had already begun to look for a second site. A crisp breeze, a cool evening, and a small crowd at the door: the pressure had arrived, and it had a name. The slow road home, the homeland behind the fence, and the same small crowd at the door: it was enough.

变得清楚的是：他们必须拓宽道路，却不能加宽那道裂缝。“总得有东西改变，”大使说，“而它不会是家庭。”这种可能性已经从“很可能”褪成了“也许”，而公司已经开始寻找第二个地点。一阵清爽的微风、一个凉爽的傍晚，以及门口的一小群人：压力已经到来，而且它有名字。回家那条缓慢的路、栅栏后面的故乡，以及门口的同一小群人：这些已经足够了。

### Exercises

- CH067-Q1 [context] What had the nationwide sweep closed?
  - options: the little station by the fence / the port / the resort / the mainland road
- CH067-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “吨位”?
  - options: tonnage / breadth / makeup / odds
- CH067-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: cope with the road / cope with a towel / cope with a slipper / cope with a banner
- CH067-Q4 [cloze] The head of the local company said things ______ here, slowly and then all at once.
  - options: vanish / fluctuate / migrate / widen
- CH067-Q5 [recall] What did the ambassador write down?
  - options: nothing about us without us / the tonnage figures / the insurance table / the name of the resort

### Answers

- CH067-Q1: the little station by the fence - A national review closed the small station.
- CH067-Q2: tonnage - The port's tonnage had started to move up and down.
- CH067-Q3: cope with the road - Cope combines with a road, a load, or a demand.
- CH067-Q4: vanish - He described slow erosion followed by sudden loss.
- CH067-Q5: nothing about us without us - The resident's sentence became part of the record.

## CH068 - Across Borders: The Decision / 跨越边界：决定

- theme: 旅行、交通与文化交流

**1.** The decision was made in a national convention hall, with a speaker who had come to follow the case to its end. “Who will handle our path?” a peasant from the intensive farming district asked. “A sentence in a document is not a road.” Perhaps the minister would come, and perhaps she would not; you could understand the room's mood from the empty seat in front.

决定是在一座全国性的大会堂里做出的，一位发言人来此把案子追到底。“谁来管我们这条路？”一位来自集约农业区的农民问，“文件里的一句话不是一条路。”部长也许会来，也许不会；从前面那把空椅子就能读懂屋里的情绪。

**2.** The abuse of privilege, the rejection of the due process, and the hostile rally outside were all part of the same small drama. “We may elect a council,” the speaker said, “or we may resist the whole convention.” They had to translate their preference into a policy, and the polish of the language did not make the choice easier.

对特权的滥用、对正当程序的拒绝，以及外面那场充满敌意的集会，都属于同一出小戏。“我们可以选出一个委员会，”发言人说，“也可以抵制整个大会。”他们必须把自己的偏好翻译成政策，而语言的修饰并没有让选择更容易。

**3.** A sociable ambassador, a nationalist with a broad accent, and a container of harvest samples sat in the front row. “A privilege is derived from a duty,” she said, “and our duty is to the coast.” A kilogram of rice, a cushion on the chair, and a strap on a suitcase: the props of a formal argument about who belongs.

一位爱交际的大使、一位口音很宽的民族主义者，以及一箱收成样本坐在前排。“特权源自义务，”她说，“而我们的义务在海岸。”一公斤大米、椅子上的一只靠垫、行李箱上的一条带子：一场关于谁属于这里的正式论辩的道具。

**4.** The Olympics broadcast had been interrupted by the news; the war had killed the broadcast entirely. “Small children swim here in May,” a woman said. “Perhaps they will swim in September.” The infrastructure of the little resort had begun to drift, and no minister would take the toll on the road.

奥运转播被新闻打断；战争彻底杀死了转播。“小孩子们五月在这里游泳，”一位女士说，“也许九月他们还会来。”这个小度假地的基础设施已经开始漂移，而没有哪位部长愿意承担道路的通行费。

**5.** The decision, when it came, was to originate a service rather than inherit one, and to worship nothing but the timetable itself. “They will immigrate here for the speed,” the speaker said, “not for our sentiments.” It was late, the trains had stopped, and the work of the next year was already written on the board. The regard of the country had become a job in itself. “The fact is,” the speaker said, “that a national hall can hold a thousand people and still miss the small stop where you get off.” A broad path, a due process, and a preference for the harvest over the rally: the decision would follow the facts, not the crowd.

决定最终是：开创一项服务，而不是继承一项；除了时刻表本身，不崇拜任何东西。“他们会为了速度移居到这里，”发言人说，“而不是为我们的情绪。”天已经晚了，火车停了，而下一年的工作已经写在黑板上。这个国家的关注本身已经成了一份工作。“事实是，”发言人说，“一座全国性的大厅能容纳一千人，却仍然会漏掉你下车的那个小站。”一条宽阔的路、一道正当的程序，以及对收成而非集会的偏爱：决定将跟随事实，而不是人群。

### Exercises

- CH068-Q1 [context] What did the peasant say a sentence in a document is not?
  - options: a road / a convention / a privilege / a harvest
- CH068-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “特权”?
  - options: privilege / preference / rejection / rally
- CH068-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: resist a convention / resist a cushion / resist a kilogram / resist a harvest
- CH068-Q4 [cloze] The speaker said a privilege is ______ from a duty.
  - options: derived / elected / translated / broadcast
- CH068-Q5 [recall] What did the group decide to do?
  - options: originate a service rather than inherit one / worship the old timetable / elect a new minister / abandon the coast

### Answers

- CH068-Q1: a road - He refused to accept a written sentence as infrastructure.
- CH068-Q2: privilege - The debate turned on privilege derived from duty.
- CH068-Q3: resist a convention - Resist combines with a convention, a pressure, or a change.
- CH068-Q4: derived - She argued that privilege comes from obligation.
- CH068-Q5: originate a service rather than inherit one - They chose to build a new service instead of taking over an old one.

## CH069 - Across Borders: Consequences / 跨越边界：后果

- theme: 旅行、交通与文化交流

**1.** The consequences arrived on every way into the city: the railway, the motorway, the freeway, and the expressway. After a century of iron and steam, the train had become a tourist attraction and the bus a last resort. “Our second fleet is a fleet of excuses,” the pilot said, and the crew laughed without much ease.

后果随着每一条进城的路到来：铁路、汽车道、免费高速和快速路。经过一个世纪的铁与蒸汽之后，火车成了一种旅游景点，而公交车成了最后的手段。“我们的第二支车队是一支借口组成的车队，”飞行员说，机组人员笑得并不轻松。

**2.** A commuter on the morning shuttle, a rider with a trailer, and a swimmer from the north all waited in the same lane. A shipment of dirt, a haul of timber, and a drill for the tunnel: the transport of goods had become more certain than the transport of people. “We cannot pave a passage with a constitution,” the foreigner said.

早晨穿梭班车上的一位通勤者、一位带着拖车的骑手，以及一位来自北方的游泳者，都在同一条车道上等待。一车泥土、一趟木材运输和一台隧道钻机：货物的运输已经比人的运输更可靠。“我们不能用一部宪法去铺一条通道，”那位外国人说。

**3.** The migrant workers watched the flight board with the ease of people who had done it before. “The railway is a window,” the station master said, “and a window is not a door.” A steamer at the post, a ferry in the bay, and a fleet of fishing boats gave the harbour its roar, but the shipping schedule was still written by hand.

外来务工者们看着航班信息板，带着做过很多次的人才有的从容。“铁路是一扇窗，”站长说，“而窗不是门。”邮局码头的一艘汽船、海湾里的一艘渡轮，以及一支渔船队给了港口它的轰鸣，可航运时刻表仍然是手写的。

**4.** The tourist season brought an excursion, a tour, and a crowd of passengers with cameras. The runner, the pilot, and the crew of the small aviation club had all signed the offer; the large part of the work, as always, fell on the commuters themselves. A foreigner with a return ticket and a century of patience asked the only question that mattered: who continues the line?

旅游季带来了一次远足、一场观光和一群带着相机的乘客。跑者、飞行员和小型航空俱乐部的机组都签了那份报价；而大部分工作，一如既往，落在通勤者自己身上。一位拿着回程票、有着一个世纪耐心的外国人，问出了唯一要紧的问题：谁来把这条线继续下去？

**5.** After the vote, the company continued to run the shuttle and the trailer service, and the importation of spare parts went on. Behind the station, the crew repaired a wagon with iron and hope. Our second attempt would follow the same way, and the watch on the platform was the same as the one in the office. The highway, the expressway, the trunk road, and the railroad all came to the same small nation of travellers. “It is possible that a national service will never pay,” the pilot said, “and it is certain that a war of schedules will.” The fleet, the freeway, and the freight yard: the whole apparatus of movement was built for goods, and the people came second.

表决之后，公司继续运营穿梭车和拖车服务，备件的进口也在继续。车站后面，机组用铁和希望修理着一节车厢。我们的第二次尝试会沿着同一条路走，而站台上的那块表，和办公室里的那块是同一块。高速公路、快速路、干道和铁路，都通向同一个由旅人组成的小国。“全国性的服务也许永远不会盈利，”飞行员说，“而一场时刻表之间的战争一定会。”车队、免费高速和货运场：整套运输机器都是为货物造的，人排在第二。

### Exercises

- CH069-Q1 [context] What had the train become after a century of iron and steam?
  - options: a tourist attraction / a freight line / a freeway / a ferry
- CH069-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “通勤者”?
  - options: commuter / rider / runner / migrant
- CH069-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: pave a passage / pave a constitution / pave an excursion / pave a shipment
- CH069-Q4 [cloze] The station master said the railway only lets you look: you can see the city but never enter it, because glass is not a door. What did he compare the railway to?
  - options: a window / a lane / a drill / a trailer
- CH069-Q5 [recall] What question did the foreigner ask?
  - options: who continues the line / when the steamer leaves / how much the dirt costs / where the ferry docks

### Answers

- CH069-Q1: a tourist attraction - The working railway had turned into a curiosity.
- CH069-Q2: commuter - Commuters carried most of the daily burden.
- CH069-Q3: pave a passage - Pave combines with a passage, a road, or a way.
- CH069-Q4: window - He distinguished seeing a route from entering it.
- CH069-Q5: who continues the line - The foreigner asked about continuity, not comfort.

## CH070 - Across Borders: The Bridge Forward / 跨越边界：通向下一程

- theme: 旅行、交通与文化交流

**1.** The fifth arc closed at a bridge, not at a station. A formal presentation had been drafted for the church hall in the north, and a second one for the south; a cable, a sign, and a badge on the table were all the gear they had. “The medium is the trip,” Aisha said, “and the trip is the document.”

第五段弧线结束在一座桥上，而不是一个车站。一份正式的陈述已经为北方的教堂大厅起草，另一份为南方；桌上的一根电缆、一块标牌和一枚徽章，就是他们全部的装备。“媒介就是旅程，”艾莎说，“而旅程就是文件。”

**2.** The mission had become an associate: a small organization for the protection of passengers, with a guest list, a reservation book, and a seat for every complaint. “You cannot explain a bridge to someone who has never bent under a load,” the old cowboy said. His only hobby was the railway, and his heel had worn a trace in the aisle of the carriage.

使命已经变成了一位伙伴：一个保护乘客的小组织，有一份来宾名单、一本预订簿，以及为每一条抱怨准备的一个席位。 “你无法向一个从未在重压下弯过腰的人解释一座桥，”那位老牛仔说。他唯一的爱好就是铁路，而他的脚跟已在车厢的过道上磨出了一道痕迹。

**3.** What happened next was a migration of habits rather than of people: the cruise passengers learned the ferry, and the commuters learned the shuttle. “We leave nothing behind but a log,” Maya said. “And a log is a way of saying: we were here, in the night, at this window.”

接下来发生的，是习惯的迁移而不是人的迁移：邮轮乘客学会了渡轮，通勤者学会了穿梭车。“我们除了日志什么也不留下，”曼雅说，“而日志是一种说法：我们来过，在夜里，在这扇窗前。”

**4.** The repair of the cabin took a week. A swallow nested under the bay window, a tender note arrived from the associate in the country, and a draft of the new timetable was pinned to the aisle wall. “We receive more than we create,” Lin wrote. “That is why the position matters.”

船舱的修理花了一周。一只燕子在凸窗下筑了巢，一封温柔的信从乡下的伙伴那里寄来，新时刻表的草稿钉在过道墙上。“我们接受的比创造的多，”林伟写道，“这就是位置为什么重要。”

**5.** Around the bridge, the town had begun to endure its own success: a trail for tourists, a rest stop, and a forward step that nobody had planned. “About a year from now,” the associate said, “somebody will say we did it like that on purpose. We must explain what really happened.” It was, in the end, the same promise as before, moved one bridge further along. The church by the bay had a god in its window and a finish to its stone that no repair could copy; the aisle, the seat, and the tender farewell were all part of the same formal goodbye. The mission was finished, and the associate would keep the log. A hobby became a badge, and a badge became a promise. A gender balance in the crew, a transit pass for every guest, and a plan to initiate a second trail: these were the last three items on the draft. A border is not a line on the ground; it is the distance between what you meant and what arrived.

桥的周围，小镇已经开始承受自己的成功：一条给游客的小径、一处休息站，以及一个没人计划过的向前一步。“大约一年之后，”那位伙伴说，“会有人说我们当初就是这样设计的。我们必须解释真正发生了什么。”说到底，这和从前是同一个承诺，只是又向前挪过了一座桥。海湾边的那座教堂，窗里有一位神，它的石头有一种任何修补都复制不来的收尾；过道、座位和那句温柔的告别，都属于同一个正式的告别。使命完成了，而那位伙伴会保存日志。一个爱好变成了徽章，而一枚徽章变成了承诺。船员中的性别平衡、给每位客人的一张通行证，以及启动第二条小径的计划：草稿上的最后三项。国界不是地上的一条线；它是你想说的和你送到的之间的距离。

### Exercises

- CH070-Q1 [context] Where did the fifth arc close?
  - options: at a bridge / at a station / in a church hall / on a cruise ship
- CH070-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “坚持；忍耐”?
  - options: endure / bend / swallow / initiate
- CH070-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: draft a presentation / draft a swallow / draft a badge / draft a bay
- CH070-Q4 [cloze] Aisha said the medium is the trip and the trip is the ______.
  - options: document / badge / cable / aisle
- CH070-Q5 [recall] What must the associate explain, about a year from now?
  - options: what really happened / how to bend a cable / where the church is / who owns the bridge

### Answers

- CH070-Q1: at a bridge - The arc ends on a bridge rather than at a station.
- CH070-Q2: endure - The town had begun to endure its own success.
- CH070-Q3: draft a presentation - Draft combines with a presentation, a letter, or a plan.
- CH070-Q4: document - She treated the journey itself as the record.
- CH070-Q5: what really happened - He wanted the accidental history recorded honestly.

## CH071 - The Cost of Always Being Busy: A Signal / 忙碌的代价：一个信号

- theme: 健康、运动与生活习惯

**1.** They had crossed a border for a timetable; now the deadline was inside one body. The sixth arc opened with a crisis that nobody had anticipated: Daniel collapsed on the last step of the sports center ladder. An athlete of national standard, a scout for a professional club, and a sportswoman who trained with him three times a week all said the same thing: he had been eating the food of one nap and sleeping four hours. “It was not a weapon,” the doctor said. “It was a situation.”

他们曾为一张时刻表跨越国界；而这一次，期限在一具身体里面。第六段弧线以一个没人预料到的危机开始：丹尼尔倒在体育中心梯子的最后一级上。一位国家级运动员、一位职业俱乐部的星探，以及一位每周和他一起训练三次的女运动员都说了同样的话：他吃的只够一次小睡，睡四个小时。“这不是武器，”医生说，“这是一种处境。”

**2.** I hope this becomes a lesson, not a story. The concept of improvement had been pressed on him by everyone: a bunch of small gains, a particle at a time, a dosage of training that would make him curable and quick. “You cannot persuade a body with emphasis,” the doctor said. “You can only assist it.” A cosmic disturbance, a whale, a famine, and the strike of the grocers were the metaphors the team invented to explain the delay. Make it simple, they said.

我希望这成为一堂课，而不是一个故事。“进步”这个概念被所有人压在他身上：一束小小的收获、一次一粒、让他痊愈而敏捷的一剂训练。“你无法用强调说服一具身体，”医生说，“你只能协助它。”一场宇宙扰动、一头鲸、一场饥荒，以及食品商的罢市，是团队为了解释这次延误而发明的比喻。他们说，把它讲简单些。

**3.** The reunion of the old rowing crew was still a week away, and the refrigerator in the common room was empty. Follow the training plan, the coach said; take the lift, send the form, and bring the ladder back. Who would make the first step? Our answer came from the church clinic, an hour north of the campus, where an inhabitant of the old town had starved himself into the same ward.

老赛艇队的重聚还有一周，而公共休息室的冰箱是空的。按训练计划来，教练说；乘电梯、寄表格、把梯子拿回来。谁来迈出第一步？我们的答案来自校园以北一小时的那间教堂诊所，那里有位老城的居民把自己饿进了同一间病房。

**4.** The disturbance was measurable: a diameter of two centimetres in the ankle, a pulse of a hundred and ten, and a weight that had dropped farther than any of them had guessed. “You cannot escape a body,” the doctor said. “You can only involve it in the plan.” A disabled swimmer and a surfer with a bad chin had both been through the same ward, and both had come back through the same door.

这场扰乱是可以测量的：脚踝直径两厘米、脉搏一百一十，以及一个比他们任何人猜的都掉得更远的体重。“你无法逃离身体，”医生说，“你只能把它纳入计划。”一位残疾游泳者和一位下巴受伤的冲浪者都待过同一间病房，也都从同一扇门走了回来。

**5.** The last item on the sheet was the hardest: the sportsman who could not rest, the god of the timetable, the over-full week. “Training is not a punishment,” Daniel said at last. “Kill that idea and I will follow the plan.” An athlete had made the promise; a person would have to keep it. The question was not whether anyone could work harder; it was whether the change could take place at all. Nothing could induce him to sleep, and the week was over before the plan existed.

清单上最后一项最难：那个无法休息的运动员、时刻表的神、排满的一周。“训练不是惩罚，”丹尼尔终于说，“把这个想法杀掉，我就按计划来。”一位运动员做出了承诺；而要守住它的，得是一个人。问题不是有没有人能更努力；问题是这种改变到底能不能发生。没有什么能让他入睡，而那一周在计划形成之前就已经结束了。

### Exercises

- CH071-Q1 [context] Where did Daniel collapse?
  - options: on the last step of the sports center ladder / in the church clinic / at the rowing reunion / in the refrigerator room
- CH071-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “剂量；用量”?
  - options: dosage / diameter / disturbance / emphasis
- CH071-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: make a promise / make a whale / make a famine / make a ladder
- CH071-Q4 [cloze] The doctor said that training is not a punishment and that a body cannot be ______ with emphasis.
  - options: persuaded / anticipated / starved / assisted
- CH071-Q5 [recall] What did the team invent metaphors for?
  - options: the delay / the reunion / the refrigerator / the sportswoman

### Answers

- CH071-Q1: on the last step of the sports center ladder - He collapsed where he trained every week.
- CH071-Q2: dosage - He had been given a dose of training his body could not carry.
- CH071-Q3: make a promise - Make combines with a promise, a plan, or a mistake.
- CH071-Q4: persuaded - The doctor insisted that the body must be assisted, not argued with.
- CH071-Q5: the delay - They invented metaphors to explain why the work had slipped.

## CH072 - The Cost of Always Being Busy: The First Promise / 忙碌的代价：第一次承诺

- theme: 健康、运动与生活习惯

**1.** The first promise was made in the clinic canteen, over a plate of salad and a bowl of soup. “Make the site accessible, or we stop the run,” Maya said. “That is the immediate step.” The custom in that place was to bring your own sauce, and the sweet vinegar in a little jar became, in their argument, a way of speaking about the degree of trouble they were in.

第一个承诺是在诊所食堂做出的，面前是一盘沙拉和一碗汤。“把场地变得可达，否则我们就停下这次跑步，”曼雅说，“这就是眼前这一步。”那个地方的习俗是自己带酱汁，而小罐里那点甜醋，在他们的争论中成了谈论自己麻烦程度的一种方式。

**2.** The situation was simple: the incidence of ankle injuries had doubled, the tray of bandages was always empty, and the physiotherapist had restricted the training to short sessions. “Take the wrist and the ankle seriously,” she said. “A bump ignored becomes a deficiency.” Far from being late, the intervention was exactly on time.

处境很简单：脚踝受伤的发生率翻了一倍，绷带托盘总是空的，理疗师把训练限制成了短时段。“把手腕和脚踝当回事，”她说，“被忽视的肿块会变成缺陷。”这次干预远不算晚，恰恰正及时。

**3.** They sent a form to the management and involved the orchestra of the sports club in a formation that took a quantitative view: an ounce of prevention, a pinch of salt, a gallon of water. “Understand the derivation,” the coach said. “An approximation is not a plan.” The sticky floor of the changing room was the first item on the list.

他们向管理层寄了一份表格，并让体育俱乐部的管弦乐队以一种定量的阵型参与进来：一盎司预防、一撮盐、一加仑水。“要理解它的推导，”教练说，“近似值不是计划。”更衣室黏糊糊的地板，是清单上的第一项。

**4.** The interference came from a dietary habit nobody wanted to discuss: the players chewed fast, ate standing, and stopped only when the whistle blew. “Dumb animals eat better than we do,” the goalkeeper said, and the pigeon on the windowsill agreed. The ending of the old diet had to be an outbreak of honesty, not a slogan.

干扰来自一个没人愿意谈的饮食习惯：队员嚼得快、站着吃、只在哨响时才停。“哑巴动物吃得比我们好，”守门员说，窗台上的鸽子也同意。旧饮食的终结必须是一场诚实的爆发，而不是一句口号。

**5.** The promised improvements were small and regular: a regularity of meals, a disposal of the old rules, a shrink in the menu, and the flesh of two oranges a day. “Bring the ladder and take the walk,” Daniel said. “I understand the way now.” It was a ruinous habit they were fighting, and they would fight it through an Olympiad of small mornings. The fact was that the club had lost contact with its own members. An intrusion of sponsors into the medical room, a sweet sauce on every plate, and a bowl of salad that nobody ate: the culture of the place had to change. “Take the tray and send the message,” she said. “Situation by situation, that is the only way.”

承诺的改善既小又有规律：用餐的规律、旧规则的处置、菜单的收缩，以及每天两个橙子的果肉。“把梯子拿来，去走一走，”丹尼尔说，“我现在明白这条路了。”他们要对抗的是一种毁灭性的习惯，而要对抗它，得靠一场由无数个小早晨组成的奥林匹克。事实是，俱乐部已经和自己的成员失去了联系。赞助商闯入医务室、每只盘子上都有一勺甜酱，以及一碗没人吃的沙拉：这个地方的文化必须改变。“把托盘拿起来，把消息发出去，”她说，“一个处境一个处境地来，这是唯一的办法。”

### Exercises

- CH072-Q1 [context] What did Maya say was the immediate step?
  - options: make the site accessible or stop the run / buy a new tray / hire an orchestra / restrict the menu
- CH072-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “缺乏；不足”?
  - options: deficiency / incidence / disposal / derivation
- CH072-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: restrict the training / restrict the pigeon / restrict the vinegar / restrict the orchestra
- CH072-Q4 [cloze] The coach said an approximation is not a ______.
  - options: plan / bump / jar / dose
- CH072-Q5 [recall] What was the first item on the list?
  - options: the sticky floor of the changing room / the empty bandage tray / the orchestra formation / the pigeon on the sill

### Answers

- CH072-Q1: make the site accessible or stop the run - She tied the running programme to accessibility.
- CH072-Q2: deficiency - An ignored injury becomes a real deficiency.
- CH072-Q3: restrict the training - Restrict combines with training, access, or a budget.
- CH072-Q4: plan - He rejected guesswork as a substitute for planning.
- CH072-Q5: the sticky floor of the changing room - The simplest hazard came first.

## CH073 - The Cost of Always Being Busy: A Missing Voice / 忙碌的代价：缺席的声音

- theme: 健康、运动与生活习惯

**1.** The missing voice arrived at the second step of the sports center, from an obese man who played badminton twice a week in the courtyard. “Your typical athlete is not the typical inhabitant,” he said. “I am not asking for splendor. I am asking for adequacy.” His genetic inheritance, he added, had given him a knee that hurt in the moist air and a dental bill he could not satisfy.

那个缺席的声音出现在体育中心的第二步台阶上，来自一位每周在院子里打两次羽毛球的肥胖男子。“你们的典型运动员不是典型的居民，”他说，“我不是在要求壮丽，我要求的是足够。”他补充说，他的遗传给了他一个在潮湿空气里会疼的膝盖，以及一笔他付不起的牙科账单。

**2.** The interpretation of the word health, he said, was doing the damage. “You interpret well-being as a personal project, and then you amuse yourselves with slogans.” A decorative bullet, a monumental poster, and a guitar in the corner of the auditorium were the whole of the previous campaign. The ugliness of it, he said, was the exclusion underneath.

他说，对“健康”这个词的诠释正在造成伤害。“你们把福祉解释为个人项目，然后用口号自娱。”一颗装饰性的子弹、一张纪念碑式的海报，以及礼堂角落里的一把吉他，就是上一场运动的全部。他说，它的丑陋之处在于底下那层排斥。

**3.** He asked them to donate one hour a week to a boxing class for people his size, and to notify the management that the fatality figures in the report were not an exaggeration. A westerner’s diet, a compensatory snack, and a cute cartoon about surfing had been used as evidence for a claim that no one could heal.

他请他们每周捐出一小时，为他这样体型的人开一堂拳击课，并通知管理层：报告里的死亡率数字不是夸张。一个西方人的饮食、一份补偿性的零食，以及一部关于冲浪的可爱动画，曾被用作一个没人能治愈的主张的证据。

**4.** The fuss about his weight, he said, was a distraction from a fatal assumption: that a person who cannot run has nothing to teach a club. The acting coach, the second site, and the large financial grant all arrived with the same prejudice built in. “Accustom yourselves to the slow,” he said. “Populate the class with people who are not like you.”

他说，对他体重的大惊小怪，掩盖了一个致命的假设：一个跑不动的人没有东西可以教给俱乐部。代理教练、第二处场地，以及那笔巨额资助，都带着同一种偏见到来。“让你们自己习惯于慢，”他说，“让班上坐满和你不一样的人。”

**5.** The rest of the arc would follow his advice: a lunar calendar for the classes, a recovery step for every injury, and a north-lit room for the winter sessions. His life expectancy, he told them, was no shorter than theirs if the war against his own body stopped. He watched them from the south corner, and nobody in the room could answer him. “Do not let the big room disappear,” he advised. “A helpful class is not a comedy, and a slogan is nonsense unless it can compensate for a missing ramp.” He asked who would inhabit the courtyard in the winter, and whether a westerner could ever fit a segmental shift. The management, the financial office, and the large grant all heard him; them he trusted least of all. The church, the god of the timetable, the nation of the slow, and the north wind: or something like it, he said.

这段弧线的其余部分会遵循他的建议：为课程设一份按月历、为每次受伤设一步恢复、为冬季课程设一间朝北透光的房间。他告诉他们，如果停止对自己身体的这场战争，他的预期寿命不会比他们短。他从南边的角落看着他们，屋里没有人能回答他。“不要让那间大屋子消失，”他劝告说，“一堂有用的课不是一场喜剧，而一句口号是废话，除非它能补偿一段缺失的坡道。”他问，冬天谁会来用那个院子，以及一个西方人是否适应得了一种分段轮班。管理层、财务办公室和那笔巨额资助都听到了他；而他们是他最不信任的。教堂、时刻表的神、慢的国家，以及北风：或者类似的东西，他说。

### Exercises

- CH073-Q1 [context] What did the man say he was asking for?
  - options: adequacy, not splendour / a new badminton court / a dental grant / a faster class
- CH073-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “排除；除外”?
  - options: exclusion / interpretation / exaggeration / expectancy
- CH073-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: donate an hour / donate a bullet / donate a guitar / donate a courtyard
- CH073-Q4 [cloze] He said a person who cannot run still has something to ______ a club.
  - options: teach / donate / notify / amuse
- CH073-Q5 [recall] What did he advise them to accustom themselves to?
  - options: the slow / the decorative / the monumental / the moist

### Answers

- CH073-Q1: adequacy, not splendour - He wanted a usable class, not an impressive one.
- CH073-Q2: exclusion - The campaign's real problem was who it left out.
- CH073-Q3: donate an hour - Donate combines with time, money, or equipment.
- CH073-Q4: teach - He rejected the idea that fitness is the only qualification.
- CH073-Q5: the slow - He asked them to get used to a slower pace.

## CH074 - The Cost of Always Being Busy: Evidence on the Table / 忙碌的代价：摆上桌面的证据

- theme: 健康、运动与生活习惯

**1.** The evidence meeting took an hour and was held in a local office with a door onto the street. At the middle of the table lay a solar lamp, an experimental sock, and a brass bolt from a hospital hatch. “Take the metric seriously,” the doctor said. “A centimeter on a knee is a kilometer on a career.” A car brought two more people from the area, and the business of the day began.

证据会议开了一个小时，地点是一间临街有门的本地办公室。桌子中央放着一盏太阳能灯、一只实验用袜子和一枚来自医院舱门的黄铜门闩。“把度量当回事，”医生说，“膝盖上一厘米，就是职业生涯上一公里。”一辆汽车从该区又带来两个人，这一天的事务开始了。

**2.** The heritage of the district was a prejudice about bodies: a productive worker, a marginal patient. “Send the forecast to the local industry,” Maya said. “Exclusion is not a precaution.” A bolt of sunlight through the window fell on the toe of a boot, and the watchful nurse wrote down the hour.

这个地区的遗产是一种关于身体的偏见：有生产力的工人、边缘的病人。“把预报发给本地产业，”曼雅说，“排斥不是预防。”一束阳光穿过窗户落在靴子的脚趾上，警觉的护士记下了时间。

**3.** The progression of the disease was reversible if the dependence on the nail of a habit was broken. “Underneath the obesity there is a drunkard's punctuality,” the physiotherapist said. “Periodic fasting, fertile soil, and a refinement of the diet — that is all I ask.” The deadliest thing on the table was the inside of the ledger.

如果能打破对习惯那颗钉子的依赖，病程的进展是可逆的。“在肥胖底下，是一个酒鬼式的守时，”理疗师说，“周期性禁食、肥沃的土壤，以及饮食的精炼——我要的就是这些。”桌上最致命的东西，是那本账簿的内部。

**4.** They measured the foot, the knee, and the sock in millimeters and centimeters, and compared the numbers with the fertility of the club's own records. “Bring the small ones,” the doctor said. “A centimeter is a warning, a millimeter is a fact.” An affluence of nourishment and an inferiority of sleep: the drunkard in the corner had understood it before any of them, and the habit had become the whole question. The alert on the machine was followed by a second alert, and then by a period of quiet.

他们用毫米和厘米量了脚、膝盖和袜子，并把数字与俱乐部自己记录的丰饶程度作比较。“把小数字拿来，”医生说，“一厘米是警告，一毫米是事实。”营养的丰裕与睡眠的不足：角落里的那个酒鬼比他们任何人都更早明白这一点，而习惯已经成了整个问题。机器上的警报响过之后是第二声警报，然后是一段安静的时间。

**5.** The forecast for the season was poor, and the team had to hospitalize nobody that month. “About the span of a life,” the doctor said, “a body is the only country we all live in.” A pedestrian in a car, a shop on the road, and a place by the door: the evidence, at last, was about people who had never been in the room. About the area, the business, and the local industry, the evidence was clear: a country that sends its workers home by car is not a country that walks. The office by the door, the road outside, and the street beyond it were all part of the same measurement. “Make it a habit,” the doctor said, “and take the use of the place seriously.”

这一季的预报很差，而那个月团队没有把任何人送进医院。“大约一生的跨度，”医生说，“身体是我们所有人共同生活的唯一国家。”汽车里的行人、路上的一家店，以及门边的一个位置：证据终于落在了从未进过这间屋子的人身上。关于这个地区、这门生意和本地产业，证据是清楚的：一个用汽车把工人送回家的国家，不是一个走路的国家。门边的那间办公室、外面的那条路，以及它再过去的那条街，都属于同一次测量。“把它变成一个习惯，”医生说，“并且认真对待这个场所的用法。”

### Exercises

- CH074-Q1 [context] What did the doctor say a centimetre on a knee is?
  - options: a kilometre on a career / a millimetre of luck / an hour of rest / a marginal gain
- CH074-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “可逆的”?
  - options: reversible / periodic / watchful / fertile
- CH074-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: break the dependence / break the sunlight / break the sock / break the brass
- CH074-Q4 [cloze] Maya said exclusion is not a ______.
  - options: precaution / forecast / refinement / heritage
- CH074-Q5 [recall] What did the team avoid doing that month?
  - options: hospitalizing anyone / measuring the knee / sending the forecast / buying a solar lamp

### Answers

- CH074-Q1: a kilometre on a career - Small physical damage compounds into lost work.
- CH074-Q2: reversible - The progression could be turned back if the habit changed.
- CH074-Q3: break the dependence - Break combines with a dependence, a habit, or a rule.
- CH074-Q4: precaution - She rejected exclusion dressed up as safety.
- CH074-Q5: hospitalizing anyone - No one needed hospital care that month.

## CH075 - The Cost of Always Being Busy: The Trial Run / 忙碌的代价：第一次试行

- theme: 健康、运动与生活习惯

**1.** The trial run began at the clinic with a physical test and a commitment: one month, three sessions a week, and a written log. “Your body is not a bank,” the doctor said. “You cannot borrow sleep at a deficit and pay it back with an egg.” The attitude of the whole team was measured at the door: who was able to come, who came at the hour available, and who came only to be seen. At the counter you could buy a dish of food, but not a rest.

试行在诊所开始，先做体能测试，再立一项承诺：一个月、每周三次、写一份日志。“你的身体不是银行，”医生说，“你不能在赤字状态下借来睡眠，然后用一个鸡蛋还回去。”整个团队的态度在门口就被测量了：谁能来、谁在可用的时间来了、谁只是为了被看见而来。柜台上你可以买到一盘食物，却买不到休息。

**2.** The movement of the class was slow and open. A national coach, a physiotherapist, and a student with a disability designed a session for a comfortable range of motion. “Pain is information,” she said. “Fatigue is a bill.” The strain on the knee, the tension in the belt, and the weakness in the posture were all written down, and the pressure to return too early was noted beside them.

课程的动作缓慢而开放。一位国家级教练、一位理疗师和一位有残疾的学生共同为一舒适的活动幅度设计了训练。“疼痛是信息，”她说，“疲劳是账单。”膝盖上的劳损、腰带处的紧张、姿势里的虚弱都被记录了下来，而“过早复出”的压力被记在旁边。

**3.** The mental part was harder. The absence of sleep had produced a reaction that no one had predicted: a short temper, a fixation on weight, and a hunting instinct for someone to blame. “You are ill if you cannot rest,” the doctor said. “That is not a weakness of character.” A butterfly on the window and a frog in the yard were the only patients who did not argue.

心理的部分更难。睡眠的缺席产生了一种没人预见到的反应：脾气变短、对体重执念，以及一种想找个人来怪罪的猎捕本能。“如果你无法休息，你就是病了，”医生说，“这不是性格上的弱点。”窗上的一只蝴蝶和院子里的一只青蛙，是仅有的两个不争辩的病人。

**4.** The accommodation was a room in the attic of the sports center, with a vessel of water and a colony of pigeons above the stadium. “You may stay here,” the manager said, “but maybe you should go home.” Perhaps the strain was ours, not his. Our task was to make the training tiring in a good way, and to keep the exertion inside a tolerable range.

住处是体育中心阁楼上的一个房间，有一只水罐，以及运动场上方的一群鸽子。“你可以住在这里，”经理说，“但也许你该回家。”也许这份劳损是我们自己的，不是他的。我们的任务是把训练做得累得其所，并把用力保持在可承受的范围内。

**5.** The connexion between the mouth and the mind was the lesson of the month: what he said about his own pain was not what he felt. Across the four weeks, the numbers told a story of improvised rest, a colon of small statistics, and one injury that could have been prevented. Together they wrote the second protocol, and the first line was: follow the foot, not the clock. The stress of the schedule and the worried faces at the door told the same story; the mentality of “more is better” was the hardest habit to face, in a group or alone. They had to be tolerant with the people who came late, and firm with the plan.

嘴与心之间的联系，是这个月的教训：他对自己疼痛的叙述，并不等于他的感受。四周下来，数字讲述了一个临时凑合的休息、一列小统计，以及一次本可预防的受伤。他们一起写下了第二版方案，第一行是：跟着脚走，不要跟着钟走。时间表的压力与门口那些担忧的面孔讲的是同一个故事；“多多益善”的心态是最难面对的习惯，无论在一个群体里还是独自一人。他们必须对迟到的人宽容，而对计划坚决。

### Exercises

- CH075-Q1 [context] What did the doctor say the body is not?
  - options: a bank / a clinic / a stadium / a vessel
- CH075-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “疲劳”?
  - options: fatigue / posture / exertion / tension
- CH075-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: prevent an injury / prevent a butterfly / prevent an attic / prevent a colon
- CH075-Q4 [cloze] The doctor said that being unable to rest is an illness, not a weakness of ______.
  - options: character / posture / motion / attitude
- CH075-Q5 [recall] What was the first line of the second protocol?
  - options: follow the foot, not the clock / borrow sleep from the bank / keep the exertion tolerable / write the log every hour

### Answers

- CH075-Q1: a bank - He warned against borrowing rest and repaying it later.
- CH075-Q2: fatigue - She described fatigue as a bill that arrives later.
- CH075-Q3: prevent an injury - Prevent combines with an injury, an error, or a delay.
- CH075-Q4: character - The doctor separated illness from moral weakness.
- CH075-Q5: follow the foot, not the clock - The new protocol followed the body rather than the schedule.

## CH076 - The Cost of Always Being Busy: Pressure Builds / 忙碌的代价：压力上升

- theme: 健康、运动与生活习惯

**1.** The pressure built in the morning, after the club established a new condition for the season: every athlete would give two hours a week to the open class. “In this shop we do not pay for good health,” the manager said. “We build it.” The food in the canteen changed, the drug cabinet was locked, and the defense of the old timetable finally collapsed.

压力在早晨累积起来，此前俱乐部为这个赛季确立了一个新条件：每位运动员每周为公开课贡献两小时。“在这家商店里，我们不为健康付钱，”经理说，“我们把它建起来。”食堂的食物变了，药柜上了锁，而旧时刻表的防线终于崩塌。

**2.** The worry was the bird in the hand: a pledge to play in the spring, a meal plan that attracted nobody, and an ideal diet written by somebody who had never been hungry. The suggestion of a bath and a basin of warm water produced a relief that no formula could. Immigration, infection, and consumption were the three old words on the wall, and nobody in the century had liked them.

担忧就像手里的一只鸟：一份春天参赛的誓言、一份谁都吸引不了的餐食计划，以及一份从未挨过饿的人写出的理想饮食。一次沐浴和一小盆温水的建议，产生了任何公式都带不来的舒缓。移民、传染与痨病是墙上的三个旧词，而这个世纪里没人喜欢过它们。

**3.** The symptom that mattered was silence. “A constitutional weakness is not a personal failure,” the doctor said. “But an addiction to busyness is a complication of the modern timetable.” The rhythm of the week, the outlook of the committee, and the humor of the physiotherapist all had to change together.

真正要紧的症状是沉默。“体质上的虚弱不是个人的失败，”医生说，“但对忙碌的沉迷，是现代时间表的一种并发症。”一周的节奏、委员会的前景，以及理疗师的幽默，都必须一起改变。

**4.** “You can relieve distress without torture,” she said. “Scissors, a satellite image, and a disciplined hour of sleep will do more than an invasion of advice.” The irrigation of the pitch, the ash in the basin, and the hearty soup were small mercies, and the team counted them.

“你不必用拷问来缓解苦恼，”她说，“一把剪刀、一张卫星图和一个守纪律的睡眠小时，比一场建议的入侵更有用。”球场的灌溉、盆里的灰烬，以及那锅丰盛的汤，都是小小的慈悲，而团队把它们一一记下。

**5.** The pressure did not come only from management. A fixation on numbers, an adaptation of the whole club, and the physiology of digestion were now part of the programme. “We are able to help,” Maya wrote. “We are not able to hurry a body through a century in one season.” The window of the sports center stayed open all winter, and the movement of the class went on. There was no peace in the canteen, and the opposition to the new rule was loud. “The cost is real,” the manager said, “and the way we thank the volunteers is by counting them.” An indication of diabetes in one player, a bank of tests, and a family history of illness made the argument concrete; the word “bank” came back, and this time nobody laughed at it.

压力不只来自管理层。对数字的执念、整个俱乐部的适应，以及消化的生理机制，如今都成了项目的一部分。“我们有能力帮忙，”曼雅写道，“但我们没有能力让一具身体在一个赛季里跑完一个世纪。”体育中心的窗户整个冬天都开着，而课程的动作仍在继续。食堂里没有安宁，对新规定的反对声很大。“代价是真实的，”经理说，“而我们感谢志愿者的方式，就是把他们算进去。”一名球员身上糖尿病的迹象、一组化验，以及一段家族病史，让这场论证变得具体；“银行”这个词又回来了，而这一次没有人笑它。

### Exercises

- CH076-Q1 [context] What new condition did the club establish?
  - options: every athlete gives two hours a week to the open class / every athlete buys a new diet / the canteen closes at dawn / the drug cabinet stays open
- CH076-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “并发症；复杂情况”?
  - options: complication / consumption / adaptation / irrigation
- CH076-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: relieve distress / relieve a satellite / relieve scissors / relieve a basin
- CH076-Q4 [cloze] The doctor said an addiction to busyness is a complication of the modern ______.
  - options: timetable / cabinet / pledge / satellite
- CH076-Q5 [recall] What did Maya say the team was not able to do?
  - options: hurry a body through a century in one season / help at all / change the canteen / open the window

### Answers

- CH076-Q1: every athlete gives two hours a week to the open class - Health became a shared requirement, not a private choice.
- CH076-Q2: complication - She called overwork a complication of modern scheduling.
