- CH076-Q3: relieve distress - Relieve combines with distress, pain, or pressure.
- CH076-Q4: timetable - The doctor blamed the schedule, not the person.
- CH076-Q5: hurry a body through a century in one season - She refused to promise speed the body cannot deliver.

## CH077 - The Cost of Always Being Busy: The Disagreement / 忙碌的代价：分歧

- theme: 健康、运动与生活习惯

**1.** The disagreement started with a question that nobody wanted to ask: whose body pays for the team's ambition? “We have a provision for everything except rest,” Daniel said. “We explain the diet, the play, the condition of the pitch, and the drug rules, and we never mention the actual cost.” His frustration was recent, and it was not only his.

分歧始于一个没人想问的问题：团队雄心由谁的身体来买单？“我们为一切都做了准备，唯独没有为休息做准备，”丹尼尔说，“我们解释了饮食、比赛、场地状况和用药规则，却从不提实际代价。”他的挫败是最近的，而且不只属于他。

**2.** Maya took the other side, and she took it with pleasure. “Independence means the team sets its own step,” she said. “Yes, there is a tendency to exclude the slower ones, and yes, the racial history of this sport is a veil over the whole room. But the preventive work is what we have.” The south end of the field was silent, and the north end was not.

曼雅站在另一边，并且乐在其中。“独立意味着团队自己定步伐，”她说，“是的，有一种排斥较慢者的倾向；是的，这项运动的历史上的种族问题，是罩在整个房间上的一层面纱。但预防性的工作才是我们拥有的东西。”场地南端沉默着，北端没有。

**3.** The impact of the argument was circular: the more they attacked the schedule, the more the schedule reacted. An aggression of small deadlines, a hunger for the next season, and a gun on a poster in the church hall: the frustration of the group was predictable, and so was the optimism of the coach.

这场争论的影响是循环的：他们越是攻击那张时间表，时间表反应得越厉害。一连串小截止日期的侵袭、对下一个赛季的饥渴，以及教堂大厅海报上的一把枪：这个群体的挫败是可以预见的，教练的乐观也是。

**4.** The vision of the club had two aspects, and the sight of the two together was tedious. One was the preservation of a team that could win; the other was the refreshment of people who could live. “The proverb is right,” the old physiotherapist said. “A belly full of ambition is still a hungry belly.”

俱乐部的愿景有两个方面，而把两者放在一起看是乏味的。一个是保住一支能赢的队伍；另一个是让能生活的人恢复精神。“谚语说得对，”老理疗师说，“装满雄心的肚子，仍然是一个饥饿的肚子。”

**5.** The realization came slowly: the corrective was not a deletion of the programme but a variation of it. The orbit of the training week had to include the hour nobody trained, and the pleasure of skiing in the winter had to survive the pessimism of the budget office. “The problematic part is not the plan,” she said. “It is the uniformity of the people who wrote it.” The hit came from the numbers: frequent injuries, a worse record than the year before, and an intelligence report from the physiotherapist that nobody had read. “We cannot let one plan prevail over every body,” she said. They explain the team's own food, the drug rules, and the condition of the pitch, but they never ask who pays at the hour of the training. The god of the schedule demanded a defense of every hour, and the negation of rest was its poisonous doctrine; a rosy picture through a telescopic lens is still a picture of the foot that hurts. Give the team its hour back.

领悟来得缓慢：纠偏不是删掉整个方案，而是让它有变化。训练周的轨道必须包含那个没人训练的小时，而冬天滑雪的乐趣必须挺过预算办公室的悲观。“成问题的不是计划，”她说，“而是写计划的人那种千篇一律。”打击来自数字：频繁的受伤、比前一年更差的战绩，以及一份没人读过的理疗师报告。“我们不能让一个计划压过每一具身体，”她说。他们解释队伍自己的饮食、用药规则和场地状况，却从不问在训练的那个小时里是谁在付代价。时间表的神要求为每一个小时辩护，而否定休息就是它有毒的教义；透过望远镜看到的一幅玫瑰色画面，仍然是一只疼痛的脚的画面。把那个小时还给队伍。

### Exercises

- CH077-Q1 [context] What question did Daniel say nobody wanted to ask?
  - options: whose body pays for the team's ambition / who owns the pitch / when the season starts / how much the drug rules cost
- CH077-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “预防的”?
  - options: preventive / corrective / problematic / telescopic
- CH077-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: prevail over pessimism / prevail over a gun / prevail over a proverb / prevail over a veil
- CH077-Q4 [cloze] The physiotherapist said a belly full of ambition is still a ______ belly.
  - options: hungry / rosy / poisonous / tedious
- CH077-Q5 [recall] What did Maya say was the problematic part?
  - options: the uniformity of the people who wrote the plan / the plan itself / the winter skiing / the budget

### Answers

- CH077-Q1: whose body pays for the team's ambition - He put the physical cost at the centre of the dispute.
- CH077-Q2: preventive - She defended prevention over ambition.
- CH077-Q3: prevail over pessimism - Prevail combines with pessimism, an argument, or a difficulty.
- CH077-Q4: hungry - He used the proverb to expose the hidden cost.
- CH077-Q5: the uniformity of the people who wrote the plan - She blamed the sameness of the planners, not the plan.

## CH078 - The Cost of Always Being Busy: What the Numbers Hide / 忙碌的代价：数字隐藏了什么

- theme: 健康、运动与生活习惯

**1.** The numbers hid a district of silent injuries. A consultant brought a systematic review to the council chamber: the edge of the table was worn where the same three people had signed in every day, and the width of the corridor told its own story about who could take part. “Somebody has to make the body a place we send people to,” she said, “not a place we take them from.”

这些数字掩盖了一个由沉默的伤构成的地区。一位顾问把一份系统的复审带进了委员会大厅：桌边同一处被磨旧了，因为每天签到的总是同三个人；而走廊的宽度本身就说明了谁能参与。“总得有人把身体变成一个我们送人去的地方，”她说，“而不是一个我们从中取东西的地方。”

**2.** The chest, the shirt, the cheek, and the forehead of a young athlete were photographed for the report; a surgeon in a separate office read the file with a neutral face. “The horror is not the injury,” the consultant said. “It is the fantasy that a brave person does not need an arrow pointing home.” Her accent was from the north, and her silence after that sentence was intense.

一位年轻运动员的胸口、衬衫、脸颊和额头被拍进了报告；另一位在隔壁办公室的外科医生面无表情地读完档案。“可怕的不是受伤，”顾问说，“而是那种幻想：勇敢的人不需要一支指向家的箭。”她的口音来自北方，而说完那句之后的沉默是强烈的。

**3.** The systematic part of the work was ugly: a triangular diagram, a multiplication of small risks, and a tidy list of alterations to the training week. “Maturity is not durability,” the biologist said. “A young body can withstand a season, and the handicap arrives in the tenth.” The rivalry between the two squads had compelled them to hide a miserable truth.

这项工作的系统性部分是难看的：一张三角形图、一串小风险的相乘，以及一份整洁的训练周改动清单。“成熟不等于耐久，”生物学家说，“年轻的身体能扛过一个赛季，而障碍会在第十年到来。”两支队伍之间的竞争，迫使他们藏起了一个悲惨的事实。

**4.** The numbers were paradoxical and the brilliance of them was misleading. A genius with a systematic mind, a brow full of sweat, and a skirt of afternoon light on the floor: the picture said nothing about the developmental cost of the programme. “Refine the measure,” the consultant said. “Make it perceptive, not just forceful.”

这些数字是悖论式的，而它们的卓越具有误导性。一位思路系统化的天才、一片满是汗水的额头，以及地板上一条裙子似的午后光线：这张照片对项目的发育代价什么也没说。“把度量精炼一下，”顾问说，“让它有洞察力，而不只是有力度。”

**5.** The review concluded with a plea that was not in the data: an abusive schedule, however effective, is a stupid one; the stupidity is not in the numbers, it is in the question we never ask. They sent the report to the council and brought it to the court of public opinion. For the first time in a day, the quietest person in the room was able to make the whole argument in one sentence: a body is not a bank. A separate list, a systematic edge, and a district of silent injuries: the council, the court, and the day itself could make room for what the numbers had hidden. He had to bring the place, take the move, and send the report again. Might was not the same as a complicated machine, and they had to go back to the number that nobody had taken. “The heart of it is simple,” she said. “If the plan does not involve the slowest person in the district, it is not a plan.”

这份复审以一句不在数据里的请求结尾：一份滥用的时间表，无论多有效，都是愚蠢的；愚蠢不在数字里，而在我们从不问的那个问题里。他们把报告寄给了委员会，也把它带到了舆论的法庭。那一天里第一次，屋里最安静的人能用一句话说出整个论证：身体不是银行。一份单独的清单、一处系统性的边缘、一个沉默受伤的片区：委员会、法庭，以及日子本身，都可以为数字所掩盖的东西腾出位置。他必须把地方带来、把行动接过来，并把报告再寄一次。力量不等于一台复杂的机器，而他们必须回到那个没人取用的数字。“它的核心很简单，”她说，“如果这个计划不包含这个片区里最慢的那个人，它就不是一个计划。”

### Exercises

- CH078-Q1 [context] What did the consultant say the body should be?
  - options: a place we send people to, not take them from / a bank / a separate office / a district
- CH078-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “耐久性”?
  - options: durability / maturity / brilliance / multiplication
- CH078-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: refine the measure / refine the brow / refine the skirt / refine the arrow
- CH078-Q4 [cloze] The biologist said maturity is not ______.
  - options: durability / genius / neutrality / width
- CH078-Q5 [recall] What sentence closed the argument?
  - options: a body is not a bank / the schedule is abusive / the numbers are paradoxical / the corridor is too narrow

### Answers

- CH078-Q1: a place we send people to, not take them from - She wanted the body treated as a destination, not a resource.
- CH078-Q2: durability - Maturity was distinguished from mere durability.
- CH078-Q3: refine the measure - Refine combines with a measure, a method, or a plan.
- CH078-Q4: durability - He warned that surviving one season is not maturity.
- CH078-Q5: a body is not a bank - The doctor's early sentence became the conclusion.

## CH079 - The Cost of Always Being Busy: A Better Question / 忙碌的代价：更好的问题

- theme: 健康、运动与生活习惯

**1.** The better question came from a cook in the gym canteen: who decided that exercise with a capital letter is the only therapeutic movement? “You provide a gymnasium and call it health,” she said. “My generation walked. The fish in the market swim all day and never see a gym.” The sound of a band practising in the corridor came through the wall, and nobody spoke for a moment.

更好的问题来自体育馆食堂的一位厨师：是谁决定只有大写字母开头的“运动”才算治疗性的活动？“你们提供一座体育馆，然后称之为健康，”她说，“我这一代人是走路的。市场上的鱼整天游，从来没见过健身房。”走廊里一支乐队排练的声音透过墙传过来，有一瞬间没有人说话。

**2.** The illusion was structural: a massive building with a painful absence. “You admit a person with a prescription, and you stop at the door of their week,” she said. “Recreation, music, a short walk: these are instrumental to the condition, not a distraction from it.” A chicken, a hot dish, and a plate of food were her evidence; the fact was that the club had never asked her.

这种错觉是结构性的：一座宏伟的建筑，配着一处痛苦的缺席。“你们接收一个拿着处方的人，然后在他们那一周的门槛前停下，”她说，“消遣、音乐、一段短短的步行：这些对处境本身是有用的，而不是对它的干扰。”一只鸡、一盘热菜和一盘食物就是她的证据；而事实是，俱乐部从来没有问过她。

**3.** The dominant idea in the room was that suffering is exceptional and that a breakdown is a personal failure. “A collapse is a signal from an organism,” the physiotherapist said. “A calf muscle does not lie.” The reach of the programme had to stretch past the police, the students, and the staff — or the whole exercise was a way of spending money on the already healthy.

屋里占主导的想法是：苦难是例外，崩溃是个人的失败。“崩溃是一个有机体发出的信号，”理疗师说，“小腿肌肉不会说谎。”项目所能触及的范围必须伸到警察、学生和职工之外——否则这整套练习就只是把钱花在本来就健康的人身上。

**4.** The spiritual part was harder to write. “Kindness is a facilitation, not a luxury,” the doctor said. “Sympathy without a timetable is only the imagination of help.” A landscape of small habits, a series of positive evenings, and a touch of real rest were what they had to provide; anything else was insane.

精神层面的部分更难写。“善意是一种促成，不是奢侈品，”医生说，“没有时间表的同情，只是对帮助的想象。”一片由小习惯构成的风景、一连串积极的傍晚，以及一点真正的休息，是他们必须提供的东西；除此之外的任何做法都是荒唐的。

**5.** The second series of questions was about habitat: where a body actually lives, not where it is trained. “Always understand the walk to the door,” the cook said. “That is the real event.” The effort of the coming week would be to give the programme a place in the lives of people who might never enter a gymnasium at all. Sooner or later a body will play, or it will break; there is no third option. It was difficult to say which was more dangerous, the drug or the timetable; to exert an influence on either required the same patience.

第二串问题关于栖息地：一具身体实际上住在哪里，而不是它在哪里被训练。“永远要理解走到门口那一段路，”厨师说，“那才是真正的事件。”接下来这一周的努力，是要把这套项目放进那些也许永远不会走进体育馆的人的生活里。身体迟早会玩耍，否则它就会断裂；没有第三种选项。很难说哪一个更危险，是药物还是时间表；要对其中任何一个施加影响，都需要同样的耐心。

### Exercises

- CH079-Q1 [context] What did the cook say about her generation?
  - options: it walked / it swam like fish / it practised in a band / it went to the gymnasium
- CH079-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “消遣；娱乐”?
  - options: recreation / facilitation / habitat / breakdown
- CH079-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: provide a gymnasium / provide a collapse / provide a calf / provide an illusion
- CH079-Q4 [cloze] The physiotherapist said a collapse is a signal from an ______.
  - options: organism / audience / instrument / incline
- CH079-Q5 [recall] What did the doctor call kindness?
  - options: a facilitation, not a luxury / a distraction / an illusion / a breakdown

### Answers

- CH079-Q1: it walked - She contrasted walking with purpose-built exercise.
- CH079-Q2: recreation - She counted recreation as part of the treatment.
- CH079-Q3: provide a gymnasium - Provide combines with a service, a place, or evidence.
- CH079-Q4: organism - He described the body as a system sending signals.
- CH079-Q5: a facilitation, not a luxury - She treated kindness as practical support.

## CH080 - The Cost of Always Being Busy: The Second Attempt / 忙碌的代价：第二次尝试

- theme: 健康、运动与生活习惯

**1.** The second attempt had a minimal requirement and a maximal ambition: one indoor session a week, provided the club could find a room. “We are not asking for a large provision,” Maya said. “We are asking for the series to exist.” A lucky student in a nearby suburb offered the hall of a skating club, and the nation of small habits began to take shape.

第二次尝试有着极小的必要条件与极大的雄心：每周一次室内训练，只要俱乐部能找到一间屋子。“我们不是在要求一大笔拨款，”曼雅说，“我们要求的是让这个系列存在下去。”附近郊区一位幸运的学生提供了溜冰俱乐部的场地，而那个由小习惯组成的国家开始成形。

**2.** The ratio of talk to movement was the first oddity. A nervous doctoral student measured the intake of sugar, the approximate intake of salt, and the proverbial intake of advice; the laughter that followed was genuine and slightly uneasy. “A fake plan and a conceptual one look identical on paper,” she said, “and only one of them survives contact with a suitcase.”

谈话与运动的比率是第一个奇特之处。一位紧张的在读博士生测量了糖的摄入量、盐的大致摄入量，以及“建议”这个谚语式的摄入量；随之而来的笑声是真诚的，也略带不安。“假计划和概念性的计划在纸上看起来一模一样，”她说，“而其中只有一种能挺过与一只行李箱的接触。”

**3.** The atmospheric part was harder: a windy suburb, an outer ring road, and a nightmare of bus connections. “Hospitality is not entertainment,” the easy-going coach said. “It is the interruption you do not mind.” A resemblance between the two approaches existed, but the inner one had vitality and the outer one had a poster.

气氛那部分更难：一个多风的郊区、一条外环道路，以及一场公交换乘的噩梦。“好客不是娱乐，”那位随和的教练说，“它是你不介意的那次打断。”两种做法之间确实有相似之处，但内在的那一种有生命力，外在的那一种只有一张海报。

**4.** The prevention work was almost invisible. “An occurrence of injury is not a sexual matter and not a moral one,” the physiotherapist said. “It is an event with a date.” Arousal, sleepiness, and an amusing oddity in the data all pointed the same way: the second attempt had to be boring on purpose.

预防工作几乎是看不见的。“一次受伤的发生既不是性方面的事，也不是道德的事，”理疗师说，“它是一次有日期的事件。”激发、困倦，以及数据里一处有趣的怪事，都指向同一个方向：第二次尝试必须故意做得很无聊。

**5.** The conceptual shift was to treat rest as part of the exercise. “An existent body is not a spherical one,” the coach said. “It has a history and a knee.” The sleepiness of the room at four o'clock, the smell of a conditioner bottle, and the sound of a distant match on the radio: all of it was the second attempt, and it was possibly the last one they would be given. The effort was especially difficult in the outskirts, where the music of the club never reached and a hot meal was a hit or a miss. “You watch a war of small habits,” the coach said, “and you are optimistic only because you are asleep.” The second attempt had to pass through those districts before it could call itself a programme.

概念上的转变是把休息当作训练的一部分。“一具存在的身体不是球形的，”教练说，“它有来历，也有膝盖。”四点钟屋里那股困倦、一只护发素瓶子的气味，以及收音机里一场远处比赛的声音：所有这些都是第二次尝试，而这很可能是他们最后一次机会。这份努力在市郊尤其艰难，那里俱乐部的音乐从不到达，一顿热饭也时有时无。“你看着一场小习惯之间的战争，”教练说，“而你之所以乐观，只是因为你睡着了。”第二次尝试必须穿过那些片区，才能称自己为一个项目。

### Exercises

- CH080-Q1 [context] What was the second attempt's minimal requirement?
  - options: one indoor session a week / a new gymnasium / a doctoral study / a skating club
- CH080-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “中断；打扰”?
  - options: interruption / occurrence / resemblance / conditioner
- CH080-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: treat rest as part of the exercise / treat rest as a nightmare / treat rest as an intake / treat rest as a suburb
- CH080-Q4 [cloze] The coach said hospitality is the interruption you do not ______.
  - options: mind / measure / fake / absorb
- CH080-Q5 [recall] What did the physiotherapist say an injury is?
  - options: an event with a date / a moral failure / a sexual matter / an oddity

### Answers

- CH080-Q1: one indoor session a week - They asked for a minimal, repeatable session.
- CH080-Q2: interruption - She reframed hospitality as a welcome interruption.
- CH080-Q3: treat rest as part of the exercise - Rest became a component of training, not its opposite.
- CH080-Q4: mind - The coach distinguished welcome interruption from noise.
- CH080-Q5: an event with a date - He insisted on recording when an injury occurred.

## CH081 - The Cost of Always Being Busy: Outside Pressure / 忙碌的代价：外部压力

- theme: 健康、运动与生活习惯

**1.** The outside pressure came from the hospital itself. A medical committee, a doctor of public health, and a corps of athletic trainers proposed a fast, tough piece of legislation: every club must report its injuries, its treatment, and the prevalence of disease among its members. “My game is not your game,” the coach said. “But the matter is the same.”

外部压力来自医院本身。一个医学委员会、一位公共卫生医生，以及一支运动训练队提出了一项迅速而强硬的立法：每个俱乐部都必须上报受伤情况、治疗情况，以及成员中疾病的流行程度。“我的比赛不是你的比赛，”教练说，“但事情是一样的。”

**2.** The requirement was to be vigorous and orderly: a pillar of the new regime, a topical digest of every seizure, a bodily audit of every waist. “We can tolerate a fever and a sweater,” the physiotherapist said. “We cannot tolerate starvation in a healthy athlete.” The abolition of the old exemptions was pushed through in a month.

这项要求既要有力又要有序：成为新体制的一根支柱、对每一次发作的时事性摘要、对每条腰围的身体审计。“我们可以容忍发烧和一件毛衣，”理疗师说，“我们无法容忍一个健康运动员的饥饿。”旧豁免的废除在一个月内被推动通过。

**3.** The vital statistics were alarming. A pregnant player, an influenza outbreak, and a muscular injury that would need surgery made the case for refrigeration, flour, and a competence in the kitchen. “You must absorb the fact,” the doctor said. “Strength is not the same as health.”

关键统计数据令人警觉。一位怀孕的球员、一场流感爆发，以及一处需要手术的肌肉损伤，为冷藏、面粉和厨房里的胜任能力提供了理由。“你必须吸收这个事实，”医生说，“力量不等于健康。”

**4.** The invasive part was the prescription: a healthful diet, a fast walk, and an hour of treatment that nobody could date. “You push a pillar and the pillar pushes back,” the committee said. “That is why we legislate today rather than yesterday.” The deprivation of rest, they added, is an extremity no club should be allowed to reach.

侵入性的部分是那张处方：一份有益健康的饮食、一段快步走，以及一小时没人能说清日期的治疗。“你推一根柱子，柱子也会推回来，”委员会说，“这就是我们今天而不是昨天立法的原因。”他们补充说，对休息的剥夺，是任何俱乐部都不该被允许达到的极端。

**5.** The pressure came from the country as well: a national register, a march of monthly figures, and a used-up phrase about “the healthy majority”. My answer was simple, the coach said: an attempt to measure the muscle and not the person is likely to fail, and it must fail before the committee meets again in the spring. At the hospital, a patient could become infected on any ward, and the doctors were likely to count that as part of the treatment cost. The march of figures had already begun; today it was a page, yesterday it was a rumour, and the date of publication had been set for next month. A foot, a might, and a used prescription: the register wanted all three, and the club had none of them.

压力也来自这个国家：一份全国登记册、一串逐月的数字，以及一句用旧了的“健康的大多数”。我的答案很简单，教练说：一次只测量肌肉、不测量人的尝试很可能会失败，而它必须在委员会春天再次开会之前失败。在医院里，一个病人可能在任何一个病区被感染，而医生们很可能把这算作治疗成本的一部分。数字的行进已经开始了；今天它是一页纸，昨天它是一个传言，而发布的日期定在下个月。一只脚、一份力量，以及一张用过的处方：登记册三样都想要，而俱乐部一样也没有。

### Exercises

- CH081-Q1 [context] What did the medical committee propose?
  - options: a fast, tough piece of legislation / a new gymnasium / a longer season / a diet of flour
- CH081-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “普遍；流行”?
  - options: prevalence / deprivation / refrigeration / extremity
- CH081-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: tolerate a fever / tolerate a pillar / tolerate a corps / tolerate a legislator
- CH081-Q4 [cloze] The doctor said strength is not the same as ______.
  - options: health / surgery / muscle / flour
- CH081-Q5 [recall] Why did the committee say they legislate today?
  - options: because the pillar pushes back / because the hospital closed / because the season is short / because the flour ran out

### Answers

- CH081-Q1: a fast, tough piece of legislation - The hospital demanded mandatory injury reporting.
- CH081-Q2: prevalence - The committee wanted the prevalence of disease recorded.
- CH081-Q3: tolerate a fever - Tolerate combines with a fever, a delay, or a habit.
- CH081-Q4: health - She separated visible strength from actual health.
- CH081-Q5: because the pillar pushes back - Pressure from the clubs forced earlier legislation.

## CH082 - The Cost of Always Being Busy: The Decision / 忙碌的代价：决定

- theme: 健康、运动与生活习惯

**1.** The decision was taken on the side of a shallow pool, with a board across two stones and a heavy drop of rain on the mat. “My plan is a habit, not an empire,” Maya said. “A quick look at the target tells you who is able to come.” A bar of shade, a pint of soup, and a lemon in the fruit bowl: the committee had provided lunch and nothing else.

决定是在一片浅水池边做出的，一块木板横在两块石头上，垫子上落着一大滴雨。“我的计划是一种习惯，而不是一个帝国，”曼雅说，“看一眼目标就知道谁能来。”一条荫凉、一品脱汤，以及水果盘里的一只柠檬：委员会提供了午餐，仅此而已。

**2.** The acute problem was the stomach of a young athlete: a headache, a bulb of garlic, and a plate of potato and cucumber. “You cannot kick a body into health,” the doctor said. “You can hang a spoon from a fork, but you cannot hang a habit on a threat.” The salt, the cream, and the solid mass of the diet were all on the table.

严峻的问题是那位年轻运动员的胃：头痛、一瓣大蒜，以及一盘土豆和黄瓜。“你无法把一个身体踢向健康，”医生说，“你可以把勺子挂在叉子上，但你无法把一个习惯挂在威胁上。”盐、奶油和那份饮食的坚实分量都摆在桌上。

**3.** The triangle of obligations was simple: the national federation, the campus health service, and our own small project. “Follow the body and the bank will follow,” she said. “Kill the pressure and you kill the plan.” The review of the last month showed a fall in injuries and a rise in attendance, and the crown of the argument was a story about a naked skater on the ice.

义务的三角关系很简单：国家联合会、校园健康服务，以及我们自己这个小项目。“跟着身体走，银行就会跟上，”她说，“把压力杀掉，计划也就死了。”上个月的复审显示受伤减少、出勤上升，而这场论证的顶点，是一个关于一位赤裸滑冰者在冰上的故事。

**4.** The decision was to make rest a formal part of training, with a mat, a mixer, and a pump in the corner of the gym. “An incline is not a barrier,” the coach said. “It is a slower way up.” A nickel in the vending machine, a pair of pants in the locker, and a filling in a tooth: the small costs of a large change.

决定是让休息成为训练的正式部分，角落里放一张垫子、一台搅拌机和一台打气筒。“斜坡不是障碍，”教练说，“它是一条更慢的上坡路。”自动售货机里的一枚五分镍币、储物柜里的一条裤子，以及牙上的一处补缀：这场大改变的小代价。

**5.** They came to the committee with one sentence and one number: two hours a week, provided free, for every member of the club. The committee asked who would pay, and the answer was our own budget line. “Who loses?” the chair asked. “Nobody who is able to come,” Maya said, “and that is the whole point.” The decision was difficult, and it had to come today; the committee would ask who paid, and my answer was the same as theirs. Able bodies, a bank, a national register: the plan had to provide something that could reach a body before it broke. A shoulder, an apple, a skate, and a music stand: the room had everything except a programme for an addict of busyness, and the game would be lost without one.

他们带着一句话和一个数字来到委员会：每周两小时，免费提供，面向俱乐部每一位成员。委员会问谁来付钱，答案是我们的预算科目。“谁受损？”主席问。“凡是能来的人都不受损，”曼雅说，“而这正是全部要点。”这个决定很难，而且必须今天做出；委员会会问谁付钱，而我的答案和他们的一样。能行的身体、一家银行、一份全国登记册：这个计划必须提供某种东西，能在一具身体垮掉之前触达它。一副肩膀、一个苹果、一块滑板，以及一个乐谱架：这个房间里什么都有，唯独没有给忙碌成瘾者的方案，而没有它，这场比赛就会输。

### Exercises

- CH082-Q1 [context] What did Maya say her plan was?
  - options: a habit, not an empire / an empire / a target / a triangle
- CH082-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “尖锐的；急性的”?
  - options: acute / naked / solid / shallow
- CH082-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: follow the body / follow the nickel / follow the mixer / follow the crown
- CH082-Q4 [cloze] The coach said an incline is not a barrier but a slower way ______.
  - options: up / down / around / back
- CH082-Q5 [recall] What did the committee ask?
  - options: who would pay / who would skate / who would cook / who would row

### Answers

- CH082-Q1: a habit, not an empire - She refused to build a large institution.
- CH082-Q2: acute - The athlete's stomach problem was acute, not chronic.
- CH082-Q3: follow the body - Follow combines with a body, a rule, or a method.
- CH082-Q4: up - He described the incline as a slower route, not a wall.
- CH082-Q5: who would pay - The committee asked about funding the two hours.

## CH083 - The Cost of Always Being Busy: Consequences / 忙碌的代价：后果

- theme: 健康、运动与生活习惯

**1.** The consequences arrived in the kitchen. Everyone in the central company could see the change in the food: brown rice instead of white, a drop of coffee in the morning, and a cheese sandwich instead of a chocolate bar. “The ingredient is not the point,” the physician said. “The mood is.” Thus the board asked a firm from the agency to give the club a new menu.

后果出现在厨房里。中央公司的每个人都能看到食物的变化：白米换成糙米，早上加一滴咖啡，巧克力棒换成奶酪三明治。“成分不是重点，”内科医师说，“心情才是。”因此董事会请了一家代理机构为俱乐部制定新菜单。

**2.** The crucial question was the stove. A cooker that nobody had cleaned, a bundle of herbs in a silver clip, and a garbage bin beside the window: the condition of the kitchen decided the appetite of the whole team. “You cannot play on a hungry lung,” the cook said. “You can only look at the window and wish.”

关键的问题是炉灶。一台没人清洗过的炊具、一捆夹在银色夹子里的香草、窗边一个垃圾桶：厨房的状况决定了整支队伍的食欲。“你不能靠一副饥饿的肺去比赛，”厨师说，“你只能看着窗户，然后许愿。”

**3.** The stimulus was not the chocolate but the way it was given. A mild powder, a fraction of a banana, and a yoghurt with lemonade were enough for a stressful afternoon; a drastic bundle of stimulants was not. “A nerve is not a toy,” the physician wrote to the general manager. “A stimulant taken impulsively is a debt.”

刺激物不是巧克力，而是它被给予的方式。一点温和的粉末、一小片香蕉和一份加了柠檬水的酸奶，就足以应付一个紧张的下午；一大捆兴奋剂则不行。“神经不是玩具，”医师写信给总经理说，“冲动地服下兴奋剂就是欠债。”

**4.** The deterioration of one player scared them all. A tumor the size of a staple, a lung full of dioxide, and an atomic scan later, the club joined the union of clubs that had asked for a national consolidation of the data. The utilization of the gym had to be examined, and the broom in the corner became a symbol.

一名球员的病情恶化让他们都害怕了。一个订书钉大小的肿瘤、一肺的二氧化物，以及一次原子扫描之后，俱乐部加入了一个向国家申请数据统一整理的俱乐部联盟。体育馆的利用情况必须被审查，而角落里的那把扫帚成了一个象征。

**5.** The headmistress of the local school, a member of the board, asked the question that closed the meeting: “What do you give a body that has no appetite?” The answer was a kitchen with a window, a stove that worked, and a routine that did not depend on the mood of the cosmos. The side of the argument that nobody wanted to hear was the only one that could be defended. The defense of the old kitchen was that the drug of habit is cheap; the daily grind of cooking for fifty people, however, was not intact. “You cannot buy stimulation in a packet without becoming impulsive,” the headmistress said. A broom, a staple gun, and a packet of chocolate were the only tools left in the room, and the physician wrote down every item.

当地学校的女校长、董事会成员，问出了结束会议的那个问题：“你给一具没有食欲的身体什么？”答案是：一间有窗的厨房、一台能用的炉子，以及一套不取决于宇宙心情的日常。论证中没人愿意听的那一面，恰恰是唯一站得住脚的那一面。为旧厨房辩护的说法是：习惯这种药很便宜；然而，为五十个人做饭的日常苦役并不是毫发无损的。“你无法买一包刺激物而不变得冲动，”女校长说。屋里只剩下了一把扫帚、一把订书机和一包巧克力，而内科医师把每一样都记了下来。

### Exercises

- CH083-Q1 [context] What did the physician say was the point?
  - options: the mood, not the ingredient / the chocolate / the rice / the broom
- CH083-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “恶化；衰退”?
  - options: deterioration / consolidation / utilization / stimulation
- CH083-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: clean the stove / clean the mood / clean the cosmos / clean the lung
- CH083-Q4 [cloze] The physician wrote that a nerve is not a ______.
  - options: toy / staple / fraction / powder
- CH083-Q5 [recall] What did the headmistress ask?
  - options: what you give a body with no appetite / who owns the kitchen / when the scan is due / why the gym is used

### Answers

- CH083-Q1: the mood, not the ingredient - He valued the setting over the shopping list.
- CH083-Q2: deterioration - One player's worsening condition alarmed the club.
- CH083-Q3: clean the stove - Clean combines with a stove, a room, or data.
- CH083-Q4: toy - He warned against treating a nerve as something to play with.
- CH083-Q5: what you give a body with no appetite - She reduced the debate to one concrete question.

## CH084 - The Cost of Always Being Busy: The Bridge Forward / 忙碌的代价：通向下一程

- theme: 健康、运动与生活习惯

**1.** The sixth arc closed with a consultation and a regional register. “Imagine a castle of habits,” the physiotherapist said. “Every joint has a motive, and every motive has a remedy.” The minister, the official, and the player sat at the same table, and everyone at it had a different viewpoint about what to emphasize in the report.

第六段弧线以一次咨询和一份地区登记册收尾。“想象一座习惯的城堡，”理疗师说，“每个关节都有动机，每个动机都有疗法。”部长、官员和球员坐在同一张桌边，而桌上每个人对报告该强调什么都有不同的观点。

**2.** The visual evidence was distressful. A crack in the ambulance door, a lower spine injured in a fall, and a case of flu that had spread through the whole squad: the correspondence between the figures and the stories was exact, and it was suggestive of a remedy nobody wanted to fund. “Do not let a bad motive interfere with a good measure,” the doctor said.

视觉证据令人痛苦。救护车门上的一道裂缝、一次跌倒中受伤的下段脊柱，以及一场席卷整支队伍的流感：数字与故事之间的对应是精确的，而且暗示着一种没人愿意出资的疗法。“不要让糟糕的动机干扰好的措施，”医生说。

**3.** From the standpoint of immunity, the remedy was dull: sleep, a massage, a medicinal spoonful, and a jog in the park. Pork and feed had more prominence in the budget than a massage, and the vulnerability of the older members was the tiresome part of the argument.

从免疫力的立场看，疗法很乏味：睡眠、一次按摩、一匙药物，以及在公园里慢跑。猪肉和饲料在预算里的地位比一次按摩更突出，而年长成员的脆弱，是这场论证里最烦人的部分。

**4.** The destructive part was the provocation in the press. A single headline could expel a volunteer, cheer a rival, and refresh an old quarrel in a week. “You cannot extinguish a rumour with a labourious paragraph,” Maya wrote, and then corrected the spelling to laborious.

破坏性的部分是媒体上的挑衅。一个标题就能在一周内开除一名志愿者、为对手欢呼，并让一场旧争吵重新鲜活起来。“你无法用一段苦役般的文字熄灭一则谣言，”曼雅写道，随后把拼写改成了 laborious。

**5.** The hurt was real and it was direct: a bony shoulder, an uncovering of the past, and a case of pneumonia that the hospital could not explain. The cohesive lesson was that a body is not a castle with one gate; it is a set of bridges, and every bridge has a limit that someone must discover before the ambulance does. I must ask what my job is here, the physiotherapist said: a service, a game, or a church? A vulnerable joint can disintegrate in a week, and a remedial hour is not a favour — it is the health of the whole squad. “Do not let the god of the timetable hit the table again,” she said, and the hospital sent its report to the ministry that afternoon. Thus the work of the ministry was to turn the report into a table. The club had learned to budget for rest; no one had yet learned to budget for the fear of stopping.

伤害是真实的，也是直接的：一副瘦骨嶙峋的肩膀、一段被揭开的往事，以及一例医院解释不了的肺炎。内在一致的教训是：身体不是只有一道门的城堡；它是一组桥，每座桥都有一个限度，必须有人赶在救护车之前发现它。我必须问，我在这里的工作是什么，理疗师说：一项服务、一场比赛，还是一座教堂？一个脆弱的关节能在一周内瓦解，而一个小时的补救不是恩惠——它是整支队伍的健康。“不要让时刻表的神再敲一次桌子，”她说，而那天下午医院把报告送到了部里。于是部里的工作就是把那份报告变成一张表格。

### Exercises

- CH084-Q1 [context] What did the physiotherapist ask them to imagine?
  - options: a castle of habits / a regional register / an ambulance / a spoonful of medicine
- CH084-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “脆弱；易受伤害”?
  - options: vulnerability / immunity / prominence / provocation
- CH084-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: extinguish a rumour / extinguish a joint / extinguish a castle / extinguish a viewpoint
- CH084-Q4 [cloze] The doctor said a bad motive must not ______ with a good measure.
  - options: interfere / expel / refresh / injure
- CH084-Q5 [recall] What did Maya compare a body to at the end?
  - options: a set of bridges / a castle / a register / an ambulance

### Answers

- CH084-Q1: a castle of habits - He used the castle as an image of layered routine.
- CH084-Q2: vulnerability - The older members' vulnerability was the awkward topic.
- CH084-Q3: extinguish a rumour - Extinguish combines with a rumour, a fire, or a hope.
- CH084-Q4: interfere - He separated motive from measurement.
- CH084-Q5: a set of bridges - She replaced the castle image with bridges that each have a limit.

## CH085 - What the System Remembers: A Signal / 系统记住了什么：一个信号

- theme: 科学、技术与互联网

**1.** The club had finally agreed to pay for rest; the next failure was in a system that never slept. The seventh arc opened with a signal that nobody had asked for: a download of the club's website that included a file nobody had uploaded. The board took one look at the log, and the third member of the team, a base of operations in the innovation lab, was asked to explain the exposure. “It is a delay in the wrong direction,” Aisha said. “It is a resource problem, not a wish problem.”

俱乐部终于同意为休息付钱；而下一次失败，出现在一个从不睡觉的系统里。第七段弧线以一个没人要求的信号开始：俱乐部网站的一次下载，里面包含了一个没人上传过的文件。董事会只看了一眼日志，团队的第三位成员——创新实验室里的一个行动基地——就被要求解释这次暴露。“这是方向错误的延误，”艾莎说，“这是资源问题，不是愿望问题。”

**2.** The aim was a reduction in the concentration of personal data on the server. A video of a training session, a cookie in the browser, and an echo of an old backup were the only things anyone could find. “Take the filter and the bearing off the machine,” the officer said. “Make the composition of the file smaller.” Indeed, the file was a compound of six unrelated documents.

目标是降低服务器上个人数据的集中度。一段训练录像、浏览器里的一块饼干，以及一次旧备份的回声，是任何人都能找到的全部东西。“把过滤器和方位从机器上拿下来，”官员说，“把文件的构成变小。”的确，那个文件是六份不相关文档的化合物。

**3.** The projection was worse than the delay: a microphone left on during a massage, a fax to the wrong office, and a kettle of water boiling beside a microwave. “A logo is not proof of anything,” Maya said. “An omission is.” The side of the lab that held the servers was the side nobody could enter without a boot on each foot.

预测比延误更糟：按摩时忘关的麦克风、发错办公室的传真，以及在微波炉旁烧开的水壶。“标志不能证明任何事，”曼雅说，“遗漏才能。”实验室里存放服务器的那一侧，是没有人能不穿靴子走进去的一侧。

**4.** The biology of the problem was a charm and a jaw. “Biology is not the issue,” the biochemical engineer said. “The issue is that we cannot take a volt of trust and charge it to a customer.” The imperial habit of shipping a product first and asking later had to be broken; the mount of documents, the swing of the curve, and the exposure all pointed the same way.

这个问题的生物学部分是一份魔力和一个下颌。“生物学不是问题所在，”生物化学工程师说，“问题在于我们不能取一伏特的信任，然后把它记在客户账上。”那种先发货、后询问的帝国式习惯必须被打破；文件的架、曲线的摆动，以及这次暴露，都指向同一个方向。

**5.** The reflection that followed was short. “We bring the situation to the board and take the possible offer,” Lin wrote. “We place the note in the register and send the same message to everyone.” The might of a small team is its willingness to show the file before someone else does. The board, the officer, and the situation on the server all pointed to the same place: we might bring the offer, take the place, and make it possible to send a smaller file. A wish is not a plan; indeed, the look on the officer's face said that the cause of the leak was still unknown. The focus of the pass was simple: down the list, item by item, we would eat the fat of the file, take the barrier out of the exhaust pipe, and let the notation in the margin involve every reader.

随之而来的反思很短。“我们把情况带到董事会，接受任何可能的提议，”林伟写道，“把说明放进登记簿，并把同样的消息发给每个人。”一支小团队的力量，就在于它愿意在别人之前先把文件拿出来。董事会、那位官员，以及服务器上的情况，都指向同一个地方：我们可以把提议带过去，把位置接过来，并让发一个更小的文件成为可能。一个愿望不是计划；的确，那位官员脸上的表情说明，泄漏的原因仍然不明。这一轮梳理的重点很简单：顺着清单，一项一项地把文件里的肥肉吃掉，把排气管里的障碍取出来，并让页边的标注涉及每一位读者。

### Exercises

- CH085-Q1 [context] What did the download contain?
  - options: a file nobody had uploaded / a training video / a cookie / a fax
- CH085-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “遗漏；疏忽”?
  - options: omission / exposure / projection / notation
- CH085-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: take a reflection / take a volt / take a jaw / take a kettle
- CH085-Q4 [cloze] Maya said a logo is not proof of anything, but an ______ is.
  - options: omission / echo / upload / imperial
- CH085-Q5 [recall] What did Lin say the might of a small team is?
  - options: its willingness to show the file first / its server room / its video archive / its microphone

### Answers

- CH085-Q1: a file nobody had uploaded - An unrecognised file appeared in the public download.
- CH085-Q2: omission - She argued that what is left out proves more than a logo.
- CH085-Q3: take a reflection - Take combines with a reflection, a risk, or a decision.
- CH085-Q4: omission - The missing consent mattered more than the branding.
- CH085-Q5: its willingness to show the file first - He defined strength as early disclosure.

## CH086 - What the System Remembers: The First Promise / 系统记住了什么：第一次承诺

- theme: 科学、技术与互联网

**1.** The first promise of the arc was made in the innovation lab, with a keyboard, a mouse, and a printer on the table. “Every system we access will have an argument behind it,” Maya said. “A program is not a promise; a promise is a program with a reason.” We would take the third position: not the base of the network, but the code at the door.

这段弧线的第一个承诺是在创新实验室里做出的，桌上放着一个键盘、一只鼠标和一台打印机。“我们访问的每一个系统背后都要有一个论证，”曼雅说，“程序不是承诺；承诺是一个有理由的程序。”我们将采取第三种立场：不是网络的底座，而是门口的代码。

**2.** The physics of the promise was simple: energy in, data out, and no chemical left behind. A cell of six volunteers, a machine that nobody had switched off, and an operation manual in a format nobody could read: the effort of the first week was to leave nothing open. “You cannot calculate trust,” the engineer said. “You can only monitor it.”

这个承诺的物理学很简单：能量进来，数据出去，不留任何化学残留。一个六人的小组、一台没人关过的机器，以及一本没人读得懂格式的操作手册：第一周的努力就是不留任何开口。“你无法计算信任，”工程师说，“你只能监测它。”

**3.** The event that made it urgent was a virus. A bug in the driver, a worm in the backup, and a crash of the printer on the night before the launch: the track of the damage led to a dump of old files that had never been registered. “A scanner is not a conscience,” Aisha wrote. “An indicator is not a control.”

让它变得紧迫的那件事是一个病毒。驱动程序里的一个错误、备份里的一条蠕虫，以及发布前夜打印机的崩溃：损害的轨迹指向一堆从未登记过的旧文件。“扫描仪不是良心，”艾莎写道，“指示器不是控制。”

**4.** The position was difficult, and it had to be provided in writing. Every device, every software package, and every online service would have a named person responsible for it; the hardware in the lab would be inventoried, and the logic of every access rule would be explained to the volunteers in one page.

这个立场很难，而且必须以书面形式提供。每个设备、每个软件包、每项在线服务都要有一个具名的负责人；实验室里的硬件要清点入册，每条访问规则的逻辑都要用一页纸向志愿者解释清楚。

**5.** The technology was not the point, and even the biotechnology group in the next building knew it. A hacker in cyberspace, a register of permissions, and a network that remembered more than any human could: thus the phase of the design had to begin with deletion. “Create less,” Lin wrote, “and the Internet will have less to remember.” It was a promise about forgetting, and it arrived on time. We have a difficult effort ahead: to create a base, to take the third pass at the code, and to reach a position we can provide in writing. Down the corridor the night shift was playing music, or at least something like it. Thus the event of the launch had to leave a year of work in a form that could survive us. A computer, a source, a science, and an engineering team: the club had none of them, and the code was written by volunteers.

技术不是重点，连隔壁大楼里的生物技术小组也知道这一点。网络空间里的一个黑客、一份权限登记簿，以及一个比任何人类都记得更多的网络：因此设计的这一阶段必须以删除开始。“少创造一些，”林伟写道，“互联网要记住的就会少一些。”这是一个关于遗忘的承诺，而它准时到达了。前面还有一场艰难的努力：建立一个基底、对代码做第三遍检查，并达到一个我们能以书面形式给出的立场。走廊那头，夜班在放音乐，或者至少是类似的东西。因此，发布会这件事必须以某种能比我们活得更久的形式，留下一年的工作。一台电脑、一个来源、一门科学，以及一支工程团队：俱乐部一样都没有，而代码是由志愿者写的。

### Exercises

- CH086-Q1 [context] What did Maya say a promise is?
  - options: a program with a reason / a keyboard / a printer / a virus
- CH086-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “指示器；指标”?
  - options: indicator / register / format / hardware
- CH086-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: monitor access / monitor a virus / monitor a printer / monitor a keyboard
- CH086-Q4 [cloze] Aisha wrote that a scanner is not a ______.
  - options: conscience / driver / worm / backup
- CH086-Q5 [recall] Where did Lin say the design phase had to begin?
  - options: with deletion / with the hardware / with the network / with the biotechnology group

### Answers

- CH086-Q1: a program with a reason - She tied each system to a stated justification.
- CH086-Q2: indicator - An indicator shows a state; it does not control it.
- CH086-Q3: monitor access - Monitor combines with access, a system, or a cost.
- CH086-Q4: conscience - A scanner records without judging.
- CH086-Q5: with deletion - He argued for collecting less before designing more.

## CH087 - What the System Remembers: A Missing Voice / 系统记住了什么：缺席的声音

- theme: 科学、技术与互联网

**1.** The missing voice arrived as an email from a remote terminal in a room nobody visited. I am the person whose address is in your release, it said. I never gave permission for the storage of my data, not once, and not in any form. There was no error in the technical sense; the error was in the mechanism. As a scientific matter, the execution of the instruction had been exact, and that was the problem.

那个缺席的声音以一封电子邮件的形式到来，发自一间没人去过的房间里的远程终端。我是你发布信息里的那个地址，它说。我从未允许你存储我的数据。从技术意义上说没有错误；错误在于机制。从科学的角度看，那条指令被精确地执行了——而问题正在这里。

**2.** The platform had three menus, four packets of settings, and a translation of the consent form that nobody could read. A circuit of old machines, a tube of glue, and a sink full of cables: the lab had the look of an explosion in a chemistry set. The generator hummed, and the electronics on the shelf were covered in dust.

这个平台有三个菜单、四包设置，以及一份没人读得懂的同意书译文。一圈旧机器、一管胶水，以及一个装满线缆的水槽：实验室看起来像一套化学实验用具里发生了爆炸。发电机嗡嗡响着，架子上的电子元件落满了灰。

**3.** The interaction between the input and the output had never been tested with a real person. A disc of old backups, a magnet beside the storage, and a prompt on the screen asking for a resolution: the whole apparatus had one weakness, and it was the human at the end of it.

输入与输出之间的交互，从未与一个真实的人测试过。一张旧备份的光盘、存储旁的一块磁铁，以及屏幕上要求给出解决方案的提示：整台设备只有一个弱点，而它就是末端的那个人。

**4.** There was a fragmentation of responsibility: nobody owned the address list, and nobody could explain the absorption of personal data into a marketing outlet. The interpreter of the rules, a cracker of small puzzles, and the king of the local podcast all said the same thing: a service that cannot be explained cannot be consented to.

责任是碎片化的：没有人拥有那份地址名单，也没有人能解释个人数据是如何被吸收进一个营销渠道的。规则的译解者、一个擅长破解小谜题的人，以及当地播客之王，都说了同一件事：一项无法被解释的服务，是无法被同意接受的。

**5.** Compatible systems, telecommunications, and a mechanism for withdrawal: those were the three things the person asked for. The force of the request was quiet, and the hope behind it was still there, waiting quietly. It was a scientific problem, and the science was not in the machinery; not one atom of the data had been taken with relaxation. The computer, the cell, the source, and the energy were all in the same attempt to build a program that technology could not quietly rewrite. Over the next billion operations, the club would have to pick one: the convenience of the system, or the consent of the person inside it.

兼容的系统、电信，以及一套撤回的机制：这是那个人要求的三件事。这个请求的力量是安静的，而它背后的希望仍在。这是一个科学问题，而科学不在机器里；没有一个原子的数据是在放松状态下取得的。电脑、电池、来源和能量，全都在同一个尝试里——去建立一个技术无法悄悄改写的程序。在接下来的十亿次操作中，俱乐部必须做出选择：系统的便利，还是其中那个人的同意。

### Exercises

- CH087-Q1 [context] What did the person say about permission?
  - options: they never gave it for the storage of their data / they gave it twice / they wanted a new terminal / they asked for a podcast
- CH087-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “机制；机构”?
  - options: mechanism / resolution / fragmentation / execution
- CH087-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: execute an instruction / execute a magnet / execute a menu / execute a sink
- CH087-Q4 [cloze] The group concluded that a service which cannot be explained cannot be ______ to.
  - options: consented / executed / translated / integrated
- CH087-Q5 [recall] What did the person ask for?
  - options: compatible systems, telecommunications, and a mechanism for withdrawal / a new platform / a larger storage / a translation into three languages

### Answers

- CH087-Q1: they never gave it for the storage of their data - The consent had never been given in the first place.
- CH087-Q2: mechanism - The fault lay in the system's design, not its execution.
- CH087-Q3: execute an instruction - Execute combines with an instruction, a plan, or a command.
- CH087-Q4: consented - Consent requires comprehension.
- CH087-Q5: compatible systems, telecommunications, and a mechanism for withdrawal - The request was technical and specific.

## CH088 - What the System Remembers: Evidence on the Table / 系统记住了什么：摆上桌面的证据

- theme: 科学、技术与互联网

**1.** The evidence meeting was held at the base of the lab, on a table with four chairs and one broken foot. “This is not a talk about privacy,” Maya said. “It is a talk about proof.” The reply from the officer was short: a criterion was missing, and without a criterion the whole action could not be justified.

证据会议开在实验室的底座上，一张桌子配四把椅子，其中一条腿是断的。“这不是一场关于隐私的谈话，”曼雅说，“这是一场关于证据的谈话。”官员的回答很短：缺少一条准则，而没有准则，整个行动就无法被证明正当。

**2.** The powerful part of the evidence was its basis: a currency of small permissions, a race to collect more, and an expansion of the storage that nobody had costed. A policeman, a brewer of beer, and a seller of alcohol had all signed the same form, and none of them could say what they had agreed to.

证据中强有力的部分在于它的基础：一种小额许可的货币、一场收集更多的竞赛，以及一次没人核算过成本的存储扩张。一名警察、一位啤酒酿造者和一位卖酒的人都在同一张表格上签了字，而他们没有一个能说出自己同意了什么。

**3.** The fragments were scattered down the corridor: a pie chart in the wrong folder, a legend on a map of the pine forest, a rod of aluminium in a stack of junk beside the bin. “We can formulate the rule now,” Lin said. “Nothing is collected on a maybe.”

碎片散落在走廊里：一张放错文件夹的饼图、一片松林地图上的图例，以及垃圾桶旁一堆垃圾里的一根铝棒。“我们现在可以把规则写出来了，”林伟说，“任何东西都不能凭‘也许’来收集。”

**4.** The correction came in two parts: a clarification of the purpose and a subtraction of everything that could not be multiplied by consent. “A surge of data is not the same as a proof of trust,” the officer said. “A warehouse of wares is not a service.”

订正分两部分：对目的的澄清，以及减去一切无法与同意相乘的东西。“数据的一次激增不等于信任的证明，”官员说，“一仓库的货物也不是一项服务。”

**5.** The invaders were not hackers but habits. An armament of small defaults, a preservative clause in the contract, and a singularity of purpose — to keep the launch on schedule — had to be equipped against. “My game is simple,” Maya said. “Moderation is not a disable switch, and transparency is not alphabetic.” As far as the group was concerned, the third pass through the data was the only honest one. The cooperation between the hospital, the health office, and the lab had been off for an hour when the first correction arrived. “You cannot subtract a promise from a contract and call it a destination,” the officer said. A flame of temper, a quick pick of the wrong file, and a short run of bad decisions: the force of habit was the only invader left in the room, and it was the hardest one to expel.

入侵者不是黑客，而是习惯。一套由小默认值组成的军备、合同里一条防腐式的条款，以及目的上的奇异性——不惜一切保住发布日程——都必须被装备起来对抗。“我的比赛很简单，”曼雅说，“适度不是禁用开关，透明也不是按字母排序。”就这个小组而言，对数据进行的第三轮梳理才是唯一诚实的一轮。医院、卫生办公室和实验室之间的合作已经中断了一个小时，第一个更正才到来。“你不能从合同里减去一个承诺，然后称它为一个目的地，”那位官员说。一股火气、一次错拿文件、一连串糟糕的决定：习惯的力量是屋里唯一剩下的入侵者，而它也是最难被驱逐的。

### Exercises

- CH088-Q1 [context] What did Maya say the talk was about?
  - options: proof / privacy / beer / expansion
- CH088-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “澄清；净化”?
  - options: clarification / correction / moderation / singularity
- CH088-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: justify an action / justify a fragment / justify a pie / justify a rod
- CH088-Q4 [cloze] Lin said nothing may be collected on a ______.
  - options: maybe / fragment / legend / surge
- CH088-Q5 [recall] What did the officer say a warehouse of wares is not?
  - options: a service / a proof / a game / a correction

### Answers

- CH088-Q1: proof - She reframed privacy as a question of evidence.
- CH088-Q2: clarification - The first fix was clarifying the purpose of collection.
- CH088-Q3: justify an action - Justify combines with an action, a cost, or a claim.
- CH088-Q4: maybe - Collection had to rest on a stated basis, not a possibility.
- CH088-Q5: a service - He distinguished stored goods from actual service.

## CH089 - What the System Remembers: The Trial Run / 系统记住了什么：第一次试行

- theme: 科学、技术与互联网

**1.** The trial run entered its next phase on a Tuesday, with a package of four forms and a program on a computer that only one person could operate. “The importance of a clause is not theoretical,” Aisha said. “It is the difference between a percentage and a person.” Currently the club could not say what percent of its data was actually needed.

试行在周二进入下一阶段，带着一包四份表格，以及一个只有一个人会操作的程序。“条款的重要性不是理论上的，”艾莎说，“它是百分比与一个人之间的差别。”目前俱乐部说不清自己的数据里究竟有百分之多少是真的需要的。

**2.** The pattern was easy to attach to the wrong thing. A bold heading, a horizontal line, and a segment of the catalog were underlined for emphasis; the uncertainty was in the oven of the whole operation, where a shortage of clarity could bake a bad decision. “Omit the flavor,” Maya wrote. “Maximize the precision.”

模式很容易被附到错误的东西上。一个加粗的标题、一条水平线，以及目录中的一个片段被加了下划线以示强调；不确定在于整个运作的炉灶里——清晰度的短缺可能烤出一个糟糕的决定。“把味道省掉，”曼雅写道，“把精确度最大化。”

**3.** The diversity of the volunteers was the only inclusion that mattered. A bulky fixture on the wall, a stationary bicycle in the corner, and a wrapped package of aluminium foil: the lab had a density of objects and a shortage of space, and the wrapping alone took an hour to remove.

志愿者的多样性是唯一要紧的包容性。墙上一件庞大的固定装置、角落里一辆静止的自行车，以及一包用铝箔包好的包裹：实验室有物体的密度，却缺少空间，而光拆包装就花了一个小时。

**4.** The theoretical part of the trial was a liberation from the old assumption that more data means more safety. “Astronomy can measure a millimetre of movement at the other end of the sky,” the engineer said. “We cannot measure a decision in our own office.” The stoppage of the launch was, in that sense, a form of mechanization in reverse.

试行中理论性的部分，是从“数据越多越安全”这个旧假设中获得解放。“天文学能测出天空那一端一毫米的移动，”工程师说，“我们却测不出自己办公室里的一个决定。”从这个意义上说，发布的停止是一种反向的机械化。

**5.** The dynamics of the next month were simple: one action a week, one review of the system, and one honest talk about what was off and what was on. “Look at the movement of the data, not the bank balance,” she said. “We are not able to earn consent; we can only four times a year show that we did not lose it.” The side of the argument that carried the day was the smallest one: furnish the reader with clarity, and the technology will follow. The height of the shelf, the aluminum foil, and the revision of the catalog had to be assembled into one document before the audit. “You cannot integrate a body of evidence by holding it in your hand,” the engineer said. A revision a week, a body of notes, and a hold on the launch: that was the whole plan.

下个月的动态很简单：每周一个行动、一次系统复盘，以及一次关于什么关着、什么开着的诚实谈话。“看数据的运动，不要看银行余额，”她说，“我们没有能力赢得同意；我们只能一年四次说明我们没有把它弄丢。”当天占了上风的，是最小的那一面：给读者提供清晰，技术会跟上。架子的高度、铝箔，以及目录的修订，必须在审计之前汇集到一份文件里。“你无法靠把它拿在手里来整合一堆证据，”工程师说。每周一次修订、一堆笔记，以及对发布的暂缓：这就是整个计划。

### Exercises

- CH089-Q1 [context] What did Aisha say the importance of a clause is?
  - options: the difference between a percentage and a person / a theoretical matter / a stop page / a heading
- CH089-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “精确；精度”?
  - options: precision / density / clarity / shortage
- CH089-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: attach a clause / attach a millimetre / attach a density / attach a fixture
- CH089-Q4 [cloze] Maya wrote that they should omit the flavour and maximize the ______.
  - options: precision / stationary / segment / liberation
- CH089-Q5 [recall] What did the engineer say astronomy can measure?
  - options: a millimetre of movement far away / a decision in an office / a shortage of clarity / a wrapped package

### Answers

- CH089-Q1: the difference between a percentage and a person - She measured the clause by its human effect.
- CH089-Q2: precision - They preferred precision to flavour in the wording.
- CH089-Q3: attach a clause - Attach combines with a clause, a condition, or a file.
- CH089-Q4: precision - She wanted plain, exact language.
- CH089-Q5: a millimetre of movement far away - He contrasted astronomical precision with office blindness.

## CH090 - What the System Remembers: Pressure Builds / 系统记住了什么：压力上升

- theme: 科学、技术与互联网

**1.** At this stage of development, the international airport project had a long record of safe tests. The local team used a computer screen to view every runway, road, parking area, and service car across the full range of the site, from the control-room door to the nearest street. As Lin entered, a red statement appeared: GET NEW DATA. It was not the style of warning they had designed, nor did it give the next step. They had little time to wait.

项目开发进行到这一阶段时，这套国际机场系统已经有很长的安全测试记录。本地团队用电脑屏幕查看整个场地范围内的每条跑道、道路、停车区域和服务车辆，从控制室门口一直到最近的街道。林伟走进来时，屏幕出现一条红色提示：“获取新数据。”这既不是他们设计的警告样式，也没有说明下一步该做什么，而他们没有多少时间等待。

**2.** A group of engineers began to draw the system in the limited space on a wall. Each column showed one period in the data sequence; each row showed a different condition. A blue card represented food deliveries, a white card represented a medical drug shipment, and a gold card represented a high-security flight. A thick string formed the link between each record and the person who approved it. The visual combination exposed a gap. A red cross marked the exact link where the sequence broke.

一组工程师开始在有限的墙面空间里画出系统。每一列代表数据序列中的一个时期，每一行代表一种不同状态。蓝卡代表食品运输，白卡代表医药货物，金卡代表高安全级别航班。一根粗绳表示每条记录与批准人之间的联系。这个可视化组合暴露出一个缺口，一个红色的叉标出了序列断裂的确切环节。

**3.** The database could capture a driver's face but also contained a gene field copied from another version. That field had no useful purpose here. A negative flag should have blocked it under privacy regulation, yet an old construction template had carried it across. In contrast, the system failed to record damage on the lip of a fuel tray. The screen displayed noise instead of a precise measurement.

数据库可以捕捉司机的面部，却还包含一个从其他版本复制来的基因字段。该字段在这里没有任何用途。按照隐私规定，否定标记本应阻止它，但一份旧建筑模板把它带了过来。相反，系统没有记录燃料托盘边缘的损伤，屏幕显示的是噪声而非精确测量。

**4.** Outside, workers used tape and a tin seal to protect a cable box. A hammer lay beside the yard marker, and a warning bell beat against the wind. No one could press the emergency button until the control-room director confirmed the fault. Lin removed the copied field, but the remainder of the sequence still needed review. The force of habit had allowed a technical shortcut to play the role of policy, leaving no safety net when the copied field passed inspection.

室外，工人用胶带和锡封保护电缆箱。锤子放在码标旁，警铃在风中不断敲响。控制室主管确认故障前，任何人都不能按紧急按钮。林伟删除了复制字段，但序列的其余部分仍需检查。习惯的力量让一个技术捷径扮演了政策的角色，也让复制字段通过检查时没有任何安全网。

**5.** The team decided to get independent approval before reopening the runway. They would block unnecessary personal data, cross-check every card, and give each operator a clear phrase for reporting an error. Thus the delay became evidence rather than embarrassment. The airport remained safe, and the group gained time to rebuild the system without hiding what had happened.

团队决定在重新开放跑道前取得独立批准。他们会拦截不必要的个人数据，交叉核对每张卡片，并给每名操作员一句清晰的报错用语。因此，这次延误成了证据，而不是难堪。机场保持安全，团队也获得时间，在不隐瞒事件的前提下重建系统。

### Exercises

- CH090-Q1 [context] What did the visual cards expose?
  - options: a gap in the data sequence / a closed airport restaurant / a broken car door / a new international road
- CH090-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “剩余部分”?
  - options: remainder / contrast / column / runway
- CH090-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: privacy regulation / privacy hammer / privacy tin / privacy yard
- CH090-Q4 [cloze] The team would ______-check every card.
  - options: cross / press / beat / seal
- CH090-Q5 [recall] Why did the team delay reopening the runway?
  - options: to obtain independent approval / to buy a gold card / to move the wall / to shorten the road

### Answers

- CH090-Q1: a gap in the data sequence - The wall diagram showed that one link in the approval data was missing.
- CH090-Q2: remainder - Remainder means what is left after another part is removed or used.
- CH090-Q3: privacy regulation - Regulation naturally combines with privacy, safety, or industry rules.
- CH090-Q4: cross - Cross-check means verify information using another source.
- CH090-Q5: to obtain independent approval - The team chose verification before speed.

## CH091 - What the System Remembers: The Disagreement / 系统记住了什么：分歧

- theme: 科学、技术与互联网

**1.** The disagreement was about the level of the result. “If we extend the trial, the wait is permanent,” Maya said. “If we pull it back, the current of data will drag us to the start.” At the end of a long talk, four of the group held one view and the rest held another, and the period of the launch had already been extended twice.

分歧在于结果的层级。“如果我们延长试行，等待就变成永久的，”曼雅说，“如果我们把它撤回来，数据的潮流就会把我们拖回起点。”在一场漫长的谈话结束时，小组里四个人持一种看法，其余人持另一种，而发布的期限已经被延长了两次。

**2.** The action they agreed on was small: acquire one more sensor, restore the old lamp, and cap the pipe that emitted the raw carbon. The mask, the tissue, and the plain dot on the screen were the only evidence in the room, and the dim light made the skeleton of the machine look like a creature.

他们达成一致的行动很小：再取得一个传感器、修好那盏旧灯，并把那根排放生碳的管子盖上盖子。面具、薄纸，以及屏幕上那个平淡无奇的点，是屋里仅有的证据，而昏暗的灯光让机器的骨架看起来像一个生物。

**3.** A chemist in the biochemistry lab explained the mechanics of the failure: a lag in the acceleration of the reaction, an explosive mixture of hydrogen and gasoline vapour, and a pitch of the sensor that nobody had ever calibrated. Across the corridor, the busy lab and the quiet one were a mile apart in length but not in kind, and they had to get the answer together.

生物化学实验室的一位化学家解释了失效的机理：反应加速中的一段滞后、氢与汽油蒸气的爆炸性混合物，以及一个从未被校准过的传感器倾角。走廊对面，忙碌的实验室和安静的实验室在长度上相隔一英里，在性质上却并非如此，而他们必须一起得到答案。

**4.** “You may not quit,” the officer said. “The formality of a signature is not a cure.” The endless bounce of the cursor, the bright exit sign, and the small dots that circulated on the screen: it was a phenomenon that none of the four could name.

“你不可以辞职，”官员说，“签名的礼节不是一剂解药。”光标无休止地跳动、明亮的出口标志，以及屏幕上循环移动的小点：这是一种四个人都叫不出名字的现象。

**5.** They were asked to hold the line on one point only: nothing would be acquired, not one byte, without a written reason. At this elevation, with the anchorage of a hundred years of practice behind them, the answer was not going to be simple. One team would recover; one would not.

他们被要求只在一个点上守住底线：任何东西都不能被取得，一个字节也不行，除非有书面的理由。在这样的高度上，身后有一个世纪实践的锚地，答案不会简单。一支队伍会恢复过来；另一支不会。

**6.** Perhaps the launch would be off for a season; perhaps for good. The development of the service and the safety of the people in it were on the same table, and the time had come to choose which one would pay. Nobody in the room wanted to be the one who said it first. Across the table, the four of them could not get past the same question, and the end of the period was still on.

也许发布会停一个季度；也许是永久。这项服务的发展与其中人们的安全摆在同一张桌上，而选择谁付代价的时候到了。屋里没有人愿意做第一个说出这句话的人。桌子对面，他们四个人都绕不过同一个问题，而这个周期的终点仍然悬着。

### Exercises

- CH091-Q1 [context] What did Maya say would happen if they extended the trial?
  - options: the wait would become permanent / the data would be deleted / the lab would close / the sensor would fail
- CH091-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “现象”?
  - options: phenomenon / formality / anchorage / elevation
- CH091-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: restore the lamp / restore the mask / restore the dot / restore the skeleton
- CH091-Q4 [cloze] The officer said the formality of a signature is not a ______.
  - options: cure / lag / pitch / drag
- CH091-Q5 [recall] What single point did they agree to hold?
  - options: nothing would be acquired without a written reason / the launch would go ahead / the lab would be closed / the sensor would be replaced

### Answers

- CH091-Q1: the wait would become permanent - An open-ended extension would never end.
- CH091-Q2: phenomenon - The cursor behaviour was a phenomenon they could not name.
- CH091-Q3: restore the lamp - Restore combines with a lamp, a right, or a file.
- CH091-Q4: cure - A signature alone does not fix the underlying problem.
- CH091-Q5: nothing would be acquired without a written reason - They agreed on one non-negotiable rule.

## CH092 - What the System Remembers: What the Numbers Hide / 系统记住了什么：数字隐藏了什么

- theme: 科学、技术与互联网

**1.** The numbers hid the main product: trust. A list of four hundred tokens, a standard of measurement that nobody had signed, and a principle that the group had agreed on in the morning were all that stood between the service and the next audit.

这些数字掩盖了主要的产品：信任。一份四百个代币的清单、一项没人签署过的计量标准，以及小组在早上达成的一条原则，就是这项服务与下一次审计之间的全部屏障。

**2.** The theory was elegant and the practice was not. An object in the system could be exchanged for a key, a key for a match, a match for a wheel of electricity, and the whole exchange bound the user to a contract nobody had read. “A flag on a digital form is not a signature,” the analyst said.

理论是优雅的，实践不是。系统中的一个物体可以换一把钥匙，一把钥匙换一场比赛，一场比赛换一个电力的轮子，而整个交换把用户绑定到一份没人读过的合同上。“数字表格上的一面旗子不是签名，”分析师说。

**3.** The alarm had been patched twice, and the patch had a defect. A reverse of the battery polarity in the assembly, a crystal in the elevator sensor, and a developer who had left the company: the assembly line of small errors had one characteristic in common, and it was invisible to the accounting.

警报被修补过两次，而那个补丁有一个缺点。装配中电池极性的反接、电梯传感器里的一块晶体，以及一位已经离职的开发者：这条小错误的装配线上有一个共同特征，而它对会计是不可见的。

**4.** The instrument that measured the response had a zero point nobody had calibrated since the previous century. “If the base is wrong, the transformation is theatre,” the regulator said. A calendar of releases, an anchor for the bracket, and a token in the conductor's hand: the automation was only as good as the person who watched it.

测量反应的那台仪器，自上个世纪以来就没有人校准过它的零点。“如果基点是错的，那个变换就是戏剧，”监管者说。一份发布日历、一个支架的锚点，以及指挥者手中的一个代币：自动化的好坏，只取决于看着它的那个人。

**5.** The intermediate step, which everyone wanted to skip, was the plain one: ask the user what the light on the sun-facing panel actually meant to them. After the third pass, finally, they pulled the report and waited at the gate for the answer — and the answer was not in the numbers at all. At the end, the level of detail was the problem. “AIDS does not care about our calendar,” the analyst said. “We might analyze a thousand results and still not thank the person who waited.” The engine of the current report, the conductor of the morning shift, and the panel facing the sun: none of them could tell down from up in the chart. A century of accounting had taught them one thing: the standard is the instrument, and the instrument is the person holding it in the morning.

每个人都想跳过的那一步，恰恰是最朴素的一步：去问用户，那块朝向太阳的面板上的灯，对他们究竟意味着什么。第三轮之后，他们终于抽回了报告，在门口等待答案——而答案根本不在数字里。到最后，细节的层级本身就是问题。“艾滋病不在乎我们的日历，”分析师说，“我们也许分析了一千个结果，却仍然不会感谢那个等待的人。”这份现行报告的引擎、早班的售票员，以及朝太阳的那块面板：它们中没有一个能在图表里分清上和下。一个世纪的会计学教会了他们一件事：标准就是仪器，而仪器就是早上拿着它的那个人。

### Exercises

- CH092-Q1 [context] What main product did the numbers hide?
  - options: trust / a calendar / a battery / a crystal
- CH092-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “缺陷；缺点”?
  - options: defect / reverse / anchor / bracket
- CH092-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: calibrate an instrument / calibrate a token / calibrate a flag / calibrate a calendar
- CH092-Q4 [cloze] The analyst said a flag on a digital form is not a ______.
  - options: signature / battery / crystal / wheel
- CH092-Q5 [recall] What was the step everyone wanted to skip?
  - options: asking the user what the light meant / replacing the battery / patching the alarm / calibrating the elevator

### Answers

- CH092-Q1: trust - Trust was the real product the numbers concealed.
- CH092-Q2: defect - The patch itself carried a defect.
- CH092-Q3: calibrate an instrument - Calibrate combines with an instrument, a sensor, or a scale.
- CH092-Q4: signature - A digital flag is not informed consent.
- CH092-Q5: asking the user what the light meant - The plain step was the one everyone skipped.

## CH093 - What the System Remembers: A Better Question / 系统记住了什么：更好的问题

- theme: 科学、技术与互联网

**1.** A better question arrived as a message on a laptop in the middle of a long period of development: does this system enable a person to leave as easily as it enables them to join? The group had drawn up a summary of the program, but nobody had written the exit line.

一个更好的问题在一段漫长开发期的中途，以笔记本电脑上的一条消息到来：这个系统让人离开，是否和让人加入一样容易？小组拟出了程序的一份摘要，却没有人写下退出的那一行。

**2.** The precise measurement of privacy turned out to be simple: the number of steps required to cancel an account. A summary with numerous footnotes, a brilliant formulation of the principle, and a heap of approvals from the main office were useless if the answer was forty-seven clicks on a tablet.

对隐私的精确测量原来很简单：注销一个账户需要多少步。一份带众多脚注的摘要、一条精彩的原理表述，以及总部的一堆批准，如果答案是平板上四十七次点击，那全都是无用的。

**3.** The false assumption was that simplicity is a weakness. “Simplicity is not the superiority of the lazy,” the analyst said. “It is the only thing a slender budget can buy.” A vitamin company had asked for an attribute they could not explain, and a dating service had asked for a beverage sponsor: both wanted data they could not justify.

那个错误的假设是：简单是一种弱点。“简单不是懒惰者的优越，”分析师说，“它是微薄的预算唯一买得到的东西。”一家维生素公司要求一个他们解释不了的属性，一家约会服务要求一个饮料赞助商：两者都想要他们无法自证的数据。

**4.** The paradox was mathematical. In arithmetic, a zero costs nothing; in privacy, a zero is the only permissible value for the data you do not need. A cardboard box, a torch, and a calculator on the desk made the point better than the geometry on the whiteboard.

这个悖论是数学性的。在算术里，一个零不花任何成本；在隐私里，零是你不需要的数据唯一可容许的取值。桌上一个纸板箱、一支火把和一台计算器，比白板上的几何更好地说明了这一点。

**5.** The consensus was to minimize, not to delete: every field would need an owner, and every owner would need to fold their data into one page. The elegance of the new form was that a user could read it aloud; the true test of a high-tech system is whether a microscopic line of password text can be explained by the person who typed it.

共识是最小化，而不是删除：每个字段都要有一个负责人，而每个负责人都要把自己的数据折成一页。新表格的优雅之处在于用户能把它朗读出来；一个高科技系统真正的考验，在于输入密码的那个人能否解释那一行显微镜般细小的文字。

**6.** The networking diagram had a shadow of its own: a fertilizer of small features that nobody had asked for. “We cannot solve a trust problem with an extra field,” Maya wrote. “We can only stop adding them.” The next revision would be shorter than the previous one, and that would be the whole argument. It was difficult to see the manipulation in a form that looked so plain; the math was renewable, but an astronomical budget is not. The effort to get the product to the people who need it, to provide the music of a simple interface, and to reach them in time was the real design work.

那张网络图有它自己的影子：一堆没人要求过的小功能，像肥料一样堆着。“我们无法用多加一个字段来解决信任问题，”曼雅写道，“我们只能停止继续加。”下一次修订会比上一版更短，而这就是全部的论证。很难在一份看起来如此朴素的东西里看出那种操纵；数学是可以更新的，而一笔天文数字的预算不是。把产品送到需要它的人手上、为一个简单界面提供那种音乐感，并及时触达他们——这才是真正的设计工作。

### Exercises

- CH093-Q1 [context] What was the better question?
  - options: does the system let a person leave as easily as join / how many fields can we add / who owns the laptop / which sponsor pays best
- CH093-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “悖论；自相矛盾的话”?
  - options: paradox / consensus / formulation / attribute
- CH093-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: cancel an account / cancel a vitamin / cancel a beverage / cancel a torch
- CH093-Q4 [cloze] The analyst said simplicity is not the superiority of the ______.
  - options: lazy / slender / brilliant / microscopic
- CH093-Q5 [recall] What did Maya say they could only do?
  - options: stop adding fields / delete the system / hire a sponsor / shorten the password

### Answers

- CH093-Q1: does the system let a person leave as easily as join - She tested the system by its exit, not its entrance.
- CH093-Q2: paradox - Zero cost in arithmetic, zero value in privacy.
- CH093-Q3: cancel an account - Cancel combines with an account, a plan, or a meeting.
- CH093-Q4: lazy - He defended simplicity as economy, not laziness.
- CH093-Q5: stop adding fields - She argued for subtraction rather than invention.

## CH094 - What the System Remembers: The Second Attempt / 系统记住了什么：第二次尝试

- theme: 科学、技术与互联网

**1.** The second attempt began with a bit of routine maintenance and a kilo of new cable. Its power came from a smaller motor, a quieter drive, and an electronic mixture of sensors that could be arranged in any order. “If the component fails, the whole activity stops,” the engineer said. “That is the point.”

第二次尝试从一点例行维护和一公斤新电缆开始。它的动力来自一台更小的马达、更安静的驱动，以及一种可以任意排列的电子传感器混合物。“如果组件失效，整个活动就停下，”工程师说，“这正是要点。”

**2.** The concert in the park, the bush beside the path, and the script of the presentation were all part of the same routine. The intensity of the mechanical work was low; the intensity of the mathematics was not. A trigger in the software, a hook in the interface, and a chaos of small updates had to be replaced by feedback.

公园里的音乐会、小径旁的灌木，以及演示的脚本，都属于同一套例行安排。机械工作的强度不高；数学的强度则不然。软件里的一个触发器、界面上的一个钩子，以及一团混乱的小更新，都必须被反馈取代。

**3.** The laser scanned the surface, the gravity sensor dipped, and the suspension of the scan was logged twice. “Consistency is not repetition,” the analyst said. “A repeated test that changes nothing proves nothing.” An odor of burnt plastic, a graphic of the new methodology, and a projector that erased a digit: the lab was learning to trust its own instruments.

激光扫描表面，重力传感器下沉，扫描的悬停被记录了两次。“一致不是重复，”分析师说，“一个什么都没改变的重复杂测试，什么都证明不了。”一股烧焦塑料的气味、一张新方法论的图表，以及一台抹掉了一位数字的放映机：实验室正在学着信任自己的仪器。

**4.** The voltage had to be upgraded before the consolidation of the two servers. An elimination of three old linkages, a cohesion of the remaining ones, and a relativity of standards between the two labs made the merge slower than anyone expected.

在两台服务器合并之前，电压必须先升级。消除三条旧的连接、剩下几条的内聚，以及两个实验室之间标准的相对性，让这次合并比任何人预想的都慢。

**5.** As an end to the attempt, they arranged one public session: a pull of the plug, a message on the screen, and a current of questions from the audience. “We can enable the exit,” Maya said. “We cannot enable the trust.” It was a level of honesty that the training had not prepared them for, and they waited at the end of the day for the result.

作为这次尝试的结束，他们安排了一场公开会议：拔掉插头、屏幕上的一条消息，以及观众席涌来的一股问题。“我们能够开通退出，”曼雅说，“我们无法开通信任。”这是训练没能让他们准备好的一种诚实程度，而他们在一天结束时等待着结果。

**6.** The mile of cable, the wait of a week, and the pick of the third option were, in the end, the same decision: to keep the system small enough to be explained by the person it remembers. It was an attempt at something small, and the training had prepared them for none of it. The current had turned; the force of the argument was on their side, and they could enable the end they wanted without a fight. A capture of one screen and a consolidate of two registers: the last step was to make the record smaller than the thing it described.

那一英里电缆、那一周的等待，以及对第三个选项的选择，说到底都是同一个决定：让这个系统小到能被它所记住的那个人解释清楚。这是一次针对某种小东西的尝试，而训练没有让他们对其中任何一样做好准备。潮流已经转向；论证的力量在他们这一边，而他们可以不必争斗就促成他们想要的结局。截取一个屏幕、合并两份登记簿：最后一步，是让记录比它所描述的东西更小。

### Exercises

- CH094-Q1 [context] What did the engineer say happens if the component fails?
  - options: the whole activity stops / the cable melts / the park closes / the projector starts
- CH094-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “一致性；黏稠度”?
  - options: consistency / cohesion / linkage / relativity
- CH094-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: upgrade the voltage / upgrade the odor / upgrade the bush / upgrade the script
- CH094-Q4 [cloze] What did the analyst say an unchanged repetition proves?
  - options: nothing / everything / the methodology / the digit
- CH094-Q5 [recall] What could the group enable, and what could it not?
  - options: the exit, but not the trust / the trust, but not the exit / the laser, but not the motor / the concert, but not the park

### Answers

- CH094-Q1: the whole activity stops - The design deliberately fails as a whole.
- CH094-Q2: consistency - She distinguished consistency from mere repetition.
- CH094-Q3: upgrade the voltage - Upgrade combines with voltage, a system, or a version.
- CH094-Q4: nothing - An unchanged repeat carries no information.
- CH094-Q5: the exit, but not the trust - They could build the mechanism, not the confidence.

## CH095 - What the System Remembers: Outside Pressure / 系统记住了什么：外部压力

- theme: 科学、技术与互联网

**1.** The outside pressure came as a session of the faculty board, where a clear definition was demanded of the term consent. “Your outline must include the literal spelling of every field,” the director said. “A copy of the form is not a transaction.” The four of them had to speak to a room that held all the power and none of the facts.

外部压力以院务委员会的一次会议形式到来，会上要求对“同意”这个词给出一个清楚的定义。“你们的大纲必须包含每个字段的字面拼写，”主管说，“表格的一份副本不是一笔交易。”他们四个人不得不对着一间掌握着全部权力、却不掌握任何事实的房间说话。

**2.** The gap between the border of the law and the boundary of decency was the real subject. A baseball game on the radio, a graph on the wall, and a symbol of the university above the entrance: the interval between them was the space where the project lived. Health data was not included in the review, and that was exactly the point.

法律边界与体面边界之间的那道缺口，才是真正的主题。收音机里的一场棒球赛、墙上的一幅图表，以及入口上方大学的那个象征：它们之间的间隔，就是项目所栖身的空间。健康数据没有被纳入复审，而这恰恰是重点。

**3.** My reluctance was not about compliance. “We must undertake a renovation of the whole process,” Maya said. “We cannot fetch consent with a modification of the wording.” A thief of small permissions, a scatter of skipped steps, and a detection system that nobody trusted: this was penetration by habit, not by attack.

我的不情愿与合规无关。“我们必须对整个过程做一次革新的翻修，”曼雅说，“我们无法靠改动措辞来取得同意。”一个小额许可的小偷、一片被跳过的步骤的散落，以及一个没人信任的探测系统：这是习惯造成的渗透，而不是攻击。

**4.** The view from the lab was different. “Put the standardization in place, normalize the browser logs, and the coherence will follow,” the engineer said, with a confidence that the data did not support. The empty shelf, the late submission, and the walk to the door were, to him, a subtraction of detail.

从实验室看出去的观点不同。“把标准化落实到位，把浏览器的日志规范化，连贯性自然就有了，”工程师说，带着一种数据并不支持的自信。那个空架子、那次迟交，以及走到门口的那段路，在他看来都只是细节的减法。

**5.** The gravitation of the argument pulled them toward conformity: rank the fields, divide the risks, and file the rest. “Not off, not on,” Lin wrote. “The system must hold a parallel record of what we refused to collect.” The talk in the corridor was that the board would stop the project anyway.

这场论证的引力把他们拉向遵从：给字段排序、把风险分开，其余的归档。“既不是关，也不是开，”林伟写道，“系统必须保留一份平行记录，记下我们拒绝收集的东西。”走廊里的说法是，董事会无论如何都会叫停这个项目。

**6.** Its action, in the end, was small: a summary of four pages, a perspective on the person, and a statement of what the project would not do. “If you cannot understand it, you cannot consent to it,” she said. The game was not over, and the walk back to the lab was long. The significance of the start was that they had to resume the work with fewer people, and the four of them could not agree on where to begin.

它最终的行动很小：四页纸的摘要、一个关于当事人的视角，以及一份说明这个项目不会做什么的声明。“如果你无法理解它，你就无法同意它，”她说。这场游戏还没有结束，而回实验室的那段路很长。这个开端的重要意义在于：他们必须用更少的人重新开始这项工作，而他们四个人在从哪里开始时意见不一。

### Exercises

- CH095-Q1 [context] What did the board demand a clear definition of?
  - options: consent / the graph / the baseball game / the shelf
- CH095-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “遵从；顺从”?
  - options: compliance / penetration / reluctance / coherence
- CH095-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: undertake a renovation / undertake a thief / undertake a scatter / undertake a graph
- CH095-Q4 [cloze] The director said a copy of the form is not a ______.
  - options: transaction / boundary / symbol / session
- CH095-Q5 [recall] What did Lin say the system must keep?
  - options: a parallel record of what we refused to collect / a copy of every form / a graph of every field / a rank of every risk

### Answers

- CH095-Q1: consent - The board wanted consent defined precisely.
- CH095-Q2: compliance - Her reluctance was not about obeying the rules.
- CH095-Q3: undertake a renovation - Undertake combines with a renovation, a task, or a risk.
- CH095-Q4: transaction - A form is not the same as a concluded agreement.
- CH095-Q5: a parallel record of what we refused to collect - He wanted the refusals documented too.

## CH096 - What the System Remembers: The Decision / 系统记住了什么：决定

- theme: 科学、技术与互联网

**1.** The decision was made in the basement of the lab, in a room with a mop in the corner and a full row of boxes on the shelf. “We begin with less and add more only when the term is clear,” Maya said. “That is the deal.” The board had asked for a second version, and the preparation for it took the whole summer.

决定是在实验室的地下室做出的，房间里角落立着一把拖把，架子上整整齐齐一排箱子。“我们以更少开始，只有当术语清楚了才增加更多，”曼雅说，“这就是约定。”董事会要求出第二版，而为此的准备花掉了整个夏天。

**2.** The technique was to cut the form at the centre and reduce it to one page. A guide for the staff, a layer of plain language over every field, and an exclusive cycle of review were the whole scheme. “Count the steps a person has to take,” she said. “That number is the scale of our respect.”

方法是把表格从中间切开，缩减到一页。一份给员工的指南、覆盖每个字段的一层平实语言，以及一个排他性的复审循环，就是整个方案。“数一数一个人要走多少步，”她说，“那个数字就是我们尊重的尺度。”

**3.** The smell of coffee and the smell of burnt dust came up from the pipes; the transmission of a report from the top floor was interrupted twice. “The trend is linear and the probability is not,” the analyst said, with caution. “A tremendous burst of enthusiasm is not continuity.”

咖啡的气味和烧焦灰尘的气味从管道里涌上来；从顶层传来的一份报告被打断了两次。“趋势是线性的，而概率不是，”分析师谨慎地说，“一阵巨大的热情不是连续性。”

**4.** The justification for every line had to be written beside it. “Do not organize the fields by department,” Lin said. “Organize them by the question a person asks.” A roller of tape, a footprint of mud by the door, and a modifier that changed the meaning of a sentence: the details of a serious redesign.

每一行的正当理由都必须写在它旁边。“不要按部门来组织字段，”林伟说，“按一个人会问的问题来组织。”一卷胶带、门口一个泥泞的脚印，以及一个改变句意的修饰语：这些就是一次严肃重设计的细节。

**5.** The jump from four fields to one was the hardest part. “We cannot interrupt the launch forever,” the director said. “A board will not watch a disintegration in slow motion.” He was right, and the group had to put the decision in writing by the next session.

从四个字段跳到一个是难的部分。“我们不能永远中断发布，”主管说，“董事会不会看着一场慢动作的瓦解。”他说得对，而小组必须在下次会议之前把决定写成书面。

**6.** The scene they agreed on was quiet: enter the room, look at the board, and start with the smallest version. If the nation wanted more, it would have to say so in the language of the form itself. The center of the argument was easy to see from the side of the room: the pattern would not change unless they intensified the pressure, and a large drop in the number of fields would include nothing but the essentials. A war of memos had already begun. The view from the basement was short, but the pattern was clear.
