他们商定的场景很安静：进屋、看一眼板子，然后从最小的版本开始。如果这个国家想要更多，就得用表格自己的语言说出来。从房间的这一侧看，这场论证的中心很容易看清：除非他们加大压力，否则模式不会改变；而字段数量的大幅削减，将只包含最必要的东西。一场备忘文件的战争已经开始了。从地下室看出去的视野很短，但模式是清楚的。

### Exercises

- CH096-Q1 [context] What did Maya say they would begin with?
  - options: less, and add more only when the term is clear / a full row of boxes / a new basement / a second board meeting
- CH096-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “正当理由；辩护”?
  - options: justification / preparation / probability / transmission
- CH096-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: reduce a form / reduce a mop / reduce a roller / reduce a footprint
- CH096-Q4 [cloze] The analyst warned that a tremendous burst of enthusiasm is not ______.
  - options: continuity / caution / a trend / a scale
- CH096-Q5 [recall] How did Lin want the fields organised?
  - options: by the question a person asks / by department / by cost / by alphabet

### Answers

- CH096-Q1: less, and add more only when the term is clear - They chose subtraction first.
- CH096-Q2: justification - Every line needed a written reason beside it.
- CH096-Q3: reduce a form - Reduce combines with a form, a cost, or a risk.
- CH096-Q4: continuity - Enthusiasm is not the same as steady practice.
- CH096-Q5: by the question a person asks - He organised the form around the user's question.

## CH097 - What the System Remembers: Consequences / 系统记住了什么：后果

- theme: 科学、技术与互联网

**1.** The consequences arrived as a long list about the difference between a database and a memory. A camera in the corridor, a recording of a phone call, and an entry in the log: the extent of what the system kept was larger than its developers had realised, and the recovery of deleted files was easier than anyone wanted to admit.

后果以一份关于数据库与记忆之间差异的长清单形式到来。走廊里的一台摄像机、一次通话的录音，以及日志里的一条记录：系统所保留的范围比它的开发者意识到的更大，而被删除文件的恢复比任何人愿意承认的都容易。

**2.** The creation of a new norm took a shelf, a locker, and a supervisor. “Adjust the layout, modify the form, and repeat the test,” the supervisor said. “A scratch on a disk is a hazard, not a highlight.” A mechanic with a brandy in his hand and a noisy variant of the old procedure on his desk: the same story in two forms.

一个新规范的创建用掉了一个架子、一个储物柜和一位主管。“调整布局、修改表格，并重复测试，”主管说，“磁盘上的一道划痕是危险，不是亮点。”一位手拿白兰地的技工，以及他桌上一份嘈杂的旧流程变体：同一个故事的两种形式。

**3.** The fluctuation in the data was the contention of the whole week. A sip of whisky, a pear on the plate, and a video of a chemistry demonstration: the system had recorded all of it, and none of it had been declared. The microscope of the imaging unit could see an electron; it could not see the question.

数据的波动是整周的争点。一小口威士忌、盘子里一只梨，以及一段化学演示的视频：系统把这一切都记录了下来，而其中没有一项被申报过。成像部门的显微镜能看到一个电子；它看不到那个问题。

**4.** The reinforcement of the rule was, in the end, a matter of photography and petroleum. A recorder in the corner, a radar on the roof, and an up-to-date list of what was affordable: the group standardised the visible part and automated the invisible one.

这条规则的强化，说到底与摄影和石油有关。角落里的一台录音机、屋顶上的一台雷达，以及一份最新的、可负担的清单：小组把可见的部分标准化，把不可见的部分自动化。

**5.** The exhaustion of the team was the price. An intruder had negated three weeks of work with one wrong file, and the group had to begin the period again. “More of the same will not do it,” Maya wrote. “We must do less, and do it long.”

团队的耗尽是代价。一个入侵者用一份错误的文件否定了三周的工作，小组不得不重新开始这个周期。“再多做同样的事没有用，”曼雅写道，“我们必须做得更少，并且做得更久。”

**6.** The power of the small decision, its ability to become a routine, was the lesson of the term. About the full report, the group had one line to add at the end: a difference is not a defect, and a defect is not a difference. A painting on the wall, a tip from the supervisor, and an adjustment of the recording standard: the group would standardize the visible part. The camera in the entry, the extent of the database, and the creation of a second recovery point all had to be reviewed before the period ended. In time, the country would get the development it was paying for, or it would get a smaller bill instead.

那个小决定的力量，以及它成长为一种日常的能力，是这一学期的教训。关于那份完整的报告，小组只在末尾加了一行：差异不是缺陷，缺陷也不是差异。墙上的一幅画、主管的一句提示，以及对记录标准的一次调整：小组要把可见的部分标准化。入口处的摄像机、数据库的范围，以及第二个恢复点的创建，都必须在周期结束前被复查。到时候，这个国家会得到它正在付钱买的发展，否则它会得到一张更小的账单。

### Exercises

- CH097-Q1 [context] What was larger than the developers had realised?
  - options: the extent of what the system kept / the number of supervisors / the price of petroleum / the size of the microscope
- CH097-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “争点；争论”?
  - options: contention / fluctuation / reinforcement / exhaustion
- CH097-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: adjust the layout / adjust the brandy / adjust the pear / adjust the whisky
- CH097-Q4 [cloze] The supervisor said a scratch on a disk is a hazard, not a ______.
  - options: highlight / variant / recorder / locker
- CH097-Q5 [recall] What line did the group add to the report?
  - options: a difference is not a defect / more is better / the system must grow / the database is memory

### Answers

- CH097-Q1: the extent of what the system kept - The system retained far more than intended.
- CH097-Q2: contention - The fluctuations were the week's central dispute.
- CH097-Q3: adjust the layout - Adjust combines with a layout, a plan, or a setting.
- CH097-Q4: highlight - Damage is a risk, not a feature.
- CH097-Q5: a difference is not a defect - They refused to treat variation as failure.

## CH098 - What the System Remembers: The Bridge Forward / 系统记住了什么：通向下一程

- theme: 科学、技术与互联网

**1.** The seventh arc closed with a scheme that fitted into a bucket. Ten fields, a pause on the launch, and a capacity of one page: the implementation of the new form was so small that the director laughed. “A scientist would call this a minor result,” he said. “A physicist would call it a clean one.”

第七段弧线以一个能装进一只桶的方案收尾。十个字段、发布的一次暂停，以及一页纸的容量：新表格的实施如此之小，连主管都笑了。“科学家会把这叫做一个次要的结果，”他说，“物理学家会把它叫做一个干净的结果。”

**2.** The route to the review was even shorter than they expected. A mistake in the old category list, a loss of two weeks, and an enormous inquiry from the national office: none of it changed the output. “Specify the minimum, assign the maximum, and fill the rest with nothing,” Maya said.

通往复审的路线比他们预想的更短。旧类别清单里的一个错误、两周的损失，以及国家办公室一份庞大的质询：这些都没有改变产出。“规定最小值、指派最大值，其余的用空来填，”曼雅说。

**3.** The housing of the servers, the continuous hum of the virtual machines, and the aged lorry that brought the new equipment: the physical part of the system had a duration of about ten years. “Put the restriction in the code, not in the contract,” the engineer said. “Compatibility is a promise we can keep.”

服务器的外壳、虚拟机持续不断的嗡鸣，以及运来新设备的旧卡车：系统物理部分的持续时间大约十年。“把限制写进代码，而不是写进合同，”工程师说，“兼容性是一个我们能守住的承诺。”

**4.** The contamination of one dataset by another was a small bomb in the middle of the file. “We can undo this,” the analyst said. “We cannot undo a person's loss of trust with a query.” The quantity of champagne ordered for the launch was, in the end, a minus rather than a plus.

一份数据集被另一份污染，是文件中央的一枚小炸弹。“这个我们可以撤销，”分析师说，“但我们无法用一次查询撤销一个人失去的信任。”为发布订购的香槟数量，说到底是一个减号，而不是加号。

**5.** The group came back to the same question: who is the system for? The wait at the entrance, the level of detail on the form, and the clear view of the exit were the whole answer. “If it includes us, it will include the person after us,” Lin said, and followed the last line of the report to its end.

小组回到同一个问题：这个系统是为谁做的？入口处的等待、表格上细节的层级，以及出口那一目了然的视野，就是全部的答案。“如果它包含我们，它也会包含我们之后的那个人，”林伟说，并一路跟着报告的最后一行读到了结尾。

**6.** The inquiry would continue, the national list would grow, and the next period would begin with a smaller system than the one they had pulled apart. That was the result they could put in front of the board — and the one they could defend. The difference between a scheme and a mistake is the loss it can absorb; the route to the review, the category of the output, and the housing of the servers all had to fit inside a capacity of one page. The national inquiry would expand, the restriction would be specified, and our continuous coverage would be measured in weeks. A quota every week, an upright posture at the desk, and a frequency of ten checks a month would imply a smaller and cleaner system, and nothing here would kill the project. The start of the next period would be smaller, and that was the point of the whole exercise. The system remembered less and the trust grew; that was the only exchange rate that ever worked.

质询会继续，全国清单会变长，而下一个周期会从一个比他们拆开的那个更小的系统开始。这就是他们能摆到董事会面前的结果——也是他们能为之辩护的那一个。一个方案与一个错误之间的差别，在于它能吸收多大的损失；通往复审的路径、产出的类别，以及服务器的外壳，都必须装进一页纸的容量里。全国质询会扩大，限制会被明确写出，而我们的持续覆盖将以周为单位计量。每周一个配额、在桌前挺直的姿态，以及每月十次检查的频率：这些意味着一套更小、更干净的系统，而这里没有任何一样会杀死这个项目。下一个周期的开始会更小，而这就是整件事的意义所在。

### Exercises

- CH098-Q1 [context] How large was the new scheme?
  - options: it fitted into a bucket / it filled the basement / it needed ten lorries / it required a new building
- CH098-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “持续时间”?
  - options: duration / capacity / coverage / quantity
- CH098-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: assign a quota / assign a bucket / assign a lorry / assign a pause
- CH098-Q4 [cloze] The analyst said a loss of trust cannot be undone with a ______.
  - options: query / bucket / quota / minus
- CH098-Q5 [recall] What did Lin say would happen if the system includes them?
  - options: it will include the person after us / it will grow faster / it will need more servers / it will be delayed again

### Answers

- CH098-Q1: it fitted into a bucket - The scheme was deliberately tiny.
- CH098-Q2: duration - The physical equipment would last about ten years.
- CH098-Q3: assign a quota - Assign combines with a quota, a task, or a role.
- CH098-Q4: query - Trust cannot be restored by a database command.
- CH098-Q5: it will include the person after us - He tested inclusion by the next user, not the current one.

## CH099 - The River Answers Back: A Signal / 河流的回应：一个信号

- theme: 环境、自然与可持续发展

**1.** They had just deleted a system for remembering too much; the river did not care what they had learned. The eighth arc began with a slight shake in the base of the field station, and within an hour the river was pouring over the road. “We need to define the flood level, not merely to desire it,” the director said. Almost every gauge was reading a number that was nearly the same as the last, and none of them was consistent with the water in the yard.

他们刚刚删掉了一个记得太多的系统；而这条河并不在乎他们学到了什么。第八段弧线以野外站地基的一阵轻微震动开始，一小时内河水就漫过了道路。“我们需要给洪水水位下一个定义，而不是只去渴望它，”主管说。几乎每一个水位计读出的数字都与上一个差不多，而没有哪一个与院子里的水情一致。

**2.** A substantial part of the old model was built on a photographic memory of ten dry years. “The yield is not proportional to the rain,” a leading hydrologist said. “The main product of a bad model is suspicion.” He watched the water with a grin that was almost fearful.

旧模型相当大的一部分建立在十年干旱的照相式记忆上。“产量并不与降雨成比例，”一位主要的水文学家说，“一个糟糕模型的主要产物是怀疑。”他带着一种几乎可以说是恐惧的咧嘴笑看着水面。

**3.** The unique thing about the district was its aptitude for holding water: ample ponds, a sizable canal, and a spontaneous habit of keeping the ditches clear. “You cannot eliminate a flood,” he said. “You can irrigate a field with it.” A weird, dreamy light on the water made the whole scene feel illusory, and the team could not render it in numbers.

这个地区独特之处在于它蓄水的天资：充足的池塘、一条相当大的水渠，以及一种自发保持沟渠畅通的习惯。“你无法消除洪水，”他说，“你可以用它来灌溉一块田。”水面上那怪异而梦幻的光让整个场景显得像是幻觉，而团队无法把它换算成数字。

**4.** While the engineers sweated over the diagnosis, a legendary boatman diagnosed the problem in one sentence: the river was capable of a third channel, and the district had withdrawn from it. “Incidentally, that is a rendering of the same story my grandfather told,” he said, and he was thirsty for someone to believe him.

当工程师们为诊断而满头大汗时，一位传奇般的船夫用一句话诊断了问题：这条河有能力形成第三条河道，而这个地区已经从那里退了出来。“顺便说一句，这正是我祖父讲过的同一个故事的翻译，”他说，而他渴望有人相信他。

**5.** The report was shaky and the agreement was agreeable only in name. “Add the local readings and cut the modelled ones,” Maya said. “The implicit assumption is that the river is incidental to our data. It is not.” Approximately a hundred people had signed, and they could agree on one point: their accounts were more consistent than the instruments.

报告是摇晃不稳的，“一致”也只是名义上的一致。“把本地的读数加进去，把建模的数字砍掉，”曼雅说，“那个含蓄的假设是：河流对我们的数据只是附带的。它不是。”大约有一百人签了名，而他们能在一点上取得一致：他们的叙述比仪器更连贯。

**6.** Down at the field station, the team began to pass the old model through a smaller filter: even a ten per cent error was too much when a full street was under water, and less would have to mean more. The director left the door open for the way back, and nobody closed it. The term was significant: a similar event had happened once before, and nobody had written it down.

在野外站里，团队开始让旧模型通过一个更小的过滤器：当一整条街都在水下时，哪怕百分之十的误差也太大，而“更少”必须意味着“更多”。主管为返程留了门开着，谁也没有去关。这个学期是重要的：类似的事件从前发生过一次，而没有人把它写下来。

### Exercises

- CH099-Q1 [context] What did the director say they needed to do about the flood level?
  - options: define it, not merely desire it / ignore it / photograph it / sell it
- CH099-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “天资；才能”?
  - options: aptitude / suspicion / rendering / diagnosis
- CH099-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: eliminate an error / eliminate a grin / eliminate a pond / eliminate a boatman
- CH099-Q4 [cloze] The hydrologist said the main product of a bad model is ______.
  - options: suspicion / yield / amplitude / moisture
- CH099-Q5 [recall] What did the boatman say the river was capable of?
  - options: a third channel / a new dam / a longer bridge / a dry summer

### Answers

- CH099-Q1: define it, not merely desire it - He wanted a stated threshold, not a wish.
- CH099-Q2: aptitude - The district had a natural talent for holding water.
- CH099-Q3: eliminate an error - Eliminate combines with an error, a risk, or a cost.
- CH099-Q4: suspicion - A weak model produces doubt rather than prediction.
- CH099-Q5: a third channel - The river had a route the district had abandoned.

## CH100 - The River Answers Back: The First Promise / 河流的回应：第一次承诺

- theme: 环境、自然与可持续发展

**1.** The first promise was made while the water was still high, and it was almost too simple to be believed: the field station would measure the river from the north bank and the east bank at the same time. “A couple of gauges are not a system,” the director said, “but they are a step, and we need a step.”

第一个承诺是在水位还很高的时候做出的，而且它几乎简单到令人难以置信：野外站将同时在北岸和东岸测量这条河。“一对水位计算不上一个系统，”主管说，“但它们是第一步，而我们需要一步。”

**2.** The natural sciences had a different language from the local one, and the difference was nearly a gulf. “You bring me ice and temperature and a computer program,” a farmer said, “and I bring you an ear for what the water is doing.” The geologist agreed, and the two of them began to write the same list.

自然科学与本地语言不同，而这种差异几乎成了一道鸿沟。“你给我冰、温度和一套电脑程序，”一位农民说，“而我给你一只能听懂水在做什么的耳朵。”地质学家同意了，两人开始写同一份清单。

**3.** The kit was odd but distinct: a spade, a cage of bamboo for the bank, a permit for the pit, and a gentle hose that could bake nothing but mud. “Contrary to the report, the tiger and the bee are not the problem,” the farmer said. “The weed is.”

装备古怪但各有不同：一把铲子、一个竹笼护岸、一份开挖坑的许可，以及一根只能把泥烤硬的温和水管。“与报告相反，老虎和蜜蜂不是问题，”农民说，“杂草才是。”

**4.** The insight that followed was accidental, and it was a matter of soda and onion: the samples had been taken from a picnic spot where the water was barely moving. A survivor of the last flood described the swell at the mouth of the drain, and the team wrote it down as a source.

随之而来的洞见是偶然的，而且与苏打水和洋葱有关：样本取自一处水面几乎不动的野餐地点。一位上次洪水的幸存者描述了排水口处的涌浪，团队把它作为一条来源记录了下来。

**5.** The promise was to breed a small, boring, useful habit: one reading a day, at the same hour, from a vegetarian cook, a hen keeper, and a beekeeper, all of them equipped with the same cheap kit. “Nutrient levels and shrimp counts are fine,” the director said, “but the data has to come from the people who live on the bank.”

这个承诺是培育一个微小、无聊而又有用的习惯：每天同一时刻读一次数，由一位素食厨师、一位养母鸡的人和一位养蜂人来做，他们配备同样的廉价工具。“营养水平和虾的数量都很好，”主管说，“但数据必须来自住在岸边的人。”

**6.** The altitude of the station explained nothing and the technology explained everything else. By the end of the week, the promise had a shape: five families, one form, and a register that nobody could quietly correct. The essence of it was naturally boring: a drink of water, a bowl of peas, and a pipe of tobacco were the farmer's whole diet. A deaf neighbour and a criminal record in one family made the map of the district stranger than a church or a god, and the new readings were similar to the ones from the east bank. A pop of static from the radio ended the meeting.

站点的海拔什么也解释不了，而技术解释了其余的一切。到那一周结束时，这个承诺有了形状：五个家庭、一张表格，以及一份谁也无法悄悄修改的登记簿。它的本质天然就是无聊的：一杯水、一碗豌豆和一根烟斗，就是那位农民的全部饮食。一个聋邻居和一家人身上的犯罪记录，让这个片区的地图比一座教堂或一位神更奇怪，而新的读数与东岸的那些相似。收音机里的一阵静电噪声结束了会议。

### Exercises

- CH100-Q1 [context] What did the first promise commit the station to do?
  - options: measure the river from both banks at once / build a new dam / move the field station / buy a computer program
- CH100-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “洞察力；见识”?
  - options: insight / essence / permit / altitude
- CH100-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: breed a habit / breed a gulf / breed a permit / breed a picnic
- CH100-Q4 [cloze] The farmer said the problem was not the tiger or the bee but the ______.
  - options: weed / swell / soda / shrimp
- CH100-Q5 [recall] Who was to take the daily readings?
  - options: five families on the bank / the hydrologist alone / the director / a university team

### Answers

- CH100-Q1: measure the river from both banks at once - The promise was simultaneous measurement.
- CH100-Q2: insight - The accidental sampling error produced a real insight.
- CH100-Q3: breed a habit - Breed combines with a habit, a variety, or a distrust.
- CH100-Q4: weed - The ordinary weed, not the dramatic animal, was the problem.
- CH100-Q5: five families on the bank - The readings would come from residents, not officials.

## CH101 - The River Answers Back: A Missing Voice / 河流的回应：缺席的声音

- theme: 环境、自然与可持续发展

**1.** The missing voice arrived at the end of a long afternoon, when two of the seven families on the list had not been visited, and five had. “I am probably one of the eight you decided to skip,” the woman said. “Thirty years I have watched this river, and I can sing you its fifty moods. Recall the last chart: it was made on the other side.”

那个缺席的声音在一个漫长的下午末尾到来，当时名单上七个家庭中有两个还没被访问，五个已经访问过。“我大概就是你们决定跳过的八个人之一，”那位女士说，“三十年我看着这条河，我能给你唱出它五十种情绪。回想上一张图表：它是在对岸做出来的。”

**2.** She proposed that the questionnaire contain a page for what people remembered, not only for what they could count. Forty minutes later, sixty of her neighbours had signed it, and the struggle to generate fifteen more signatures turned into a small festival.

她提议问卷里要有专门一页记录人们记得的东西，而不只是他们能数出来的东西。四十分钟后，她的六十位邻居签了名，而多争取十五个签名的奋斗变成了一场小小的节日。

**3.** “You accuse us of being tired,” she said, “and you are nineteen years late with the eighty questions you want answered.” A ninety-year-old man with an eleven-metre rope said he could still accomplish more than the drone overhead. “Do not yell at the water,” he added. “Listen to it.”

“你们指责我们累了，”她说，“而你们迟了十九年才来问那八十个你们想得到答案的问题。”一位拿着十一米绳子的九十岁老人说，他仍然能做成比头顶那架无人机更多的事。“不要对水喊叫，”他补充说，“听它。”

**4.** The chart she drew had eighteen lines, and one of them was a cattle path. “A globe is not a questionnaire,” she said, “and a preacher is not a hydrologist.” The surplus of data in the station did not include the dialect of the river; the refrain of her account was the same every year.

她画的那张图表有十八条线，其中一条是牛道。“地球仪不是问卷，”她说，“传道人也不是水文学家。”站里数据的剩余里，并不包含这条河的方言；她叙述的叠句每年都一样。

**5.** It was difficult to quantify a scarcity of trust. A smoky skyline, a separatist slogan on a wall, and a pushy official with a clipboard: the group had to innovate or admit the place was not reparable. “Take the activity and the place together,” Lin wrote, “and you have the only sustainable measure we have.”

很难量化一种信任的稀缺。一片烟雾弥漫的天际线、墙上一条分离主义标语，以及一位拿着写字板咄咄逼人的官员：小组必须创新，否则就得承认这个地方不可修缮。“把活动和地方放在一起看，”林伟写道，“你就得到了我们唯一拥有的可持续的度量。”

**6.** The director sent the drive with the recordings back to the lab and asked the team to bring the same energy to the field next week. Its power would come from the people who lived on the bank, the different situation of each family, and a make of evidence that even a ten-year-old could follow. The reader who was seventy and the child who was eleven sang the refrain together; the oldest was ninety, and he preached the same lesson without a pulpit. The dialect of the river was the only chart that mattered, and ten people had to strive against a scarce supply of patience. As a force, it did not involve the state. The cattle, the globe, and the skyline were all part of the same questionnaire.

主管把装着录音的驱动器寄回实验室，并要求团队下周把同样的精力带到现场。它的力量将来自住在岸边的人、每个家庭不同的处境，以及一种连十岁孩子都能跟上的取证方式。七十岁的那位读者和十一岁的那个孩子一起唱着那段叠句；最年长的那位九十岁，他没有讲坛，却宣讲着同一堂课。这条河的方言是唯一要紧的图表，而十个人必须在耐心稀缺的情况下努力。作为一种力量，它并不涉及国家。那些牛、那个地球仪，以及那条天际线，都属于同一份问卷。

### Exercises

- CH101-Q1 [context] How many families had been visited when the woman arrived?
  - options: five of the seven / all eight / two of the seven / none
- CH101-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “剩余；过剩”?
  - options: surplus / scarcity / refrain / dialect
- CH101-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: quantify a scarcity / quantify a skyline / quantify a slogan / quantify a spade
- CH101-Q4 [cloze] The old man said a preacher is not a ______.
  - options: hydrologist / questionnaire / dialect / surplus
- CH101-Q5 [recall] What did Lin say was the only sustainable measure?
  - options: activity and place together / the number of drones / the height of the skyline / the length of the rope

### Answers

- CH101-Q1: five of the seven - Two families had been left out.
- CH101-Q2: surplus - Plenty of data, but not the local kind.
- CH101-Q3: quantify a scarcity - Quantify combines with a scarcity, a change, or a benefit.
- CH101-Q4: hydrologist - She dismissed borrowed authority over the river.
- CH101-Q5: activity and place together - He tied measurement to the people who live there.

## CH102 - The River Answers Back: Evidence on the Table / 河流的回应：摆上桌面的证据

- theme: 环境、自然与可持续发展

**1.** The evidence meeting was held in a cottage upstairs from the field station, in a room with a rug, a cupboard, and a bathroom nobody had cleaned. “This is a story about moisture, not about politics,” the hydrologist said. “Nothing on this table was measured in a theater.”

证据会议开在野外站楼上的一间小屋里，房间里有一块小地毯、一个碗柜，以及一间没人打扫过的浴室。“这是一个关于水分的故事，不是关于政治的故事，”水文学家说，“这张桌子上没有任何东西是在戏院里测量出来的。”

**2.** The belief in the old model had a theatrical quality: an angel of a forecast, a handful of statistics, and an insect of a detail that everyone ignored. An airplane passed overhead; downstairs, a dolphin on a poster watched the room.

对旧模型的信念带有一种戏剧性：一位天使般的预报、一把统计数字，以及一个所有人都忽略的昆虫般的细节。一架飞机从头顶飞过；楼下，海报上的一只海豚看着这间屋子。

**3.** The exterior of the argument was oily and wavy, like a waterproof sheet. A monkey in a cage, a goat on a rope, and a pistol in a drawer were, in the report, mere illustrations. “Put the two together,” Maya said, “and you have the emptiness of a takeoff without a runway.”

这场论证的表面是油滑而起伏的，像一张防水布。笼子里的一只猴子、绳子上的一只山羊，以及抽屉里的一把手枪，在报告里都只是单纯的插图。“把这两样放在一起看，”曼雅说，“你就得到了没有跑道的起飞的虚空。”

**4.** The sailing club had kept a boiler and a dryer in the same cupboard as their records, and the records had survived. “Watertight, not waterproof,” the boatman said. “That is the difference between a claim and a fact.” A crush of photographs lay on the table, each one wavy with damp.

帆船俱乐部把一台锅炉和一台烘干机与他们的记录放在同一个碗柜里，而记录保存了下来。“水密，不是防水，”船夫说，“这就是主张与事实之间的区别。”桌上摊着一堆照片，每一张都因潮湿而起伏不平。

**5.** The fragmentary evidence was enough for one conclusion: the district needed a skyscraper of data reduced to a mouthful of truth. Two of the five gauges were off by almost four per cent, and a centigrade reading from the north bank had been taken while the water was still rising.

这些碎片式的证据足以得出一个结论：这个地区需要把一座摩天大楼的数据缩减成一口真相。五台水位计中有两台偏差接近百分之四，而北岸的一个摄氏读数是在水位还在上涨时测的。

**6.** “We do not need nudity of detail,” Lin said. “We need a hairy, awkward, exact account of what happened on the night of the flood.” The meeting ended with a single page, and the page included the river's own answer. Nothing on the table was mere decoration: a handful of statistics from the upstairs room, a belief written on the exterior of the cottage, and an oily wavy record from the sailing club. An airplane, a dolphin, a monkey, an insect: the city's icons had nothing to do with moisture, and the emptiness of the report was the emptiness of a takeoff without a runway. The dryer, the boiler, and the rug were all on the list. Almost every sentence in the file had one statistic behind it: an eyebrow raised, a woollen rug, and a record kept ashore. Four of the five items on the shelf were probably included by mistake, and the start of the action was to put them off the table while the need for moisture data stayed on it. There was no talk of a view from the tower, or of the water as scenery. The radio gave a pop and went quiet; downstream, past the toilet, an apartment block stood empty.

“我们不需要细节的裸露，”林伟说，“我们需要一份多毛的、笨拙的、精确的记录，讲清洪水那一夜到底发生了什么。”会议以一页纸结束，而那一页里包含了河流自己的回答。桌上没有任何东西只是装饰：楼上房间里的一把统计数字、写在村舍外墙上的一种信念，以及帆船俱乐部一份油滑起伏的记录。一架飞机、一只海豚、一只猴子、一只昆虫：这座城市的图标与水分毫无关系，而报告的空洞，就是一场没有跑道的起飞的空洞。烘干机、锅炉和地毯都在清单上。文件里几乎每一句话背后都有一个统计数字：一条扬起的眉毛、一块羊毛地毯，以及一份在岸上保存的记录。架子上的五样东西里有四样大概是误放进去的，而行动的第一步就是把它们从桌上拿开，同时把对水分数据的需要留在桌上。没有人谈到从塔上看到的景色，也没有人把水当作风景。收音机发出一声爆响就安静了；下游，过了厕所，一排公寓空着。

### Exercises

- CH102-Q1 [context] What did the hydrologist say the story was about?
  - options: moisture / politics / the theater / the sailing club
- CH102-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “不透水的；无懈可击的”?
  - options: watertight / waterproof / oily / wavy
- CH102-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: take a reading / take a nudity / take a dolphin / take a cupboard
- CH102-Q4 [cloze] The boatman said the difference between a claim and a fact is being ______.
  - options: watertight / theatrical / fragmentary / hairy
- CH102-Q5 [recall] How many gauges were off, and by how much?
  - options: two of five, by almost four per cent / all five, by ten per cent / one of two, by a mouthful / four of five, by one degree

### Answers

- CH102-Q1: moisture - He insisted on treating it as a physical question.
- CH102-Q2: watertight - Watertight evidence is closed to doubt; waterproof alone is not.
- CH102-Q3: take a reading - Take combines with a reading, a risk, or a decision.
- CH102-Q4: watertight - He distinguished a closed argument from a resistant surface.
- CH102-Q5: two of five, by almost four per cent - The measurable error was small but real.

## CH103 - The River Answers Back: The Trial Run / 河流的回应：第一次试行

- theme: 环境、自然与可持续发展

**1.** The trial run began on the anniversary of the last flood, either in the valley or on the mountain top, depending on whom you asked. “The story has to reflect the geology, not the gloom,” the director said. A couple of gauges, a full breakfast, and a shiny mirror on the wall of the field center: the deal was to measure for a summer and then cut the model.

试行在上一场洪水的周年日开始，有人说是在山谷里，有人说是在山顶上，取决于你问谁。“这个故事必须反映地质，而不是阴郁，”主管说。一对水位计、一顿丰盛的早餐，以及野外中心墙上的一面闪亮的镜子：这笔交易是测量一个夏天，然后砍掉模型。

**2.** The pattern was different from what the spatial model predicted. Roughly a third of the readings exceed the limit, and the soil in the hollow near the refinery holds water the way a solvent holds dye. “Add the groundwater and reduce the surface flow,” Maya said. “More of the same will give us nothing.”

模式与空间模型的预测不同。大约三分之一的读数超过了限值，而精炼厂旁凹地里的土壤蓄水，就像溶剂吸附染料。“把地下水加进去，把地表流量减下来，”曼雅说，“再多做同样的事只会让我们一无所获。”

**3.** The next step outdoors was a roast and a brunch in the same afternoon: a banquet for the volunteers, with stuffing, a meaty soup, and an air conditioner running in the shed. “For the sake of the data,” Lin said, “keep the equipment cool and the people warm.”

户外的下一步是在同一个下午安排一场烤肉和一顿早午餐：为志愿者办一场宴会，有填料、一碗多肉的汤，以及棚子里开着的一台空调。“为了数据的缘故，”林伟说，“让设备凉着，让人暖着。”

**4.** The icy vapor from the cooler, a stain on the concrete, and a slap of glue on the pipe: the volunteers worked without a sword or a couch, and the liquor stayed in the cupboard. “Justice is a smooth process, not an event,” the boatman said.

冷却器冒出的冰冷蒸气、混凝土上的一处污渍，以及管子上拍上的一层胶：志愿者们干活时既没有刀剑也没有沙发，酒留在柜子里。“公正是个平稳的过程，不是一场事件，”船夫说。

**5.** A bone of the old design was that it had no mirror for its own error. “The global picture and the local picture have to sit at the same table,” she said. “A paint mark on a pole is not a measurement.” The airport on the ridge and the sky above it were the only parts of the district the model could see.

旧设计的一根骨头是：它没有一面照见自身错误的镜子。“全局的图景和本地的图景必须坐在同一张桌旁，”她说，“杆子上的一道油漆记号不是一次测量。”山脊上的机场和它上方的天空，是这个模型唯一能看见的部分。

**6.** Less, in the end, was the whole story: less sediment, less loss, and a plan that was practicable in a wet summer. Air conditioning in the field station and a gut feeling about the river were not the same evidence, but the team had to write both down. The term would end on the next anniversary. The air conditioning in the shed and the air conditioner on the wall were both part of the trial.

到最后，“更少”就是整个故事：更少的泥沙、更少的损失，以及一个在湿润夏天可实行的方案。野外站的空调和对这条河的直觉不是同一种证据，但团队必须把两者都写下来。这个学期将在下一个周年日结束。棚子里的空调设备和墙上的那台空调器，都是这次试行的一部分。

### Exercises

- CH103-Q1 [context] What did the director say the story had to reflect?
  - options: the geology / the gloom / the airport / the banquet
- CH103-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “可实行的”?
  - options: practicable / spatial / shiny / meaty
- CH103-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: reduce the surface flow / reduce a couch / reduce a sword / reduce a banquet
- CH103-Q4 [cloze] The boatman said that justice is a smooth process, not an ______.
  - options: event / airport / anniversary / umbrella
- CH103-Q5 [recall] What were the only parts of the district the model could see?
  - options: the airport and the sky above it / the valley and the hollow / the refinery and the dock / the soil and the water

### Answers

- CH103-Q1: the geology - He wanted physical evidence, not atmosphere.
- CH103-Q2: practicable - The plan had to be doable in a wet summer.
- CH103-Q3: reduce the surface flow - Reduce combines with a flow, a cost, or a risk.
- CH103-Q4: event - He treated fairness as an ongoing process.
- CH103-Q5: the airport and the sky above it - The model saw only the large, visible landmarks.

## CH104 - The River Answers Back: Pressure Builds / 河流的回应：压力上升

- theme: 环境、自然与可持续发展

**1.** The pressure built at the low point of the season, when the entire group had two hours a week and a simple question: can we keep the record going for another period? “Either we pay the five families or we draw the whole thing down,” the director said. The difference would show up at the foot of the bank, where the water gets its first range.

压力在这一季的低点累积起来，当时整个小组每周只有两个小时，以及一个简单的问题：我们还能把这个记录再维持一个周期吗？“要么我们付钱给那五个家庭，要么我们把整件事拉倒，”主管说。“差别”会在岸脚显现出来，那里是水面获得它第一段范围的地方。

**2.** The wind came off the lake and the storm took the wing off the shed. A dry pool of acid, a shower of hail, and a pipe with a broken collar: the restoration cost more than the sensors, and the bacteria in the wetland did not care about the budget.

风从湖上吹来，风暴掀掉了棚子的一个翼。一池干涸的酸、一阵冰雹，以及一根领口破裂的管子：修复的费用超过了传感器，而湿地里的细菌并不在乎预算。

**3.** The group tried to hide the problem in a disguise of honesty. “A straw, a balloon, and a stool are not a laboratory,” the boatman said. “The oxygen in that water is the only record that matters.” A cereal of local grain, a drip of oil, and a tick of the clock kept the camp running.

小组试图以诚实为伪装把问题藏起来。“一根麦管、一只气球和一张凳子不是实验室，”船夫说，“那水里的氧才是唯一要紧的记录。”一份本地的谷物早餐、一滴油，以及时钟的一下滴答，让营地维持运转。

**4.** The twin pressures were money and time. A barbecue for the volunteers, a paste of soap and ash, and a crocodile warning on the bank: the project was long, and probably too long for the hour it had.

两股压力是钱和时间。为志愿者办的一场烤肉、一坨肥皂加灰的糊，以及岸边一条鳄鱼警告：这个项目很长，而且对这仅有的一小时来说大概太长。

**5.** The ozone and the climate range had both been added to the programme, and neither of them could be measured from a single pole. “We get the tongue of the river and the tongue of the ministry in the same room,” Maya wrote, “and we call that parliamentary language.”

臭氧和气候范围都被加进了方案，而这两者都无法从一根杆子上测出来。“我们把河流的舌头和部里的舌头放进同一个房间，”曼雅写道，“然后把它叫做议会语言。”

**6.** The next step was the bait and the splash: a small mark on the water, a photograph, and a list of the twelve things the group would no longer pretend to know. The period ended with the promise of an anniversary review, and with the concrete pier still unfinished. A flower on the bank, a neck of land, a blind corner and a cough in the night: the development of the district was written in small things, not in the storm. The entire group could see it and could not fix it in one period.

下一步是饵与飞溅：水面上的一个小记号、一张照片，以及一份清单，列出小组不再假装知道的那十二件事。这个周期以一场周年复审的承诺结束，而那座混凝土码头仍未完工。岸边的一朵花、一段地峡、一个死角，以及夜里的一声咳嗽：这个片区的发展写在小事里，而不是写在风暴里。整个小组都看得见它，却无法在一个周期内修好它。

### Exercises

- CH104-Q1 [context] What simple question did the group face?
  - options: can we keep the record going another period / who owns the lake / when is the barbecue / how deep is the pool
- CH104-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “细菌”?
  - options: bacteria / bait / ozone / straw
- CH104-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: draw the project down / draw the cereal / draw the balloon / draw the banqueting
- CH104-Q4 [cloze] The boatman said a straw, a balloon and a stool are not a ______.
  - options: laboratory / pier / collar / barbecue
- CH104-Q5 [recall] What did the period end with?
  - options: the promise of an anniversary review / a finished pier / a new laboratory / a cancelled programme

### Answers

- CH104-Q1: can we keep the record going another period - The question was about continuity, not ambition.
- CH104-Q2: bacteria - The wetland bacteria were indifferent to the budget.
- CH104-Q3: draw the project down - Draw combines with a project, a conclusion, or a line.
- CH104-Q4: laboratory - Improvised equipment is not an instrument of record.
- CH104-Q5: the promise of an anniversary review - The period closed on a scheduled review.

## CH105 - The River Answers Back: The Disagreement / 河流的回应：分歧

- theme: 环境、自然与可持续发展

**1.** The disagreement was about the ground itself, and it began with a tree. “You plant a dam and the sea takes the delta,” the ecologist said. “You leave the mud alone and the animal comes back. Both of us are against the fog of a single answer.”

分歧就出在地本身，而且是从一棵树开始的。“你筑一道坝，海就会夺走三角洲，”生态学家说，“你让泥滩保持原样，动物就会回来。我们两人都反对单一答案的那层迷雾。”

**2.** The cultural argument was harder: a branch of the ministry wanted a rock zone for the media, a planet-friendly slogan, and a mobile exhibition. “A bow and a fossil are worth more to a museum than to a river,” the director said. “We can be even more careful and still be late.”

文化层面的论证更难：部里的一个分支想要一片给媒体的岩石区、一句对地球友好的口号，以及一个移动展览。“一把弓和一块化石对博物馆的价值大于对一条河的价值，”主管说，“即便我们再小心，也依然是迟的。”

**3.** The scheme on the table had nothing to do with milk, pepper, or juice: it was about who owned the horizon. “If we wait for a scheme that protects the whole ecological axis, we will wait for ten years,” Lin said. “If we pull the scheme apart, we lose the peat and the shell beds.”

桌上的方案与奶、胡椒或果汁都无关：它关乎谁来拥有那条地平线。“如果我们等一个保护整个生态轴的方案，我们要等十年，”林伟说，“如果我们把方案拆开，我们就失去泥炭和贝壳层。”

**4.** The exploitation of the zone was already visible: a jet of warm water, a cube of concrete, a plague of algae, and a plough that turned the dew into dust. “The ecology of this zone is not a cultural ornament,” the ecologist said.

对这一地带的开发已经看得见了：一股温水射流、一块混凝土立方体、一场藻类瘟疫，以及一张把露水翻成尘土的犁。“这一地带的生态不是一件文化装饰品，”生态学家说。

**5.** The compromise they could live with was smaller than either side wanted. A leather boot, a wool blanket, a comb, and a packet of bacon were the whole of the field kit; from that list they reproduced a plan with a single branch.

他们能接受的妥协比双方想要的都小。一只皮靴、一条羊毛毯、一把梳子，以及一包熏猪肉就是野外装备的全部；他们从这份清单里复现出一个单分支的方案。

**6.** The end of the argument was that nothing was settled and everything was clearer. “We are able to measure a pollutant and not able to agree on a horizon,” the director said. “The next story will have to begin with that.” The mate of the ecologist brought bedding, corn, and a bag of peanut; the smog over the bank was the only thing at the low level of the river that the body could feel. The movement of the water and the result of the survey were on the same page for once. This planet has one river and one plan, and the plan must include both, unbuilt pier or not.

这场争论的结局是：什么都没解决，而一切都更清楚了。“我们有能力测量一种污染物，却没有能力就一条地平线达成一致，”主管说，“下一个故事必须从这里开始。”那位生态学家的伙伴带来了被褥、玉米和一袋花生；岸边那层烟雾，是身体在河流低水位处唯一能感觉到的东西。水的运动和调查的结果，这一次在同一页上。这颗行星只有一条河和一个方案，而方案必须同时包含两者，无论那座码头建不建。

### Exercises

- CH105-Q1 [context] What did the ecologist say happens if you plant a dam?
  - options: the sea takes the delta / the tree grows faster / the fog lifts / the milk spoils
- CH105-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “污染物质”?
  - options: pollutant / plague / plough / dew
- CH105-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: reproduce a plan / reproduce a bow / reproduce a comb / reproduce a fossil
- CH105-Q4 [cloze] The ecologist said the ecology of the zone is not a cultural ______.
  - options: ornament / axis / pollutant / horizon
- CH105-Q5 [recall] What did the director say the group was able and not able to do?
  - options: measure a pollutant but not agree on a horizon / agree on a horizon but not measure / build a dam but not a museum / plant a tree but not a plough

### Answers

- CH105-Q1: the sea takes the delta - Hard engineering moves the loss downstream.
- CH105-Q2: pollutant - They could measure a pollutant precisely.
- CH105-Q3: reproduce a plan - Reproduce combines with a plan, a result, or an experiment.
- CH105-Q4: ornament - She refused to treat ecology as decoration.
- CH105-Q5: measure a pollutant but not agree on a horizon - Precision in measurement, deadlock in purpose.

## CH106 - The River Answers Back: What the Numbers Hide / 河流的回应：数字隐藏了什么

- theme: 环境、自然与可持续发展

**1.** The numbers hid the water itself. Into the file went a well of readings, a bed of sediment, and an element of weather that no instrument had recorded; the atmosphere of the field station was almost cheerful, and the condition of the river was not.

这些数字掩盖了水本身。一口井的读数、一层沉积物，以及一个没有任何仪器记录过的天气要素，都被放进了文件；野外站的气氛几乎是愉快的，而河流的状况不是。

**2.** The main product of the process was a clean record of the wrong question. “We give the river a channel and call it data,” the boatman said. “The land, the forest, and the native trees are not in your table.” The male and female volunteers agreed on that, and the food for the meeting was an eggplant stew.

这个流程的主要产物是一份关于错误问题的干净记录。“我们给河一条河道，然后称之为数据，”船夫说，“土地、森林和本地树种都不在你的表里。”男女志愿者都同意这一点，会上的食物是一锅茄子。

**3.** The root of the problem was a lock on the model: only one kind of evidence could get in. A stream of dust from the dock, a pot of cotton, and a mineral sample in a pad of wax: the craft of the old survey was precise and blind to the river bed.

问题的根源是模型上的一把锁：只有一种证据进得来。从码头吹来的一阵尘土、一罐棉花，以及一块封在蜡垫里的矿物样本：旧勘测的技艺很精确，却对河床视而不见。

**4.** The spray from the washing of the trap, the rip in the organ pipe, and the screw that nobody could find: the establishment had replaced a measurement with a maintenance schedule. “We are against the light of a single number,” Maya said.

陷阱清洗时的水花、风琴管上的一道裂口，以及谁也找不到的那颗螺丝：这个机构已经用一份维护日程替换了一次测量。“我们反对单一数字的那束光，”曼雅说。

**5.** The calling of the volunteers was to look at the earth and the ocean in the same hour, with the same need to know and the same willingness to be wrong. A rib of the old dam, a gum tree, and a calorie table were not enough to explain a flood.

志愿者的使命是在同一个小时里看土地和海洋，怀着同样的求知需要，以及同样愿意出错的意愿。旧坝的一根肋骨、一棵橡胶树和一张热量表，都不足以解释一场洪水。

**6.** While the report was being written, the river went down and came back up. The numbers hid that too, and the group agreed to publish the whole page, similar in shape to the first one but with a ground line at the bottom. The dust, the pot, the pad, and the spray: all of it was ours. “The river gives us the question,” the boatman said, “and the establishment gives us the form.” The stream, the lock, the dock, and the trap were all in the same chapter, and the craft of the survey was to see them together. The air, the star, and the nature of the river were not in the drug of a single number; either we play fairly or we do not play at all.

报告还在写的时候，河水落下又涨了回来。数字把这一点也掩盖了，而小组同意把整页都公布出来——形状与第一页相似，但底部多了一条基准线。那些尘土、那口锅、那个垫子和那阵水花：全都是我们的。“河流给我们问题，”船夫说，“而机构给我们表格。”那条溪流、那座水闸、那个码头和那个陷阱，都在同一章里，而调查的技艺就是把它们放在一起看。空气、星星和这条河的本性，都不在单一数字的药剂里；要么我们公平地玩，要么我们根本不玩。

### Exercises

- CH106-Q1 [context] What did the numbers hide?
  - options: the water itself / the volunteers / the eggplant / the dock
- CH106-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “大气；气氛”?
  - options: atmosphere / establishment / element / channel
- CH106-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: keep a record / keep a pollutant / keep a wax / keep a rib
- CH106-Q4 [cloze] The boatman said the land, the forest and the native trees are not in your ______.
  - options: table / organ / ocean / trap
- CH106-Q5 [recall] What did the group agree to publish?
  - options: the whole page, including what the numbers hid / only the final average / the maintenance schedule / the calorie table

### Answers

- CH106-Q1: the water itself - The figures described everything except the river.
- CH106-Q2: atmosphere - The mood at the station and the river's state diverged.
- CH106-Q3: keep a record - Keep combines with a record, a promise, or a lock.
- CH106-Q4: table - The local landscape was absent from the data table.
- CH106-Q5: the whole page, including what the numbers hid - They chose full disclosure over a clean summary.

## CH107 - The River Answers Back: A Better Question / 河流的回应：更好的问题

- theme: 环境、自然与可持续发展

**1.** The better question came from a vegetable stall on the west floor of the market, over a cup of tea at the fair: who decided that the river is a drain and not a well? “The difficulty is not the water,” the trader said. “It is the investment in the cement.” A couple of us had come in from the land, and the air was full of grain dust.

更好的问题来自集市西侧地面上一个卖蔬菜的摊位，在展会上一杯茶的功夫里：是谁决定这条河是一条排水沟，而不是一口井？“困难不在水，”摊主说，“而在水泥上的投资。”我们中有两个人是从陆地上来的，空气里满是谷物的粉尘。

**2.** The map on the wall showed a canal that had been transformed into a landing for boats, and a ditch that had become a booth for carrots and pink radishes. “A simple process is not the same as a natural one,” she said. “You can freeze a seed, but you cannot freeze abundance.”

墙上的地图显示出一条已被改造成船只码头的运河，以及一条变成了胡萝卜和粉色萝卜摊棚的沟渠。“一个简单的过程不等于一个自然的过程，”她说，“你可以冷冻一颗种子，但你不能冷冻丰盛。”

**3.** The false assumption had a long leg and a low floor: the idea that a river is passive. “Hit the water with a stick and it waves back,” the boatman said. “The cloud above us, the tooth of the current, the leak in the bank: it is all one brain.”

那个错误的假设有一条长腿和一个低矮的地板：河流是被动的。“用棍子敲水，它会回你一道波浪，”船夫说，“我们头顶的云、水流的牙齿、岸边的渗漏：这都是同一个大脑。”

**4.** The whistle of the midday train, the bang of a lift door, and the crawl of mud through the canal: the district needed a different question, not a bigger pump. “Ask what the water wants,” she said, “and the temper of the whole meeting changes.”

正午火车的汽笛、电梯门的一声巨响，以及泥浆在水渠里的爬行：这个地区需要的是一个不同的问题，而不是一台更大的水泵。“问问水想要什么，”她说，“整场会议的脾气就变了。”

**5.** A feather, a dive into the channel, and a jar of ink in the booth: the small things had been measured and the big things had not. “Inflation is not a river problem,” Maya wrote, “and a button on a map is not a fact.”

一根羽毛、一次跃入河道，以及摊棚里一瓶墨水：小事被测量了，而大事没有。“通货膨胀不是河流问题，”曼雅写道，“而地图上的一个按钮不是事实。”

**6.** The better question, in the end, was about the polar opposite of the old one: instead of asking how to remove water, ask where the clay and the mud want it to go. The lavatory at the back of the station, the hip of the embankment, and the water in the well all had to be re-measured. “A different map, not a better pump,” the director said. The star above the station, the bed of the channel, and the nature of the nose that could smell rain: all of it belonged to the same question.

说到底，那个更好的问题与旧问题正好是两极相反：与其问怎样把水排掉，不如问黏土和泥想要它去哪里。站后的厕所、堤岸的臀部，以及井里的水，都必须重新测量。“换一张地图，不是换一台更好的泵，”主管说。站上方的星星、河道的床，以及那个能闻出雨来的鼻子的本性：所有这一切都属于同一个问题。

### Exercises

- CH107-Q1 [context] What did the trader say the difficulty was?
  - options: the investment in the cement / the price of tea / the grain dust / the weather
- CH107-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “丰盛；充足”?
  - options: abundance / inflation / temper / landing
- CH107-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: freeze a seed / freeze a booth / freeze a canal / freeze a whistle
- CH107-Q4 [cloze] The boatman said that if you hit the water with a stick it will ______ back.
  - options: wave / crawl / freeze / dive
- CH107-Q5 [recall] What did the director say was needed?
  - options: a different map, not a better pump / a bigger pump / a new canal / a taller embankment

### Answers

- CH107-Q1: the investment in the cement - The obstacle was financial, not hydrological.
- CH107-Q2: abundance - She contrasted a frozen seed with real abundance.
- CH107-Q3: freeze a seed - Freeze combines with a seed, a pipe, or a price.
- CH107-Q4: wave - He described the river as responsive, not passive.
- CH107-Q5: a different map, not a better pump - He wanted a better question rather than more force.

## CH108 - The River Answers Back: The Second Attempt / 河流的回应：第二次尝试

- theme: 环境、自然与可持续发展

**1.** The second attempt used the same set of instruments in a different environment, and the total looked modest on paper. “We are not building a nuclear plant,” the director said. “We are trying to keep one island of dry ground dry.” A pair of boots, a narrow pack, and a straight line on a map: that was the whole plan.

第二次尝试使用同一套仪器，但环境不同，而总量在纸面上看起来不大。“我们不是在建一座核电站，”主管说，“我们只是想让一座干地的孤岛保持干燥。”一双靴子、一个狭窄的背包，以及地图上一条直线：这就是全部方案。

**2.** The environmental setting was the difficulty. “The resistance of this soil to water is a mathematical joke,” the geologist said. “The rain will soak it, and the wet season will destroy the shore road.” The destruction of the last shed by the flood was still visible from the station.

环境的背景设置就是困难所在。“这片土壤对水的抵抗力是个数学笑话，”地质学家说，“雨水会把它浸透，而湿季会毁掉岸边的路。”上一场洪水毁掉的那间棚屋，从站里还能看见。

**3.** The team measured the steam and the emission of the old pump, and found the discharge of warm water into the narrow channel. “A revolutionary approach is not needed,” the boatman said. “A passive one is. The tolerance of this bank is almost used up.”

团队测量了旧水泵的蒸汽和排放，并发现了温水排入那条狭窄河道的情况。“不需要革命性的做法，”船夫说，“需要一个被动的做法。这片河岸的容忍度几乎用尽了。”

**4.** The despair of the second week was real: a nest of birds drowned in the ditch, a fountain of mud in the yard, and the calm surface of the canal hiding a soak of water underneath. “The surroundings do not care about our schedule,” Maya wrote.

第二周的绝望是真实的：沟渠里一窝被淹死的鸟、院子里一股泥的喷泉，以及运河平静水面上掩盖着的一层浸水。“周围的环境不在乎我们的时间表，”曼雅写道。

**5.** The greenhouse on the ridge had survived, and its salty liter of water had condensed by morning. “Excite the volunteers with the small successes,” the director said. “The big ones are rare, and the small ones contain the whole method.”

山脊上的温室活了下来，它那一公升咸水到早上已经凝结了。“用小小的成功去激励志愿者，”主管说，“大成功很罕见，而小成功里包含着整套方法。”

**6.** The second attempt added discrimination to the model: eight sites, seven gauges, two banks, and one rule. A bit of oil on the hinge of the gate, a drive to the west shore, and the plant on the riverbed were all part of the same activity. Its might was its modesty, and it was, probably, the last attempt of the season. The essential point was the integration of the two surveys, and the power of the argument was against a soluble answer: five trees, one ass, and a bitter joke about the weather. The tree by the gate and the sediment in the water had to be measured in the same week, and the whole attempt was probably a lesson in humility rather than a plan.

第二次尝试给模型加上了辨别力：八个地点、七台水位计、两岸，以及一条规则。门铰链上的一点油、一趟去西岸的车程，以及河床上的植物，都属于同一项活动。它的力量在于它的谦虚，而这大概是这一季的最后一次尝试。要紧的一点是两份调查的整合，而论证的力量是针对一个可溶解的答案：五棵树、一头驴，以及一个关于天气的苦涩玩笑。门口那棵树和水里的沉积物必须在同一周测量，而整次尝试大概是一堂关于谦逊的课，而不是一个计划。

### Exercises

- CH108-Q1 [context] What did the director say they were trying to keep dry?
  - options: one island of dry ground / the whole valley / the greenhouse roof / the west road
- CH108-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “抵抗力；阻力”?
  - options: resistance / emission / tolerance / discharge
- CH108-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: soak the soil / soak the emission / soak the despair / soak the discrimination
- CH108-Q4 [cloze] The boatman said the tolerance of the bank was almost used ______.
  - options: up / off / in / out
- CH108-Q5 [recall] What had condensed by morning in the greenhouse?
  - options: a litre of salty water / a litre of oil / a litre of steam / a litre of rain

### Answers

- CH108-Q1: one island of dry ground - The goal was small and specific.
- CH108-Q2: resistance - The soil's resistance to water was the difficulty.
- CH108-Q3: soak the soil - Soak combines with soil, a cloth, or a schedule.
- CH108-Q4: up - The bank's capacity for damage was nearly exhausted.
- CH108-Q5: a litre of salty water - The greenhouse distilled water overnight.

## CH109 - The River Answers Back: Outside Pressure / 河流的回应：外部压力

- theme: 环境、自然与可持续发展

**1.** The outside pressure arrived at midday on a rainy afternoon, when an overseas advisor and an astronomer came to the backyard with a list. “You will modernize the station or we will place an intermediary from the capital,” the advisor said. “The chairperson wants the same view from every bank.” Patience was thinner than the fur on a camel.

外部压力在一个多雨的下午正午时分到来，一位海外顾问和一位天文学家带着一份清单来到后院。“你们要么让这个站现代化，要么我们从首都派一位中间人来，”顾问说，“主席希望每一个河岸看到同样的景象。”耐心比骆驼身上的毛还薄。

**2.** The respondent at the meeting was a teenager who kept a listener's notebook. “The bowel of this valley does not need a new garment,” she said. “It needs a mend.” A delightful gardening programme, an entertaining film, and an enjoyable concert had all been offered instead.

会上那位应答者是个少年，他带着一本倾听者的笔记。“这个山谷的肠子不需要一件新衣服，”她说，“它需要一处缝补。”一份令人愉快的园艺项目、一部有趣的电影，以及一场让人享受的音乐会，都被拿来作为替代方案。

**3.** The pressure was to enlarge, tighten, and harden: more concrete, more cages, more instrumentation. “Loosen the rules instead,” the carpenter said. “A garment that is too stiff will not clothe a growing child.” The electrician, the guitarist, the violinist, and the landlady all signed the same page.

压力是要扩大、勒紧、硬化：更多混凝土、更多笼子、更多仪器。“不如把规则放松，”木匠说，“一件太僵硬的衣服裹不住一个正在长大的孩子。”电工、吉他手、小提琴手和女房东都在同一页上签了名。

**4.** The danger was that the data would deteriorate if the budget were cut, or that the plan would materialize only as a monument. “Do not scold the volunteers for a slow week,” the boatman said. “Solidify the method and let the numbers modernise themselves.” The noon reading had been missed twice.

危险在于：如果预算被砍，数据会恶化；或者这个计划只会以一座纪念碑的形式实现。“不要因为一个慢下来的一周就责骂志愿者，”船夫说，“把方法固化下来，让数字自己去现代化。”正午的读数已经错过了两次。

**5.** The counter-pressure was a raincoat, a pair of boots, and a chairperson who was willing to wait. “A pleasing report is not a persuasive one,” the advisor admitted, and the Celsius readings began to make sense to him.

反向的压力是一件雨衣、一双靴子，以及一位愿意等待的主席。“一份令人喜爱的报告不等于一份有说服力的报告，”顾问承认了，而那组摄氏读数开始对他有了意义。

**6.** The condition imposed was simple and hard: three years of readings, published, before any construction. The group accepted the term, the west floor of the station became the archive, and the afternoon ended with a full set of signatures. The air was clear at noon, and the combat over the budget was not. A listener at the back asked madam chairperson to stiffen the rule rather than the concrete; nothing in the process would go into the story without the water readings, and the view from the well had to include us. The same set of instruments reached the same result twice, and the music from the guitarist covered the noise of the drills. The advisor would provide the list, put the money on the table, and start the clock.

强加的条件既简单又艰难：三年的读数，公开出版，之后才能动工。小组接受了这个条件，站里的西侧地面成了档案室，而这个下午在一整套签名中结束。正午时分空气是清的，而关于预算的争斗不是。后排一位听者请女主席把规则收紧，而不是把混凝土加厚；没有水的读数，流程里的任何东西都不会进入这个故事，而从井里看出去的视野必须包含我们。同一套仪器两次得到同一个结果，而吉他手的音乐盖过了钻机的声音。顾问会提供清单、把钱放到桌上，并让计时开始。

### Exercises

- CH109-Q1 [context] What did the advisor threaten?
  - options: to place an intermediary from the capital / to close the station / to cut the readings / to move the archive
- CH109-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “恶化；变坏”?
  - options: deteriorate / solidify / harden / enlarge
- CH109-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: tighten the rules / tighten the raincoat / tighten the listener / tighten the carrot
- CH109-Q4 [cloze] The carpenter said a garment that is too stiff will not ______ a growing child.
  - options: clothe / scold / mend / loosen
- CH109-Q5 [recall] What condition was imposed?
  - options: three years of published readings before any construction / a new monument / an overseas director / a bigger budget

### Answers

- CH109-Q1: to place an intermediary from the capital - The threat was administrative takeover.
- CH109-Q2: deteriorate - A budget cut would make the data deteriorate.
- CH109-Q3: tighten the rules - Tighten combines with rules, a budget, or a screw.
- CH109-Q4: clothe - He compared rigid rules to clothing that cannot grow.
- CH109-Q5: three years of published readings before any construction - Data came before concrete.

## CH110 - The River Answers Back: The Decision / 河流的回应：决定

- theme: 环境、自然与可持续发展

**1.** The decision came between two things the group could not have both of: a metal lock on the canal and a forest that keeps its own timber. “Someone has to choose,” the activist said, “and the achievement will be small either way.” Rarely had the station been so close to a real question.

决定落在两样二者不可兼得的东西之间：运河上的一把金属水闸，以及一片保留自己木材的森林。“总得有人选，”活动人士说，“而无论选哪边，成就都很小。”这个站很少如此靠近一个真正的问题。

**2.** The agriculture office wanted a restaurant, a cellar of dairy and beef, and a chain of snack stalls selling goose sausage. “A darling of a plan,” the chairperson said, “but the forestry figures are not persuasive, and the rainy season is pressing.”

农业部门想要一家餐馆、一个装满乳品和牛肉的地窖，以及一连串卖鹅肉香肠的小吃摊。“方案很可爱，”主席说，“但林业的数字不具说服力，而雨季正逼近。”

**3.** The decision was to dine indoors and to keep the utmost care for the west floor. A cinema, a zoo, and a robotic milking shed were all deferred; the climatic data and the misty mornings mattered more than the dessert. “Seldom has a project been so unglamorous,” Maya wrote, “and seldom so necessary.”

决定是在室内用餐，并对西侧地面保持最大的谨慎。一家电影院、一座动物园和一座机器人挤奶棚都被推迟；气候数据和雾蒙蒙的早晨比餐后甜点更重要。“很少有项目如此不风光，”曼雅写道，“也很少如此必要。”

**4.** The pressing question was where the money would come from, and the answer was an achievement of a different kind: a maid, a carpenter, and a violinist each donated one day a month. A fable about a victorious goose and a marvelous eraser was printed for the school.

紧迫的问题是钱从哪里来，而答案是另一种成就：一位女仆、一位木匠和一位小提琴手每月各捐出一天。为学校印了一则关于一只胜利的鹅和一块了不起的橡皮的寓言。

**5.** The difficult part was to finalize a plan that reduced the add-on work, cut the center of the old scheme, and dealt with the either-or of agriculture and forestry. The term began with a full floor of volunteers and less money than the summer.

困难的部分是要定稿一份削减附加工作、砍掉旧方案中心，并处理农业与林业之间二选一的计划。这个学期以一个满员的志愿者楼层和比夏天更少的钱开始。

**6.** Within the decision there was a smaller one: to begin with the west bank, to attempt the readings before the deal was signed, and to keep the top of the file blank until the reading arrived. “We are between two rivers now,” the boatman said. “That is the only place worth standing.” The difficulty of the decision was either small or total: the floor of the cellar, the west room, and the center of the town all needed less, and the term would begin with a full set of reduced plans. The scene at the restaurant was less dramatic than the fable, and more useful. A summer of readings had reduced the argument to one page. A chopstick, a plate of meat, and an idiomatic phrase borrowed from the zoology department: doubtless the smaller accomplishment was to add nothing to the menu.

在这个决定之内还有一个更小的决定：从西岸开始，在签署协议之前先尝试那些读数，并让文件顶部保持空白，直到读数到来。“我们现在处在两条河之间，”船夫说，“那是唯一值得站立的地方。”这个决定的困难要么很小，要么是全部：地窖的地面、西边的房间，以及镇中心，都需要更少，而这个学期将以一整套缩减后的方案开始。餐馆里的那一幕没有寓言那么戏剧化，却更有用。一个夏天的读数把这个论证压缩成了一页纸。一根筷子、一盘肉，以及一个从动物学系借来的惯用语：毫无疑问，那个更小的成就是没有给菜单上加任何东西。

### Exercises

- CH110-Q1 [context] Between what two things did the group have to choose?
  - options: a metal lock on the canal and a forest kept for timber / a cinema and a zoo / beef and goose / an eraser and a fable
- CH110-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “紧迫的；迫切的”?
  - options: pressing / misty / victorious / idiomatic
- CH110-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: finalize a plan / finalize a goose / finalize a maid / finalize a cellar
- CH110-Q4 [cloze] The chairperson said the forestry figures were not ______.
  - options: persuasive / marvelous / robotic / climatic
- CH110-Q5 [recall] What did the group decide to keep blank until the reading arrived?
  - options: the top of the file / the west floor / the cellar / the restaurant menu

### Answers

- CH110-Q1: a metal lock on the canal and a forest kept for timber - Water control and woodland could not both be maximised.
- CH110-Q2: pressing - The funding question could not be postponed.
- CH110-Q3: finalize a plan - Finalize combines with a plan, a text, or a budget.
- CH110-Q4: persuasive - The forestry numbers did not convince the chair.
- CH110-Q5: the top of the file - They refused to write a conclusion before the data.

## CH111 - The River Answers Back: Consequences / 河流的回应：后果

- theme: 环境、自然与可持续发展

**1.** The consequences came as a difference between two medical opinions. A republican deputy and a red cross doctor stood on the beach and watched the helicopter bring a patient out of the valley. “The illness is not the flood,” the doctor said. “It is the water we drink after it.” A rapid flash of lightning lit the jungle behind them.

后果表现为两种医学意见之间的差异。一位共和派议员和一位红十字医生站在海滩上，看着直升机把一个病人从山谷里带出来。“病不在洪水里，”医生说，“而在洪水之后我们喝的水里。”一道迅速的闪电照亮了他们身后的丛林。

**2.** The health data of the district had a bias that nobody had written down: the people who filled in the forms were the ones who could breathe easily. “My hospital sees the same set,” she said. “The simple things, the low things, the things someone never reports.” A steak and a jar of oil on the table were the whole of a good week.

这个地区的健康数据有一个没人写下来的偏差：填表的人是那些呼吸顺畅的人。“我的医院看到的是同一套人，”她说，“简单的事、低处的事，以及某人从不汇报的事。”桌上的一块牛排和一罐油，就是好一周的全部。

**3.** The outdoor workers had cholesterol and thunder in their ears and nothing else. “You can transplant a kidney but not a river,” the doctor said, and the transplantation of the old dam was, in her view, an inferior solution. A ski slope on a volcanic hillside, a staircase of concrete, and a magnetic scan of the soil: the project had produced better evidence than the ministry.

户外工作者耳朵里有胆固醇和雷声，别的什么也没有。“你可以移植一颗肾，但不能移植一条河，”医生说，而旧坝的移植在她看来是个较差的方案。火山坡上的滑雪道、一段混凝土楼梯，以及一次对土壤的磁性扫描：这个项目产出的证据比部里更好。

**4.** The earthquake of the previous year had buried a family under a stair, and nobody in the ministry had quaked. “Sunshine and a favourable wind are not a strategy,” the deputy said, and his words went forth into the record.

前一年的地震把一家人埋在了一段楼梯下面，而部里没有人颤抖过。“阳光和顺风不是一种策略，”议员说，而他的话传了出去，进了记录。

**5.** The lively part of the meeting was the quarrel about the silicon in the river: a paw of mud, a plow in the field, a stocking full of samples, and a vapour of doubt. “You can bathe in this water,” he said, “but you cannot shave in it and call it clean.”

会上生动活泼的部分是关于河里硅的争吵：一只带泥的爪子、田里的一张犁、一只装满样本的长袜，以及一团怀疑的蒸气。“你可以在这种水里沐浴，”他说，“但你不能在里面刮胡子，然后称它干净。”

**6.** The energetic minority won one point: a home-monitoring scheme with a transplant check, a set of readings within the same week, and a re-measurement after each flood. The environment, the doctor said, is not a game between two parties. A favorable wind, a biblical flood, and a continent of mud: nothing would hinder the plan more than a short breath. The price of a transplant was advantageous, and the eagle could soar above it all.

精力充沛的少数人赢得了一点：一项家庭监测计划，配一次移植检查、一周内的同一套读数，以及每次洪水之后的重新测量。环境，医生说，不是两党之间的一场比赛。一阵顺风、一场圣经式的洪水，以及一片泥泞的大陆：没有什么比一口短缺的呼吸更能阻碍这个计划了。移植的代价是有利的，而那只鹰可以翱翔在这一切之上。

### Exercises

- CH111-Q1 [context] What did the doctor say the illness was?
  - options: the water people drink after the flood / the flood itself / the helicopter noise / the steak
- CH111-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “偏见；斜纹”?
  - options: bias / flash / vapour / quake
- CH111-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: transplant a kidney / transplant a river / transplant a beach / transplant a staircase
- CH111-Q4 [cloze] The deputy said sunshine and a favourable wind are not a ______.
  - options: strategy / transplant / vapour / paw
- CH111-Q5 [recall] What did the energetic minority win?
  - options: a home-monitoring scheme with a re-measurement after each flood / a new beach / a ski slope / a cholesterol clinic

### Answers

- CH111-Q1: the water people drink after the flood - The illness followed the flood, not the water itself.
- CH111-Q2: bias - The reporting had a structural bias.
- CH111-Q3: transplant a kidney - Transplant combines with an organ, a practice, or a tree.
- CH111-Q4: strategy - Good weather is not a plan.
- CH111-Q5: a home-monitoring scheme with a re-measurement after each flood - The minority won a small, repeatable mechanism.

## CH112 - The River Answers Back: The Bridge Forward / 河流的回应：通向下一程

- theme: 环境、自然与可持续发展

**1.** The eighth arc closed with a balance sheet that fitted on one page. An adult reader, a rich outsider, and a southern environmentalist sat on the board of the new station: three people, four directions, and a compass that pointed at the northwestern corner of the map.

第八段弧线以一份能装进一页纸的平衡表收尾。一位成年读者、一位富有的外来者，以及一位南方的环保人士坐在新站的董事会里：三个人、四个方向，以及一只指向地图西北角的指南针。

**2.** The northern and eastern factories had agreed to a compromise on pollution: a rubber seal on every pipe, a knife by the fence for the sampling bags, and a bare strip of ground along the bank. “Basically, we consume the river and we call it industry,” the director said. “That is the monster we built.”

北方和东方的工厂就污染达成了一项妥协：每根管子上一个橡胶密封、栅栏边一把用来装采样袋的刀，以及岸边一条裸露的地带。“基本上，我们消耗这条河，然后称之为工业，”主管说，“那就是我们造出的怪物。”

**3.** The statue in the square was of a fairy with a lily, and beside it stood an ambitious plan for a planetary park. “A burger and a hay field are not the same purchase,” the eastern member said, and the outsider agreed. “A poison is a poison, wherever it is poured.”

广场上的雕像是位带百合的仙女，旁边立着一个关于行星公园的雄心方案。“一个汉堡和一片干草田不是同一笔采购，”东方成员说，而那位外来者同意了。“毒药就是毒药，倒在哪里都一样。”

**4.** The southeastern and southwestern members wanted a halfway solution; the northeastern and northwest members wanted a full one. A pen, a buck, and a millionaire's promise: the board had the materials for a compromise, and none of them for a clean decision.

东南和西南的成员想要一个折中方案；东北和西北的成员想要一个彻底的方案。一支钢笔、一块钱，以及一位百万富翁的承诺：董事会拥有了妥协的材料，却没有一个能做出干净决定的材料。

**5.** The foremost question was who would own the readings after the project ended. “Mankind does not own a river,” the environmentalist said. “It only rents the right to drink from it.” The board adopted a declarative sentence with a hyphen in the middle: community-owned, publicly-read.

首要的问题是项目结束后这些读数归谁所有。“人类并不拥有河流，”环保人士说，“它只是租用了从中饮水的权利。”董事会通过了一句带连字符的陈述句：社区所有、公开可读。

**6.** The loss of the old scheme was easier to accept than the board had expected. Against the advice of the plant managers, the members agreed to publish ten years of data with only a side note about the cost. A millionaire and a billionaire could not fill the gap that an ox and an animal in the field had left. The southeast and the northeast wanted even more; the red look on the director's face said the scheme was losing. “Do not contaminate the well,” the outsider said. The hay, the lily, and the fairy were decoration; the balance sheet was the only document that mattered. Ten trees, one plant, and a board of four: the compromise was thin. A republican member joined the board and asked for a written rule. The river had answered every question they asked it and none of the ones they avoided.

旧方案的损失比董事会预想的更容易接受。不顾工厂经理们的反对，成员们同意公开十年的数据，只加一条关于成本的旁注。一位百万富翁和一位亿万富翁填不上田里那头牛和那只动物留下的空缺。东南和东北想要更多；主管脸上那副红涨的神情说明这个方案正在输。“不要污染那口井，”外来者说。干草、百合和仙女只是装饰；平衡表才是唯一要紧的文件。十棵树、一个厂，以及一个四人董事会：这个妥协很薄。一位共和派成员加入了董事会，并要求一条成文的规则。这条河回答了他们问它的每一个问题，却没有回答任何一个他们回避的问题。

### Exercises

- CH112-Q1 [context] How many people sat on the board of the new station?
  - options: three / ten / four / two
- CH112-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “妥协；折中”?
  - options: compromise / balance / statue / compass
- CH112-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: consume the river / consume the lily / consume the hyphen / consume the statue
- CH112-Q4 [cloze] The environmentalist said mankind only rents the right to ______ from the river.
  - options: drink / pour / purchase / bury
- CH112-Q5 [recall] What did the board agree to publish?
  - options: ten years of data / a new statue / the factory accounts / a planetary park plan

### Answers

- CH112-Q1: three - Three members, four directions.
- CH112-Q2: compromise - The factories and the station reached a compromise.
- CH112-Q3: consume the river - Consume combines with a resource, a product, or time.
- CH112-Q4: drink - She described access to water as tenancy, not ownership.
- CH112-Q5: ten years of data - They published the long record.

## CH113 - Who Pays and Who Decides: A Signal / 谁承担，谁决定：一个信号

- theme: 社会问题、经济与公共生活

**1.** The river had taught them to publish the weak numbers; the city would teach them who pays for them. The ninth arc opened with a blow to the city budget and a question that was not relevant to the election: who pays for the water treatment plant? A presidential briefing, a congressional hearing, and a budgetary review had all been scheduled for the same morning. “The favourite answer is a slogan,” the mayor said. “The feasible one is a fare.”

这条河教会他们公开难看的数字；而这座城市会教会他们，谁为这些数字付钱。第九段弧线以对城市预算的一记重击，以及一个与选举无关的问题开场：谁为水处理厂付钱？一场总统简报、一场国会听证和一次预算审查都被安排在了同一个上午。“最讨喜的答案是口号，”市长说，“可行的那个是票价。”

**2.** The signal was a banknote and a coin on the table. “Enforcement without a receiver is theft,” the treasurer said. “Comply or pay, but someone has to collect.” A basketball court, a tennis court, and a volleyball court had been promised in the same document, and the basket of revenue was empty.

信号是桌上的一张钞票和一枚硬币。“没有收款人的执法就是盗窃，”司库说，“要么遵从，要么付钱，但总得有人来收。”同一份文件里承诺了篮球场、网球场和排球场，而收入的篮子却是空的。

**3.** The briefing was authoritative and the grammar was flawless. “We must rid the budget of the habitual exception,” she said. “A privileged chimney and an ordinary one should undergo the same inspection.” The elephant in the room was the candidacy of the deputy mayor, whose moody indifference to the numbers was well known.

简报是权威性的，语法无可挑剔。“我们必须把习惯性的例外从预算里清除掉，”她说，“有特权的烟囱和普通的烟囱应该经历同样的检查。”房间里那头大象是副市长参选这件事，而他对数字那种喜怒无常的漠不关心是众所周知的。

**4.** A bystander at the hearing asked the only grammatical question of the day: who is the subject of this sentence? A persistent leftist on the council, a prayer group, and a burial society all asked the same thing. “A sin is not a line in a budget,” one of them said.

听证会上一名旁观者问出了当天唯一一个语法问题：这句话的主语是谁？议会里一位持久的左派、一个祈祷团体和一个殡葬协会都问了同样的事。“罪过不是预算里的一行，”其中一个人说。

**5.** The proposal was to facilitate the collection of a small fee and to discard the rest. “If we err, we should err on the side of the poor,” the mayor said, with more grace than the room expected. A coin, a banknote, and a basket of receipts were passed around the table.

提案是便利一笔小额费用的收缴，并把其余的都丢弃。“如果我们犯错，就该犯在穷人那一边，”市长说，其优雅超出屋里的预期。一枚硬币、一张钞票和一篮收据在桌上传递。

**6.** The next step was a public register of every exemption, published while the process was still open. It was the first signal that the trade-off would be visible, and the room went quiet for a while. Everyone between the two sides needed a while to agree, and almost nobody wanted to go into the water question again. The air in the room was thick; the land values, the process, and the stars of the evening news were all on the same page. Thus the need for a public register became obvious to us, and someone at the back began to write it down. Within the hour, the favorite proposal was to purify the water fairly and to share the cost.

下一步是公开每一份豁免的登记册，并在流程仍然开放时就发布出来。这是第一个信号：取舍将被看见，而屋里静了一会儿。两边之间的每个人都需要一段时间才能一致，而几乎没有人愿意再回到那个水的问题上。屋里的空气是稠的；地价、流程，以及晚间新闻里的明星，全都在同一页上。因此，公开登记册的必要性在我们看来变得显而易见，而后排有人开始把它记下来。不到一小时，最受欢迎的建议就是公平地净化水并分摊费用。

### Exercises

- CH113-Q1 [context] What question did the chapter open with?
  - options: who pays for the water treatment plant / who won the election / when the courts open / how many sports courts to build
- CH113-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “可行的；适宜的”?
  - options: feasible / relevant / persistent / authoritative
- CH113-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: collect a fee / collect a chimney / collect a burial / collect a prayer
- CH113-Q4 [cloze] The treasurer said enforcement without a receiver is ______.
  - options: theft / grace / bias / candidacy
- CH113-Q5 [recall] What was to be published while the process was still open?
  - options: a register of every exemption / the election result / the sports budget / the mayor's briefing

### Answers

- CH113-Q1: who pays for the water treatment plant - Funding, not politics, opened the arc.
- CH113-Q2: feasible - A fare was the feasible answer.
- CH113-Q3: collect a fee - Collect combines with a fee, a tax, or evidence.
- CH113-Q4: theft - A rule without a collector is confiscation.
- CH113-Q5: a register of every exemption - They published exemptions while debate continued.

## CH114 - Who Pays and Who Decides: The First Promise / 谁承担，谁决定：第一次承诺

- theme: 社会问题、经济与公共生活

**1.** The first promise of the arc was made in a room with a red banner and a bear on the city crest: the meeting would show the evidence, not the party line. “A democratic process is not a party conference,” the representative said. “It is a jury with an audience.”

这段弧线的第一个承诺是在一间挂着红色横幅、市徽上有一只熊的房间里做出的：会议将展示证据，而不是党派立场。“民主程序不是党代表大会，”那位代表说，“它是一个有听众的陪审团。”

**2.** The poor would be hurt first and the corporate lobby last, and everybody in the room knew it. “We must introduce the numbers before the vote,” Maya wrote in her article. “A minority of ten thousand people cannot bear a revolution in fares alone.” The transcript of the meeting would be published in full, including the confession that the first estimate had been wrong.

穷人会最先受伤，企业游说集团最后，而屋里的每个人都知道这一点。“我们必须在表决之前把数字摆出来，”曼雅在她的文章里写道，“一万人的少数群体承担不起票价上的一场革命。”会议的文字记录将全文公布，包括承认第一次估算错了这件事。

**3.** The conservative member, the progressive member, and the politician from the east district all had a bond with the utilities. “We acknowledge the allegation,” the mayor said. “We do not accept the nuisance of a closed budget.” A jail sentence for piracy of the city's software was on the same agenda, which made the room laugh bitterly.

保守派成员、进步派成员，以及来自东区的政客，都与公用事业公司有一条纽带。“我们承认这一指控，”市长说，“我们不接受一个封闭预算带来的麻烦。”议程上还有一条对盗用城市软件的海盗行为的监禁判决，这让屋里苦笑起来。

**4.** The revenue question was harder than the taxation question. “A terrorism trial, a terrorist arrest, a vacation cancelled: none of that helps us fund a treatment plant,” the treasurer said. “What helps is a fare that a poor family can pay.”

收入问题比税收问题更难。“一场恐怖主义审判、一次恐怖分子抓捕、一个被取消的假期：这些都不能帮我们给水处理厂筹到钱，”司库说，“有用的是一个贫穷家庭付得起的票价。”

**5.** The promise was to publish the succession of costs and the defection of the suppliers before the next session. A contentious reformation of the fare system, a condemnation from the lobby, and a socialize programme for the water users were all on the list.

这个承诺是在下次会议之前公开成本的连续变化和供应商的背弃。票价体系一场有争议的改革、来自游说集团的一份谴责，以及一个面向用水者的社会化项目，都在清单上。

**6.** The imagine part was the hardest: to ask the couple at the fair, the leg of the west market, and the natural scepticism of the tea stall what they would pay. “A direct question is worth a stick of evidence,” Lin said. “We will introduce nothing that the audience cannot see.” Different people, the same difficulty: a direct question about the east market, the fair, the floor of the west hall, and the leg of the investment. We could imagine the hurt of a poor family, but the tea and the stick of evidence had to be produced before the vote. The world versus the city, a brain versus a budget, a republican versus a progressive: a bankruptcy, a convert, and an outing were all that the party could offer.

需要想象的部分最难：去问展会上的那对夫妇、西市场那条腿，以及茶摊天生的怀疑——他们愿意付多少。“一个直接的问题抵得上一根证据的棍子，”林伟说，“我们不引入任何听众看不见的东西。”不同的人，同样的困难：一个关于东市场、展会、西厅地面和这笔投资那条腿的直接问题。我们可以想象一个贫穷家庭的伤痛，但在表决之前，茶和那根证据的棍子必须被拿出来。世界对城市、大脑对预算、共和派对进步派：一场破产、一个皈依者，以及一次郊游，就是这个党能提供的全部。

### Exercises

- CH114-Q1 [context] What did the representative say a democratic process is?
  - options: a jury with an audience / a party conference / a corporate lobby / a taxation office
- CH114-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “收入；税收”?
  - options: revenue / fare / bond / nuisance
- CH114-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: introduce a measure / introduce a nuisance / introduce a bear / introduce a banner
- CH114-Q4 [cloze] The mayor said they acknowledge the allegation but do not accept the ______ of a closed budget.
  - options: nuisance / succession / defection / piracy
- CH114-Q5 [recall] What would be published in full?
  - options: the transcript of the meeting / the corporate accounts / the jury list / the fare schedule

### Answers

- CH114-Q1: a jury with an audience - He framed the process as public judgement.
- CH114-Q2: revenue - The revenue question was harder than taxation.
- CH114-Q3: introduce a measure - Introduce combines with a measure, a scheme, or a guest.
- CH114-Q4: nuisance - He conceded the claim but not the process complaint.
- CH114-Q5: the transcript of the meeting - The record would be published verbatim.

## CH115 - Who Pays and Who Decides: A Missing Voice / 谁承担，谁决定：缺席的声音

- theme: 社会问题、经济与公共生活

**1.** The third meeting of the congress was held in a pub down the road from city hall, and the missing voice was already there. “The claim is that the fee is free,” a cleaner from the county said. “The truth is that the benefit passes down to the base and never reaches us.” The budget for the treatment plant contained a sum of eight million euro and no line for the people who would pay it.

国会第三次会议开在市厅路下的一家酒馆里，而那个缺席的声音其实早就在那儿了。“说法是这笔费用是免费的，”县里来的一位清洁工说，“实情是好处顺着底部流下去，从来到不了我们这儿。”水处理厂的预算里有一笔八百万欧元的总额，却没有一行留给将要付钱的人。

**2.** “We have a procedure, a species of language, and an internal review,” the mayor said. “That is how a republic works.” She was obedient to the rule and wholly unaware that the rule had a limitation. “Every revolution begins with a small accumulation of annoyance,” the cleaner said. “This is the seven hundredth one.”

“我们有程序、有一类语言、有一次内部审查，”市长说，“共和国就是这样运作的。”她对规则很顺从，也完全没有意识到这条规则本身有一个局限。“每一场革命都始于一桩小小的烦恼的积累，”清洁工说，“这是第七百桩。”

**3.** The consultant from the bureau showed a slide about productivity and evolution, and a second about the operational capability of the new plant. “A superior design is not the same as a deed,” an economist in the row behind said. “You have found a tragedy, not a plan, and the underground pipe is misleading.”

局里来的顾问讲了一页关于生产力与演化的幻灯片，又讲了一页关于新厂的运营能力。“优秀的设计不等于一件行为，”后排一位经济学家说，“你们找到的是一场悲剧，不是一个方案，而那条地下管道是误导人的。”

**4.** “Do not undermine the small businesses and call it virtue,” the cleaner said. “A civilian and a governor both drink the same water.” The ministerial party, the dance hall, the disco, and the pub had all sent someone, and the nationality of the taxpayer was never mentioned.

“不要破坏小本生意，然后称之为美德，”清洁工说，“平民和总督喝的是同一口水。”部长的党、舞厅、迪斯科和酒馆都派了人来，而纳税人的国籍从未被提到。

**5.** The embarrassment of the meeting was the set of two maps: one showing the eight districts that would pay, and one showing the five that would drink. “We probably need a third map,” Maya wrote. “The same oil, the same pipe, and a different set of names.”

会议令人困窘之处是那两张地图：一张显示将要付钱的八个区，一张显示将要喝水的五个区。“我们大概需要第三张地图，”曼雅写道，“同样的油、同样的管道，却是另一组名字。”

**6.** The voice that had been missing turned out to contain the whole argument. “Show us the passage of the money,” the cleaner said, “and the world will decide for itself.” The meeting ended without a vote, and the next one was set for the following week. The evidence would confront the council with a twist it did not want; an aid package and a supper in the pub were not a settlement, and the county knew it.

那个一直缺席的声音，其实包含着整个论证。“把钱的流经给我们看，”清洁工说，“世界会自己作决定。”会议在没有表决的情况下结束，下一次定在下一周。这些证据将用一个委员会不想要的反转来面对它；一揽子援助和酒馆里的一顿晚餐不是和解，而这个县知道这一点。

### Exercises

- CH115-Q1 [context] Where was the third meeting of the congress held?
  - options: in a pub down the road from city hall / in the city hall chamber / in the treatment plant / in a disco
- CH115-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “积聚；累积”?
  - options: accumulation / limitation / capability / embarrassment
- CH115-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: undermine a business / undermine a slide / undermine a sum / undermine a pub
- CH115-Q4 [cloze] The economist said a superior design is not the same as a ______.
  - options: deed / budget / species / republic
- CH115-Q5 [recall] What did the cleaner ask the meeting to show?
  - options: the passage of the money / the new design / the eight districts / the minister's letter

### Answers

- CH115-Q1: in a pub down the road from city hall - The congress met in a pub, not a chamber.
- CH115-Q2: accumulation - Grievances accumulate into a revolution.
- CH115-Q3: undermine a business - Undermine combines with a business, a claim, or trust.
- CH115-Q4: deed - A design is not an action.
- CH115-Q5: the passage of the money - She wanted the flow of funds made visible.

## CH116 - Who Pays and Who Decides: Evidence on the Table / 谁承担，谁决定：摆上桌面的证据

- theme: 社会问题、经济与公共生活

**1.** The evidence on the table concerned a point of law and a pile of old bills. “The previous administration left a wholly irregular account,” the auditor said. “There is no factual basis for the figure, and the province has been lodging the cost in the wrong column.” Nobody was afraid to say it aloud this time.

桌上的证据涉及一个法律要点和一堆旧账单。“上一届政府留下了一个完全不合规的账户，”审计员说，“那个数字没有事实依据，而这个省一直把成本寄存在错误的栏目里。”这一次没有人害怕把它大声说出来。

**2.** The incident that made the case was a robbery of the meter box and a riot at the water office. “A scandal of this size is not occasional,” an economist said. “It is inevitable while the system dissolves into a hundred small payments.” A jealous official had disturbed the record, and the golden seal on the monument outside had been stolen twice.

让这个案子成立的事件是电表箱被抢和水务办公室的一场骚乱。“这种规模的丑闻不是偶然的，”一位经济学家说，“只要系统瓦解成上百笔小额付款，它就是不可避免的。”一位嫉妒的官员扰乱了记录，而外面纪念碑上的金印已被偷过两次。

**3.** The stable next to the yard, the foggy morning, and the haze over the river all pointed to the same corner. “We cannot climb out of this with encouragement and amusement,” she said. “The cornerstone of the argument is a single account, kept in its entirety.”

院子旁的牛棚、雾蒙蒙的早晨，以及河上的阴霾，都指向同一个角落。“我们无法靠鼓励和消遣爬出这个坑，”她说，“这场论证的奠基石是一个单一的账户，而且要完整地保存。”
