**4.** The weather of the meeting turned when a shopkeeper spoke about a continual stream of small payments. “My jealousy of the big firms is not the point,” he said. “The point is that the meter melts my margin and the guarantee boils away.”

会议的天气在一个店主谈到持续不断的小额付款时变了。“我对大公司的嫉妒不是重点，”他说，“重点是电表熔化我的利润，而保证金就这样煮沸蒸发。”

**5.** The committee asked for a convenient summary of a monstrous file. “A stable budget and a solemnity of procedure are not the same thing,” the mayor said. “Legislate quickly and the impossibility becomes a grievance.”

委员会要求对一个庞大的档案给出一份方便的摘要。“稳定的预算和程序的庄重不是同一回事，”市长说，“匆忙立法，那种不可能就会变成一桩冤情。”

**6.** The evidence was complete enough to name the misfortune: a system with no single owner, a spoil of small favours, and a shade of doubt over every receipt. “The total is not the story,” the auditor said. “The story is who signed the lodging bills.” The benefit of the congress was a data set and a computer program that could name every county claim; the cell of auditors had nothing but a machine and a story. “This is not science,” the auditor said. “It is a system with a source and an operation, and it grieves me that it works.” A grand disorder, a shady ledger, and a stability bought with a grievous loan: the whole fulfilment of the account turned out to be instantaneous and false. Free energy was not a technology the council owned, and the county knew that too.

证据完整到足以给这桩不幸命名：一个没有单一主人的系统、一份小额人情的战利品，以及笼罩在每张收据上的一层怀疑。“总数不是故事，”审计员说，“故事是谁在那些寄存账单上签了字。”这场国会的好处是一份数据集和一个能为每一笔县级索赔命名的电脑程序；而审计小组只有一台机器和一个故事。“这不是科学，”审计员说，“这是一个带有来源和运作的系统，而它居然运转，这让我难过。”一场宏大的混乱、一本可疑的账，以及用一笔沉重的贷款买来的稳定：这份账目的全部履行，结果是瞬间的、虚假的。免费能源不是议会拥有的技术，而这个县也知道这一点。

### Exercises

- CH116-Q1 [context] What did the auditor say about the previous account?
  - options: it was wholly irregular / it was balanced / it was too small / it was published
- CH116-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “不可避免的”?
  - options: inevitable / occasional / irregular / convenient
- CH116-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: keep an account / keep a haze / keep a riot / keep a monument
- CH116-Q4 [cloze] The auditor said the story is who signed the ______ bills.
  - options: lodging / golden / foggy / monstrous
- CH116-Q5 [recall] What did the committee ask for?
  - options: a convenient summary of a monstrous file / a new monument / a smaller budget / a longer procedure

### Answers

- CH116-Q1: it was wholly irregular - The account was entirely out of order.
- CH116-Q2: inevitable - The economist called the scandal structurally inevitable.
- CH116-Q3: keep an account - Keep combines with an account, a promise, or a record.
- CH116-Q4: lodging - The signatures on the lodging bills were the real story.
- CH116-Q5: a convenient summary of a monstrous file - They wanted the huge file reduced to a summary.

## CH117 - Who Pays and Who Decides: The Trial Run / 谁承担，谁决定：第一次试行

- theme: 社会问题、经济与公共生活

**1.** The trial run began with an act of the council and a fund of real money. “The issue is not the answer, it is the order of the questions,” the mayor said. “We will assess one ward, then decide.” Security, welfare, and liability were all in the proclamation, and so was the obligatory payment of two pounds a month.

试行以议会的一项法案和一笔真金白银的基金开始。“问题不在答案，而在问题的次序，”市长说，“我们先评估一个选区，再作决定。”安全、福利与责任都写进了那份公告，强制性的每月两英镑缴款也在其中。

**2.** The finding of the first week was a surprise: the appeal of the scheme was strongest among the poor. “They are not chasing charity,” the social worker said. “They are buying certainty.” A complaint from an elite household, a pile of forms, and a monetary flaw in the tariff were the first three items on the list.

第一周的发现令人意外：这套方案在穷人中最有吸引力。“他们不是在追慈善，”社工说，“他们是在买确定性。”一户精英家庭的投诉、一堆表格，以及费率中一处货币上的缺陷，是清单上的头三项。

**3.** The truth of the trial was that a small payment produced a large satisfaction. “The economy of a wallet is not the economy of a city,” the treasurer warned, “and a boom in small deposits can float a false confidence.” The point was written on the board in a chip of chalk.

试行的真相是：一笔小额付款产生了很大的满足感。“钱包的经济学不是城市的经济学，”司库警告说，“小额存款的繁荣可以托起一种虚假的信心。”这一点被用一截粉笔写在了黑板上。

**4.** The discovery of the second week was the involvement of the retailers. “Either the shop pays or the citizen does, whereas both of them already pay twice,” a shopkeeper said. The handicapped residents of the third ward were the group the scheme had missed entirely.

第二周的发现是零售商的卷入。“要么店主付，要么市民付，然而两者早已付了两次，”一位店主说。第三选区的残疾居民，是这套方案完全漏掉的群体。

**5.** The valid objection came from the defendant in a pending case about the water bill. “A competent administration does not need perfection,” she said. “It needs a flaw it can admit.” The authorization for the second trial was signed the same afternoon.

有效的异议来自一位待审水费案中的被告。“一个称职的行政当局不需要完美，”她说，“它需要一个它敢于承认的缺陷。”第二次试行的授权在当天下午就签了字。

**6.** The containment of the cost, the answer to the liability question, and the announcement of the next stage were the three things the meeting produced. “There is no support in a proclamation,” Lin wrote. “There is support in a payment that arrives every month.” The point of the trial was simple, and it had to be shown. We had to pick between a force and an argument, and between a thing and a promise. “Introduce the payment, show the evidence, and let the meeting decide,” the mayor said. It was the same party and the same poor ward, either way, and someone within the world of city hall had to be wrong first. As an institution, the council was liable for the imposition of the fee, and a turkey dinner was not the answer.

成本的抑制、责任问题的答案，以及下一阶段的公告，是这次会议产出的三样东西。“公告里没有支持，”林伟写道，“支持在于每个月按时到来的一笔付款。”这次试行的要点很简单，而且必须被展示出来。我们必须在一种力量与一个论证之间、在一件东西与一个承诺之间做选择。“引入这笔付款，把证据拿出来，让会议决定，”市长说。无论怎么选，都是同一个党和同一个贫困选区，而市政厅这个世界里必须有人先出错。作为一个机构，议会要为这项费用的征收负责，而一顿火鸡晚餐不是答案。

### Exercises

- CH117-Q1 [context] What did the mayor say the issue was?
  - options: the order of the questions / the size of the fund / the security guards / the water bill
- CH117-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “责任；债务”?
  - options: liability / welfare / satisfaction / finding
- CH117-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: assess a ward / assess a wallet / assess a turkey / assess a chip
- CH117-Q4 [cloze] The treasurer warned that a boom in small deposits can float a false ______.
  - options: confidence / liability / proclamation / authorization
- CH117-Q5 [recall] What did the defendant say a competent administration needs?
  - options: a flaw it can admit / a perfect system / a larger fund / a longer trial

### Answers

- CH117-Q1: the order of the questions - The sequence of decisions was the real issue.
- CH117-Q2: liability - The liability question had to be answered.
- CH117-Q3: assess a ward - Assess combines with a ward, a risk, or a cost.
- CH117-Q4: confidence - Small deposits can inflate false confidence.
- CH117-Q5: a flaw it can admit - She asked for honesty rather than perfection.

## CH118 - Who Pays and Who Decides: Pressure Builds / 谁承担，谁决定：压力上升

- theme: 社会问题、经济与公共生活

**1.** The pressure built when the government changed the funding rule for the water utility. “The state will not carry the maintenance of a plant that the market cannot finance,” the minister said. “A dollar of public money has to buy a right, not a habit.” The declaration was short and the objection was long.

当政府修改水务公用事业的资金规则时，压力就累积起来了。“国家不会承担一家市场无法融资的工厂的维护费，”部长说，“一美元公共资金必须买来一项权利，而不是一个习惯。”那份宣告很短，而异议很长。

**2.** The social argument was harder than the economic one. “A policy that works in the capital does not work in a community of four thousand,” a sociologist said. “The wealth of a society is not the same as the wealth of a sector.” The left wing of the council and the liberal bloc both claimed the phrase as their own.

社会层面的论证比经济层面的更难。“一项在首都有效的政策，在四千人的社区里无效，”一位社会学家说，“一个社会的财富不等于一个部门的财富。”议会的左翼和自由派集团都把这句话当成了自己的。

**3.** The civil question was who counted as a citizen. “A tax on water is not the same as a fee,” a socialist economist wrote. “One is a bill, the other is a bail for a utility that has already failed once.” The legal opinion on the table said the two could not be separated.

市民层面的问题是谁算作公民。“对水征的税和对水收的费不是一回事，”一位社会主义派经济学家写道，“一个是账单，另一个是为一家已经失败过一次的公用事业公司的保释金。”桌上的法律意见说，两者无法分开。

**4.** The popular politics of the moment made the economics harder. “The market is not a democracy,” the mayor said, “and the interest of the taxpayer is not the interest of the citizen.” A dozen rates, four audits, and one red line across the page: the argument had a real shape at last.

当时的流行政治让经济问题更难。“市场不是民主，”市长说，“纳税人的利益不等于公民的利益。”十二种费率、四次审计，以及横跨页面的一条红线：这场论证终于有了真实的形状。

**5.** The pressure from the banks was simple: a declaration of socialism would close the credit line. “A dollar of funding deferred is a dollar of maintenance lost,” the treasurer said. The republican member disagreed, and so did the socialist member, and both of them were right.

来自银行的压力很简单：一份社会主义的宣告会关掉授信额度。“被推迟的一美元资金，就是失去的一美元维护，”司库说。共和派成员不同意，社会主义派成员也不同意，而两个人都是对的。

**6.** The act of the council was to hold the rate, off the record and on the condition of a second audit. “We can talk about capitalism later,” the mayor said. “The pipe under this street will not wait.” The pressure had produced a decision, and the decision had produced the next pressure. The act of the council, the order of the day, and the real support of the four wards: all of it was on the record. Free water for the poor, a red line for the rich, and a republican objection from the left: the talk had a shape at last, and the issue was no longer off the table. The law was federal, the economy was local, and the citation of Marxism in a footnote about imperialism was, as one member said, a remark about civilisation rather than about the water bill. The only action left to the council was to wait, and the low point of the debate was still ahead.

议会的法案是维持费率不变，不记录在案，并以第二次审计为条件。“资本主义我们可以晚点再谈，”市长说，“这条街下面的管子不会等。”压力促成了一项决定，而这项决定又催生了下一轮压力。议会的法案、当日的议程，以及四个选区真正的支持：全都在记录上。给穷人的免费水、给富人的一条红线，以及来自左翼的一位共和派的反对：这场谈话终于有了形状，而这个问题不再被搁在桌下。法律是联邦的，经济是地方的，而在一篇关于帝国主义的脚注里引用马克思主义，正如一位成员所说，是一句关于文明的评论，而不是关于水费的。议会剩下的唯一行动就是等待，而这场辩论的低点还在前面。

### Exercises

- CH118-Q1 [context] What did the minister say a dollar of public money must buy?
  - options: a right, not a habit / a pipe / a subsidy / a new plant
- CH118-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “公用事业；功用”?
  - options: utility / sector / capital / bail
- CH118-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: carry the maintenance / carry the socialism / carry the sociologist / carry the imperialism
- CH118-Q4 [cloze] The mayor said the market is not a ______.
  - options: democracy / utility / declaration / citation
- CH118-Q5 [recall] What condition did the council attach to holding the rate?
  - options: a second audit / a new tax / a public vote / a federal grant

### Answers

- CH118-Q1: a right, not a habit - Public money had to purchase entitlement, not routine.
- CH118-Q2: utility - The water utility could not finance its own maintenance.
- CH118-Q3: carry the maintenance - Carry combines with maintenance, a cost, or a risk.
- CH118-Q4: democracy - He separated market choice from civic decision.
- CH118-Q5: a second audit - The rate hold required another audit.

## CH119 - Who Pays and Who Decides: The Disagreement / 谁承担，谁决定：分歧

- theme: 社会问题、经济与公共生活

**1.** The disagreement inside the municipality was about the occupancy of a coastal lawn, and it grew out of a murder that had happened in the next village. “The law is strong,” the counselor said, “and the reliance of the public on a solemn policy is not a myth.” The two sides did not differ about the facts; they differed about the order in which the facts mattered.

市当局内部的分歧是关于一片沿海草坪的占用，而它源于邻村发生的一起谋杀。“法律是强有力的，”参事说，“公众对一项庄严政策的信赖不是神话。”双方在事实上的看法并无不同；他们的不同在于事实重要性的次序。

**2.** The residential community wanted a peaceful locality with a naval memorial and an extended lawn. The outside investor wanted an honorary citizenship, a charming hotel, and a noble clientele. “You cannot accommodate both,” the heiress said. “One of them will conceal the other, and the government will call it persuasion.”

住宅社区想要一个有海军纪念碑和一片延伸草坪的平静地方。外来投资者想要一个荣誉市民身份、一家迷人的酒店，以及一群贵族客户。“你无法同时容纳两者，”女继承人说，“其中一个会掩盖另一个，而政府会把它叫做说服。”

**3.** The controversy was institutional as much as social. “A racist clause in a private contract is still injurious to the public interest,” the observer wrote. “The domination of one market by one family is not economic freedom.” The beggar at the gate of the municipality had a different point of view, and no one asked him for it.

这场论战既是社会性的，也是制度性的。“私人合同里的一条种族主义条款，对公共利益依然是有害的，”观察者写道，“一个市场被一个家族支配，不是经济自由。”市当局门口的乞丐有另一种观点，而没有人问他要。

**4.** The romantic story of the coastal estate had turned into a fictional one: a mythical founder, a murderous rival, and an alleged deception at the heart of the deed. “We are emphatic about the wrong thing,” Lin said. “The right question is who pays for the drainage, not who owns the myth.”

沿海庄园那个浪漫的故事已经变成了一个虚构的故事：一位神话般的创始人、一个凶狠的对手，以及契约核心处一桩被断言的欺骗。“我们在错误的事情上语气强烈，”林伟说，“真正的问题是谁为排水付钱，而不是谁拥有那个神话。”

**5.** The community and the state disagreed about the next step, and the disagreement was the most honest thing in the room. “A society that cannot say who is responsible will always look for a foreigner or a beggar,” Maya wrote. “That is how a supremacy of the loud becomes a policy.”

社区与国家在下一步上不一致，而这种不一致是屋里最诚实的东西。“一个说不出谁该负责的社会，总会去找一个外国人或者一个乞丐，”曼雅写道，“大声者的至上就是这样变成政策的。”

**6.** The solemn conclusion of the meeting was that nothing had been decided. The extended lawn, the naval museum, and the honorary title stayed on the table, and the room, for once, was quiet enough to hear the sea. It was vain to argue about religion and money against a council that had already decided; the extended lawn and the honest question were the only things left, and the next village would hear about it either way.

会议庄严的结论是什么都没有决定。那片延伸的草坪、那座海军博物馆，以及那个荣誉头衔仍留在桌上，而屋子里这一次安静到能听见海。在一个已经作出决定的议会面前争论宗教和金钱是徒劳的；那片延伸的草坪和那个诚实的问题是他们仅剩的东西，而邻村无论如何都会听说这件事。

### Exercises

- CH119-Q1 [context] What did the two sides differ about?
  - options: the order in which the facts mattered / the facts themselves / the price of the lawn / the naval memorial
- CH119-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “欺骗；诡计”?
  - options: deception / persuasion / occupancy / domination
- CH119-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: conceal a fact / conceal a lawn / conceal a village / conceal a beggar
- CH119-Q4 [cloze] The observer wrote that the domination of one market by one family is not economic ______.
  - options: freedom / myth / deception / reliance
- CH119-Q5 [recall] What did Lin say the right question was?
  - options: who pays for the drainage / who owns the estate / who funds the museum / who holds the title

### Answers

- CH119-Q1: the order in which the facts mattered - The dispute was about priority, not accuracy.
- CH119-Q2: deception - An alleged deception sat at the heart of the deed.
- CH119-Q3: conceal a fact - Conceal combines with a fact, a cost, or a motive.
- CH119-Q4: freedom - Market domination is not economic freedom.
- CH119-Q5: who pays for the drainage - He moved the question from myth to cost.

## CH120 - Who Pays and Who Decides: What the Numbers Hide / 谁承担，谁决定：数字隐藏了什么

- theme: 社会问题、经济与公共生活

**1.** The numbers now required a room of their own, and the reality they described was worse than the practical one. “The figure for the circle of payers is a square peg,” the editor wrote. “It does not fit at any angle.” The observer and the adviser had both signed the headline, and neither of them was willing to defend it in public.

这些数字如今需要一间自己的房间，而它们所描述的现实比实际的那个更糟。“缴款者那个圆的数字是一根方钉，”编辑写道，“它在任何角度都嵌不进去。”观察者和顾问都在那则大标题上签了字，而两人都不愿公开为它辩护。

**2.** The ban on new connections was a severe violation of the fabric of the old town. “A defensive policy with an offensive punishment is not morality,” the adviser said. “It is a denial dressed as a bargain.” A harsh winter, a reluctant council, and a loyalty to the old tariff had produced the whole crisis.

对新接入的禁令，是对老城构造的一处严重违反。“一项用攻击性惩罚来执行的防卫政策不是道德，”顾问说，“那是披着交易外衣的否认。”一个严酷的冬天、一个不情愿的议会，以及对旧费率的一种忠诚，造成了整场危机。

**3.** The insert in the periodical was an invitation to the public to publicize its own bills. “Fame is not the point,” the editor said. “A household that is penalized for using water is a fact, not a headline.” The consultative committee met in a church hall and played chess afterwards.

期刊里的那份插页，是邀请公众公开自己的账单。“名声不是重点，”编辑说，“一个因为用水而被处罚的家庭是一个事实，不是一条头条。”咨询委员会在一座教堂大厅里开会，会后下了一盘国际象棋。

**4.** The cruelty of the numbers was their symmetry: the circle of payers and the circle of users were drawn in the same square. “A cruel inference is still an inference,” the adviser said. “You can forbid the word and you cannot forbid the arithmetic.”

这些数字的残酷在于它们的对称：缴款者的圆和使用者的圆画在同一个方框里。“残酷的推论仍然是推论，”顾问说，“你可以禁止那个词，却无法禁止算术。”

**5.** The betrayal was in the timing. “We were told to oversee a process and not a war,” the observer wrote. “The warfare of two departments has produced a diplomacy of delays.” The editor's denial of the first draft was, in the end, correct.

背叛在于时机。“我们被告知要监督一个流程，而不是一场战争，”观察者写道，“两个部门的交战产出了一套拖延的外交。”编辑对第一稿的否认，最终是对的。

**6.** The real issue came into the room at last: who draws the circle, and with what instrument. The air in the hall was cold, the water in the well was low, and the group had, for the first time, a long list and a short time. The order of the day was not an act of hell but a development we had to get through; the inviting insert in the periodical told us so. The room, the square, and the air in it were all part of the same period, and the editor asked us to defend the headline once more.

真正的问题终于进了房间：谁来画这个圆，用什么工具画。大厅里的空气是冷的，井里的水是低的，而这个小组第一次有了一份长清单和一段短时间。当日的议程不是一桩地狱般的行为，而是一段我们必须走完的发展；期刊里那份邀请人的插页就是这么告诉我们的。这个房间、这个广场，以及其中的空气，都属于同一个时期，而编辑要求我们再为那则大标题辩护一次。

### Exercises

- CH120-Q1 [context] What did the editor say the figure for the circle of payers was?
  - options: a square peg / a circle / a headline / a bargain
- CH120-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “推论；推断”?
  - options: inference / violation / denial / betrayal
- CH120-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: oversee a process / oversee a chess game / oversee a fabric / oversee a headline
- CH120-Q4 [cloze] The adviser said an offensive punishment is not ______.
  - options: morality / diplomacy / fame / loyalty
- CH120-Q5 [recall] What did the observer say the warfare of two departments had produced?
  - options: a diplomacy of delays / a new tariff / a shorter winter / a public apology

### Answers

- CH120-Q1: a square peg - The figure did not fit its own frame.
- CH120-Q2: inference - The conclusion was an inference from symmetrical data.
- CH120-Q3: oversee a process - Oversee combines with a process, a budget, or a trial.
- CH120-Q4: morality - Punishment is not the same as a moral policy.
- CH120-Q5: a diplomacy of delays - Departmental conflict produced procedural delay.

## CH121 - Who Pays and Who Decides: A Better Question / 谁承担，谁决定：更好的问题

- theme: 社会问题、经济与公共生活

**1.** The better question was announced in parliament and then repeated in a conversation on the steps: who decides what a household needs? “We prefer to ask how much water is missing,” the member said. “We should ask who is entitled to it.” The launch of the new tariff was, ultimately, a launch of a different question.

那个更好的问题在议会里被宣布，随后又在台阶上的一场交谈里被重复：谁来决定一个家庭需要什么？“我们更愿意问缺多少水，”那位议员说，“我们应该问谁有权得到它。”新费率的启动，说到底是一个不同问题的启动。

**2.** The strange part was how little it took to convince the room. “You can devise a rate that corresponds to a real bill,” the economist said. “A proposition with a definite number beats a certainty with none.” The contradiction at the heart of the old system was that it was both conclusive and wasteful.

奇怪之处在于说服这间屋子所需的东西如此之少。“你可以设计一个与真实账单相符的费率，”经济学家说，“一个带明确数字的提议胜过一种没有数字的确定性。”旧体系核心处的矛盾是：它既是决定性的，又是浪费的。

**3.** “We proclaim validity and we cannot verify the meter,” the clerk said. “We infer mastery from a conquest of paperwork.” The generosity of the old exemption was, in fact, economical only for the rich, and anyhow nobody had ever checked the verification.

“我们宣告有效性，却无法核实水表，”职员说，“我们从一堆文书的征服中推断出精通。”旧豁免的慷慨其实只对富人合算，而且无论如何从来没有人核对过那项验证。

**4.** The final draft was shorter by half, and its permanence was the point. “A rule that survives a change of government is worth more than a rule that sucks up the whole budget,” the chair said. “We have hidden behind a falsehood: that a complicated form is a fair one.”

最终稿缩短了一半，而它的持久性正是要点。“一条能挺过政府更替的规则，比一条吸干整个预算的规则更有价值，”主席说，“我们一直躲在一个谎言后面：复杂表格就是公平表格。”

**5.** The inducement to cheat was the exemption itself. “Where there is hiding, there is a lie,” Maya wrote. “Where there is a definite limit, there is a defensible argument.” The missing element was not money but room: a level at which a poor household could still pay.

作弊的诱因就是豁免本身。“有隐匿的地方就有谎言，”曼雅写道，“有明确限值的地方就有可辩护的论证。”缺失的不是钱而是空间：一个贫穷家庭仍然付得起的水平。

**6.** The invitation to the public was issued under the new rule, and the first question at the meeting was the better one. The end of the session, the government and the market on the same page, the result of the whole period: a question about need rather than about price. The adventure of the launch was that it could fail; the final conversation, the strange proposition, and the missing number all had to correspond to something real. We would rather be conclusive than generous, and ultimately that was the inducement we had to remove. Then the parliament would announce the figure publicly. If we now validate the rule, we can show the party that the law, the interest, and the policy are on the right side; it would be contradictory to attain permanence without an infinity of patience. The social world and the state would have to agree before the level changed.

对公众的邀请在新规则下发出，而会上的第一个问题就是那个更好的问题。这场会议的结束、政府与市场在同一页上、整个时期的结果：一个关于需要而非关于价格的问题。这次发布的冒险之处在于它可能失败；最后的对话、那个奇怪的提议，以及那个缺失的数字，都必须与某种真实的东西相符。我们宁愿做决定性的那个，也不愿做慷慨的那个，而说到底，那正是我们必须移除的诱因。然后议会会公开宣布这个数字。如果我们现在验证这条规则，我们就能向那个党表明：法律、利益和政策都在正确的一边；没有无限的耐心而想获得持久，是自相矛盾的。社会世界与国家必须先取得一致，水平才会改变。

### Exercises

- CH121-Q1 [context] What did the member say they should ask?
  - options: who is entitled to the water / how much water is missing / what the meter costs / how long the form is
- CH121-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “矛盾；反驳”?
  - options: contradiction / proposition / validity / permanence
- CH121-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: verify a meter / verify a lie / verify a conquest / verify an inducement
- CH121-Q4 [cloze] The chair said a rule that survives a change of government is worth more than one that sucks up the whole ______.
  - options: budget / parliament / conversation / adventure
- CH121-Q5 [recall] What did Maya say is where there is hiding?
  - options: there is a lie / there is a bargain / there is generosity / there is mastery

### Answers

- CH121-Q1: who is entitled to the water - She replaced a quantity question with a rights question.
- CH121-Q2: contradiction - The old system was both conclusive and wasteful.
- CH121-Q3: verify a meter - Verify combines with a meter, a claim, or a figure.
- CH121-Q4: budget - Durability matters more than total spend.
- CH121-Q5: there is a lie - Concealment breeds falsehood.

## CH122 - Who Pays and Who Decides: The Second Attempt / 谁承担，谁决定：第二次尝试

- theme: 社会问题、经济与公共生活

**1.** The second attempt was designed to win a majority and to keep the profit inside the city. The author of the plan, a concerned juror, and a religious charity were all present. “A reasonable visitor can see the ring of poverty around the center,” the author said. “Sufficient spending is not the same as administrative courage.”

第二次尝试的设计目标，是赢得多数并把利润留在城里。方案作者、一位关切的陪审员，以及一家宗教慈善机构都在场。“一位讲道理的访客能看到中心周围那圈贫困，”作者说，“充足的经费不等于行政上的勇气。”

**2.** The winner of the first round was the so-called independent list, and its members were angry about the alleged secrecy of the customs office. “A guilty suspect is not a conviction,” the investigator said. “We confine the investigation, not the people.” The prize of the second round was a restrictive but diplomatic settlement.

第一轮的胜者是那份所谓的独立名单，其成员对海关被指称的保密做法感到愤怒。“一个有罪的嫌疑人不等于一项定罪，”调查者说，“我们限制的是调查，不是人。”第二轮的奖品是一份有限制但讲外交的和解方案。

**3.** The organizational problem was that the victims of the old tariff had no juror of their own. “We deprive the poorest ward of a voice and then we call the result a democracy,” the pollster wrote. “That is how you victimize a neighborhood legally.”

组织上的问题是：旧费率的受害者没有自己的陪审员。“我们剥夺最贫困选区的发言权，然后把结果称为民主，”民意调查人写道，“这就是合法地使一个街区受害的方式。”

**4.** The rage in the hall was real and it was not meritorious. “A gangster and a charity both use a stony face,” the juror said. “The difference is the ledger.” The confession of the clerk about the drainage accounts was the turning point of the evening, and nobody had expected it.

大厅里的愤怒是真实的，而且无功劳可言。“歹徒和慈善机构都用一张无情面孔，”陪审员说，“区别在于那本账簿。”职员关于排水账户的坦白是当晚的转折点，而没有人预料到。

**5.** The affluent ward and the chaotic one were set at the same standard for the first time. “We must confess that the first attempt failed on the same set of numbers,” Lin said. “The light, the oil, and the main pipe were all counted and none of them was compared.”

富裕选区和混乱选区第一次被放在同一个标准下。“我们必须坦白，第一次尝试是在同一套数字上失败的，”林伟说，“光、油和主管道都被统计过，而没有一样被比较过。”

**6.** The second attempt was trying, and it was not happy work. “We enjoy the theory and we bleed over the drainage,” the treasurer said. “That is the whole of the job.” The benefit of the doubt, the claim of the congress, and the environment of the room had all changed by the end of the session. The winner was concerned about the victim, and the ring of poverty was the only prize worth having. A reasonable and sufficient spending plan, an angry juror, and a guilty suspect: all of them were part of the same trying year. We would confess the limit and confine the scheme. The main product of the first attempt was a set of numbers that only a strong theory could defend; the oil, the light, and the drainage were the same standard then and now, and the theory would fail if the free water were a lie or if the guilt were never admitted.

第二次尝试很难受，也不是快乐的工作。“我们享受理论，却在排水问题上流血，”司库说，“这就是这份工作的全部。”到会议结束时，疑罪从无的余地、国会的诉求，以及屋里的环境，都已经变了。胜出者关心的是受害者，而贫困的那一圈是唯一值得拥有的奖品。一份合理且充足的支出计划、一位愤怒的陪审员，以及一个有罪的嫌疑人：它们都是同一个难受年份的一部分。我们会坦白这个限度，并把这个方案限制住。第一次尝试的主要产物是一堆只有一个强理论才能辩护的数字；油、光与排水，当时和现在都是同一个标准，而如果免费的水是一个谎言，或者那份罪责从未被承认，这个理论就会失败。

### Exercises

- CH122-Q1 [context] What was the second attempt designed to do?
  - options: win a majority and keep the profit in the city / raise the tariff twice / close the customs office / elect a new juror
- CH122-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “剥夺；使丧失”?
  - options: deprive / confine / victimize / confess
- CH122-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: confine an investigation / confine a charity / confine a prize / confine a pollster
- CH122-Q4 [cloze] The investigator said a suspect is not a ______.
  - options: conviction / confession / bargain / settlement
- CH122-Q5 [recall] According to Lin, what had been counted but never compared?
  - options: the light, the oil, and the main pipe / the votes and the jurors / the customs and the charity / the profit and the prize

### Answers

- CH122-Q1: win a majority and keep the profit in the city - The second scheme aimed at both votes and local benefit.
- CH122-Q2: deprive - The poorest ward was deprived of a voice.
- CH122-Q3: confine an investigation - Confine combines with an investigation, a cost, or a scope.
- CH122-Q4: conviction - Suspicion is not proof.
- CH122-Q5: the light, the oil, and the main pipe - The figures were collected but never compared.

## CH123 - Who Pays and Who Decides: Outside Pressure / 谁承担，谁决定：外部压力

- theme: 社会问题、经济与公共生活

**1.** The outside pressure arrived on a wet afternoon as an advisory letter from the attorney of a private owner, and the effect on the room was immediate. “You may not build a plant on land that is under a legal consideration,” the lawyer wrote. “A vote is not a lawful title.” The judge had already signed the previous order, and the news reached city hall through a message from a concerned voter.

外部压力在一个潮湿的下午到来，形式是一封来自一位私人业主代理律师的顾问函，而它对房间的影响立竿见影。“你不能在一块处于法律考量之下的土地上建厂，”律师写道，“一次投票不是合法的产权凭证。”法官已经签署了此前那道命令，而消息通过一位忧心选民的信息传到了市厅。

**2.** The crime was not the fine itself but the implication behind it. “The offering of a deposit and a clean display of good reputation will not neutralize a charge of bribery,” the attorney said. “A volume of evidence is not the same as a lawful deed, and you cannot legalize an offence with an advertisement.”

罪行不在于罚款本身，而在于它背后的牵连。“寄存一笔定金和展示一份干净的好名声，并不能使一项行贿指控中和，”代理律师说，“证据的容量不等于一件合法行为，你也无法用一则广告把一桩过错合法化。”

**3.** “We are not here to grab the land,” Lin said. “We require the room to build what the neighborhood needs and nothing else.” The heir of the estate, a supporter of the resort plan, and an advisory board of two retired engineers all agreed that the attraction would bring a gross profit and a stable base of customers.

“我们不是来抢地的，”林伟说，“我们需要这块地方来建邻里真正需要的东西，仅此而已。”庄园的继承人、度假方案的一位支持者，以及一个由两位退休工程师组成的顾问委员会，都同意这个景点会带来可观的毛利和稳定的客源。

**4.** The press conference that followed was a strange narration. “They condemn us for racism and surrender to a bull market,” a resident said. “The rebellion of a small neighborhood is not a pretence of rebellion; it is a refusal to confuse a shorts season with a water shortage.”

随后的新闻发布会是一场奇特的叙述。“他们谴责我们种族主义，却向牛市投降，”一位居民说。“一个小社区的造反不是假装的造反；它是拒绝把短裤的季节和缺水的季节混为一谈。”

**5.** The gay shopkeeper, the religious author, and the happy volunteer all had a point that the room was missing: the plant would embarrass the town and enable nothing. “The real victim of an illegal pump is a household that cannot appeal,” the author wrote.

那位快活的店主、虔诚的作家和快乐的志愿者，都提出了房间里没人想到的一点：这座厂会让镇子难堪，却什么也促成不了。“非法水泵真正的受害者，是那个无从申诉的家庭，”作家写道。

**6.** The advisory opinion changed the terms. The previous proposal was withdrawn, the deposit was returned, and the group was given thirty days to produce a lawful alternative. “A point is enough to start with,” the attorney said. The reality now was that the plant could not reside on that ground, and nobody could fix it with a win in the press; we would not enjoy the next month, but the law was the law.

顾问意见改变了条件。此前的提案被撤回，定金被退还，小组获得了三十天时间拿出一份合法的替代方案。“有一个点就够开始了，”代理律师说。眼下的现实是，那座厂不能建在那块地上，而没有人能靠一场新闻上的胜利把它修好；我们不会喜欢下一个月，但法律就是法律。

### Exercises

- CH123-Q1 [context] What did the attorney say a vote is not?
  - options: a lawful title / a deposit / an advertisement / a narration
- CH123-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “贿赂行为”?
  - options: bribery / offence / rebellion / surrender
- CH123-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: build a plant / build a volume / build a pretence / build a bull
- CH123-Q4 [cloze] The attorney said you cannot legalize an offence with an ______.
  - options: advertisement / attorney / inheritance / afternoon
- CH123-Q5 [recall] How long did the group have to produce a lawful alternative?
  - options: thirty days / one afternoon / two years / one week

### Answers

- CH123-Q1: a lawful title - A vote does not confer ownership.
- CH123-Q2: bribery - The deposit raised a charge of bribery.
- CH123-Q3: build a plant - Build combines with a plant, a case, or trust.
- CH123-Q4: advertisement - Publicity cannot make an offence lawful.
- CH123-Q5: thirty days - The advisory opinion set a thirty-day deadline.

## CH124 - Who Pays and Who Decides: The Decision / 谁承担，谁决定：决定

- theme: 社会问题、经济与公共生活

**1.** The decision was taken in a flat above a retail unit, and it answered a simple question: how do we let a building fall into use without selling the land? “The role of the council is to occupy the ground and to halt the sale,” the advocate said. “A cheque and a poster will not rescue a public service.”

决定是在一家零售店楼上的一间公寓里做出的，它回答了一个简单的问题：我们如何让一栋建筑投入使用而不卖掉土地？“议会的职责是占用这块地并中止出售，”拥护者说，“一张支票和一张海报救不了一项公共服务。”

**2.** The understanding in the room was that the danger was ordinary and the emergency was not. “We have a warning and we have a fire escape; what we do not have is a necessity,” the narrator of the meeting said. “A tale about tradition is not a plan, and a guess is not a production figure.”

屋里的共识是：危险是平常的，而紧急状态不是。“我们有警告，也有消防通道；我们没有的是必要性，”会议讲述者说。“一个关于传统的故事不是计划，一个猜测也不是产量数字。”

**3.** The sharp part of the discussion was the refusal of the retailer. “We can forgive a delay and we cannot forgive a betrayal,” Maya wrote. “The innocence of a small shop does not endanger the water supply, and the urgency of the bank does not liberate the council.”

讨论中最尖锐的部分是零售商的拒绝。“我们可以原谅一次延误，却无法原谅一次背叛，”曼雅写道，“一家小店铺的无辜不会危及供水，而银行的紧迫也不会解放议会。”

**4.** The advocate for the tenants proposed a concerted scheme: a strip of shops on a lease, a flat for the caretaker, and an equivalence between the old rate and the new one. “A reformist measure is worth more than a supposition about the future,” she said.

租户的拥护者提出了一项商定的方案：一条带租约的商铺、一间给管理员的公寓，以及旧费率与新费率之间的等价。“一项改良主义的措施比一个关于未来的推测更有价值，”她说。

**5.** The vote was seven to five, and the effect was immediate. “We do not immunize a city against scarcity by depressing its rates,” the treasurer said. “We build a habit of paying.” The council would occupy the building within the year, and the sale would halt at the end of the term.

表决结果是七比五，影响立竿见影。“我们无法靠压低费率让一座城市对匮乏免疫，”司库说，“我们建立的是一种付钱的习惯。”议会将在年内占用那栋楼，而出售将在学期末中止。

**6.** The real issue had been between two ways of holding power: to own or to steward. The activity of the council, its drive to build, and the support of the tenants had produced a decision that neither side could call a victory. Someone had to weave the two plans together, and only then would the order of the day become an act instead of a lie. The advocacy of the tenants was a promise not to betray them, and the building would not sell itself, and the only thing left was the vote.

真正的问题一直介于两种掌权方式之间：拥有，还是照管。议会的那场活动、它建设的动力，以及租户的支持，共同促成了一项双方都无法称之为胜利的决定。总得有人把两套方案编织在一起，只有这样，当日的议程才会变成一项行动，而不是一个谎言。租户的拥护是一份不背叛他们的承诺，而那栋楼不会自己卖掉自己，剩下的只有表决。

### Exercises

- CH124-Q1 [context] What did the advocate say the role of the council was?
  - options: to occupy the ground and halt the sale / to sell the land / to raise the rate / to immunize the city
- CH124-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “推测；推想”?
  - options: supposition / equivalence / necessity / advocacy
- CH124-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: halt a sale / halt a poster / halt a cheque / halt a narrator
- CH124-Q4 [cloze] Maya wrote that the innocence of a small shop does not ______ the water supply.
  - options: endanger / liberate / depress / forgive
- CH124-Q5 [recall] What was the vote?
  - options: seven to five / five to four / unanimous / ten to two

### Answers

- CH124-Q1: to occupy the ground and halt the sale - She wanted stewardship rather than sale.
- CH124-Q2: supposition - A reformist measure beat a supposition.
- CH124-Q3: halt a sale - Halt combines with a sale, a scheme, or a decline.
- CH124-Q4: endanger - A small shop does not threaten the water supply.
- CH124-Q5: seven to five - The decision was narrow but clear.

## CH125 - Who Pays and Who Decides: Consequences / 谁承担，谁决定：后果

- theme: 社会问题、经济与公共生活

**1.** The consequences of the decision met the council at a municipal meeting in the town hall, where every section of the public had a view. “This is a political question, not a type of administrative check,” the legislator said. “A certain number of voters will pay, and a common award of praise will not cover it.”

决定的后果在市政厅的一次市政会议上与议会相遇，那里公众的每一个部分都有自己的看法。“这是一个政治问题，不是某种行政检查，”立法委员说，“一定数量的选民将要付钱，而一份普遍的赞扬奖章不能覆盖它。”

**2.** The normal variety of complaints arrived within the week: a protest from the park committee, an enquiry from the retail federation, and a demonstration outside the mall. “We must put the plot apart from the argument,” the mayor said. “A brief in a pocket is not a policy.”

正常种类的投诉在一周内到来：公园委员会的抗议、零售业联合会的询问，以及商场外的一场示威游行。“我们必须把这块地和这场争论分开，”市长说，“口袋里的摘要不是政策。”

**3.** The split in the council was along an unexpected line: the communist member and the fashionable liberal voted together. “Capitalism and communism share one thing,” she said. “Both of them want a compact answer to a complicated question.”

议会里的分裂沿着一条意外的界线：共产主义成员和时髦的自由派投了同一票。“资本主义和共产主义共享一点，”她说，“两者都想要一个对复杂问题的紧凑答案。”

**4.** The wedding of two staff members turned into a quiet celebration of the new rule, and a recitation of the old tariff was read aloud as a joke. “A spin of the same wheel will not grant us a conclusion,” the treasurer said. “We must enforce what we execute and execute what we enforce.”

两位职员的婚礼变成了一场对新规则的安静庆祝，而旧费率的一段背诵被当作笑话朗读出来。“同一个轮子再转一次不会给我们一个结论，”司库说，“我们必须执行我们所施行的，施行我们所执行的。”

**5.** The claw of the prohibition was the deposit. “The wilderness outside the town is not ours to invade,” a resident said, and the similarity between his words and the rebel's brief was remarked on by everyone. “A rebellious tone is not a rebellious deed.”

禁令的那只爪子是定金。“镇外的荒野不是我们该侵入的，”一位居民说，而他的措辞与那位反叛者的摘要之间的相似性被所有人注意到了。“反叛的语调不是反叛的行为。”

**6.** The social view of the government, the money in the market, and the right of the community to be included: the three of them were now on the same page, and the state had, for the first time, a policy it could start to defend. The government, the community, and the market were included in the same social view, and the law was on the right side. Let us put the money where the policy can start, and let the state show how it works; the public interest was not a normal interest, and the certain thing was that the town would check it. The municipal section of the mall was apart from the rest, and we had to do one specific thing about it.

政府的社会视角、市场里的金钱，以及社区被纳入的权利：这三者如今在同一页上，而国家第一次有了一项它能够开始为之辩护的政策。政府、社区和市场都被纳入了同一种社会视角，而法律站在正确的一边。让我们把钱放在政策能够起步的地方，让国家展示它是怎么运作的；公共利益不是一种普通的利益，而确定无疑的是，镇上会核查它。商场里属于市政的那一段与其余部分隔开，而我们必须为此做一件具体的事。

### Exercises

- CH125-Q1 [context] What did the legislator say the question was?
  - options: political, not administrative / administrative, not political / legal, not social / fashionable, not common
- CH125-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “禁令；禁止”?
  - options: prohibition / recitation / celebrations / federation
- CH125-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: enforce a rule / enforce a wedding / enforce a claw / enforce a mall
- CH125-Q4 [cloze] She said capitalism and communism both want a compact answer to a complicated ______.
  - options: question / pocket / park / celebration
- CH125-Q5 [recall] What did a resident say the wilderness outside town was not?
  - options: ours to invade / ours to protect / ours to sell / ours to park

### Answers

- CH125-Q1: political, not administrative - The dispute was political, not procedural.
- CH125-Q2: prohibition - The prohibition's teeth were financial.
- CH125-Q3: enforce a rule - Enforce combines with a rule, a ban, or a payment.
- CH125-Q4: question - Both ideologies wanted a compact answer.
- CH125-Q5: ours to invade - He rejected treating the wilderness as territory.

## CH126 - Who Pays and Who Decides: The Bridge Forward / 谁承担，谁决定：通向下一程

- theme: 社会问题、经济与公共生活

**1.** The ninth arc closed with a public forum in the center of town, where the manner of the meeting was as important as its content. “A logical argument will appear convincing and an authentic one will appear true,” the mayor said. “We must avoid the first and produce the second.” A sorry silence followed.

第九段弧线以镇中心的一场公开论坛收尾，会上会议的方式与内容同样重要。“合乎逻辑的论证会显得有说服力，而真实的那种会显得真实，”市长说，“我们必须避免前者，产出后者。”随之而来的是一阵遗憾的沉默。

**2.** The interested parties were the postman, a widower, and a resourceful bachelor who had come to advertise a loan scheme. “There is a misunderstanding about what visibility means,” the postman said. “A correct figure that nobody can see is a joke with a tear in it.”

利益相关方是那位邮递员、一位鳏夫，以及一位机智的单身汉，他是来宣传一套贷款方案的。“对可见性有个误解，”邮递员说，“一个没人看得见的正确数字，是一个带泪的笑话。”

**3.** The summit of the evening was the explanatory brief, which was adequate, applicable, and mercifully short. “We can detect the foreseeable error only if we publish it,” the treasurer said. “An exemplary account is one that a disputable line can survive.”

当晚的高峰是那份说明性摘要，它充分、可适用，而且幸运地简短。“只有把它公布出来，我们才能发现那个可预见的错误，”司库说，“一份示范性的账目，是那种一条有争议的行目也能挺过去的账目。”

**4.** The argumentative member provoked the only real dispute of the night. “A purposeful policy is not the same as a manhood contest,” she said. “An erroneous forecast is harmless if the correction arrives in time. An apologetic one is not.”

那位好辩的成员挑起了当晚唯一真正的争论。“一项有目的的政策不等于一场男子气概的比赛，”她说，“一个错误的预报如果纠正及时就是无害的。一个忙着道歉的预报不是。”

**5.** The visibility of the accounts, the mercy of a payment plan, and the truthful narration of the failure were the three things the meeting produced. “A satisfactory result would be a boring one,” Maya wrote. “That is what we should aim for.”

账目的可见性、还款方案的仁慈，以及对失败如实的叙述，是这次会议产出的三样东西。“一个令人满意的结果会是无聊的，”曼雅写道，“那才是我们该瞄准的。”

**6.** The existence of the scheme no longer depended on a single vote. A certain section of the town, a common type of household, and the strong support of the center had built something that could survive a change of council. The next arc would begin with a loan, a summit, and a question about who owns the record. The town, the section, the type, and the term: four words that a strong effect could build into a common center. We must add more to the account and cut less from the record. The political meeting would begin again in the winter, and the certain things would then be the full list and the strong evidence. The build of the scheme mattered more than its size. Aside from the publicity, the fascinating and arguable part was that an observant reader could check every line. They had published who pays; the harder page, who decides, was still blank.

这套方案的存在不再取决于一次表决。镇上某个特定的部分、某一类常见的家庭，以及中心地带强有力的支持，已经建起了某种能挺过议会更替的东西。下一段弧线将以一笔贷款、一场峰会，以及一个关于谁拥有记录的问题开始。那个镇、那个区段、那种类型，以及那个期限：四个词，一种强效果可以把它们建成一个共同的中心。我们必须给账目增加更多，而从记录里削减更少。政治会议会在冬天重新开始，而到那时确定的东西会是完整的清单和强有力的证据。这套方案的构建比它的规模更要紧。除了那点宣传之外，迷人的、可争辩的一点是：一位细心的读者能核对每一行。他们已经公布了谁付钱；而更难的那一页——谁来决定——仍然是空白的。

### Exercises

- CH126-Q1 [context] What did the postman say visibility is misunderstood as?
  - options: a correct figure nobody can see / a loan scheme / a forum / a correction
- CH126-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “足够的；适当的”?
  - options: adequate / erroneous / arguable / purposeful
- CH126-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: detect an error / detect a tear / detect a widower / detect a postman
- CH126-Q4 [cloze] The treasurer said an exemplary account is one that a disputable line can ______.
  - options: survive / provoke / advertise / avoid
- CH126-Q5 [recall] What did Maya say a satisfactory result would be?
  - options: a boring one / a fascinating one / a tearful one / an apologetic one

### Answers

- CH126-Q1: a correct figure nobody can see - Unseen accuracy is not accountability.
- CH126-Q2: adequate - The brief was adequate and brief.
- CH126-Q3: detect an error - Detect combines with an error, a fault, or a bias.
- CH126-Q4: survive - A real account survives a contested line.
- CH126-Q5: a boring one - She preferred durability to drama.

## CH127 - A Choice That Has a Cost: A Signal / 有代价的选择：一个信号

- theme: 心理、决策、责任与人生选择

**1.** They had published every exemption and still lost the room; now the only question left was what each of them would choose. The tenth arc began in a museum of urban history, where the choice of a career seemed to require an assessment that nobody had written. “A birth in a village and a degree in the city do not produce the same consciousness,” the director said. “You have a steady record and a supreme reputation, and now you must decide what it is for.”

他们已经公布了每一份豁免，却仍然失去了那间屋子；如今剩下的唯一问题是，他们各自会选择什么。第十段弧线开始于一座城市历史博物馆，在那里，职业的选择似乎需要一份没人写过的评估。“出生在村庄和在城市获得学位，并不会产生同样的意识，”主管说，“你有一份稳定的履历和一个至高的名声，现在你必须决定它是为了什么。”

**2.** The dilemma was polite and fierce at once. “I appreciate the offer,” Lin said, “but an elegant title is not the same as integrity.” A prospective employer had sent a memo and a memorandum about the modernization of the plant; a second letter mentioned its modernisation in the same breath, and a third asked about neutrality in a dispute the plant had already lost.

这个困境既有礼貌又凶猛。“我感激这份邀约，”林伟说，“但一个优雅的头衔不等于正直。”一位潜在的雇主寄来一份备忘录和一份关于工厂现代化的备忘文件；第二封信在同一口气里提到了它的另一种拼法；第三封信问的是在一场工厂已经输掉的争议中的中立。

**3.** The wildlife survey was the reminder of what the group actually owned: a decent dataset, a doubtful forecast, and a dying weather station. “A reversal of the verdict would require a decisive report,” Maya wrote. “We can imitate a respectable process and we cannot fake the perseverance behind it.”

野生动物调查提醒了他们真正拥有什么：一份尚可的数据集、一份可疑的预报，以及一座垂死的气象站。“要翻转这个裁决，需要一份果断的报告，”曼雅写道，“我们可以模仿一个体面的流程，却无法伪造它背后的毅力。”

**4.** The puzzle of the second week was the acidity of the river. “A permissive rule and a corrupt reading have the same effect on a sample,” the analyst said. “The difference is who signs it.” Her willpower had carried the survey through a coarse season, and the group had not noticed.

第二周的难题是河流的酸性。“一条纵容的规则和一次腐败的读法，对样本产生同样的影响，”分析师说，“区别在于谁签的字。”她的意志力让这项调查撑过了一个粗糙的季节，而小组没有注意到。

**5.** The reproductive data and the urbanisation figures arrived on the same morning. “We urbanise a district and we call it growth,” the planner said. “To urbanize is not the same as to improve.” The hesitation in the room was honest, and it was the first useful signal of the whole arc.

生殖数据与城市化数字在同一个早上到来。“我们把一个区都市化，然后称之为增长，”规划者说，“使都市化不等于改善。”屋里那份犹豫是诚实的，而它是整段弧线第一个有用的信号。

**6.** The insanity of the summer had been the attempt to do both jobs, and the healthy response was to let one of them go. “A fire in the building would not require less of us,” the director said. “It would require the same people, now, with a true answer.” It was a pleasant fortune, and it did not appear by accident. We had to guess how the production of a survey could fill the role of a job, and we could avoid the fall only by refusing to sell the museum's data. A sincere sorry would not do; a quiet defiance would.

这个夏天的荒唐之处在于想同时做两份工作，而健康的回应是放掉其中一份。“楼里着火不会要求我们更少，”主管说，“它会要求同样这些人，在此时此刻，给出一个真实的答案。”这是一份令人愉快的运气，而它不是偶然出现的。我们必须猜测，一份调查的生产如何才能填满一份工作的角色，而只有拒绝出售博物馆的数据，我们才能避开那次跌落。一句真诚的道歉没有用；一次安静的蔑视才有用。

### Exercises

- CH127-Q1 [context] Where did the tenth arc begin?
  - options: in a museum of urban history / in a weather station / at the plant / in a village school
- CH127-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “困境；两难”?
  - options: dilemma / reversal / hesitation / defiance
- CH127-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: fake perseverance / fake wildlife / fake acidity / fake neutrality
- CH127-Q4 [cloze] The analyst said a permissive rule and a corrupt reading have the same effect on a ______.
  - options: sample / museum / memo / career
- CH127-Q5 [recall] What did Lin say an elegant title is not the same as?
  - options: integrity / a reputation / a memo / a degree

### Answers

- CH127-Q1: in a museum of urban history - The final arc opened in a museum.
- CH127-Q2: dilemma - The career choice was a real dilemma.
- CH127-Q3: fake perseverance - Process can be imitated; perseverance cannot be faked.
- CH127-Q4: sample - Permissiveness and corruption distort a reading equally.
- CH127-Q5: integrity - He separated status from honesty.

## CH128 - A Choice That Has a Cost: The First Promise / 有代价的选择：第一次承诺

- theme: 心理、决策、责任与人生选择

**1.** The first promise was made at the graduation ceremony, in the cold of a western winter: each graduate would publish one chapter of the group's history. “The goal is not the size of the report,” the director said at the meeting, “it is the duty to hand over the primary record.”

第一个承诺是在毕业典礼上做出的，在一个西方冬天的寒冷里：每位毕业生将公开这个小组历史中的一章。“目标不是报告的规模，”主管在会上说，“而是移交第一手记录的责任。”

**2.** The average reader of the index would never see the distribution of the evidence, and that was the point. “A dead archive is a kind of lie,” Maya said. “A present-tense account is a kind of evidence.” The spirit of the assignment was to write down the origin of every number, including the ones with a fault.

索引的普通读者永远不会看到证据的分布，而重点正在这里。“一座死的档案是一种谎言，”曼雅说，“一份现在时态的记述是一种证据。”这项任务的精神是写下每一个数字的来源，包括那些有毛病的。

**3.** The moment of conversion came with a photograph. “This image is identical to the one in the party's leaflet,” Lin said. “We have tied our history to a political leaflet, and we did not notice.” The stake was not money but the taste of the whole project.

转变的时刻随着一张照片到来。“这张影像与那份党传单上的一模一样，”林伟说，“我们把我们的历史系在了一份政治传单上，而我们没有注意到。”赌注不是钱，而是整个项目的情调。

**4.** The rough draft of the fifth chapter had a tie to an antique map and a reserve of unprocessed field notes. “A trick of presentation will not save a weak index,” the director said. “A sacrifice is required, and it is the sacrifice of the pretty sentence.”

第五章的粗略草稿与一张古董地图和一批未整理的野外笔记有关。“呈现上的一个诀窍救不了一个虚弱的索引，”主管说，“需要一种牺牲，而它就是牺牲那句漂亮的句子。”

**5.** The confusion in the room was about ownership. “Only ten of us wrote it, and even the town has a claim,” the editor said. “We cannot cling to a private sphere and call it public scholarship.” The reward for publishing was, in the end, the release itself.

屋里的混乱是关于归属的。“只有我们十个人写了它，而连镇上也有份，”编辑说，“我们不能紧抓着一个私人领域，然后称之为公共学术。”公开的报酬，说到底，就是那次公开本身。

**6.** A faint frost on the window, a stroke of the pen, and the preview of the last page: the promise was convert into a series of small assignments, and the convertible one was the only one the group could honestly sign. The ten of us met then, and the section on the party was the hardest. A type of town, a certain world, a town that would not die: the evidence was political even when we said that it was not. We had to show the break in the record and let the reader see it, and only the town could decide what to publish. The present page would seem to choke on its own inclination.

窗上一层淡淡的霜、笔的一划，以及最后一页的预览：这个承诺被转换成一连串小任务，而可转换的那一个，是小组唯一能诚实地签下的一个。我们十个人那时聚在一起，而关于那个党的那一节最难写。一种城镇、一个特定的世界、一个不会死去的城镇：即使我们说它不是政治的，这些证据也是政治的。我们必须展示记录中的断裂，并让读者看见它，而只有那个镇能决定公布什么。眼前这一页似乎会被自己的倾向噎住。

### Exercises

- CH128-Q1 [context] What would each graduate publish?
  - options: one chapter of the group's history / one photograph / one index / one leaflet
- CH128-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “牺牲；祭品”?
  - options: sacrifice / conversion / inclination / reserve
- CH128-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: hand over a record / hand over a frost / hand over a sphere / hand over a stroke
- CH128-Q4 [cloze] Maya said a dead archive is a kind of ______.
  - options: lie / index / preview / reward
- CH128-Q5 [recall] What did the director say was required?
  - options: the sacrifice of the pretty sentence / a larger report / a new photograph / a private archive

### Answers

- CH128-Q1: one chapter of the group's history - Each graduate published one chapter.
- CH128-Q2: sacrifice - The sacrifice was stylistic, not financial.
- CH128-Q3: hand over a record - Hand over combines with a record, a key, or a duty.
- CH128-Q4: lie - A dead archive still makes a false claim.
- CH128-Q5: the sacrifice of the pretty sentence - Clarity required giving up fine writing.

## CH129 - A Choice That Has a Cost: A Missing Voice / 有代价的选择：缺席的声音

- theme: 心理、决策、责任与人生选择

**1.** The missing voice belonged to a clown at the children's ceremony during the settlement week. “You never asked the age of the people who will live under this roof,” he said. “You have a purpose and a vote, and you have not asked whether the house is impossible to heat.”

那个缺席的声音属于安置周期间儿童典礼上的一个小丑。“你们从来没有问过将要住在这个屋顶下的人的年龄，”他说，“你们有一个目的和一次表决，而你们没有问过这栋房子是不是无法取暖。”

**2.** The apparent answer was attractive: select a smaller unit, choose a cautious price, and forget the rest. “Sorrow is not a clinical condition,” he said, “and a guidance note is not counsel.” A stir in the hall made the identification of the real problem evident: there was no equality in the plan.

表面的答案是吸引人的：选一个更小的单元、挑一个谨慎的价格，其余的忘掉。“悲伤不是一种临床状态，”他说，“一份指导说明不是法律顾问。”大厅里的一阵骚动让真正问题的身份变得明显：这份方案里没有平等。

**3.** The equality he meant was measured in decades. “A materialist reads a house as an asset,” he said. “A materialist should also read it as a lifetime.” His idealism, he admitted, was cheerful and his realism was not; the seeming contradiction was the whole of his argument.

他所说的平等是以十年为单位计量的。“唯物主义者把房子读成一项资产，”他说，“唯物主义者也应该把它读成一辈子。”他承认，他的理想主义是欢快的，而他的现实主义不是；这个表面上的矛盾就是他论证的全部。

**4.** The solidity of the building was never in doubt, and the eternity of a mortgage was. “You can visualize a family in it, and you cannot visualize a family paying for it,” he said. “A clear-cut plan would say who repays and when.”

这栋楼的坚固性从未被怀疑，而被怀疑的是一笔按揭的永恒。“你可以在里面想象一个家庭，却无法想象一个家庭在付它的钱，”他说，“一份明确的方案会说明谁偿还、何时偿还。”

**5.** The blame for the confusion lay with the guidance, not with the family. “A cautious buyer and a cheating seller can look alike on paper,” Maya wrote. “The difference is the second page.” The voice he raised was mad by their standards and just by his own.

混乱该归咎于那份指导，而不是那个家庭。“一个谨慎的买家和一个作弊的卖家在纸上看起来可以很像，”曼雅写道，“区别在第二页。”他提高的嗓音按他们的标准是疯狂的，按他自己的标准是公正的。

**6.** The settlement was revised by one clause: every unit would be sold with a heating assessment attached. It was almost nothing, and it was nearly everything, and the group agreed to appear at the next meeting with the figure. They agreed almost at once, and I nearly said nothing. We need a similar kind of free claim, and a true history is not a chapter that dies. A kind moment, an image, and a win were not enough while nearly half the residents were absent. The break in the plan appeared as a missing page, and almost everyone who could vote did; the need was similar to the earlier survey. We must decide whether the dead govern us; a house is alive in a way that materialism cannot visualise, and the death of the old plan did not settle the question. The settlement would visualise the cost instead.

安置方案因一条条款而被修改：每个单元出售时都必须附一份取暖评估。这几乎什么都不是，也几乎就是一切，而小组同意带着这个数字出现在下次会议上。他们几乎立刻就同意了，而我几乎什么也没说。我们需要一种类似的、自由的诉求，而一部真实的历史不是一章会死去的东西。在将近一半居民缺席的时候，一个善意的时刻、一张图像和一场胜利都不够。方案里的断裂以一张缺页的形式出现，而几乎所有能投票的人都投了；这种需要与早先那次调查相似。我们必须决定死者是否在统治我们；一栋房子是活的，其方式唯物主义无法想象，而旧计划的死亡并没有解决这个问题。这份安置方案将转而把代价想象出来。

### Exercises

- CH129-Q1 [context] What had the group never asked?
  - options: the age of the people who would live under the roof / the price of the land / the size of the ceremony / the name of the clown
- CH129-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “平等；相等”?
  - options: equality / solidity / eternity / realism
- CH129-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: repay a mortgage / repay a clown / repay a ceremony / repay a stir
- CH129-Q4 [cloze] He said a materialist reads a house as an asset and should also read it as a ______.
  - options: lifetime / ceremony / guidance / clown
- CH129-Q5 [recall] What clause was added to the settlement?
  - options: every unit sold with a heating assessment / a smaller unit size / a longer mortgage / a children's ceremony

### Answers

- CH129-Q1: the age of the people who would live under the roof - The plan ignored who would actually live there.
- CH129-Q2: equality - Equality was measured in decades of payment.
- CH129-Q3: repay a mortgage - Repay combines with a mortgage, a loan, or a kindness.
- CH129-Q4: lifetime - A house is both an asset and a lifetime.
- CH129-Q5: every unit sold with a heating assessment - One clause attached a physical fact to the sale.

## CH130 - A Choice That Has a Cost: Evidence on the Table / 有代价的选择：摆上桌面的证据

- theme: 心理、决策、责任与人生选择

**1.** The evidence meeting was held tonight because the news could not wait, and the argument on the table was about a defective pearl. “I believe the report and I cannot approve it,” the auditor said. “Everything upon it rests on one page, and that page was written prior to the test.”

证据会议在今晚召开，因为消息等不了，而桌上的争论关乎一颗有缺陷的珍珠。“我相信这份报告，但我无法批准它，”审计员说，“它之上的一切都依赖一页纸，而那一页是在测试之前写的。”

**2.** The dispute concerning the twentieth chapter was simple: accept the figure or deny it. “You insist that we respond tonight,” Lin said. “I would rather remind you that a compelling thesis can still be wrong.” A generous reader would argue the same point, and a cop at the door would not.

关于第二十章的争论很简单：接受这个数字，或者否认它。“你坚持要我们今晚回应，”林伟说，“我更愿意提醒你，一篇有说服力的论文仍然可能是错的。”一位慷慨的读者会提出同样的观点，而门口那位警察不会。

**3.** The group had promised not to pretend. “We will declare the error and we will not seize the moment to look good,” Maya wrote. “Onto every page we will put the name of the person who checked it, and okay means checked.”

小组承诺过不假装。“我们会声明这个错误，也不会抓住这个时机让自己好看，”曼雅写道，“在每一页上，我们都会写上核对者的名字，而‘可以’的意思就是已核对。”

**4.** The prior year had been wound backwards: a nightly habit of re-doing each report, an entrepreneurial push that frightened the volunteers, and a forgetful editor who had misplaced one page. “Weep if you like,” the auditor said, “but stabilize the process first.”

前一年是被倒着缠回去的：每晚重做每份报告的习气、一次吓得志愿者发抖的创业式推进，以及一位把某一页放错了地方的健忘编辑。“你想哭就哭吧，”审计员说，“但请先把流程稳住。”

**5.** The surrounding argument was about the underwear factory and the couple who owned it. “It is too easy to rob a small firm of its name,” Lin said. “A fine is not the same as a fair verdict, and an exception is not a rule.”

外围的争论关乎那家内衣厂和拥有它的那对夫妇。“太容易就剥夺一家小公司的名声了，”林伟说，“一笔罚款不等于一个公正的裁决，而一个例外不是一条规则。”

**6.** The choice of the twentieth chapter was to publish everything except the private correspondence, and to try the whole record against the purpose it claimed. “A generous account is not a flattering one,” the auditor said, and the meeting closed without an argument. Age is different from death, and during the year we decided to keep every point about the effect. The fine was never news, the purpose was never just, and the couple who built it seemed to be under a different sky. The choice was to decide during the same meeting, and to choose the harder option; it was never only about the fine, and the news, the point, and the effect were the same as before. Although we cannot swear to every page, we welcome the check, and we will not entertain a version that hides it.

第二十章的选择是：除了私人通信以外全部公开，并尝试用整份记录去检验它所声称的目的。“一份慷慨的记述不是一份谄媚的记述，”审计员说，而会议在没有争执的情况下结束。年龄不同于死亡，而在这一年里我们决定保留关于那个影响的每一个要点。那笔罚款从来不是新闻，那个目的从来不是公正的，而建造它的那对夫妇似乎生活在另一片天空下。这个选择是在同一次会议上作出决定，并选择更难的那个选项；它从来不只关乎那笔罚款，而新闻、那个要点以及那个影响，都和从前一样。虽然我们无法为每一页发誓，但我们欢迎这次核对，也不会去迎合一个把它藏起来的版本。

### Exercises

- CH130-Q1 [context] Why was the meeting held tonight?
  - options: because the news could not wait / because the pearl arrived / because the cop insisted / because the factory closed
- CH130-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “有缺陷的；欠缺的”?
  - options: defective / compelling / forgetful / entrepreneurial
- CH130-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: seize a moment / seize a pearl / seize underwear / seize a thesis
- CH130-Q4 [cloze] Maya wrote that on every page they would put the name of the person who ______ it.
  - options: checked / robbed / frightened / entertained
- CH130-Q5 [recall] What did the auditor say a generous account is not?
  - options: a flattering one / a short one / a nightly one / a private one

### Answers

- CH130-Q1: because the news could not wait - The timing was forced by external news.
- CH130-Q2: defective - The pearl was defective and so was the record.
- CH130-Q3: seize a moment - Seize combines with a moment, an opportunity, or evidence.
- CH130-Q4: checked - Each page would carry its checker's name.
- CH130-Q5: a flattering one - Generosity in reporting is not flattery.

## CH131 - A Choice That Has a Cost: The Trial Run / 有代价的选择：第一次试行

- theme: 心理、决策、责任与人生选择

**1.** The trial run began with a limited prospect and a heated argument about the heat itself: could a beautiful old building be made cool without being ruined? “I suggest we test two rooms and let the results occur,” Lin said. “We intend to do a real evaluation, not a prediction.” The feeling in the room was that the issue would embarrass them both.

试行以一个有限的前景和一场关于热本身的激烈争论开始：一座美丽的老建筑能否在不被毁掉的前提下变凉？“我建议我们测两个房间，让结果自己发生，”林伟说，“我们打算做一次真正的评估，而不是一个预测。”屋里的感觉是：这个问题会让他们两人都难堪。

**2.** The passion of the meeting was genuine and the accuracy of the numbers was not. “We have too much expectation and too little sensitivity,” the engineer said. “A wealthy client will opt for the outward look; a poor tenant will perceive the inward cost.” The five of them had, probably, two months and one chance.

会议的热情是真诚的，而数字的准确性不是。“我们有太多期待，太少敏感度，”工程师说，“富有的客户会选外表的观感；贫穷的租客会感知内在的代价。”他们五个人大概有两个月时间和一次机会。

**3.** The possibility of a postponement arrived with the first cold night. “Yesterday the plan was obvious, and tomorrow it will be scandalous,” the director said. “We underestimated the vigor of the winter, and we let the hope of a quick fix madden us.”

延期的可能性随着第一个寒夜到来。“昨天这个方案还是显而易见的，明天它就会是丑闻，”主管说，“我们低估了冬天的活力，也让“快速修复”的希望把我们逼疯了。”

**4.** The disgust that followed was not intentional; it came from a bad measurement. “You can explode with passion and still be wrong,” Maya wrote. “Happiness is not a design criterion, and goodness is not a discount.” The inward and the outward temperature had been recorded twenty times, and the history of the building had been read only once.

随之而来的厌恶不是故意的；它来自一次糟糕的测量。“你可以满怀热情地爆发，仍然出错，”曼雅写道，“快乐不是一项设计标准，善良也不是一个折扣。”室内和室外的温度被记录了二十次，而这栋建筑的历史只被读过一次。

**5.** “How do we waken the council without overwhelming it?” the engineer asked. “Damn the schedule; a two-week delay is better than a scandal.” The sensitivity of the sensor, the accuracy of the survey, and the real cost of the work were all, yet again, in the same unhappy column.

“我们如何在不压垮委员会的前提下唤醒它？”工程师问。“管它什么日程；两周的延误好过一桩丑闻。”传感器的灵敏度、调查的准确性，以及工程的真实成本，又一次全都落在同一个不幸的栏目里。

**6.** The trial run ended with a small, useful result and one new constraint: the group would have to be awake to the difference between what they deem true and what they can prove. The choice that had a cost was the choice to publish the weak number first. Although we believe in the plan, the order of the moment is to try a smaller version, because a passionate and dramatically wrong forecast is not foresight. A promise can bind two people without binding the outcome, and a kind fall is still a fall.

试行以一个小小的有用结果和一条新的约束结束：小组必须清醒地看到他们“认为”为真与“能够证明”之间的差别。有代价的选择，是先公开那个难看的数字。虽然我们相信这个计划，但此刻的次序是先试一个更小的版本，因为一份热情而又错得离谱的预报不是远见。一个承诺可以把两个人绑在一起，却绑不住结果，而一次温和的跌倒仍然是跌倒。

### Exercises

- CH131-Q1 [context] What did Lin suggest they do?
  - options: test two rooms and let the results occur / cancel the survey / buy a new sensor / postpone for a year
- CH131-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “敏感；灵敏度”?
  - options: sensitivity / accuracy / vigor / foresight
- CH131-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: underestimate the winter / underestimate the goodness / underestimate the disgust / underestimate the happiness
- CH131-Q4 [cloze] Maya wrote that happiness is not a design ______.
  - options: criterion / prediction / prospect / postponement
- CH131-Q5 [recall] What was the new constraint of the trial run?
  - options: to be awake to the difference between belief and proof / to publish nothing / to shorten the delay / to buy two sensors

### Answers

- CH131-Q1: test two rooms and let the results occur - They chose a limited trial over a prediction.
- CH131-Q2: sensitivity - The instrument's sensitivity mattered more than expectation.
- CH131-Q3: underestimate the winter - Underestimate combines with a season, a cost, or a rival.
- CH131-Q4: criterion - Happiness cannot be used as a design criterion.
- CH131-Q5: to be awake to the difference between belief and proof - They committed to separating belief from evidence.

## CH132 - A Choice That Has a Cost: Pressure Builds / 有代价的选择：压力上升

- theme: 心理、决策、责任与人生选择

**1.** The pressure built during the second winter, and the price of the good name of the group was suddenly very high. “We must consider the value of the store as well as the story,” the director said. “A fixed rate is worth less than a fixed habit.” The state, the market, and the social interest were, yet again, in the same room.

压力在第二个冬天累积起来，而小组好名声的价格忽然变得非常高。“我们必须把这家店的价值和这个故事一起考虑，”主管说，“一个固定的费率不如一个固定的习惯值钱。”国家、市场和社会利益，又一次在同一个房间里。

**2.** The deep problem was that nobody wanted to carry the burden of a withdrawal. “Lay the offer on the table and let them refuse it,” Maya said. “An option that we never offer is an imitation of a choice.” The overwhelming part was the timing: the law required an answer during a week when nobody was in the office.

深层的问题在于没有人愿意承担撤出的重负。“把这份报价摊在桌上，让他们拒绝，”曼雅说，“一个我们从不给出的选项，是对选择的模仿。”压倒性的部分是时机：法律要求在一个没人在办公室的那一周里给出答复。

**3.** The cry of the tenants was reasonable and the instinct of the council was not. “Just because a policy is political does not mean it must be mean,” the treasurer said. “A rare chance and a smart advance could still meet in the middle.”

租客的呼声是合理的，而议会的本能不是。“仅仅因为一项政策是政治性的，并不意味着它必须是刻薄的，”司库说，“一个罕见的机会和一次聪明的进展，仍然可以在中间相遇。”

**4.** The advance they were offered was an addition to the budget with a deep condition attached: an equivalent cut somewhere else. “Do not excuse the burden with a toy,” Lin said. “The depth of the cut matters more than the height of the promise.”

他们得到的那项进展是对预算的一笔追加，附着一个深层的条件：在别处等额削减。“不要用一个玩具来为这个重负开脱，”林伟说，“削减的深度比承诺的高度更要紧。”

**5.** The fixed sums, the treasure of the archive, and the worthy names on the wall were all counted backward from the result. “Persistence is not deliberation,” the auditor said. “A deliberation that inflates the estimate is a gamble with public money.”

固定的金额、档案里的财宝，以及墙上那些值得尊敬的名字，全都是从结果往前倒数出来的。“坚持不是审议，”审计员说，“一场把估算吹大的审议，是用公共资金赌博。”

**6.** The shameful part was the withdrawal of the second audit. A rouse of hope, an invalid measurement, and a demand for respect: the pressure had produced a decision that nobody could call good, and the group had to decide whether to sign it. The policy was never just during the meeting, and the money under the political story was not nothing. The right and the social state had met before; the market, the law, the interest, and the government had all signed. A bad carry, a fixed miss, a high cry, and a respect that was equal: the price was worth it, and the deep value was in the store, not in the value of the option. Ahead of the meeting, a fan of the old scheme and a burden of paperwork were laid on the same table.

可耻的部分是第二次审计的撤回。一次希望的觉醒、一次无效的测量，以及对尊重的索求：压力促成了一项没人能称之为好的决定，而小组必须决定是否签字。这项政策在会议期间从来不是公正的，而政治故事底下的钱也不是无关紧要的。右派与社会国家从前见过面；市场、法律、利益集团和政府都已签了字。一次糟糕的搬运、一次固定的失误、一声高声的叫喊，以及一份平等的尊重：这个代价是值得的，而深厚的价值在那家店里，不在那个选项的价值里。会议之前，一个旧方案的拥护者和一堆文书负担被摆在了同一张桌上。

### Exercises

- CH132-Q1 [context] What did the director say a fixed rate is worth less than?
  - options: a fixed habit / a fixed price / a toy / a fan
- CH132-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “负担；重载”?
  - options: burden / withdrawal / timing / instinct
- CH132-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: inflate an estimate / inflate a toy / inflate a treasure / inflate a fan
- CH132-Q4 [cloze] Maya said an option that we never offer is an imitation of a ______.
  - options: choice / store / burden / cry
- CH132-Q5 [recall] What was the shameful part?
  - options: the withdrawal of the second audit / the addition to the budget / the cry of the tenants / the height of the promise

### Answers

- CH132-Q1: a fixed habit - A stable practice beats a stable price.
- CH132-Q2: burden - Nobody wanted to carry the burden of withdrawal.
- CH132-Q3: inflate an estimate - Inflate combines with an estimate, a price, or a claim.
- CH132-Q4: choice - An unoffered option is only an imitation of choice.
- CH132-Q5: the withdrawal of the second audit - The audit was pulled back.

## CH133 - A Choice That Has a Cost: The Disagreement / 有代价的选择：分歧

- theme: 心理、决策、责任与人生选择

**1.** The disagreement was about whose name would stand on the report, and these two friends had never argued about anything so precious. “Moreover, the architect is accountable to the client,” the director said. “Furthermore, a partial account is hazardous to everyone.” Hers was the safer road and yours was the braver one.

分歧关乎谁的名字会出现在报告上，而这两位朋友从未为如此珍贵的事争吵过。“而且，建筑师对客户负有责任，”主管说，“此外，一份偏袒的记述对每个人都是危险的。”她那条是更安全的路，你那条是更勇敢的路。

**2.** The cliff outside the office was occasionally used as a symbol: safe ground above, a dangerous drop below. “You shrug at the risk because it is the villager who dare not refuse,” the nephew of the client said. “That is not courage; it is astonishment dressed as bravery.”

办公室外的悬崖偶尔被当作一个象征：上面是安全的地面，下面是危险的落差。“你对风险耸耸肩，因为不敢拒绝的是村民，”客户的侄子说，“那不是勇气；那是被当成勇敢的惊愕。”

**3.** The heroic version of the story was also the tragic one. “An inventor and a bankrupt have the same instinct,” the niece said, shaking her head. “One is courageous and the other is merely dizzy.” The stainless reputation of the firm was, likewise, a supplement to the argument rather than a part of it.

这个故事英勇的版本也是悲剧的版本。“一个发明家和一个破产者有着同样的本能，”侄女摇着头说，“一个勇敢，另一个只是晕眩。”公司那无瑕疵的名声，同样只是这场论证的一份补充，而不是它的一部分。

**4.** The peculiarity of the case was that everyone involved was anxious about the same thing and nobody would say it. “We are terrified of being cowardly,” Maya wrote, “and so we take the riskiest option and call it principle.” The amazing part was how little difference the decision made to the outcome.

这个案子的奇特之处在于，所有相关的人都为同一件事忧虑，却没有人愿意说出来。“我们害怕变得怯懦，”曼雅写道，“于是我们选最冒险的那条路，然后把它叫做原则。”令人惊异的是，这个决定对结果的影响有多小。

**5.** The amazement in the room turned to grief when the heroism of the previous year was examined again. “Besides, a young couple's chances are not yours to spend,” Lin said. “The value of a value is that it can be considered before the price is paid.”

当上一年的英雄主义被重新审视时，屋里的惊异变成了悲伤。“再说，一对年轻夫妇的机会不是你该花掉的，”林伟说，“一种价值的价值在于它可以在付出代价之前被考虑。”

**6.** The compromise was to sign the report together, with the high figure and the low figure on the same page. The stronger claim would appear first, the weaker one second, and the name of whoever disagreed would be printed beside it. Moreover, these two things are either risky or hazardous, and everything depends upon whose name is on the top. The value and the price are too high for a try, although a strong claim can still appear. A peculiarity of the case is that a partial account is invariably more precious to the author than to the reader. Whom do we believe? The answer must be accepted, and a terrific outcome would grieve us; a good carry and a bad number are equal in the same ledger.

妥协的方案是一起签署报告，把高数字和低数字放在同一页上。较强的主张排在前面，较弱的后，而无论谁不同意，他的名字都会印在旁边。而且，这两样东西要么是冒险的，要么是危险的，而一切都取决于顶上是谁的名字。对一次尝试来说，价值和价格都太高，虽然一个强有力的主张仍然可以出现。这个案子的一处奇特之处是：一份偏袒的记述对作者来说，总是比对读者更珍贵。我们该相信谁？答案必须被接受，而一个极好的结果会让我们悲伤；一次漂亮的搬运和一个糟糕的数字，在同一本账簿里是相等的。

### Exercises

- CH133-Q1 [context] Who did the director say the architect is accountable to?
  - options: the client / the villager / the bank / the niece
- CH133-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “负有责任的”?
  - options: accountable / hazardous / precious / anxious
- CH133-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: take a risk / take a cliff / take a shrug / take an astonishment
- CH133-Q4 [cloze] Maya wrote that we are terrified of being ______.
  - options: cowardly / dizzy / terrific / partial
- CH133-Q5 [recall] What was the compromise?
  - options: sign the report together with both figures on one page / let the architect sign alone / withdraw the report / ask the nephew to decide

### Answers

- CH133-Q1: the client - The architect answers to the client.
- CH133-Q2: accountable - Accountability was the real subject of the dispute.
- CH133-Q3: take a risk - Take combines with a risk, a chance, or a decision.
- CH133-Q4: cowardly - Fear of cowardice drove the riskier choice.
- CH133-Q5: sign the report together with both figures on one page - They published both the strong and weak claims.

## CH134 - A Choice That Has a Cost: What the Numbers Hide / 有代价的选择：数字隐藏了什么

- theme: 心理、决策、责任与人生选择

**1.** The numbers hid a bitter riddle: an impressive gain that consisted entirely of one good month. “Therefore the trend is an insult to the reader,” the auditor said. “A symbolic pat on the back is not a systematic check, and an exact figure can still be a sour one.”

这些数字隐藏了一个苦涩的谜：一个令人印象深刻的增长，完全由一个月的好业绩构成。“因此这个趋势是对读者的侮辱，”审计员说，“一次象征性的拍肩不是一次系统的检查，而一个精确的数字仍然可能是一个酸涩的数字。”

**2.** The behavioral evidence was audio, and it was loud. “Consequently, an ambition devoted to a single quarter will necessarily wander,” Maya wrote. “The implement we need is a precautionary one, not a stylish one.” Soon the council would knock and ask for the perfect version of the same page.

行为层面的证据是音频，而且很响。“因此，献身于单一季度的野心必然会游荡，”曼雅写道，“我们需要的工具是预防性的，而不是时髦的。”很快委员会就会来敲门，索要同一页纸的完美版本。

**3.** The acceptable range was narrow and the excuse for missing it was not rational. “A dent in the record is a clue, not a crime,” the director said. “Be straightforward about the assumption and the reader will forgive the rest.”

可接受的范围很窄，而错过它的借口并不合乎理性。“记录上的一处凹痕是一条线索，不是一桩罪，”主管说，“对假设保持直率，读者就会原谅其余的。”

**4.** The irrational part was the vanity of the author. “A juicy quote and a silky paragraph will not make a dent disappear,” Lin said. “A dependable number is worth more than a reflective one, and both are worth more than a swipe at the opposition.”

不理性的部分是作者的虚荣。“一句多汁的引语和一段柔滑的段落不会让凹痕消失，”林伟说，“一个可靠的数字比一个沉思的数字更有价值，而两者都比对对手的一次挥击更有价值。”

**5.** The resolute answer was to publish the desertion figures alongside the growth figures. “These are the same room and the same simple choice,” the auditor said. “We can suggest that the low quarter was seasonal, and we cannot pretend that it did not occur.”

果断的答案是：把流失的数字与增长的数字放在一起公布。“这些是同一个房间和同一个简单的选择，”审计员说，“我们可以暗示那个低季度是季节性的，却无法假装它没有发生过。”

**6.** The choosy reader would want the whole series, and the whole series was now required by the term of the contract. The numbers would hide nothing, and the group would have to live with what they showed. Therefore the hard number arrived soon, and it was not perfect: the series consisted necessarily of one loud knock and one quiet scream. Consequently, we must wander through every acceptable implement and comprise the exact clue in the straightforward line. A bitter ambition devoted to a rational story is still irrational, and a pat on the back is not a behavioral finding. The chance to require a better figure is now. A stylish and juicy vanity is not a dependable swipe, and the excitement of the entire series must be checked systematically. We intend to test the feeling of the reader as well. Whom shall we ask, and whose name stands on the page, and is the number good yet? The reader would seem to want the whole series.

挑剔的读者会想要整个序列，而整个序列如今是合同条款所要求的。数字将不再隐藏任何东西，而小组必须与它们所显示的东西共处。因此，那个难看的数字很快就来了，而它并不完美：这个序列必然由一声响亮的敲击和一声安静的尖叫组成。结果，我们必须在每一件可接受的工具里游荡，并在那条直白的线上包含那条准确的线索。一份献身于一个理性故事的苦涩野心，仍然是不理性的，而在背上拍一下不是一项行为学发现。要求一个更好数字的机会就在现在。一种时髦而多汁的虚荣不是一次可靠的挥击，而整个序列的兴奋必须被系统地核查。我们打算也测试读者的感受。我们该问谁，而页上放着谁的名字，这个数字现在算好了吗？读者似乎想要整个序列。

### Exercises

- CH134-Q1 [context] What did the impressive gain consist of?
  - options: one good month / a year of growth / two audits / an audio recording
- CH134-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “可信任的；可靠的”?
  - options: dependable / reflective / symbolic / precautionary
- CH134-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: make a dent / make an insult / make a riddle / make a swipe
- CH134-Q4 [cloze] The auditor said a symbolic pat on the back is not a ______ check.
  - options: systematic / silky / juicy / stylish
- CH134-Q5 [recall] What did the group decide to publish?
  - options: the desertion figures alongside the growth figures / only the growth figures / the audio file only / a perfect summary

### Answers

- CH134-Q1: one good month - The whole gain came from a single month.
- CH134-Q2: dependable - A dependable figure beats a reflective one.
- CH134-Q3: make a dent - Make combines with a dent, a choice, or a mistake.
- CH134-Q4: systematic - Encouragement is not systematic checking.
- CH134-Q5: the desertion figures alongside the growth figures - They published losses beside gains.

## CH135 - A Choice That Has a Cost: A Better Question / 有代价的选择：更好的问题

- theme: 心理、决策、责任与人生选择

**1.** The better question came out of a long argument about authority: who has the right to make a decision that will outlive the person who makes it? “We think about the election and not about the ten years after it,” the chair said. “A ruling that nobody can revise is not a rule; it is a veto on the future.”

那个更好的问题出在一场关于权威的漫长争论里：谁有权做出一个将比做决定的人活得更久的决定？“我们想的是选举，而不是它之后的十年，”主席说，“一项没人能修订的裁定不是规则；它是对未来的否决权。”

**2.** The sense of the room was that the past had been treated as a guarantee. “Our planning has been built on one assumption, and the assumption is a fashion,” Maya wrote. “We are responsible for a selection of facts, not for all of them, and the waste is in the ones we discarded.”

屋里的感觉是：过去一直被当作一份担保。“我们的规划建立在一个假定之上，而这个假定是一种时尚，”曼雅写道，“我们只对一部分事实负责，而不是全部，浪费就在我们丢弃的那些里。”

**3.** The hearing produced a representation of the risk that nobody had prepared for: a variable that was treated as secondary, and a hedge that had become the whole venture. “Be conscious of the motivation behind a number,” the auditor said. “A coherent report can still be a rude one.”

听证会产出了一份没人准备好的风险陈述：一个被当作次要的变量，以及一道已经变成整笔冒险的对冲。“要意识到一个数字背后的动机，”审计员说，“一份连贯的报告仍然可能是一份粗鲁的报告。”

**4.** The abstract part of the question was the hardest: what kind of thinking do we reward? A cue from the dial, an inspiration from the sole survivor of the last flood, and a decision made in peril: the room had a dozen answers and no method for choosing between them.

这个问题抽象的部分最难：我们奖励的是哪种思考？刻度盘上的一个提示、来自上一场洪水唯一幸存者的灵感，以及在险境中做出的一个决定：屋里有十几种答案，却没有在它们之间做选择的方法。

**5.** “Do not speculate about the dead,” the boatman said. “Consider the living.” The daring part of the proposal was to publish the disagreement itself, with the condensation of six months of argument on a single page, and the careful record of who had said what.

“不要揣测死者，”船夫说，“考虑活着的人。”这份提案的大胆之处在于把分歧本身公布出来，把六个月的争论浓缩在一页纸上，并仔细记录谁说了什么。

**6.** Therefore the better question was not what the group should decide, but who should be able to revise it. The price of that question was high, the good and the bad answers would both be carried forward, and the moment had arrived to write it down. Against the history of the valley, the value of a selective record was the only thing we could find. The mind does not die with an author, and then the archive must answer for itself; the sole and the ruling were the same word in a different sense. We had to find a way to keep the mind of the project alive after its authors moved on.

因此，更好的问题不是小组该决定什么，而是谁应当有权修订它。这个问题的代价很高，好的和坏的答案都会被带向未来，而写下它的时刻已经到了。与这个山谷的历史相对照，一份有选择的记录的价值，是我们唯一能找到的东西。心智不会随着作者死去，而此后档案必须为自己作答；唯一与裁定，是同一个词在不同意义上的用法。我们必须找到一种办法，让这个项目的心智在它的作者们离开之后继续活着。

### Exercises

- CH135-Q1 [context] What did the chair say a ruling nobody can revise is?
  - options: a veto on the future / a guarantee / a fashion / a cue
- CH135-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “否决权”?
  - options: veto / peril / hedge / dial
- CH135-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: make a decision / make a peril / make a condensation / make a sole
- CH135-Q4 [cloze] The boatman said not to speculate about the dead but to consider the ______.
  - options: living / ruling / hearing / variable
- CH135-Q5 [recall] What was the better question?
  - options: who should be able to revise the decision / how much the decision costs / when the election is held / which facts to discard

### Answers
