- CH135-Q1: a veto on the future - An unrevised ruling binds those who come after.
- CH135-Q2: veto - The rule functioned as a veto.
- CH135-Q3: make a decision - Make combines with a decision, a choice, or a mistake.
- CH135-Q4: living - He redirected attention from the past to the present.
- CH135-Q5: who should be able to revise the decision - She reframed the problem around revisability.

## CH136 - A Choice That Has a Cost: The Second Attempt / 有代价的选择：第二次尝试

- theme: 心理、决策、责任与人生选择

**1.** The second attempt was designed to answer a question about life rather than about money: what should a person feel when the work he built is handed to somebody else? “He himself shall decide,” the director said. “She herself shall decide. And the future will refer to neither of them by name.”

第二次尝试的设计目的，是回答一个关于生活而不是关于钱的问题：当一个人亲手建起来的工作被交到别人手里，他应当有什么感受？“他自己来决定，”主管说，“她自己也来决定。而未来不会用名字提到他们中的任何一个。”

**2.** The behavior of the two friends was studied by a psychologist during the whole week. “Affect is not character,” she warned. “A circumstance can make an honest person behave badly, and a reform can make a bad habit look like a moral choice.”

这两位朋友的行为被一位心理学家研究了整整一周。“情感不是品格，”她警告说，“一种处境可以让一个诚实的人行为糟糕，而一场改革可以让一个坏习惯看起来像一个道德选择。”

**3.** The complex part was the appointment of a successor. “We retain the responsibility and we appoint the accountability,” the auditor said. “The alternative is a psychological knot: anxiety about the fate of the archive and no authority over it.”

复杂的部分是继任者的任命。“我们保留责任，并任命可问责性，”审计员说，“替代方案是一个心理学上的结：对档案命运的焦虑，以及对它毫无权限。”

**4.** The psychology of the pair was striking. “Every automatic click of the mouse carries an ethic inside it,” Lin said. “The ethics of a small team are embedded in its habits, not in its rules.” A corruption of the process would be visible only to a reader with a conscience.

这对搭档的心理状态很醒目。“鼠标的每一次自动点击里都含有一种伦理，”林伟说，“一支小团队的伦理嵌在它的习惯里，而不在它的规则里。”流程的腐败，只有一个有良心的读者才看得见。

**5.** The optional parts of the handover were the souvenir, the kite, the yoga mat, and the stormy letter of resignation that nobody sent. “An idealistic plan and a liver complaint have the same effect on a schedule,” Maya wrote, and the joke landed because it was true.

交接中可选的几样东西是纪念品、风筝、瑜伽垫，以及那封没人寄出的暴风雨般的辞职信。“一个理想主义的计划和一场肝病对日程有着同样的影响，”曼雅写道，而这个笑话之所以好笑，是因为它是真的。

**6.** The second attempt consisted of one page, one signature, and one rule that the friends agreed to obey under pressure. The age of the project was now part of its value, and the water in the well was still the same water. It was important to dream of a secure archive; the evil was not in the ambition but in the corruption of it. The air, the water, and the well were the same as before, and the process was the only thing under our control. These things we never said out loud: that we had thought about leaving, and that the win mattered less than the method. During the second year the authority to appoint was ours, and now it is not. Under it all lay a simple fact, and it was just as true the second time. We had to find a way into the live archive, and it was left to us.

第二次尝试由一页纸、一个签名，以及一条两位朋友同意在压力下遵守的规则构成。这个项目的年龄如今成了它价值的一部分，而井里的水仍然是同样的水。梦想一个安全的档案是重要的；恶不在那份雄心，而在它的腐坏。空气、水和井都和从前一样，而流程是我们唯一能控制的东西。有些事我们从未说出口：我们想过离开，而胜利没有方法重要。第二年的时候，任命的权力在我们手里，而现在不在。在这一切之下有一个简单的事实，而它第二次同样成立。我们必须找到进入那份活的档案的路，而这件事落在了我们身上。

### Exercises

- CH136-Q1 [context] What did the second attempt answer a question about?
  - options: life rather than money / money rather than life / the election / the weather
- CH136-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “良心”?
  - options: conscience / corruption / anxiety / fate
- CH136-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: appoint a successor / appoint a kite / appoint a souvenir / appoint a liver
- CH136-Q4 [cloze] The psychologist warned that affect is not ______.
  - options: character / behavior / ethics / reform
- CH136-Q5 [recall] What did the auditor say the alternative is?
  - options: a psychological knot / a larger budget / a new archive / an optional souvenir

### Answers

- CH136-Q1: life rather than money - The handover question was about meaning.
- CH136-Q2: conscience - Only a reader with a conscience would notice the corruption.
- CH136-Q3: appoint a successor - Appoint combines with a successor, a committee, or a date.
- CH136-Q4: character - Emotion is not the same as character.
- CH136-Q5: a psychological knot - Accountability without authority creates anxiety.

## CH137 - A Choice That Has a Cost: Outside Pressure / 有代价的选择：外部压力

- theme: 心理、决策、责任与人生选择

**1.** The outside pressure arrived as a call from the publisher's receptionist, on an occasion that nobody had marked in advance: the whole archive would be printed, or the contract would cease. “Whatever we do, the outcome is the same,” the director said. “We have barely three weeks, and the topic is not ours alone.”

外部压力以一个电话的形式到来，来自出版社的前台，而这是一个没人提前标记过的场合：要么整份档案付印，要么合同终止。“无论我们怎么做，结果都一样，”主管说，“我们只剩不到三周，而这个话题不只是我们的。”

**2.** The hesitant answer was to renovate the old chapters temporarily and publish them afterward. “Do it beforehand,” the receptionist said. “A sudden blessing is still a blessing.” Somewhere in the building a printer started, and the hurry became real.

迟疑的答案是先把旧章节临时翻新，之后再出版。“那就提前做，”前台说，“突如其来的祝福仍然是祝福。”大楼里某处一台打印机启动了，而匆忙变成了真的。

**3.** The loyal reader, the shy volunteer, and the inspirational teacher were the only people who could check the whole series. “A worthless paragraph is worse than a missing one,” Maya wrote. “A miraculous figure is usually a deficient one.”

那位忠诚的读者、那位害羞的志愿者和那位鼓舞人心的老师，是唯一能通查整个系列的人。“一段没有价值的段落比一段缺失的段落更糟，”曼雅写道，“一个奇迹般的数字通常是一个有缺陷的数字。”

**4.** The instinctive reaction of the group was to glance at the mode of the distribution and stop there. “A holy number and a momentary one look alike on a page,” the auditor said. “The pronoun in the sentence tells you who is responsible, and the verb tells you when.”

小组的本能反应是瞥一眼分布的模式然后就停下。“一个神圣的数字和一个瞬间的数字在一页纸上看起来一样，”审计员说，“句子里的代词告诉你谁该负责，动词告诉你什么时候。”

**5.** The thirst for a clean ending was strong, and the ending would not be clean. “A virtuous account and a sacrificial one are different kinds of writing,” Lin said. “One costs money; the other costs somebody.”

对干净结局的渴望很强烈，而结局不会是干净的。“一份品德高尚的记述和一份牺牲式的记述是两种不同的写作，”林伟说，“一种花的是钱；另一种花的是某个人。”

**6.** The pressure produced a decision that was neither heroic nor worthless: an hourly check for three weeks, a temporary revision, and a promise that the next edition would carry everything. Whenever the group published one page, the editor asked for another, and the work never once became easy. Although we believe in the build, the effect of everything else is too hard to measure. He himself shall feel the pressure, and she should, too. Therefore we shall try to bring the whole record to the press, and the important thing is the life of the reader rather than the life of the author. It was upon this page that the two friends agreed, and it is to this page that the archive returns. The hard part is not the printing; it is the choosing, and everything else is a matter of time. We want to say, before anything else, that we have tried ever since: somehow, anywhere, sometime, we scarcely had a saying in it, and no guarantee whatsoever, because the deadline was not ours to move. There was no bridge chapter left to write, and that, too, was a fact they had to publish.

压力促成了一项既不英勇也不无价值的决定：连续三周每小时核对、一次临时修订，以及一个承诺——下一版会带上全部内容。每当小组公布一页，编辑就再要一页，而这份工作一次也没有变得容易。虽然我们相信这次建构，但其他一切的效果都太难衡量。他自己将要感受那股压力，她也应当如此。因此我们将试着把整份记录送到印刷厂，而要紧的是读者的生命，而不是作者的生命。两位朋友正是在这一页上达成了一致，而档案也回到这一页。困难的部分不是印刷，而是选择，其余的一切都只是时间问题。我们想在任何事之前先说一句：我们此后一直在尝试：不知怎么，无论在何处，总在某个时候，我们几乎说不上话，也毫无任何保证，因为那个期限不是我们能挪动的。已经没有一座“桥”的章节可写了，而这一点，也是他们必须公布的事实。

### Exercises

- CH137-Q1 [context] What was the choice the publisher offered?
  - options: print the whole archive or the contract would cease / raise the price or delay / hire a receptionist or stop / renovate or sell
- CH137-Q2 [meaning] Which word means “奇迹的；不可思议的”?
  - options: miraculous / momentary / instinctive / virtuous
- CH137-Q3 [collocation] Choose the natural phrase.
  - options: cease production / cease a pronoun / cease a glance / cease a thirst
- CH137-Q4 [cloze] The auditor said the pronoun tells you who is responsible and the verb tells you ______.
  - options: when / where / why / how much
- CH137-Q5 [recall] What did the group promise about the next edition?
  - options: it would carry everything / it would be shorter / it would be cheaper / it would be secret

### Answers

- CH137-Q1: print the whole archive or the contract would cease - The publisher set a binary condition.
- CH137-Q2: miraculous - A miraculous figure is often a defective one.
- CH137-Q3: cease production - Cease combines with production, a practice, or an argument.
- CH137-Q4: when - The verb locates the action in time.
- CH137-Q5: it would carry everything - They promised a complete next edition.
